### [1] [Joint Bayesian Inference of Dense Matter and f(Q) Gravity with Nuclear and Neutron Star Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14553)
*N. K. Patra,Sneha Pradhan,P. K. Sahoo,Kai Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在对称 teleparallel f(Q) 引力框架下，结合核物质状态方程（EOS）的不确定性，通过贝叶斯联合推断方法，同时约束致密物质与修正引力参数，并利用多信使天文观测（如GW170817、NICER）和核物理实验数据进行综合分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为厘清致密星体物理（如中子星物态）与修正引力理论（对称 teleparallel f(Q) 引力）之间的耦合关系，并系统评估 EOS 不确定性对引力参数限制的影响。

Method: 采用线性 f(Q) 模型，基于核饱和密度附近的泰勒展开 EOS，开展核物质参数与修正引力参数的联合贝叶斯推断；整合核饱和性质、手征有效场论计算、重离子碰撞、核经验数据、GW170817潮汐形变后验分布及NICER质量-半径测量等多源约束。

Result: 对典型1.4倍太阳质量中子星，得到半径 R_{1.4}=11.28^{+0.35}_{-0.31} km，潮汐形变 Λ_{1.4}=151.4^{+44.9}_{-31.2}，最大质量 M_max=2.22^{+0.29}_{-0.16} M_⊙；修正引力参数与多数星体观测量仅呈弱直接相关，但在引力参数内部存在显著简并。

Conclusion: 必须考虑 EOS 不确定性，才能准确刻画致密物质物理与修正引力之间的相互作用；本工作为利用核物理与多信使中子星观测检验对称 teleparallel 引力提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: We investigate neutron star (NS) properties in symmetric teleparallel $f(Q)$ gravity while consistently accounting for uncertainties in the dense matter equation of state (EOS). We focus on the linear $f(Q)$ model and perform a joint Bayesian inference of the nuclear matter and modified gravity parameters using a Taylor expansion EOS around nuclear saturation density. The analysis incorporates complementary constraints from nuclear saturation properties, chiral effective field theory calculations of pure neutron matter, heavy-ion collision, and empirical nuclear data, the GW170817 tidal deformability posterior, and NICER mass--radius measurements. The resulting posterior distributions simultaneously constrain the dense matter and gravity sectors and quantify their correlations within a common framework. For a canonical $1.4\,M_\odot$ NS, we obtain $R_{1.4}=11.28^{+0.35}_{-0.31}\,\mathrm{km}$ and $Λ_{1.4}=151.4^{+44.9}_{-31.2}$, while the maximum mass is constrained to $M_{\rm max}=2.22^{+0.29}_{-0.16}\,M_\odot$. The modified gravity parameters exhibit only weak direct correlations with most stellar observables, while a pronounced degeneracy remains within the gravity sector. Our results show that accounting for EOS uncertainties is essential for quantifying the interplay between dense matter physics and modified gravity and provide a systematic framework for confronting symmetric teleparallel gravity with nuclear and multimessenger NS observations.

</details>


### [2] [Optical and ADM Tomography from Directed Causal Arrival Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14554)
*Maxim Churilov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于定向零测时和时空体积的洛伦兹几何局域重构方法，在给定钟叶结构和空间坐标规范的前提下，利用静态/稳态/时变情况下的到达时间数据，实现了对光学度规、共形尺度及ADM度规的代数可逆、稳定且可验证的重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现背景依赖的时空几何操作性重构，避免依赖背景无关量子理论，需在给定钟叶与坐标规范下，仅用可观测的标量测量（如光信号到达时间与体积密度）来局域确定洛伦兹度规。

Method: 基于定向零测时与时空体积密度，结合ADM分解，推导未来零锥面及其代数逆；设计最小方向采样方案（d(d+3)/2个单向到达测量），建立半代数一致性检验，并分析短时冻结估计与有限分辨率反演的误差界。

Result: 证明d(d+3)/2个单向到达测量在d维空间中足以稳定局域重构度规；给出误差界O(√a + ζ)，并实现代数与数值双重验证；提出具有认证鲁棒性和误差拒绝边界的半代数一致性检验。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了操作性、规范依赖的时空层析定理，不依赖于背景无关量子理论，为引力实验与类比引力系统中的几何重构提供了严格数学基础与实用算法框架。

Abstract: We formulate a local reconstruction of Lorentzian geometry from directed null arrival times and spacetime volume, assuming a supplied clock foliation and spatial coordinate gauge. Static arrival data recover the optical metric $\bar h=N^{-2}h$, while a normalized volume density fixes the remaining conformal scale up to a global constant. In the stationary case, the Randers arrival function separates optical geometry and shift. For time-dependent ADM metrics we derive the exact future null indicatrix, its algebraic inverse, and a minimal finite-direction sampling scheme: $d(d+3)/2$ one-way arrival measurements suffice in $d$ spatial dimensions, are necessary for stable local recovery within the specified scalar-measurement class, and admit an exact semialgebraic consistency test with certified robustness and rejection margins under bounded measurement error. A short-time freezing estimate and finite-resolution inverse give metric error $O(a/ε+ε+ζ)$ for arrival error $a$, probe scale $ε$, and volume error $ζ$; optimizing yields $O(\sqrt a+ζ)$. Algebraic and numerical checks reproduce the inversion and stability formulas. The result is an operational, gauge-conditional tomography theorem; it does not infer the clocks or arrival oracle from a background-free quantum theory.

</details>


### [3] [Energy exchange confined to the perturbations: the frozen dark sector confronted with Planck, DESI, supernovae and cosmic shear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14556)
*M. G. Richarte,L. F. Guimarães,S. J. Landau,M. Leizerovich*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了暗物质与暗能量在微扰尺度上发生能量交换的‘冻结模型’，利用多组宇宙学观测数据（Planck、DESI、DES、KiDS等）约束耦合参数Σ，并分析其对H₀张力、S₈张力及CMB各向异性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为缓解ΛCDM模型中不同观测数据间存在的宇宙学参数张力（如H₀和S₈），探索暗物质-暗能量相互作用作为可能的新物理机制。

Method: 采用冻结模型（背景演化同ΛCDM，仅微扰层面存在无量纲耦合参数Σ），结合Planck 2018 CMB与透镜数据、DESI DR2 BAO、DES超新星（Dovekie校准）、KiDS-Legacy与DES Y3弱引力剪切数据，进行贝叶斯参数估计与模型比较。

Result: Planck单独给出Σ = −0.22 ± 0.11；加入DESI和超新星后Σ = −0.24 ± 0.09（ln B = 1.5），但该偏好在排除强耦合区域后消失；加入剪切数据后Σ趋近于0（0.01 ± 0.08），且模型被显著排斥（ln B = −1.3）；H₀与宇宙年龄始终与ΛCDM一致，SH0ES H₀偏差仍达4.4σ；Σ < 0增强结构增长，加剧Planck–剪切S₈张力（从1.9σ升至2.5–2.7σ）。

Conclusion: 冻结模型虽可部分缓解BAO/超新星与Planck之间的H₀相关张力，但会恶化S₈张力，且整体证据不支持其优于ΛCDM；未来可通过ISW效应、低红移剪切信号及星系团计数红移分布进一步独立检验该耦合。

Abstract: We present cosmological constraints on the frozen model, in which dark matter and dark energy exchange energy only at the level of the perturbations. The expansion history is that of flat $Λ$CDM, while on sub-horizon scales a dimensionless coupling $Σ$ enhances ($Σ<0$) or suppresses ($Σ>0$) the growth of the dark-matter density contrast. We use Planck 2018 CMB and lensing data, DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO), the DES five-year supernovae (Dovekie recalibration), and the KiDS-Legacy and DES Year 3 cosmic-shear amplitudes, corrected for the model's growth. Planck alone gives $Σ=-0.22\pm0.11$, a preference driven by the lensing smoothing of the acoustic peaks and the low large-scale temperature power. Since BAO and supernovae do not constrain $Σ$, the coupling acts only through the Planck posterior, lowering the Planck--DESI tension from $1.9σ$ to $1.0$--$1.5σ$, and that between Planck plus supernovae and DESI from $2.4$--$2.5σ$ to $1.7$--$2.1σ$. Combining Planck, DESI and supernovae we find $Σ=-0.24\pm0.09$ and $\ln B=1.5$ relative to $Λ$CDM, a preference that vanishes when the strongest-coupling region is excluded. The enhanced growth, however, raises the Planck--shear tension from $1.9σ$ to $2.5$--$2.7σ$. With shear included, $Σ=0.01\pm0.08$ and $S_8=0.808\pm0.025$, while $\ln B=-1.3$ favors $Λ$CDM. $H_0$ and the age of the Universe agree with $Λ$CDM for every data combination, and the SH0ES discrepancy remains at $4.4σ$. Without shear, the favored coupling predicts half the $Λ$CDM integrated Sachs--Wolfe amplitude at $z=0.5$ and an enhanced shear signal in the lowest tomographic bins. These signatures and the redshift distribution of cluster counts test the coupling independently of the amplitude of the fluctuations.

</details>


### [4] [Spins do not add: why rotating stars do not help spin-up black holes in binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14610)
*Filippo Camilloni,Konrad Topolski,Keneth Miler,Luciano Rezzolla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发现，在黑洞-中子星并合过程中，中子星自旋增大反而导致最终黑洞自旋减小，这与黑洞双星情况相反；该反直觉现象源于潮汐瓦解引发的非线性动力学过程，导致大量角动量通过抛射物质流失，甚至可与引力波辐射的角动量相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究黑洞-中子星并合中最终黑洞自旋演化规律，挑战传统基于黑洞双星的认知。

Method: 通过大量不同自旋和质量比的黑洞-中子星并合数值模拟，分析潮汐瓦解过程中的角动量输运机制。

Result: 发现中子星自旋增大导致最终黑洞自旋减小；潮汐瓦解驱动的动力学抛射物造成显著角动量损失，部分情况下与引力波辐射的角动量损失相当。

Conclusion: 黑洞-中子星并合中的自旋演化比预期更复杂，不能简单类比黑洞双星，并对恒星级黑洞自旋演化的建模提出新挑战。

Abstract: It is well-known that in the merger of a black-hole binary with spins aligned with the orbital angular momentum, the spin of the final black hole always increases with the spin of the components. We show that this very intuitive behavior is violated in black hole--neutron star binaries, where the spin of the final black hole actually decreases as the spin of the neutron star increases. Using a large set of binaries with different spins and mass ratios, we identify the mechanism behind this surprising result in the nonlinear dynamics of the tidal disruption, which drives a substantial loss of angular momentum via dynamical ejecta, in some cases comparable to that radiated via gravitational waves. Our results highlight that the spin evolution of stellar-mass black holes involved in binary mergers is different from what normally assumed, far more complex, and interesting.

</details>


### [5] [Conic Tilings of Black Hole Images by Radial Closest Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14625)
*George N. Wong,Andrew Mummery*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞图像中光线的最近接近半径等值线在Kerr和Plebanski-Demianski时空中的几何结构，发现其为圆锥曲线，并给出物理弧段选取的局部判据及其适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞图像中光线传播的径向结构，建立图像坐标与时空几何之间的直接几何映射关系。

Method: 通过回溯光线轨迹定义最近接近半径，分析其等值线在Bardeen坐标及PD类时空局部坐标下的几何形式；利用PD场方程导出的系数恒等式和主零方向对齐性质，推导物理弧段的局部斜率判据，并构造包含临界曲线的多项式方程。

Result: 证明在满足正质量、正能量及径向度规函数为正的连通区域内，径向势能斜率的局部检验足以确定物理弧段；给出r=0区域及Ovcharenko-Podolsky扩展中该判据失效的反例；导出统一描述Kerr和PD临界曲线的单个多项式方程。

Conclusion: 黑洞图像的‘返回图像’可被系统解释为光子径向传播的几何地图，该映射不依赖于辐射模型，提升了从观测图像反演时空几何的能力。

Abstract: Each point in a black hole image corresponds to a light ray, whose minimum radial coordinate when traced backward from the observer measures how deeply it probes the spacetime. We show that contours of constant closest-approach radius form arcs of conic sections in standard Bardeen image coordinates for Kerr, and in natural local image coordinates for finite-radius observers in the broader Plebanski-Demianski (PD) family of Einstein-Maxwell solutions. At each radius, the condition of vanishing radial velocity defines a complete conic; the physical contour consists of those points whose rays reach that radius before encountering an outer turning point. For positive Killing energy and positive mass, we prove that throughout connected positive-radius regions with positive radial metric function and allowed motion at both endpoints, a local test of the radial potential's slope is sufficient to select the physical arcs. This simplification follows from a coefficient identity imposed by the PD field equations and alignment of the principal null directions. We give explicit counterexamples in a domain crossing r=0 and in the separable Ovcharenko-Podolsky extension, where an outer turning point invalidates the local test. The conic family also yields a single polynomial equation containing the critical curve in Kerr and PD. These results turn the returning image into a geometric map of radial photon propagation, independent of the emission model. (This abstract has been abridged to meet the arXiv character limit.)

</details>


### [6] [Post-adiabatic effects sourced by radiation reaction for inspiraling, eccentric binaries in post-Newtonian theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14627)
*Rohit S. Chandramouli,Nijaid Arredondo,Lidia J. Gomes Da Silva,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文使用重求和多尺度分析方法推导了偏心双星系统在辐射反作用下的首阶后绝热修正，揭示了绝热近似精度对初始轨道相位的依赖性，并指出初始条件对演化解的影响及波形 gauge 依赖性的消除机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝热近似被广泛用于引力波建模，但其在高度偏心、长寿命双星系统中的精度尚不明确，尤其受初始轨道相位影响，需系统研究后绝热修正。

Method: 采用与近恒等平均变换密切相关的重求和多尺度分析方法，从领头阶辐射反作用出发推导首阶后绝热修正，并分析初始条件与坐标选取对演化方程和波形的影响。

Result: 发现偏心率与半长轴的演化方程本身无显式首阶后绝热修正，但其解通过初始条件引入该修正；导出了抵消轨道参数 gauge 依赖性的引力波极化修正项；指出在径向转向点初始化时首阶振荡消失，而其他位置初始化会导致显著相位误差，首阶后绝热修正可大幅减小该误差。

Conclusion: 初始条件对绝热及后绝热近似的精度至关重要，尤其对长寿命高偏心双星；忽略初始化效应将导致错误评估模型准确性，必须纳入引力波波形建模与数据分析中。

Abstract: The adiabatic approximation, in which eccentric binaries evolve under orbit-averaged fluxes, is widely used in gravitational-wave and astrophysical modeling. We use resummed multiple-scale analysis, which is closely related to near-identity averaging transformations, to derive post-adiabatic corrections from leading-order radiation reaction and explain how the accuracy of the adiabatic approximation depends on the initial orbital phase. While the secular evolution equations for the eccentricity and semi-major axis receive no explicit first post-adiabatic corrections, their solutions acquire such corrections through the initial conditions. We also derive the change in the functional form of the gravitational-wave polarizations that cancels the gauge dependence of the orbital elements at first post-adiabatic order, ensuring agreement between waveforms evaluated at the same physical event in different coordinates. At a radial turning point, the first post-adiabatic oscillations of the semi-major axis and eccentricity vanish, so the initial osculating energy and angular momentum agree with their orbit-averaged values up to second post-adiabatic corrections. When initialized elsewhere along the orbit, the adiabatic approximation can accumulate significant dephasing, which the first post-adiabatic approximation can substantially reduce. Both dephasings can change sign across parameter space, consistent with competing contributions from successive orders. Accounting for initialization is therefore essential for assessing the accuracy of these approximations, particularly for long-lived, highly eccentric binaries.

</details>


### [7] [Gravitational observables from a post-Carrollian expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14644)
*Max Niedermaier,Mahdi Sedighi Jafari*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过构建Carroll引力中的超局域Dirac可观测量，挑战了广义相对论中可观测量必为非局域的传统观点，并提出一种基于Carroll极限的‘后Carroll展开’方法来系统构造爱因斯坦引力中的局域Dirac可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为广义相对论因微分同胚不变性而缺乏局域可观测量，本文旨在质疑并修正这一归因，探索是否存在真正局域且物理意义明确的引力可观测量。

Method: 基于Carroll引力（爱因斯坦引力的超相对论极限）构造无穷多个精确的超局域Dirac可观测量；进而发展‘后Carroll展开’，以空间梯度或牛顿常数倒数的分数幂为展开参数，逐阶构造爱因斯坦引力中的局域Dirac可观测量。

Result: 首次在完全微分同胚不变的Carroll引力中发现无穷多精确超局域Dirac可观测量；提出并初步实现一种系统性构造爱因斯坦引力局域Dirac可观测量的‘后Carroll展开’框架，每阶截断均为局域。

Conclusion: 微分同胚不变性本身并不必然导致可观测量的非局域性；Carroll极限为构造爱因斯坦引力中的局域物理可观测量提供了新路径和有效工具。

Abstract: Einstein gravity, with a few exceptions, only admits nonlocal observables. This feature is often attributed to diffeomorphism invariance, since a gravitational field can thereby locally be transformed away. We show this attribution to be incorrect by constructing infinitely many exact ultralocal Dirac observables in (velocity dominated aka electric) Carroll gravity. The latter describes Einstein gravity's ultrarelativistic limit, is fully diffeomorphism invariant, and of independent interest in a variety of contexts. Starting from these Carroll observables a systematic construction of Dirac observables for Einstein gravity is outlined. The resulting "post-Carrollian" expansion is organized by the number of spatial gradients or fractional inverse powers of Newton's constant, where each partial sum is local.

</details>

*Nishita Jadoo,Zachariah B. Etienne*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的非正交参考度规实现方法，并在协变移动穿刺形式下验证了其在Kasner精确解和双黑洞并合模拟中的有效性与鲁棒性，展示了其对结构化网格平滑去细化的灵活性支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值相对论模拟大多采用正交参考度规，限制了网格设计的灵活性；为支持跨多尺度（如黑洞附近高分辨与波区低分辨）的高效模拟，需发展通用非正交参考3-度规实现。

Method: 实现了协变移动穿刺框架下的通用非正交参考3-度规；采用fisheye风格及球面/椭球形变形的参考度规，结合无自适应网格加密的结构化网格进行数值演化；对Kasner精确初值和双黑洞系统开展收敛性与波形提取验证。

Result: Kasner解演化结果随网格分辨率提高而收敛至精确解；双黑洞模拟在强变形参考度规下仍保持稳定，无数值病态；成功提取至ℓ=8的光滑引力波形；椭球形fisheye映射有效减少z方向网格点数。

Conclusion: 非正交参考度规是可行且稳健的，能灵活支持结构化网格的平滑去细化，为高精度、多尺度双黑洞数值模拟提供了新工具。

Abstract: To our knowledge, numerical relativity (NR) simulations in the covariant moving-puncture formalism have only been performed with orthogonal reference metrics. Here, we demonstrate a new general non-orthogonal reference 3-metric implementation. We first validate this reference metric implementation by numerically evolving the exact Kasner initial data with a fisheye-style reference 3-metric, demonstrating numerical results that converge to the exact solution with increasing grid resolution. Next, we validate our approach by evolving binary black holes (BBHs) in the moving-puncture formulation, from inspiral through merger and ringdown. Even with a large deformation in the reference geometry, introduced to allow a large change in grid refinement to enable the simulation to span the relevant length scales, from high resolutions near the BHs to low resolutions in the wave zone, no pathologies are observed. Smooth waveforms for up to $\ell=8$ are extracted on a grid without mesh refinement boundaries. We demonstrate the flexibility of tuning structured grids through the reference metric by employing spheroidal modifications of the spherical fisheye map to reduce the number of grid points in the $z$ direction when the orbit is in the $xy$-plane. These results support the use of smoothly derefined structured grids for BBH evolutions.

</details>


### [9] [Ernst-Potential Driven Recovery of Kerr with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14650)
*Veselin G. Filev,Stoytcho S. Yazadjiev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文使用人工神经网络通过最小化正则化的Ernst sigma模型作用量，直接恢复Kerr黑洞解，并在不同自旋参数下实现了高精度的度规分量重建和物理参数预测。

Transverse source motion during the observation makes their magnifications and relative time delay evolve, producing evolving amplitude and phase modulations absent from standard CW templates. Using lensed and unlensed injections in Gaussian noise, we quantify their effect on a Hough-based semicoherent search through a semicoherent mismatch. Recovery is governed by the competition between lensing amplification and waveform modulation. Smaller impact parameters generally improve recovery through stronger magnification, while higher transverse velocities partially offset this gain through faster modulation, especially at small impact parameters and short source-lens distances, where image interference is appreciable. The response also depends on the intrinsic signal strength. Loud signals exhibit small fractional changes, with net losses in some configurations, whereas weaker signals yield broader mismatch distributions extending to more negative values, indicating greater enhancement. A population study, sampling lensing parameters from astrophysically motivated velocity and source-lens distance distributions, reproduces the strong dependence on signal strength. Differences between populations are modest, but high-velocity, short source-lens distance populations tend towards larger mismatches. Lensing effects therefore cannot be accounted for by rescaling search sensitivity with the magnification alone; reinterpreting directed Galactic Center search sensitivities and upper limits under the lensing hypothesis requires injection studies with the full lensed waveform across the lensing parameter space.

</details>

Abstract: For a twist-free null hypersurface, the Hollands-Wald-Zhang (HWZ) representative $S_{\rm loc}=(4G)^{-1}\int(1-Vθ)dA$, local on each cross-section (cut), obeys an exact off-shell Raychaudhuri identity with no term linear in the expansion, so no final equilibrium cut is needed. Conversely, requiring this balance for positive affine parameter on every small bundle and null direction is equivalent to the Einstein equation, given conservation and the stated normalization. The Gauss-Bonnet combination adds no new modes: around its supersymmetric vacuum, the quadratic action is a rescaled copy of that of the two-derivative theory, and this fixes the spectrum of the Ricci tensor squared extension. We also compute the central charges $a$ and $c$ and the flavor central charge exactly in the couplings. The vacuum equation is $a$-maximization, and the kinetic terms of the massless graviton and vector multiplets are proportional to $c$ and to the flavor central charge, so that the critical points, where the massive spin-2 multiplet becomes degenerate with the graviton multiplet, are zeros of $c$. Finally, around supersymmetric AdS$_3\timesΣ_2$ backgrounds of the Gauss-Bonnet extension, the two-dimensional central charges are reproduced by $c$-extremization, while the spectrum is generically not unitary and contains towers of states whose energies stay bounded at large Kaluza-Klein levels. Abstract: It is well known that radiation produced by scattering an electron off multiple sources depends on the length of the mean free path between these scatterings. When this is sufficiently small, the spectrum is suppressed due to destructive interference, a famous result known as the Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal (LPM) effect. This is usually modelled approximately, with scattering centres treated linearly (e.g., as screened Coulomb potentials), high-energy probes and soft emitted photons. In this paper, we consider a scenario where multiple scatterings can be computed exactly: charged probe scattering on multiple null electromagnetic shockwaves. Here, the amplitudes for elastic scattering and single photon emission can be determined exactly, for arbitrary kinematics, in terms of the shock profiles in the transverse plane. We use these amplitudes to compute the radiation density (doubly-averaged over shock locations in the transverse plane and lightfront time) in the regimes of independent and interfering scatterings. For shock profiles which are radially symmetric in the transverse plane (including the ultraboosted Coulomb field), we recover the known LPM suppression, albeit with more general kinematics. The fusion diagram (or string-net) $\mathcal{F}$ on $\mathbb{B}$ is given by the Aasen-Fendley-Mong construction. There is also a ``bedding surface'' $\mathbb{S}$ between $\mathbb{D}$ and $\mathbb{B}$, and the height variables (or Ising spins inside/outside of the domain wall) are naturally living on $\mathbb{S}$. The bulk spacetime is discretized by the 2-skeleton $P$ which interpolates $\mathcal{D}$ and $\mathcal{F}$. When the defect $\mathcal{D}$ is absent, the 2-skeleton $P$ ends on the bedding surface $\mathbb{S}$ and $P$ is interpreted as the bulk worldsheet of the string-net $\mathcal{F}$.

</details>

Abstract: The Lorentzian gravitational path integral over homogeneous and isotropic metrics in a closed universe with positive cosmological constant is dominated by complex saddle points, such as the Hartle-Hawking saddles. For the one- and two-site Feynman-Witten tree diagrams, we derive difference equations in the kinematic variables which close on a finite set of sums, giving a discrete kinematic flow. Abstract: In quantum error correction (QEC), the goal is to engineer an encoding that protects logical information against the erasure of a physical subsystem. In local many-body quantum systems, by contrast, nature provides a subsystem inclusion $\mN\subset \mM$ in a fixed reference state $\ketΩ$. Asking whether the inclusion admits a reference-preserving code that protects subsystem $\mN$ against erasure of the complement is equivalent to asking whether $\mN$ is preserved under the modular dynamics of $\mM$ in this state (modular autonomy). We define the subsystem flow, which measures the departure from this error correction regime. Away from autonomy, the infinitesimal flow quantifies relative-entropy loss, while the history algebra and persistent core describe which observables the modular orbit generates and retains. We call an inclusion ergodic when modular orbits generate everything and retain nothing. Analytic continuation of subsystem flow yields Petz recovery maps and a family of positive inclusion parent Hamiltonians. We show that, in general, evolving with these parent Hamiltonians allows $\mN$ to leave $\mM$, and in some cases, it can generate the entire $\mM\vee\mM'$. Inclusions with a common cyclic and separating vector generate everything, and standard inclusions of factors, such as those of nested subregions in QFT, are ergodic. Moreover, the $L^2$-nuclearity property implies that the spectrum of parent Hamiltonians is discrete. We define a dissipative semigroup by repeating the Petz recovery map, whose fixed-point algebra is the modular persistent core and whose spectrum is gapped under the assumption of $L^2$-nuclearity. We conjecture that the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of inclusion parent Hamiltonians can be used to define local spectral measures of quantum chaos.

</details>

We demonstrate that the same Majorana fermions sit at the junctions of the symmetry lines, exactly where the fusion of two $U(1)$ elements wraps around the circle. From the lattice algebra of lines and junctions we derive the $R$- and $F$-symbols in the bulk and on the gapped boundary, and show that for odd $k$ the boundary $F$-symbol obeys the Gu-Wen relation for the $U(1)$ symmetry defect, without passing through a bosonic shadow. Restricting to $\mathbb{Z}_2\subset U(1)$, we reproduce the mod-4 anomalous phase from a mapping torus and the fermionic stacking rule. As a simple application of this SymTFT, we also determine which subgroups $\mathbb{Z}_d\subset U(1)$ can be gauged and the symmetry of the resulting theories. This is a first step towards deriving, from the anomaly inflow of a continuous symmetry, the junction data of global anomalies beyond group cohomology.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate inducing data for three dimensional asymptotically flat BMS gravity on hyperbolic Virasoro orbits with winding, which contain no constant supermomentum, and the restriction of formal smooth equivariant sections to global Lorentz orbits. Using the developing map of the associated Hill operator we rederive their stabilizer and show that, in the spin double cover, it is the direct product of a line with a cyclic group of even order. The character of the cyclic factor is therefore a discrete inducing label independent of the continuous one, unlike for the massive BMS particles, where it is fixed by the spin. At points with the corresponding finite rotational symmetry, restriction of formal smooth equivariant sections to a global Lorentz orbit yields the finite cyclic equivariance condition, while an independently defined unitary global branch realizes the associated finite difference equation. At points with timelike projected momentum we derive the resulting ladder of Poincare helicities, show that the projected rest energy is not an orbit invariant, construct locally flat winding BMS geometries with hyperbolic Lorentz holonomy, prove that the smooth real periodic near horizon current model excludes these winding orbits, and obtain selection rules and closed form expressions for finite monodromy resolved orbital functionals. A unitary realization on the full infinite dimensional orbit is not constructed, and the interpretation of the cyclic sectors in quantum gravity remains conjectural.

### [96] [Holographic viscous transport in scalar-coupled Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16936)
*Tao Ma,Song He,Li Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过全息对偶方法，在五维Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet模型中，直接从应力张量关联函数计算剪切与体粘滞系数，并与基于视界公式的预测进行对比，验证了Eling-Oz公式在标量场依赖耦合情形下的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验全息等离子体中剪切与体粘滞系数的视界公式（特别是Eling-Oz公式）在非平凡引力背景（含Gauss-Bonnet项和标量场）下的有效性，并提供边界计算结果作为基准。

Method: 在匹配格点QCD物态方程的五维Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet模型中，计算UV有限的重正化应力张量推迟关联函数；求解完整依赖于Gauss-Bonnet耦合α的涨落方程；对比视界守恒流法与边界Kubo公式所得η/s和ζ/s。

Result: 1) 常数耦合下，视界守恒流法与边界Kubo结果一致；2) 标量依赖耦合下，两种方法均发现η/s存在极小值；3) 一阶α截断的视界法低估ζ/s峰值高度但温度位置不变；4) 未修正的Eling-Oz公式ζ/s=(4π)⁻¹(∂φₕ/∂ln s)²精确复现边界ζ/s结果。

Conclusion: Eling-Oz公式即使在含Gauss-Bonnet修正且标量耦合依赖的情形下仍保持有效，其1/(4π)前因子无需修改；本文为各类视界粘滞公式提供了可靠的边界基准验证。

Abstract: We test horizon formulas for the shear and bulk viscosities of a holographic plasma against viscosities extracted directly from retarded stress-tensor correlators. We work in a five-dimensional Einstein-dilaton-Gauss-Bonnet model calibrated to the lattice QCD equation of state at zero baryon density. UV-finite renormalized correlators are obtained by including the required curvature and scalar counterterms, and the fluctuation equations are solved while retaining the full dependence on the Gauss-Bonnet coupling $α$, without using a horizon conservation law or an entropy-production formula. For constant coupling, the horizon conserved-current calculation reproduces the boundary Kubo results in both channels. For the scalar-dependent coupling studied here, both methods find a minimum in the shear-viscosity-to-entropy-density ratio $η/s$. In the bulk channel, the conserved-current calculation truncated at first order in $α$ yields a lower peak height in $ζ/s$, with the peak temperature nearly unchanged relative to the boundary result. For both couplings, the unmodified Eling-Oz (EO) formula, $ζ/s=(4π)^{-1}(\partialφ_h/\partial\ln s)^2$, reproduces the boundary result for $ζ/s$, where $φ_h$ is the horizon value of the scalar. The prefactor $1/(4π)$ is thus retained even when the Gauss-Bonnet interaction shifts $η/s$ from this value. These results provide a direct boundary benchmark for horizon prescriptions and support the unmodified EO formula beyond constant coupling.

</details>

Abstract: We obtain infinite spin representations of the super-Poincaré algebra from a higher-derivative superparticle action on a super-Minkowski background in 3, 4, 6 and 10 dimensions. Super-twistor transforms are performed, and used to deduce first-quantised spectra.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个具体的量子模型来实现二维德西特时空的dS/CFT对应，弥补理论中缺乏明确对偶量子系统的问题。

Method: 构造一个基于矩阵乘积态的量子信道模型，该模型在特定极限下关联无弦交叉的SYK模型；分析其随机行走行为及其涌现的SL(2,R)对称性，并将其与dS未来边界对称性对应；推导其时间变量下的Schwarzian作用量及盘状振幅；计算固定dS₂背景中自由无质量标量场在Bunch-Davies真空下的两点函数。

Result: 模型成功复现了二维dS Jackiw-Teitelboim引力的disk amplitude；Bunch-Davies真空中自由标量场的两点函数与模型预测一致；SL(2,R)对称性被确认为dS未来边界的等距对称性。

Conclusion: 该量子信道模型为二维de Sitter引力提供了首个具象、可计算的dS/CFT对偶描述，将dS时空几何与量子多体系统动力学联系起来，支持dS/CFT猜想的可行性。

Abstract: We propose a quantum-mechanical model dual to two-dimensional de Sitter (dS) gravity. It is a quantum channel, defined by a matrix product state that fuses one spin at each step, and is related to a limit of the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model without chord crossings. Its simplest part is a random walk with an emergent $SL(2,\mathbb R)$ symmetry, which we identify with the symmetry of the future boundary of dS. It selects a time variable in which the walk obeys the Schwarzian action and yields the disk amplitude of dS Jackiw-Teitelboim gravity. As the simplest case, we consider free massless scalar fields in the fixed background of dS$_2$. We find the agreement of the two-point function in the Bunch-Davies vacuum and the matching of symmetry. Our construction provides a concrete model for the dS/CFT correspondence for two-dimensional de Sitter gravity.

</details>


### [99] [Covariant Wigner--Weyl calculus, non-dissipative transport and the chiral anomaly in inhomogeneous systems with nontrivial momentum-space topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17178)
*M. A. Zubkov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾并扩展了非阿贝尔规范场中费米子的协变Wigner-Weyl计算方法，应用于具有动量空间非平庸拓扑的非均匀系统，并推导出若干新结果，包括反常因子分解、Fermi面不变量的留数计算及手征磁效应等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一已有成果的符号与假设，厘清概念，并拓展协变Wigner-Weyl方法在非均匀、非平庸动量拓扑系统中的应用边界。

Method: 协变Wigner-Weyl符号 calculus、指标理论、非交换几何、留数定理、Luttinger-Ward恒等式分析。

Result: 获得多项新结果：协变符号的规范无关算符刻画、积分反常的序无关性、四维闭流形上反常的严格因子分解（无需同伦假设）、Fermi面不变量的留数表达（连续和格点情形）、非哈密顿模型支持的平衡手征磁效应、以及手征分离不变量的指标配对形式。

Conclusion: 协变Wigner-Weyl框架为理解强关联、非均匀、非平庸拓扑量子物质提供了统一而有力的工具，新结果深化了对手征反常、输运效应与拓扑不变量之间联系的认识。

Abstract: We review, systematize and extend the covariant Wigner--Weyl calculus for fermions in non-Abelian gauge fields and its applications to systems inhomogeneous in coordinate space with nontrivial momentum-space topology: the non-Abelian chiral separation and quantum Hall effects, their stability under interactions, the Fermi-surface invariant of inhomogeneous systems and the non-elliptic models that carry it, with their equilibrium chiral magnetic effect, the factorization of the chiral anomaly into the instanton number and the momentum-space invariant $N_3$, and the noncommutative-geometric form of the invariants. Previous results are brought to common conventions, with hypotheses stated and notions defined. New results: the characterization of the operators whose covariant symbol is gauge-field independent and the ordering independence of the integrated anomaly; the anomaly factorization on a closed four-manifold without the homotopy hypothesis, the factorization into instanton number and $N_3$ holding exactly when the untwisted index vanishes; the Luttinger--Ward identity behind the renormalized-velocity current and the continuum conditions replacing the compactness of the Brillouin zone in the non-renormalization theorems; the identification of the Moyal $N_3$ of the chiral separation effect with the fibre degree of the principal symbol; the residue evaluation of the Fermi-surface invariant of the non-elliptic models, in the continuum and on a lattice, and the equilibrium current of the conserved charge along the magnetic field that it implies -- a chiral magnetic effect permitted by the non-Hamiltonian form of these models -- which the model with spherical Fermi surfaces trades for a chiral separation effect and a single-Weyl anomaly, and a modification of it restores at the price of the anomaly; and the index-pairing form of the chiral separation invariant.

</details>


### [100] [Love numbers for the Myers-Perry geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17261)
*Rhucha Deshpande,Oleg Lunin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了任意维度Myers-Perry几何中标量场的Love数，计算了黑洞潮汐响应中的两个关键参数：一个描述对数跑动的参数（与视界结构无关）和Love数（依赖于视界边界条件），并采用多种近似方法计算了后者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞在任意维度下的潮汐响应特性，特别是Love数及其相关参数，以深入理解黑洞的唯象性质与时空几何的关系。

Method: 利用波动方程的大渐近展开计算对数跑动参数；采用多种不同近似方法计算依赖于视界边界条件的Love数。

Result: 明确计算出Myers-Perry黑洞在任意维度下描述对数跑动的参数，并通过多种近似得到Love数；指出该跑动参数不敏感于视界结构，而Love数则依赖于视界边界条件。

Conclusion: Love数作为黑洞结构敏感量，需谨慎处理边界条件；而对数跑动参数具有普适性，可作为更稳健的黑洞表征量。

Abstract: We analyze Love numbers for scalar fields in the Myers-Perry geometry in arbitrary dimensions. The tidal responses of black holes are specified by two constants which encode a potential logarithmic running and the Love number. We argue that the parameter specifying the running is not sensitive to the structure of the horizon, and we explicitly compute this parameter for Myers-Perry black holes in arbitrary dimensions by utilizing the asymptotic expansion of the wave equation. The Love numbers rely on the details of the boundary conditions at the horizon, and we compute them using several distinct approximations.

</details>


### [101] [Twisting IKKT with branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17270)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受IKKT矩阵模型与IIB型弦理论之间猜想关系的启发，本文旨在分类该矩阵模型的超对称扭曲，涵盖动力学和运动学超荷。

Method: 采用BV形式主义处理IKKT模型，分析非对易背景下的超对称扭曲，并通过开闭弦对偶将扭曲与BCOV理论的形变联系起来。

Result: 发现了除有限N下已知两类扭曲外，在无限秩下额外的五类扭曲；这些扭曲对应IIB超引力中所有等价类的扭曲超荷；其中一类具有Spin(7)对称性的扭曲可直接重构MNS局域化结果。

Conclusion: IKKT模型的超对称扭曲不仅实现了IIB超引力中所有扭曲超荷的表示，还揭示了更高阶中心扩展超代数结构，并通过开闭对偶统一了多种世界体积理论与拓扑弦理论。

Abstract: Motivated by the conjectural relationship between the IKKT matrix model and type IIB string theory, we classify supersymmetric twists of the matrix model, allowing for both dynamical and kinematical supercharges. Beyond the two twists available at any finite $N$, which we studied in our previous work, we find five additional classes at infinite rank which require non-commuting backgrounds associated with D-branes in IIB supergravity. Together, these twists realize representatives of every equivalence class of twisting supercharges in flat-space type IIB supergravity recently classified by the first author; however, the precise form of the orbit stratification differs. This difference is related to a higher central extension of the IIB superalgebra that appears in our treatment of the IKKT model in the BV formalism. Expanding around the non-commutative backgrounds gives worldvolume theories on products of A- and B-branes, including four-dimensional BF theory on an emergent D3-brane. We relate all these twists to deformations of BCOV theory (the closed string field theory of the topological B-model) using the open-closed duality of classical string field theory. For one of the twists with Spin$(7)$ symmetry, we also show the localization of the IKKT partition function by Moore, Nekrasov, and Shatashvili can be directly reconstructed with the BV formalism, with their auxiliary field completion encoded by quadratic antifield terms. Throughout, we provide self-contained treatments of the formal techniques we employ.

</details>


### [102] [A precision bound for gravitational transseries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17274)
*Ji Hoon Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出全息可观测量在黑洞阈值以上的大N展开存在精度限制，该限制由D-膜瞬子贡献决定，并发展了基于Écalle理论的大N resurgence方法以处理e^{-N^2}阶引力鞍点渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大N极限下全息理论中瞬子效应（尤其是D-膜瞬子）对可观测量的精确性限制，以及其与黑洞相和信息悖论的联系。

Method: 运用Écalle的resurgence理论构建大N transseries，分析D-膜瞬子求和的增长性质，推导解析可和性障碍，并以1/16-BPS指数为具体例子验证瞬子对偶关系。

Result: 发现D-膜瞬子数据导致大N transseries存在解析不可和性障碍，从而确立精度界；在N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中导出了热AdS与黑洞背景间D3-膜瞬子数的对偶关系，并重现其作用量。

Conclusion: 大N全息可观测量的精度受限于瞬子结构，该限制普遍存在于黑洞相，并可能关联到双锥虫洞等量子引力结构。

Abstract: We propose that the large $N$ transseries of a holographic observable above the black hole threshold admits a precision bound, a limit on how precisely the transseries can determine the observable at finite $N$, set by the data of its D-brane instanton sectors. In doing so, we develop methods in large $N$ resurgence, based on the work of Écalle, that capture the $e^{-N^2}$ asymptotics of gravitational saddles beyond the $e^{-N}$ instantons. Under an assumption on the growth of the D-brane instanton sums that we expect in the black hole phase, we find an analytic obstruction to the resummation of the large $N$ transseries. This obstruction sets the precision bound. As our main example and consistency check, we study the $\frac{1}{16}$-BPS index of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills and its black hole. We derive relations between the D-brane instanton data on thermal AdS and on the black hole, in the form of a duality that exchanges the numbers of D-brane instantons on the two backgrounds. In the index, these relations reproduce the actions of D3-branes wrapped on the black hole. We conclude with our expectations for generic situations and their relation to the double-cone wormhole.

</details>


### [103] [The $(5,5)$ String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17275)
*Justin Kaidi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种N=(2,1)杂化弦理论，其右行模在ℝ^{2,2}上，左行模具有(5,5)符号，并通过加入II_{3,3}格得到；该理论无需零流，保持四维时空，所有态（无限塔、十六个超荷）均为无质量；中性玻色子对应Kutasov-Martinec行列式作用量，其对偶为自对偶度规耦合自对偶麦克斯韦场的三次作用量，并用单个超场加入费米子；带电态的耦合被论证为左行模物理态构成的李超代数的结构常数；作者因此提出经典靶空间理论是一种该超代数的自对偶规范理论：弦的所有态均为规范场，引力内含于规范代数之中，从而构建一种纯三次场论以描述闭弦所有模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准N=(2,1)弦需引入零流且导致高维或退化目标空间，本文旨在构造一种保持四维时空、无需零流、且所有态无质量的新型N=(2,1)杂化弦模型，以探索引力与规范理论的统一新路径。

Method: 通过构造右行模在ℝ^{2,2}、左行模具(5,5)签名并附加II_{3,3}格的杂化弦背景；分析其谱得全无质量塔；利用Kutasov-Martinec行列式作用量及其对偶形式处理中性玻色子，并扩展至超场框架包含费米子；通过物理态代数结构识别带电态耦合，论证其满足李超代数结构常数特征。

Result: 确立了该N=(2,1)杂化弦所有态均为无质量、十六超对称、四维有效；导出中性部分等价于自对偶度规+自对偶麦克斯韦的三次超场作用量；发现带电态相互作用由左行模物理态张成的李超代数结构常数决定；进而提出其经典靶空间理论为该李超代数上的自对偶规范理论，实现引力作为规范代数一部分的统一图景。

Conclusion: 该N=(2,1)杂化弦提供了一个四维、全无质量、超对称且自洽的闭弦模型；其经典动力学可完全由一个纯三次规范场论描述，其中引力场亦为规范场，暗示引力与规范力在基本层面上具有同一性；这为弦唯象学与量子引力的简化场论实现提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study an $N=(2,1)$ heterotic string with right-movers on $\mathbb R^{2,2}$ and left-movers of signature $(5,5)$, obtained by adjoining the lattice $\mathrm{II}_{3,3}$. Unlike the standard $N=(2,1)$ strings it requires no null current, and its target space remains four-dimensional. All of its states, an infinite tower with sixteen supercharges, are massless. For the neutral bosons, the Kutasov-Martinec determinant action is dual to a cubic action for a self-dual metric coupled to self-dual Maxwell fields, and we complete it with fermions using a single superfield. For the charged states we give evidence that the couplings are the structure constants of a Lie superalgebra formed by the physical states of the left-movers. We are thus led to propose that the classical target theory is a self-dual gauge theory for this superalgebra: every state of the string is a gauge field, and gravity is part of the gauge algebra. This would be a purely cubic field theory of all the modes of a closed string.

</details>


### [104] [Bound States of Two D0-Branes and the Three-Graviton Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17325)
*Sergio Sanjurjo,Themistocles Zikopoulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文数值求解了M理论中SU(2) BFSS矩阵模型的基态，验证了其对应于两个D0-膜的零能束缚态（即十一维带两单位动量的引力子），并精确复现了三引力子耦合系数，证明其受超对称性保护且可由非重整化单圈相互作用导出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解M理论中BFSS矩阵模型的基态及其与十一维引力子的对应关系，特别是验证三引力子耦合的可计算性与超对称保护机制。

Method: 通过数值方法求解SU(2) BFSS矩阵模型的基态波函数，并结合表示论分析和超对称性论证，推导三引力子耦合系数。

Result: 成功获得基态波函数，确认其具有强耦合核心与幂律渐近尾部；高精度复现Herderschee-Maldacena给出的三引力子耦合；证明该耦合受超对称保护，并由非重整化单圈图导出；给出激发束缚态的表示论解释。

Conclusion: BFSS矩阵模型能精确描述M理论中的低能引力物理，三引力子耦合是超对称保护的可观测量，数值与解析方法可协同验证M理论基本预言。

Abstract: We numerically solve for the ground state of the $SU(2)$ BFSS matrix model for M-theory. This state corresponds to the zero-energy bound state of two D0-branes or, by the BFSS conjecture, to the eleven-dimensional graviton with two units of momentum along the M-theory circle. The wavefunction exhibits a strongly coupled core, as well as an asymptotic power-law tail where the D0-branes are well separated. The coefficient of this power-law decay is the three-graviton coupling, previously computed by Herderschee and Maldacena, which our numerical solution reproduces with high accuracy. We also show that this coupling is protected by supersymmetry in the matrix model, and derive its value from the non-renormalized one-loop interaction of the two D0-branes. Lastly, we provide a simple representation-theoretic argument regarding the recently proposed excited bound states of the model.

</details>


### [105] [Decoupling Dynamics: A Tale of Two Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17327)
*Andrea Antinucci,Oscar Lewis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过动态全息规范理论来统一描述量子场论（QFT）在规范前后对称性拓扑场论（SymTFT）的方法，揭示了边界条件变化如何导致SymTFT的拓扑极限改变，并给出了Hilbert空间和算符层面的量子定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子场论中连续对称性被动力学规范后其对称性拓扑场论（SymTFT）为何发生变化，并建立一个统一、量子化的框架来描述规范前后的SymTFT。

Method: 利用(d+1)维动力学全息规范理论，通过改变其边界条件来实现不同SymTFT的拓扑极限；结合全息理论的适当极限与光子截断，在Hilbert空间与算符层面给出SymTFT的量子定义。

Result: 证明了规范前后不同的SymTFT源于同一动力学全息理论的不同拓扑极限；明确了拓扑对称性缺陷、荷与反常如何保留，而电流的动力学信息被丢弃；在(2+1)维体理论中完成完整量子分析，并在四维边界理论中重现了混合轴-矢量反常下规范矢量对称后剩余的不可逆手征对称性的SymTFT。

Conclusion: SymTFT并非固定不变，而是依赖于边界条件选择的动力学全息理论的拓扑极限；该框架为理解连续对称性规范下的对称性结构提供了统一且严格的量子描述。

Abstract: The symmetries of a $d$-dimensional QFT are known to be captured by a topological theory in one dimension higher, the Symmetry Topological Field Theory (SymTFT), which is invariant under gauging finite symmetries of the QFT, but not under dynamical gauging of continuous symmetries. We show that the distinct SymTFTs of a $d$-dimensional QFT before and after dynamical gauging of a continuous Abelian symmetry arise as different topological limits of the same dynamical holographic gauge theory in $(d+1)$-dimensions. Gauging changes the boundary condition of this dynamical theory and thereby selects a different topological limit, explaining why the associated SymTFT changes. We give a fully quantum definition of these SymTFTs at the level of Hilbert spaces and operators by combining suitable limits of the bulk holographic theory with photon truncation. This construction clarifies how topological symmetry defects, charges, and anomalies survive while the dynamical information carried by currents is discarded. We study non-anomalous and anomalous examples, providing a complete quantum analysis for $(2+1)$-dimensional bulks. For a four-dimensional boundary theory with a mixed axial-vector anomaly, we recover the SymTFT of the non-invertible chiral symmetry that remains after gauging the vector symmetry.

</details>
