### [106] [A Positive Turn: Coupled Quintessence and Cosmological Neutrino Masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14584)
*Tian-Nuo Li,William Giarè,Guo-Hong Du,Eleonora Di Valentino,Luis A. Anchordoqui,Shao-Jiang Wang,Xin Zhang,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了耦合型精质（CQ）模型是否能缓解ΛCDM框架下CMB与DESI BAO对中微子总质量的上限与振荡实验下限之间的张力，发现CQ模型在不穿越幻影边界的情况下，使中微子质量约束与振荡实验在2σ内一致，并优于其他暗能量相互作用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ΛCDM模型中CMB和DESI BAO给出的中微子总质量上限与振荡实验的下限存在张力，且允许负有效质量的分析倾向于负值，需探索新物理模型来缓解该张力。

Method: 采用具有指数质量耦合m_c(φ) ∝ e^{-βφ/M_Pl}的耦合型精质（CQ）模型，结合CMB、DESI和超新星数据进行参数约束与模型比较。

Result: CQ模型得到的中微子质量约束与CPL模型相当，在正常质量序下与振荡实验在2σ内一致，且不需标量场状态方程穿越幻影边界；其缓解张力的效果优于其他暗能量相互作用模型。

Conclusion: 暗部门相互作用能否松弛中微子质量限制，取决于其对原初CMB各向异性、几何、结构增长和引力透镜效应的综合影响；CQ模型是缓解当前中微子质量张力的一个有前景的候选模型。

Abstract: In $Λ$CDM, the CMB and DESI BAO upper limits on the total neutrino mass are in tension with the lower bounds from oscillation experiments, and analyses allowing negative effective masses favor negative values. We investigate whether a coupled quintessence (CQ) model in which dark matter interacts with a canonical scalar field $φ$ through an exponential mass coupling $m_{\rm c}(φ)\propto e^{-βφ/M_{\rm Pl}}$ can alleviate this tension. Combining CMB, DESI, and supernova data, we find that CQ yields neutrino-mass constraints comparable to those obtained in CPL and consistent with oscillation experiments within $2σ$ for normal ordering, without requiring the scalar-field equation of state to cross the phantom divide. We further show that it alleviates the tension more effectively than alternative interacting dark energy models that instead favor more negative effective masses. This comparison shows that whether dark sector interactions can relax neutrino mass bounds ultimately depends on the interplay of their effects on primary CMB anisotropies, geometry, growth, and lensing.

</details>


### [107] [Robustness of the Baryonic Acoustic Oscillation Peak Position against Monopole Fluctuations in Galaxy Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14619)
*Christopher Magnoli,Giovanni Piccoli,Jaiyul Yoo,Daniel Eisenstein*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了星系巡天中单极涨落对重子声学振荡（BAO）峰位置测量的影响，发现其虽具各向异性且幅度不可忽略，但在BAO峰附近极为平滑，可被宽带模型完全吸收，从而不影响BAO峰位置的亚百分比精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量BAO峰位置是现代星系巡天的关键目标之一；而星系样本背景平均密度通常由巡天自身观测密度估计并扣除，该过程引入的单极涨落可能影响BAO分析结果，需评估其系统误差效应。

Method: 计算单极涨落在红移空间多极相关函数中的贡献，并分析其在BAO峰位置附近的形态特征及其对BAO拟合的影响。

Result: 单极涨落在两体相关函数中具有各向异性和非可忽略的幅度，但在BAO峰附近极其平滑，几乎完全被BAO宽带模型吸收。

Conclusion: BAO峰位置在亚百分比水平上不受单极涨落引起的未知系统误差影响，BAO分析具有高度鲁棒性。

Abstract: Precise measurements of the baryonic acoustic oscillation (BAO) peak position are one of the key goals in modern galaxy surveys. We investigate the impact of the monopole fluctuations in galaxy surveys on the BAO peak position in the galaxy two-point redshift-space multipole-correlation functions. The background mean density of a galaxy sample is estimated from the survey itself by averaging the observed galaxy number density, and this averaged number density (monopole) is subtracted to obtain the observed galaxy number density fluctuation, which includes an additional contribution from the monopole fluctuations, propagating to galaxy clustering analysis. We compute the contributions of the monopole fluctuations to the redshift-space multipole-correlation functions. While the monopole contributions in galaxy two-point correlation function are anisotropic and non-negligible in amplitude, the contributions are so smooth around the BAO peak position that they are almost completely absorbed in the BAO broadband model, leaving the BAO peak position unaffected at the sub-percent level. Our results demonstrate the robustness of the BAO analysis against the unknown systematic error.

</details>


### [108] [Dark matter alters dwarf galaxy sizes and morphologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14638)
*Akaxia Cruz,Robel Geda,Alyson Brooks,Mariangela Lisanti,Annika H. G. Peter,Thomas R. Quinn,Ben Keller*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过宇宙学数值模拟，揭示了暗物质微观物理性质（如自相互作用）对矮星系结构演化（特别是半光半径）的直接影响，建立了星系尺寸与暗物质粒子性质之间的因果联系，为利用大视场巡天统计分析星系形态来探测暗物质提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮星系是宇宙中暗物质主导最显著的系统，其形态和尺度的多样性暗示暗物质与重子物理存在相互作用，但这种关系在现有模型中尚未被定量刻画。

Method: 利用包含重子物理的新一代宇宙学数值模拟，在冷暗物质（CDM）和多种自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）模型下，对同一组矮星系系统进行对比研究，分析暗物质中心密度演化与其半光半径及形态的关系。

Result: 发现暗物质中心密度的演化显著影响矮星系的半光半径，且该效应依赖于星系形态；从而确立了星系尺寸可作为暗物质微观物理的直接探针。

Conclusion: 星系尺度是暗物质粒子性质的可观测特征，该工作为未来宽视场巡天（如LSST）通过星系群体统计来约束暗物质模型提供了可预测的理论框架。

Abstract: Dwarf galaxies are the most dark-matter-dominated systems in the Universe, serving as unique laboratories for exploring the fundamental nature of the dark sector. Although the observed diversity in dwarf galaxy sizes and morphologies suggests an interplay between dark matter and baryonic physics, this relationship has remained largely unquantified in existing models. Here, we utilize a set of novel cosmological simulations with baryons to demonstrate how dark matter physics directly drives the structural evolution of these galaxies. By comparing the same systems across cold dark matter and varied self-interacting dark matter models, we find that the evolution of the central density of dark matter significantly impacts a galaxy's half-light radius, but the signal is dependent on morphology. This causal link establishes galaxy size as a direct signature of the underlying dark matter microphysics. Our findings provide a predictive framework for decoding dark matter particle properties from galactic shapes, offering a roadmap for upcoming wide-field surveys, such as the Legacy Survey of Space and Time, to probe the dark sector through the statistical analysis of galaxy populations.

</details>


### [109] [CosmoSim: Lens-parameter estimation of the Einstein Cross NGC 6505 using machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14678)
*Ben David Normann,Oda Hjemli,Hans Georg Schaathun*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CosmoSim工具的首次实际应用，通过仅在CosmoSim生成的合成图像上训练的卷积神经网络，直接从真实强引力透镜观测图像中估计透镜和源参数，并在Euclid和AGEL巡天数据上验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为验证CosmoSim工具能否直接应用于真实天文观测数据，并支持基于机器学习的强引力透镜参数估计。

Method: 使用CosmoSim生成训练数据，构建并训练卷积神经网络（CNN）；对Euclid和AGEL的21张强透镜图像进行前景透镜光去除后输入CNN预测参数；再用预测参数与文献模型分别重建爱因斯坦十字NGC 6505，并对比重建质量指标。

Result: ML预测的爱因斯坦半径与星表值一致（Euclid平均相对差0.08，AGEL为0.19），质量偏差中位数为0.12 dex；椭率存在系统性偏差，方位角比较无定论；ML重建在结构相似性、IoU和Chamfer距离上优于文献模型，Hausdorff距离相当。

Conclusion: CosmoSim可成功应用于真实观测数据，其生成的合成数据足以训练出具备实际应用能力的ML模型，为强透镜分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: CosmoSim is an open-source tool for simulating gravitational lenses and reconstructing lens mass distributions, either by ray tracing a parametric lens model or through a Roulette expansion of the lens potential. It is also used to generate the large, labelled synthetic image sets needed to train machine-learning (ML) parameter estimators. We present the first application of CosmoSim to real data. A convolutional neural network trained only on CosmoSim images estimates Singular Isothermal Ellipsoid lens and source parameters directly from observed images. We test the pipeline on 21 images of 19 strong lenses from Euclid and the AGEL survey, after removing the foreground lens light with a custom decontamination tool built on AstroPy. As a case study we reconstruct the Einstein Cross NGC 6505 both from the lens model published by the discovery team and from parameters predicted by our ML model. Both reconstructions recover the observed four-image structure, and the ML-based reconstruction performs at least as well as the literature-based one: it scores better on masked structural similarity, intersection-over-union and Chamfer distance, with an essentially equal Hausdorff distance. Across the ensemble, the predicted Einstein radii are consistent with catalogued values within the quoted uncertainties (mean relative difference 0.08 for Euclid and 0.19 for AGEL lenses), and the enclosed masses agree to within a median of 0.12 dex. The predicted ellipticities differ systematically from the catalogued values, while the comparison of orientations is inconclusive. These results establish CosmoSim as a tool that can be applied directly to observational data.

</details>


### [110] [Self-interacting Dark Matter signatures from stars extremely magnified by galaxy clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14800)
*Sung Kei Li,Yuxuan He,Yi-Ming Zhong,Jose M. Diego,Jeremy Lim,Amruth Alfred,Thomas Broadhurst,Sangjun Cha,Birendra Dhanasingham,Leo W. H. Fung,Patrick Kelly,Richard Massey,Paloma Morilla,Jose M. Palencia,Derek Perera,Liliya L. R. Williams*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）模型对龙星系中引力透镜恒星空间分布的解释能力，发现SIDM的粒子参数可决定偏度符号，并在特定截面强度（500–1000 cm²/g）下能同时拟合观测到的偏度与宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同暗物质模型预测的子结构会影响星系团透镜恒星的空间分布，尤其是相对于宏观临界曲线的偏度和宽度；此前波状暗物质已被证明优于冷暗物质（CDM），本文旨在检验自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）是否也能解释该观测特征，并约束其粒子参数。

Method: 基于速度依赖的自相互作用截面建立子晕热引力核坍缩概率模型，分析SIDM子晕密度剖面演化对透镜恒星空间分布（特别是偏度和宽度）的影响，并结合Abell 370中Dragon星系（z=0.73）的观测数据进行贝叶斯比较。

Result: SIDM是目前已知唯一一类其偏度符号由自身粒子参数决定的暗物质模型；观测数据支持大量子晕发生核坍缩并呈现陡峭幂律密度剖面（Γ ≳ 2.5）；在忽略环境效应时，对应子晕质量10⁶–10⁸ M⊙、速度弥散~1–10 km/s，最优有效截面为500–1000 cm²/g，贝叶斯因子约10²。

Conclusion: SIDM模型能在不引入新自由度的前提下，同时解释龙星系中透镜恒星分布的负偏度与宽度，为区分暗物质候选者提供了新的强约束，尤其凸显了核坍缩子晕在小尺度结构中的关键作用。

Abstract: The spatial distribution of cluster-lensed star transients with respect to the macroscopic critical curve (CC) is sensitive to underlying substructures predicted by different classes of dark matter (DM) models. \citet{Broadhurst_2025} have shown that wave-like DM composed of ultra-light bosons better explains both the width and negative skewness (towards the interior of the CC) of the lensed star spatial distribution discovered in the ``Dragon'' lensed galaxy ($z = 0.73$) in the Abell 370 (A370, $z = 0.375$) cluster field, compared with the case of regular cold dark matter (CDM) subhaloes. In this work, we consider the case of self-interacting DM (SIDM) and investigate how the lensed star spatial distribution depends on the velocity-dependent self-interaction cross-section of SIDM particles, and whether constraints can be placed on the cross-section strength at relevant velocities. Establishing a cross-section strength-dependent (sub)halo gravothermal core-collapse probability, we found that the SIDM appears to be the only known class of models thus far in which the sign of skewness is set by its own particle parameters. The observed lensed star distribution in the Dragon galaxy can be well explained, in terms of both skewness and width, when a significant fraction of subhaloes have undergone core collapse and exhibit a steep power-law density slope. Given the cluster redshift, this qualitatively favours an (effective) cross-section strength of $500 -1000\,\textrm{cm}^{2}/\textrm{g}$ for halos with mass $10^{6} - 10^{8}\,M_{\odot}$ (velocity dispersion of $\sim 1-10\,\rm{km}/\rm{s}$), when the role of environmental effects on the core-collapse (de)acceleration is not considered, and limits the three-dimensional inner density slope of the collapsed halo to $Γ\gtrsim 2.5$ (Bayes factor of $\sim 10^{2}$).

</details>


### [111] [A consistent formulation of stochastic inflation II: Reassessing its role in nonlinear cosmological perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15050)
*Diego Cruces,Tomotaka Kuroda*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文质疑了传统随机暴胀理论在定量描述宇宙扰动方面的可靠性，通过引入非马尔可夫效应和紫外涨落的二次噪声项，扩展了随机暴胀框架，并在三点关联函数上与量子场论in-in计算精确一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新评估随机方法在研究宇宙扰动中的角色和动机，特别是其是否能定量可靠地描述扰动，而不仅限于重求和红外长期发散。

Method: 扩展随机暴胀理论至非线性区域，纳入由红外-紫外相互作用引起的非马尔可夫效应及紫外涨落的二次项，从而产生非白噪声；计算德西特空间中旁观者标量场和慢滚暴胀子（含度规扰动）的三点关联函数。

Result: 所得三点关联函数与in-in量子场论对超视界非线性演化的预测完全一致；表明非马尔可夫效应和二次噪声项对定量精度至关重要。

Conclusion: 传统随机暴胀框架最多仅在线性阶具有定量合理性，更高阶需包含非马尔可夫性和高阶噪声项。

Abstract: We question whether the conventional formulation of stochastic inflation can provide a quantitatively reliable description of cosmological perturbations. More fundamentally, we reassess the role and motivations of the stochastic approach to study cosmological perturbations, distinct from its well-founded motivation for resumming IR secular divergences. To provide a quantitative basis for this reassessment, we extend stochastic inflation beyond the linear regime by incorporating non-Markovian effects arising from infrared-ultraviolet interactions and terms quadratic in ultraviolet fluctuations, which generate non-white noise. We derive three-point correlation functions for a spectator scalar field in de Sitter space and a slow-roll inflaton including metric perturbations, demonstrating exact agreement with in-in quantum-field-theoretic predictions for superhorizon nonlinear evolution. Our results show that both non-Markovian effects and quadratic (higher order) noise terms are essential for quantitative accuracy, implying that the conventional stochastic-inflation framework is, at best, quantitatively justified only at linear order.

</details>


### [112] [Non-Gaussian matter fluctuations from primordial magnetic fields and galaxy luminosity function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15188)
*Teppei Minoda,Yi Mao,Shohei Saga,Hayato Shimabukuro*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究原初磁场（PMFs）如何通过诱导非高斯密度涨落，显著增强高红移大质量晕和星系的丰度，并解释JWST观测到的z~12–16高红移亮星系超出ΛCDM预期的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释JWST在z~12–16观测到的亮星系超出标准ΛCDM预测的现象，并探究原初磁场作为潜在物理机制的作用。

Method: 基于洛伦兹力诱导的密度扰动，推导PMF导致的密度场一阶统计（PDF），发现其具有强非高斯性（尤其高密度尾增强超两个数量级）；结合Press-Schechter形式论计算晕丰度，并进一步推导z=6、12、16处星系紫外光度函数。

Result: PMF诱导的密度PDF呈现显著非高斯性，大幅提升高红移（z≳12）大质量晕与亮星系（M_UV ≲ −18）的丰度；理论预测可自然解释JWST观测到的z∼12–16亮端星系超出，无需调高恒星形成效率。

Conclusion: 原初磁场可通过产生固有非高斯密度扰动，为JWST发现的高红移亮星系超出提供简洁、自洽的宇宙学物理解释。

Abstract: Primordial magnetic fields (PMFs) represent a promising candidate for the origin of large-scale magnetic fields observed in astrophysical environments. Through the Lorentz force, PMFs generate matter density perturbations, leaving distinct imprints on the formation of cosmic structures. While previous studies have primarily characterized these imprints via the matter power spectrum --- a two-point statistic --- we demonstrate that the one-point statistics of PMF-induced density fluctuations reveal an intrinsically non-Gaussian probability distribution function (PDF), even when the PMFs themselves are statistically Gaussian. In particular, we find that the high-density tail of the PDF is enhanced by over two orders of magnitude relative to the Gaussian case. Adopting the Press-Schechter formalism, we show that this non-Gaussianity significantly boosts the abundance of rare objects, especially massive halos and those at high redshifts. We further compute the ultraviolet luminosity function of galaxies at $z=6$, $12$, and $16$, and compare our predictions with recent James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) observations that have reported an excess of high-redshift galaxies at $z\sim 12$ -- $16$ relative to standard $Λ$CDM expectations. Intriguingly, in the limiting case where the total density field inherits this PDF, our results suggest that the non-Gaussian density fluctuations sourced by PMFs can explain the bright-end ($M_\mathrm{UV}\lesssim -18$) excess at $z\gtrsim 12$, without requiring additional astrophysical enhancements to star-formation efficiency.

</details>


### [113] [$r$-Largest Order Statistics for Galaxy Cluster Cosmology I: Reconstruction of the high-mass end of the Halo Mass Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15398)
*T. Gayoux,P-S. Corasaniti*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了r-最大顺序统计（r-LOS）框架，推广了传统的极值统计（EVS）方法，利用每个体积内r个最重星系团的联合分布信息，以更高效地重建晕质量函数（HMF）高质端，并验证其对原初非高斯性的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极值统计仅利用单个最重星系团信息，统计效率低；为提升星系团宇宙学约束能力，需充分利用多个大质量星系团的联合分布信息。

Method: 构建r-最大顺序统计（r-LOS）数学框架，推导其渐近性质及与晕质量函数、广义极值分布（GEV）参数的关系；扩展“块最大值法”，将每一体积中r个最重星系团纳入分析；利用数值模拟生成的共动与光锥晕表进行验证。

Result: 成功建立r-LOS框架与HMF高质端之间的解析关系，证明可通过GEV参数反推HMF尾部；在模拟晕表中验证了该关系的准确性；发现r-LOS对原初非高斯性具有更高统计灵敏度。

Conclusion: r-LOS框架显著提升了星系团极值统计的信息利用率和宇宙学约束能力，尤其适用于稀有大质量星系团样本有限的情形，并为探测原初非高斯性提供了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce the $r$-largest order statistics ($r$-LOS) framework for galaxy clusters, a generalization of the standard extreme value statistics (EVS) approach. While conventional EVS focuses on the probability distribution of the most massive cluster within a given volume, the $r$-LOS framework exploits the additional statistical information encoded in the joint distribution of the $r$ most massive clusters, ranked in descending order of mass. We develop the corresponding mathematical formalism, discuss its asymptotic properties, and derive a set of relations relevant to galaxy cluster cosmology. In particular, we demonstrate that the high-mass end of the halo mass function (HMF) can be reconstructed from the generalized extreme value (GEV) parameters inferred using an extension of the block maxima method that incorporates the $r$ most massive clusters in each volume. This provides a means of recovering the high-mass tail of the HMF using only a small subsample of the halo population. We validate these relations using both comoving and light-cone halo catalogues from numerical simulations. Finally, we investigate the sensitivity of the $r$-LOS framework to primordial non-Gaussianity, focusing on its impact on the statistics of massive clusters.

</details>


### [114] [Constraining Dark Matter Gravitational Production with the 21cm Signal at the Cosmic Dawn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15454)
*André A. Costa,Valdir B. Bezerra*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了暗物质可能仅通过纯引力相互作用产生，并利用宇宙黎明时期的21厘米亮度温度数据对其质量施加上限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质的本质和起源尚不清楚，且尚未在实验中观测到其与标准模型粒子的明确相互作用，暗示其可能仅由引力产生。

Method: 基于宇宙黎明时期21厘米亮度温度的吸收特征，对纯引力产生的暗物质质量进行理论约束分析。

Result: 利用当前21厘米观测数据可对暗物质质量给出上限约束，未来更精确的测量有望进一步加强该约束。

Conclusion: 引力产暗物质假说可通过21厘米信号进行检验，为理解暗物质起源提供新途径。

Abstract: The origin of dark matter is one of the major problems in modern cosmology. We can infer its existence from the gravitational potential wells around astrophysical objects, but there is no convincing explanation for what its nature and origin are. Moreover, experiments for direct detection, indirect detection, or particle production in accelerators have not shown, until now, any conclusive interaction between dark matter and particles in the standard model. This may imply that dark matter does not interact with ordinary matter and originates from pure gravitational interaction. In this manuscript, we investigate the possibility to constrain the gravitational production of dark matter from the 21cm brightness temperature at the cosmic dawn. Considering only the time when the absorption profile occurs, we can impose upper limits on the dark matter's mass. Future measurements have potential to extend current constraints and help understanding the nature of dark matter.

</details>


### [115] [From $3\times2$pt to $4\times3$pt: calibrating galaxy bias and intrinsic alignments with galaxy clustering and weak lensing bispectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15471)
*Raphaël Kou,Antony Lewis*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了将星系聚类和弱引力透镜的两点与三点统计（功率谱和双谱）结合（称为4×3pt）以提升宇宙学约束，特别是暗能量参数的精度，并通过费雪预报验证其在Euclid类巡天中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用星系聚类和弱引力透镜的高阶统计（如双谱）来提取更多宇宙学信息，并校准天体物理系统误差（如星系偏差和内禀对齐）。

Method: 采用一致建模框架（TATT模型描述星系偏差和内禀对齐），结合1环功率谱与树级双谱（包括所有自相关与交叉双谱），进行Euclid类巡天的费雪预报；引入斜谱压缩以扩展至10个层析红移切片。

Result: 与CMB数据联合时，4×3pt比传统3×2pt提升约30%暗能量FOM；使用斜谱后，有/无CMB时FOM分别提升20%/37%；改进主要源于三点统计对二阶星系偏差的校准，尤其是星系自双谱和GGL双谱；GGL双谱也显著降低重子反馈与内禀对齐参数间的简并。

Conclusion: 星系聚类与弱透镜双谱的联合分析能有效校准星系偏差和内禀对齐，在第四代星系巡天中显著提升宇宙学参数（尤其是暗能量）约束精度。

Abstract: Combining galaxy clustering and weak gravitational lensing beyond two-point statistics offers a way to extract additional cosmological information while calibrating astrophysical nuisance parameters. We investigate this approach by combining 1-loop power spectra with tree-level bispectra, including all auto- and cross-bispectra of galaxy clustering and weak lensing, a combination that we refer to as $4\times3$pt by analogy with the well-known $3\times2$pt analysis. Modelling two- and three-point statistics consistently, with galaxy bias and intrinsic alignments described by the TATT model, we perform Fisher forecasts of dark energy constraints for a Euclid-like survey, with conservative scale cuts. When combined with CMB data, adding $4\times3$pt bispectra to the $3\times2$pt improves the dark energy figure of merit by about $30\%$, a gain that remains stable when using the three, four or five lowest redshift bins of our ten. To extend the analysis to ten tomographic bins, we compress the bispectra into skew-spectra, which improve the figure of merit by $20\%$ with the CMB and by $37\%$ without. We show that most of this improvement comes from the calibration of second-order galaxy bias parameters by three-point statistics, mainly by the galaxy-auto and the GGL bispectra. We also find that baryonic feedback is correlated with second-order intrinsic alignment parameters once the amplitude of the matter power spectrum is constrained by the CMB, and that bispectra reduce this correlation by calibrating intrinsic alignments, almost entirely through the GGL bispectra, a mechanism that could become more valuable with more flexible baryonic feedback models. These results demonstrate the potential of combined galaxy clustering and weak lensing bispectra to improve cosmological constraints from stage-IV galaxy surveys through the calibration of galaxy bias and intrinsic alignments.

</details>


### [116] [Enhanced Early Structure from Axiodilatons & 21cm Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15647)
*Chan Youn Weon,Carsten van de Bruck,C. P. Burgess,Jeong Han Kim,Adam Smith*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了轴子-膨胀子模型在宇宙学中的应用，特别是其对早期结构形成和21cm信号的影响，发现该模型能自然缓解ΛCDM模型中的一些疑难问题，并有望被SKA1-Low观测区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决ΛCDM模型中存在的Hubble张力、暗能量演化等疑难问题，并探索轴子-膨胀子模型在早期结构形成方面的意外优势。

Method: 基于轴子-膨胀子模型，详细推导其对21cm信号相关物理过程（如超精细跃迁、Lyα耦合、X射线加热与电离等）的影响，并结合SKA1-Low观测能力进行预测分析。

Result: 轴子-膨胀子模型可增强早期结构形成；SKA1-Low有望以2σ置信度区分耦合强度低至~0.02的膨胀子模型与标准ΛCDM；给出了质量依赖的完整宇宙学可观测量推导。

Conclusion: 轴子-膨胀子模型不仅自然缓解多个ΛCDM疑难，还对21cm宇宙学具有可观测效应，是连接粒子物理与早期宇宙观测的重要候选框架。

Abstract: Axiodilatons provide a promising unified model of the Dark Sector whose properties are motivated by the use of accidental approximate symmetries to embed the light fields needed for observations into well-motivated UV physics in a technically natural way. These models turn out to help ameliorate many current puzzles in $Λ$CDM phenomenology (e.g. the Hubble tension and the evidence for evolving Dark Energy) `for free' despite not being designed for that purpose. We here explore in more detail another serendipitous feature of these models: their tendency to enhance early structure formation, in a way that seems suggestive given the accumulating evidence for structure arising earlier than expected in $Λ$CDM. Since early structure growth is the cleanest to study we explore in detail the expected 21cm signal produced by axiodilaton models, using the same parameter choices that improve agreement with observations elsewhere in cosmology. For fixed astrophysics, we forecast that SKA1-Low could distinguish dilaton couplings as small as a few times $10^{-2}$ from $Λ$CDM at $2σ$, mainly because of the earlier formation of structure. We provide complete derivations of the mass dependence of the relevant cosmological observables, including how dilaton-dependent baryon and electron masses affect the hyperfine transition, the Ly$α$ coupling, X-ray heating and ionization during cosmic dawn and the epoch of reionization. We provide details of radiative transfer, atomic physics and the equations governing the thermal and ionization state of the intergalactic medium.

</details>


### [117] [Can we constrain cosmology with compound gravitational lenses?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15714)
*Duncan J. Bowden,Tian Li,Aprajita Verma,Thomas E. Collett,Philip Holloway*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了双源面透镜（DSPLs）中复合透镜效应引起的质量片变换对宇宙学参数（如距离比β、w0和Ωm）推断的系统性偏差，并通过模拟分析表明，提高模型复杂度和筛选高质量样本可显著减小偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双源面透镜（DSPLs）中的复合透镜效应会引入多个质量片变换，从而系统性地偏置用于宇宙学约束的距离比β，需量化并校正该偏差。

Method: 基于SDSS J0946+1006构建模拟DSPL系统，改变源1的质量分布；生成Euclid可观测的DSPL模拟星表；对β进行透镜建模与后验推断，并进一步约束wCDM宇宙学参数。

Result: 发现源1的恒星-晕质量比与恢复的β强相关；Euclid分辨率下该相关性被统计误差掩盖；使用‘金样本’可将w0和Ωm的偏差分别控制在约2σ（Euclid模拟）和1σ（HST成像）内。

Conclusion: DSPLs作为独立宇宙学探针前景广阔，但必须剔除透镜信息不可靠的系统，并在强复合透镜情形下采用足够复杂的模型以准确拟合观测。

Abstract: Compound lensing in double-source-plane lenses (DSPLs) introduces multiple mass-sheet transformations that bias the inferred distance ratios $β$ used to constrain cosmology. We present the recovered $β$ from lens models of simulated compound lenses and explore the systematic biases that arise from a mass-sheet transformation in the first source plane. We examined simulated observations based on SDSS J0946+1006, in which we varied the matter distribution of source 1. We found a strong correlation between the stellar-to-halo mass and the recovered $β$. We produced a simulated catalogue of DSPLs detectable by the Euclid telescope to see if the same systematic propagates to a more varied population. But at Euclid's resolution, the properties of source 1 were poorly constrained, and any correlated bias was lost in the statistical uncertainty. We inferred $w_0$ and $Ω_{\rm m}$ for a flat $w$CDM cosmology from our $β$ posteriors for our combined sample. We found the cosmology biased when using the full mock Euclid sample, but reduced the bias substantially using a limited, well-modelled ''gold'' sub-sample, bringing it to within $\sim2σ$ and $\sim1σ$ of the ground-truth cosmology for simulated Euclid and Hubble Space Telescope imaging, respectively. Therefore, we must eliminate systems with unreliable lensing information, and for extreme compound lensing, the model complexity must be sufficient to reproduce the lensing observations. DSPLs remain a promising cosmological probe that could provide independent and complementary constraints to existing probes.

</details>


### [118] [Quasars as a self-sufficient probe of the distance duality relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15728)
*Davide Pedrotti,Júlia Laguna-Miralles,Natalie B. Hogg*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用类星体作为自洽探针来检验距离对偶关系（duality relation），通过结合UV-X射线光度距离与光谱天体测量和混响映射（SARM）得到的角直径距离，对Mrk 509进行了概念验证，并预测了探测常数型及红移依赖型违反所需的类星体数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 距离对偶关系是标准光子传播下的基本关系，其检验通常需组合不同探针的距离测量，存在红移匹配与各向同性假设等限制；本文旨在寻找能同时测得两种距离的自洽探针（如类星体），以规避这些限制并可检验宇宙各向同性。

Method: 利用类星体的UV-X射线关系获取光度距离，结合光谱天体测量与混响映射（SARM）获得角直径距离，构建η ≡ d_L / [(1+z)² d_A] 检验量；以Mrk 509为样例进行实证分析，并基于误差传播与统计显著性要求，预测探测不同形式违反（常数型、线性红移依赖型）所需类星体数目。

Result: 对Mrk 509测得η = 0.50^{+0.61}_{-0.27}，与无违反一致（0.8σ）；预测检测常数型违反α₀=−0.075需274–840个类星体（3σ），而探测红移依赖型违反需2.6×10⁴–8×10⁴个；GRAVITY+望远镜有望使常数型检验具备可行性。

Conclusion: 类星体（特别是结合SARM技术）具备作为自洽探针检验距离对偶关系的潜力，其结果可区分哈勃张力的早期宇宙起源与Ia型超新星校准偏差或距离对偶破缺，为解决宇宙学基础问题提供新路径。

Abstract: The distance duality relation links luminosity and angular diameter distances under standard photon propagation. It is usually tested by combining distances from different probes, which requires matching their redshifts and assuming sky isotropy. Self-sufficient probes, which measure both distances for the same object, avoid the first requirement and make the second testable. We assess whether quasars can serve as such probes, combining luminosity distances from the UV--X-ray relation with angular diameter distances from spectroastrometry and reverberation mapping (SARM). As a proof-of-concept, we apply the test to the quasar Mrk~509 and find $η\equiv d_{\rm L}/[(1+z)^2d_{\rm A}] = 0.50^{+0.61}_{-0.27}$, consistent with no violation at $0.8σ$. We then forecast the number of quasars needed to detect a constant and a linearly redshift-dependent violation, motivated by the findings of Teixeira et al. (2025) with potential implications for the Hubble tension and dark energy. A constant violation, $α_0=-0.075$, equivalent to a recalibration of the SNIa distance scale, could be detected at $3σ$ with 274--840 quasars. Since SARM distances, unlike BAO, do not rely on early-time physics, this would separate an early-time origin of the Hubble tension from a SNIa miscalibration or a distance duality violation. The redshift dependence is harder to detect: 1000 quasars would exclude $η=1$ at $3.4$--$6.6σ$, but detecting the dependence itself requires $2.6\times10^4$--$8\times10^4$. GRAVITY+ could bring quasar tests of a constant violation within reach.

</details>


### [119] [Improved delensing of CMB $B$-modes with upcoming SO and LSST Year-1 mock dataset: Impact of photometric redshift errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15828)
*Kishan Deka,Chandra Shekhar Saraf,Pawel Bielewicz*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过结合Rubin天文台LSST星系巡天模拟数据与西蒙斯天文台（SO）CMB模拟数据，采用多示踪器层析方法进行CMB B模去透镜化，提升了10–20%的去透镜效率，成功去除约60%的透镜诱导B模功率；尽管光度红移误差会带来星系偏置和σ₈,₀参数偏差，但对张量标量比r的推断无显著系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度CMB观测中，探测原初引力波（PGWs）是核心目标之一，但弱引力透镜效应产生的B模污染会掩盖原初信号，因此需有效去除透镜诱导B模以提升PGWs振幅约束精度。

Method: 采用基于LSST星系巡天（含真实光度红移误差）与SO CMB模拟（含复杂银河系前景）的多示踪器层析delensing方法，联合分析CMB温度/极化与星系分布信息。

Result: 相比仅用CMB数据，多示踪器方法提升delensing效率10–20%，去除约60%的透镜诱导B模功率；光度红移误差导致星系偏置和σ₈,₀参数偏差，但对tensor-to-scalar ratio r的估计无显著系统偏差。

Conclusion: 多示踪器delensing（尤其是结合LSST与SO数据）是提升原初B模探测灵敏度的有效手段，且对关键宇宙学参数r具有鲁棒性，为未来CMB实验提供重要分析范式。

Abstract: In the era of high-precision Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) anisotropy measurements, detection of primordial gravitational waves (PGWs) is one of the main goals of current CMB experiments. Detection of a divergence-free ($B$-mode) component in CMB polarisation will provide direct evidence of inflationary PGWs in the early universe. However, weak gravitational lensing of CMB photons due to intervening mass distribution of the Universe distorts the primordial polarisation patterns, introducing an additional lensing-induced $B$-mode signal. Removing these lensing-induced $B$-modes is essential for improving constraints on the amplitude of PGWs. In this work, we present a multi-tracer delensing analysis using simulations of the Rubin Observatory LSST galaxy catalogue and Simons Observatory (SO) CMB maps. We use realistic photometric redshift errors for LSST mock catalogue and high complexity Galactic foregrounds for CMB simulations. By adopting a tomographic multi-tracer approach that combines the CMB and LSST mock data, we achieved a 10-20% higher delensing efficiency compared with using CMB data alone. Our analysis removes approximately 60% of the lensing-induced $B$-mode power. We find that photometric redshift errors in the mock galaxy catalogue introduce biases in the inferred galaxy bias and $σ_{8,0}$ parameters. However, these uncertainties do not produce a significant systematic bias in the inferred tensor-to-scalar ratio.

</details>


### [120] [Improved constraints on axion inflation with the new code AxioGen: an efficient generator of curvature perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15841)
*Matthew Reinhard,Zachary Slepian,Atsushi Taruya*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文改进了轴子通胀模型中功率谱和双谱参数f2和f3的拟合函数，提高了小ξ参数区域的精度，并利用新拟合函数对模型参数ξ及轴子-规范场耦合能标设定了新限制；同时发布了一个开源代码，用于高效生成弱反作用区轴子通胀的原初曲率扰动实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前基于近似解计算的f2和f3在小ξ参数下精度严重不足（此前工作发现其误差较大），需更精确的拟合函数以提升理论预测与观测约束的可靠性。

Method: 通过更精确求解规范场模函数，构建新的f2和f3拟合函数；结合这些函数进行参数约束分析；开发并公开一个高效数值代码，用于生成原初曲率扰动样本并支持高阶关联函数计算。

Result: 获得了比以往更精确的f2和f3拟合函数；给出了ξ的新观测约束及φ^{1/3}和φ^{2/3}势下的轴子-规范场耦合能标下限；发布了首个支持弱反作用区轴子通胀高阶统计计算的公开代码。

Conclusion: 本文显著提升了轴子通胀原初扰动理论建模的精度与实用性，为CMB和大尺度结构观测提供更可靠的理论接口。

Abstract: Axion inflation, in which a pseudoscalar inflaton couples to a $U(1)$ gauge field via a Chern-Simons interaction, has garnered significant interest due to its rich phenomenology. The power spectrum and bispectrum for this model are often parametrized by functions known as $f_2$ and $f_3$, respectively. Previous work computed $f_2$ and $f_3$ using an approximation for the gauge field mode functions. While these approximate solutions are fairly accurate ($\mathcal{O}(10\%)$) for large values of the model parameter $ξ$, which controls gauge field particle production, our recent work has shown them to be quite inaccurate for smaller values of $ξ$. We present new fitting functions for $f_2$ and $f_3$ that are significantly more accurate than those previously used. We use these functions to place new constraints on $ξ$ and also set lower bounds for the energy scale of the axion gauge field coupling for $φ^{1/3}$ and $φ^{2/3}$ inflationary potentials. Additionally, we present a new public code to efficiently generate realizations of the primordial curvature perturbation for axion inflation in the weak backreaction regime. This code enables numerical computation of higher order correlators and provides a novel path to initial conditions for large-scale structure formation.

</details>


### [121] [Cosmic Ray Heating of the Early IGM: Realistic Galaxy Populations, Reduced Efficiency, and 21-cm Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15858)
*Michael Pagano,Andrei Mesinger,Carmelo Evoli,Sruthi Ravikularaman*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了宇宙射线（CRs）在再电离前对星系际介质（IGM）的加热效应，发现由于采用更真实的动量谱和星系模型，CR加热比以往估计弱约28倍，通常弱于X射线加热；仅当CR效率远高于本地观测值时，才可能在21-cm功率谱中与X射线加热区分，而EDGES观测到的吸收 trough 平坦化需要不现实的超高CR效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究高估了宇宙射线对IGM的加热效应，因其采用简化的动能幂律谱和星系模型；本文旨在基于更物理的动量幂律谱、库仑损失机制及数据驱动的星系种群，重新定量评估CR加热的真实贡献。

Method: 在21cmFAST框架中建模CR加热：(i) 采用扩散激波加速理论预测的动量幂律能谱；(ii) 包含自由电子引起的库仑能量损失；(iii) 由原子冷却与分子冷却星系的实际观测约束种群注入CR；并与X射线加热对比分析其对全球21-cm信号及功率谱的影响。

Result: CR加热比先前估计弱约12–28倍；在本地观测支持的效率下，其贡献弱于标准X射线加热；仅当CR效率提高数倍时，才可在21-cm功率谱中与X射线加热区分；EDGES报告的平坦吸收 trough 需要CR效率超出本地超新星预算两个数量级以上，极不可行。

Conclusion: 宇宙射线在再电离前对IGM的加热作用被显著高估；在合理物理假设下，其效应远小于X射线加热，单靠全球21-cm信号无法区分二者；利用21-cm功率谱区分需非物理的高CR效率，因此CR加热难以解释当前观测异常（如EDGES）。

Abstract: Cosmic rays (CRs) accelerated by the first supernovae could heat the intergalactic medium (IGM) before reionization. Previous 21-cm simulations, assuming a kinetic-energy power-law spectrum and simplified galaxy populations, found that CR heating can rival X-ray heating from high-mass X-ray binaries, imprint a distinct signature on the 21-cm power spectrum, and flatten the absorption trough of the global 21-cm signal. Here we model CR heating in 21cmFAST with: (i) the momentum power-law spectrum predicted by diffusive shock acceleration; (ii) Coulomb losses off free electrons; and (iii) injection from data-informed populations of atomic-cooling and molecularly cooled galaxies. We find that CR heating is about an order of magnitude weaker than previously estimated because a momentum power law places little energy in the sub-relativistic protons ($\lesssim 40$ MeV) that supply 99% of the heat. For our fiducial spectral slope, at the hard end of those inferred for Galactic supernova remnants, and for matched efficiency, this reduces the heating by a factor of $\approx 12$, and together with a more realistic galaxy model, by a factor of $\approx 28$ at $z = 15$. At the efficiencies that supernovae can supply, CR heating is weaker than standard X-ray heating, and the global signal alone cannot tell the two apart. Distinguishing CR from X-ray heating with the 21-cm power spectrum requires CR efficiencies a few times higher than those expected from local observations. At such efficiencies, CR heating yields up to $\approx 55\%$ more power than X-ray heating at matched global signal (median over scales), compared with only 3-6% at a locally motivated efficiency. A flattened absorption trough, like the one claimed by EDGES, requires efficiencies over two orders of magnitude above the local ones, beyond plausible Population III supernova budgets.

</details>


### [122] [Cosmological Signals from Finite-Lifetime Domain Wall Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15860)
*Fengwei Yang,Kylar Greene,Aurora Ireland,Yuhsin Tsai*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了由亚稳态畴壁网络引发的宇宙学扰动，推导出标量和张量功率谱的解析表达式，并给出了观测约束下的畴壁能量密度上限，指出其可能产生的CMB B模信号可与暴胀信号相当甚至更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解亚稳态畴壁网络对宇宙学扰动的影响，并评估其在CMB观测中的可观测性。

Method: 采用非连接段模型，在长波极限下解析推导标量和张量功率谱，并结合观测约束分析畴壁能量密度上限及其B模信号强度。

Result: 得到标量与张量功率谱在标度律阶段具有固定振幅比；谱随网络演化增强并在坍缩时间对应尺度达到峰值；给出畴壁坍缩时能量密度的观测上限；饱和该上限时B模信号可达r~0.03量级。

Conclusion: 亚稳态畴壁网络可能产生显著的CMB B模信号，未来CMB偏振实验有望探测到这一非暴胀起源的张量扰动。

Abstract: We study cosmological perturbations sourced by metastable domain wall (DW) networks. Using the unconnected segment model, we derive analytic expressions for the scalar and tensor power spectra in the long-wavelength regime, finding a fixed ratio between their amplitudes during the scaling regime. These spectra grow as the network evolves, driven by the increasing correlation length and slower redshifting of the wall energy density relative to radiation, and peak at a scale set by the network's collapse time. Comparing the scalar spectrum with observational constraints, we derive bounds on the DW energy density at collapse, which we apply to $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and axion models. When these bounds are saturated, the resulting CMB $B$-mode signal can be comparable to or larger than the inflationary signal for a tensor-to-scalar ratio $r \sim 0.03$, potentially bringing it within reach of future CMB polarization experiments.

</details>


### [123] [Measuring the growth index in the $w_0w_a$CDM background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15881)
*Ícaro. B. S. Cortês,Léo G. Medeiros,Ronaldo C. Batista*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文测量了在w0waCDM背景模型下描述物质扰动增长的参数γ，发现其值为0.633±0.024，与ΛCDM模型下结果一致，并仍以3.4σ显著性偏离广义相对论预期值γ=0.55；主要张力来源是fσ8数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于DESI等新观测显示动力学暗能量（w0waCDM）优于ΛCDM，需检验γ参数在该模型下的取值是否仍偏离广义相对论预期，以判断‘γ张力’是否依赖于背景宇宙学模型。

Method: 联合Planck CMB、DESI BAO、Phanteon+ SNe Ia及fσ8增长数据，在w0waCDM模型框架下对γ进行贝叶斯参数估计，并对比多种数据组合以识别张力来源。

Result: 得到γ_w0wa = 0.633 ± 0.024，与先前ΛCDM下结果（0.633^{+0.025}_{-0.024}）一致；该值以3.4σ偏离GR理论值γ=0.55；多组数据组合的γ后验分布一致，且fσ8数据是主导张力的关键数据集。

Conclusion: γ张力并非源于ΛCDM背景假设，而更可能反映结构增长观测（尤其是fσ8）与广义相对论预言之间的本质不一致，对修改引力或系统误差研究具有重要启示。

Abstract: We produce a measurement of the growth index, $γ$, which is used to parametrize the scale-independent growth of linear matter perturbations on sub-horizon scales, for the $w_0w_a$CDM background model. In 2023, assuming the $Λ$CDM background, the value $γ=0.633^{+0.025}_{-0.024}$ was obtained, excluding the General Relativity value of $γ=0.55$ at $3.7σ$ statistical significance. In 2025, an analysis of baryon acoustic oscillation data from the Dark Energy Spectroscopy Instrument (DESI) demonstrated a preference for a dynamical dark energy over the cosmological constant model, $Λ$, ranging from $2.8-4.2σ$, depending on the external datasets used. Therefore it is important to determine how the $γ$ measurement is modified in the context of dynamical dark energy and verify whether the tension with the expected theoretical value persists. Considering data from Planck cosmic microwave background anisotropies, $fσ_{8}$, baryon acoustic oscillation from DESI and supernova Ia luminosity distances from Phanteon+, we find $γ_{w_0w_a}=0.633\pm0.024$, essentially the same value found for the $Λ$CDM background. This result excludes the General Relativity value for the $w_0w_a$CDM background evolution at $3.4σ$ level. We also show that the $γ$ posteriors across many datasets combinations are consistent with each other and that main driver of the "$γ$ tension" is the $fσ_{8}$ dataset.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了欧几里得任务可见光成像仪（VIS）在第一次数据发布（DR1）中交付的数据特性，包括42092幅校准图像、覆盖2092平方度天区，并评估了其在测光与天体测量方面的精度及系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为验证欧几里得任务VIS仪器在轨性能是否满足预发射指标，并提供首批高质量天文观测数据以支持后续科学研究。

Method: 基于DR1发布的42092幅VIS校准图像，对宽视场巡天中的参考恒星样本（18.5 < I_E < 19.8）进行内部与外部比对，量化测光与天体测量精度，并分析残余系统误差（如焦平面依赖性、时间相关光度偏移、颜色相关质心偏移等）。

Result: 在四分之一焦面尺度上，内/外部天体测量弥散分别为0.08和0.18 mas，测光弥散为0.76和1.4 mmag；在源级别上分别为3.4/5.6 mas和11/25 mmag；噪声下限受暗弱星系弥散光主导；整体满足预发射任务要求。

Conclusion: DR1 VIS数据质量优异，已全面达到欧几里得任务的预设科学性能指标，标志着VIS正式进入高精度宇宙学观测阶段。

Abstract: This paper describes the characteristics of the Euclid visible imager (VIS) data delivered as part of the Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1). This data set contains 42092 calibrated frames. Validated nominal exposures cover 2092 deg$^2$ of sky. We outline the minor changes made to the VIS processing function (PF) since previous releases. For the wide survey, the photometric and astrometric accuracy of the reference stellar sample $(18.5 < I_{\rm E} < 19.8)$ is excellent. The internal and external astrometric dispersions are 0.08 and 0.18 mas, respectively, at the quadrant level, and 3.4 and 5.6 mas at the source level. The corresponding internal and external photometric dispersions are 0.76 and 1.4 mmag at the quadrant level, and 11 and 25 mmag at the source level. We characterise remaining astrometric and photometric systematic effects: focal-plane-dependent variations; time-dependent photometric offsets; and colour-dependent centroid shifts. The noise floor in the deepest VIS images appears to be set by the extended diffuse light of faint galaxies. Globally, the DR1 VIS data meet pre-launch mission requirements and the 42092 images it contains provide our first substantial look at the sky as seen by VIS.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用欧几里得（Euclid）望远镜的无狭缝光谱数据，首次对四个强引力透镜系统中的背景源进行了光谱红移测量与发射线图像重建，验证了该方法在大规模欧几里得透镜样本中应用的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引力透镜建模高度依赖背景源的精确位置和红移；欧几里得望远镜兼具高分辨率多波段成像与无狭缝光谱能力，为大规模透镜研究提供了独特机遇。

Method: 利用欧几里得NISP仪器获取的无狭缝光谱数据，测定四个强透镜系统的背景源光谱红移，并重建其空间分辨的发射线图像。

Result: 成功获得四个不同尺度（星系团、星系尺度）透镜系统中背景源的光谱红移，包括两个巨弧、一个四重AGN候选体和一个爱因斯坦环；首次基于NISP光谱实现强透镜特征的光谱红移测定。

Conclusion: 该工作是欧几里得无狭缝光谱应用于强引力透镜研究的概念验证，证明该方法可推广至欧几里得任务将发现的海量透镜样本，显著提升透镜建模与宇宙学应用的精度。

Abstract: Applications of strong gravitational lensing in astrophysics and cosmology critically depend on building reliable strong lensing models, which require accurate measurements of on-sky positions of multiple images and of the redshifts of the background sources from which they originate. In this context, Euclid is a unique facility, as it simultaneously provides high-resolution multi-band photometric imaging and slitless spectroscopy for an unprecedentedly large sample of gravitational lenses, most of which are newly discovered. In this work, we exploit Euclid slitless spectroscopic data to determine the redshifts of four spatially extended lensed sources, and to reconstruct emission-line images. Specifically, two of the target sources are lensed into giant arcs by the massive galaxy clusters SPT-CLJ0411$-$4819 ($z_\mathrm{d}=0.424$, where "d" denotes the lensing deflector) and Abell 2280 ($z_\mathrm{d}=0.326$). The third system is a quadruply imaged active galactic nucleus (AGN) candidate strongly lensed by a massive galaxy at $z_\mathrm{d}=0.68$, while the fourth is a galaxy-scale Einstein ring produced by a lens galaxy at $z_\mathrm{d}=0.235$. This work represents a proof of concept for the exploitation of Euclid slitless spectroscopic data in the study of strong gravitational lensing, providing one of the first determinations of spectroscopic redshifts for strongly lensed features from NISP spectra. These results demonstrate the feasibility of applying the same methodology to the very large sample of gravitational lenses that Euclid has started to discover and is expected to identify over the course of the mission.

</details>


### [126] [FastBox: A lightweight Python package for rapid cosmological 21cm intensity mapping simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15906)
*Philip Bull,Bruno B. Bizarria,Melis O. Irfan,Geoff Murphy*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: FastBox是一个用于生成宇宙学信号（特别是21cm强度映射）三维共动盒子模拟的轻量级Python工具包，支持密度场建模、红移空间畸变、仪器系统误差、前景污染及滤波、统计量估计等功能，并可联合模拟21cm与星系示踪体以支持交叉功率谱分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为21cm强度映射实验提供快速、物理真实且灵活的端到端模拟测试平台，支撑宇宙学分析流程的开发与验证。

Method: 基于高斯/对数正态初密度场生成三维模拟；引入红移空间畸变、线性偏置、辐射计噪声、波束卷积、弥散/点源前景模型及PCA/ICA等滤波方法；支持功率谱、两点相关函数和等边三谱估计；可对同一密度场进行泊松采样生成星系样本。

Result: 实现了高效、模块化、可配置的宇宙学信号模拟框架，支持各向异性网格、多统计量估计及21cm-星系交叉分析。

Conclusion: FastBox是一个轻量但物理完备的模拟工具，适用于21cm IM实验的数据分析方法研发与系统误差研究。

Abstract: FastBox is a Python package for generating fast, physically realistic simulations of cosmological signals in three-dimensional co-moving boxes, with the primary focus of its application being 21cm intensity mapping (IM) experiments. It provides a framework for producing cosmology-dependent Gaussian and log-normal density fields, as well as modelling the effects of redshift-space distortions and linear biasing, among others. Models of instrumental systematics are incorporated, including radiometer noise and beam convolutions. Diffuse and point source foreground models are included, along with a number of foreground filtering strategies such as PCA, ICA, and transfer function correction via mock signal injection. Lastly, estimators are provided for power spectra, the two-point correlation function, and the equilateral bispectrum. The same underlying density field can also be Poisson-sampled into a discrete galaxy catalogue, so that 21cm and galaxy tracers are simulated from common initial conditions and their cross-power spectrum can be measured. Simulation grids may be cubic or anisotropic, with independent side lengths and cell counts along each Cartesian axis. FastBox is designed as a lightweight but realistic test-bench for the development and validation of end-to-end cosmological analysis pipelines.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文介绍了欧几里得任务数据发布1（DR1）中测光红移处理流程的设计，并评估了其宇宙学相关产品的性能，结果表明在较亮星等（IE<24）下性能接近任务设计要求，但在标称深度（IE<24.5）下略低于要求，LSST数据有望改善此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧几里得任务旨在通过弱引力透镜和星系团聚测量大尺度结构，以百分比精度约束暗能量状态方程参数w；为此需提供高精度的测光红移、校正测光和目标分类等宇宙学关键产品。

Method: 设计并实现DR1-Foundation中的测光红移处理流程；利用重叠的光谱红移样本（加权匹配不同流量限制目标样本）验证测光红移点估计及概率分布函数性能；同时评估恒星与星系SED、PSF建模等其他宇宙学相关输出。

Result: 在IE<24.5时，测光红移散射达设计值的40%，异常值比例为设计值的2倍；在IE<24时性能基本满足要求；该结果与发射前分析及外部数据（浅约0.3mag）一致；LSST数据将在最终宇宙学分析前缓解性能不足问题。

Conclusion: 当前DR1-Foundation的测光红移等宇宙学产品在较亮星等下已满足科学需求，虽在标称深度略逊于设计指标，但整体性能可信且可随后续数据（如LSST）改进，为欧几里得宇宙学目标奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We present the pipeline design of the photometric redshift processing function for Data Release 1 (DR1) of the $Euclid$ mission, and the validation and performance of our cosmology-related products that are released as part of DR1-Foundation. The $Euclid$ mission aims to constrain the equation-of-state parameter of dark energy, $w$, to percent-level accuracy through the measurement of large-scale structure via weak gravitational lensing and galaxy clustering. As part of DR1-Foundation we release determinations of photometric redshifts (photo-$z$), corrected photometry, and object classes that are used to form the basis of the cosmological samples. The release furthermore includes estimates of the spectral energy distributions of stars and galaxies for the careful modelling of the point-spread function for weak-lensing measurement. We estimate the performance of the photo-$z$ point estimates and probability distribution functions using overlapping spectroscopic redshifts, which are weighted to match different flux-limited target samples. We find that the current performance is somewhat below mission design requirements ($40\%$ in scatter and a factor of 2 in outlier fraction) at the nominal survey depth ($I_{\rm E}<24.5$), but closely matching requirements at slightly brighter magnitudes ($I_{\rm E}<24$), consistent with pre-launch analyses and the slightly shallower external data (up to $0.3\,\mathrm{mag}$) with respect to end-of-mission expectations. Data from Rubin-LSST will help alleviate this issue by the time of the final cosmology analyses. The other cosmology-related outputs are tested to the extent that is possible with currently available comparison data. This paper is partnered by an article describing the measurement of the physical properties of galaxies for the purpose of non-cosmological science.

</details>


### [128] [Transient early reionization allows for a high optical depth while remaining consistent with kSZ measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16005)
*Prakhar Bansal,Nicholas Kern,Dragan Huterer*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种超大质量第三星族星的自洽模型，该模型能在高红移（z≈20–25）引发早期、短暂再电离，使CMB光学深度τ提升至≈0.09，同时兼容当前kSZ与Lyα森林观测约束；并预测Simons Observatory等数据可将z>12累积光学深度约束至±0.008。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决DES数据对动力学暗能量和低中微子质量的偏好因较高CMB光学深度（τ≈0.09）而被削弱的问题，并协调低ℓ CMB与kSZ+Lyα森林对较低τ（≈0.055）的偏好矛盾。

Method: 构建新型自洽模型，描述超大质量第三星族星在z≈20–25部分电离星系际介质，计算其对kSZ信号及总光学深度τ的贡献，并结合Simons Observatory、Lyα森林和UV光度函数数据进行预报分析。

Result: 该早期再电离相可使τ升至≈0.09，且不违背现有kSZ与Lyα森林限制；Simons Observatory等联合观测有望将z>12的τ约束精度达±0.008。

Conclusion: 超大质量Pop III星驱动的早期再电离是协调不同宇宙学探针对τ分歧的可行机制，凸显高红移再电离物理建模对精确宇宙学的重要性。

Abstract: Recent works have shown that a CMB optical depth of $τ\approx0.09$, above the Planck low-$\ell$ polarization value of 0.054, would weaken the preference of DESI data for dynamical dark energy and for a low neutrino mass. Apart from the low-$\ell$ CMB, another probe that prefers a $τ\sim0.055$ is the kinetic Sunyaev--Zel'dovich (kSZ) effect when combined with the end of hydrogen reionization as measured by the Ly$α$ forest. In this work we use a new self-consistent model of supermassive Population~III stars, which partially ionize the intergalactic medium at high redshifts ($z\approx20$--$25$), to predict the kSZ signal. We show that such an early, transient reionization phase can raise $τ$ to $\approx0.09$ while remaining consistent with current kSZ and Ly-$α$ forest constraints. We forecast that the Simons Observatory, combined with Ly-$α$ forest and UV luminosity function data, can constrain the optical depth accumulated above $z=12$ to $\pm0.008$ in our fiducial supermassive Pop~III scenario.

</details>


### [129] [A flash before dawn: 21 cm signatures of transient reionization from supermassive Pop III stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16006)
*Nicholas Kern,Prakhar Bansal,Dragan Huterer*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了高电子散射光学深度τ（~0.09）对ΛCDM模型偏离的缓解作用，并提出超大质量星族III恒星作为非标准再电离模型，通过自洽模拟其多波段反馈，预测其在宇宙黎明时期21 cm信号中的独特特征，并评估其在SKA等未来射电望远镜中的可探测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前宇宙学观测（如星系巡天与CMB）倾向于偏离ΛCDM模型（如演化暗能量或小中微子质量），但较高的CMB光学深度τ（~0.09）可能削弱该倾向；然而标准再电离模型难以兼容高τ与星系光谱对再电离结束时间的限制，因此需探索非标准再电离机制，如超大质量星族III恒星。

Method: 构建首个在大型半数值宇宙学模拟中自洽纳入超大质量星族III恒星（M~10⁵ M⊙）的电离、Lyman-Werner及X射线反馈的模型，并据此预测其对宇宙黎明时期大尺度21 cm信号的影响，结合SKA等未来射电阵列进行可探测性预报。

Result: 预测SKA等未来射电望远镜可在高显著性下探测到该模型的21 cm功率谱特征，并有望将τ的模型相关约束精度达σ_τ~0.012，足以在>3σ置信度下区分本文设定的τ~0.097场景与当前Planck测量值。

Conclusion: 超大质量星族III恒星驱动的非标准再电离模型不仅可调和高τ与星系观测的张力，其独特的21 cm印记亦为检验该模型及精确测定τ提供了强有力的新探针。

Abstract: Recent data from cosmological galaxy surveys combined with cosmic microwave background (CMB) data give a preference for a departure from $Λ$CDM, favoring a model with an evolving dark energy equation of state or a small neutrino mass. However, recent works have shown that the electron scattering optical depth to the CMB ($τ$) can reduce this preference if $τ\sim0.09$, larger than that preferred by CMB low-$\ell$ E-mode polarization data. With standard monotonic hydrogen reionization histories, such a high value of $τ$ is in strong tension with spectroscopic galaxy data constraining the end of reionization. Thus, high-$τ$ models are restricted to non-standard reionization scenarios, where astrophysical sources inject an appreciable amount of ionizing photons at early times. One recently studied model for this is a supermassive population III star scenario, where radiative feedback from a self-annihilating dark matter particle enables a pathway to form $M\sim10^5 M_\odot$ stars that, upon collapse, photoionize a surrounding volume large enough to source appreciable contributions to $τ$ as a population. Here we present the first self-consistent treatment of the feedback from these supermassive population III stars within a large semi-numerical cosmological simulation, accounting for their ionizing, Lyman-Werner, and X-ray feedback on the intergalactic medium. We use it to predict the distinct signatures on the large-scale 21 cm signal at Cosmic Dawn, and forecast its detectability. We find that the 21 cm power spectrum probed by future radio survey arrays like the Square Kilometre Array could detect signatures of these supermassive population III at high significance and set model-dependent constraints on $τ$ that approach $σ_τ\sim0.012$, enough to distinguish our fiducial $τ\sim0.097$ scenario from current Planck values at $>3σ$.

</details>


### [130] [Shear, vorticity, and $f(R)$ theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16026)
*Antonino Del Popolo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩展球形坍缩模型（ESCM）在Hu-Sawicki f(R) 引力理论中对坍缩阈值、转折密度与半径及晕质量函数的影响，并评估其与广义相对论（GR）的可区分性。结果表明，f(R) 对晕丰度（尤其在高红移）有显著影响，有望作为区分f(R)与GR的有效探针；而转折半径等量因观测误差较大难以提供有效约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验f(R)引力理论与广义相对论（GR）的可观测差异，特别是通过结构形成中的非线性动力学特征（如坍缩阈值、转折半径、晕质量函数）寻找可被当前或未来巡天观测区分的信号。

Method: 采用包含剪切、涡度和变色龙屏蔽机制的扩展球形坍缩模型（ESCM），计算Hu-Sawicki f(R)模型中红移z和质量M依赖的坍缩阈值δ_c(z)与δ_c(M)、转折密度δ_t、转折半径R_t，并结合运动学边界方法（excursion set）推导晕质量函数；对比标准球形坍缩模型（SSCM）与ESCM下GR与不同|f_R0|取值（F4/F5/F6）的差异。

Result: ESCM下δ_c呈现质量、红移和|f_R0|依赖；GR与f(R)大场极限间δ_c差异为几%，而SSCM下可达~20%；δ_t差异达27–29%，R_t差异约6.5%；考虑变色龙屏蔽后，仅F5（|f_R0|=10^{-5}）在δ_t上保留~10%差异，但R_t观测不确定性（~20%）使其无法有效区分；而f(R)预测的晕丰度（尤其高红移）与GR存在可观测差异。

Conclusion: 转折半径R_t及相关量受观测误差限制，不适合作为f(R)的检验工具；而晕质量函数（尤其在高红移）是更稳健、有潜力区分Hu-Sawicki f(R)模型与广义相对论的非线性结构形成探针。

Abstract: We study how an extended spherical collapse model (ESCM), including shear, vorticity and chameleon screening, modifies the collapse threshold $δ_c(z)$ and $δ_c(M)$, the turnaround density $δ_t$, the turnaround radius $R_t$ and the halo mass function in the Hu-Sawicki $f(R)$ model, and whether these quantities can distinguish $f(R)$ from General Relativity (GR). Unlike the standard spherical collapse model (SSCM), in the ESCM $δ_c$ depends on mass, redshift and $|f_{R0}|$; we use it to compute the halo mass function with the excursion set approach. Between GR and the large-field (LF) limit of $f(R)$, $δ_c$ differs by a few percent within the ESCM and by about 20% between GR in the ESCM and the LF limit in the SSCM; $δ_t$ differs by about 27-29% and $R_t$ by about 6.5%. With screening, the difference between GR and screened $δ_c$ is 2.5% for $|f_{R0}|=10^{-4}$ (F4) and vanishes for $|f_{R0}|=10^{-6}$ (F6); for $δ_t$ it is larger than 20% (F4), about 10% (F5) and 0 (F6); for $R_t$ about 6.5% (F4), 3% (F5) and 0 (F6). Since observations exclude F4 and F6 is practically identical to GR, only F5 could discriminate, but the ~20% uncertainty on measured turnaround radii rules out $R_t$ and related quantities as tests of $f(R)$. The halo abundance predicted by $f(R)$, especially at high redshift, can instead distinguish $f(R)$ from GR.

</details>


### [131] [Origin of the angular momentum of filaments in the local Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16208)
*V. G. Gurzadyan,N. N. Fimin,V. M. Chechetkin*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了局部宇宙中宇宙学常数（即局部暗能量）如何通过影响潮汐相互作用和轨道交叉，促进宇宙细丝结构的角动量获取与维持，从而解释观测到的速度梯度旋转现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释近期在邻近宇宙细丝中观测到的、与旋转相容的速度梯度现象，探究其角动量起源。

Method: 基于球对称引力场等效于质点引力场的定理，理论分析宇宙学常数对开放细丝核心有限时间内角动量获取与保持的影响，并结合潮汐扭矩与轨道交叉机制建模。

Result: 宇宙学常数虽不直接施加轴向力矩，但可通过调制潮汐模式增长和有限时间角动量输运，间接促成细丝旋转；早期潮汐扭矩造成轴向角动量失衡，后续轨道交叉放大旋转。

Conclusion: 局部暗能量（宇宙学常数）在宇宙细丝旋转动力学中起关键调制作用，为观测到的细丝旋转提供了可行的理论机制。

Abstract: Velocity gradients compatible with rotation have been reported across certain nearby cosmic filaments. We show that rotational momentum might be generated in filaments in the local Universe in the presence of a cosmological constant (local dark energy) based on the theorem stating the general function for the equivalence of the gravitational field of a sphere and that of a point mass. We investigated how the cosmological constant modifies the finite-time acquisition and retention of angular momentum in an open filamentary core. Although its isotropic force exerts no direct axial torque, rotational motion can be seeded by tidal interactions with surrounding matter and incoming streams. Early tidal torques establish an imbalance in axial angular momentum, while the subsequent orbit crossing amplifies the resulting rotation, and ultimately, the cosmological constant is able to modify tidal-mode growth and finite-time angular momentum delivery.

</details>


### [132] [Galaxy Clustering Topology in Observations and Cosmological Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16324)
*Fuyu Dong,Changbom Park*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过分析SDSS星系大尺度分布的属拓扑（genus topology），并与多个宇宙学N体和流体动力学模拟（如HR4、IllustrisTNG、FLAMINGO等）生成的模拟星系样本进行比较，发现现代星系形成模型在重现观测属曲线方面显著优于早期模型（如HGC、HOD、SAM），但仍存在若干尺度依赖性偏差和小体积模拟中显著的宇宙方差效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验现代宇宙学模拟中星系形成模型对大尺度结构拓扑特征（特别是属曲线）的再现能力，以评估其物理真实性，并与早期模型对比。

Method: 对SDSS九个体积限制星系样本计算高斯平滑尺度为4–12 h⁻¹Mpc的属曲线；引入属偏移参数Δν_f和空洞/团块丰度参数A_V、A_C；与HR4及四个流体模拟（IllustrisTNG300/TNG100、FLAMINGO、EAGLE）的模拟星系样本进行统计比较，并考虑宇宙方差影响。

Result: HR4最束缚晕粒子模型整体匹配最优；TNG100和EAGLE在A_C上分别呈现4.5σ和7.0σ超量；Δν_f和A_V的尺度依赖性在观测中略陡于模拟，但与HR4联合差异不显著；现代模型整体优于Choi et al. (2010)所用旧模型。

Conclusion: 现代星系形成模型能较好再现观测属拓扑，验证了其基本物理框架；残余差异反映模型细节（如反馈机制、分辨率）或宇宙方差影响；未来更高密度巡天可进一步约束模型。

Abstract: We present a genus topology analysis of the large-scale distribution of galaxies in the Sloan Digital Sky Survey Data (SDSS), comparing the observed topology with mock galaxy samples from the Horizon Run 4 (HR4) $N$-body simulation and four cosmological hydrodynamical simulations. We construct nine volume-limited samples and measure their genus curves at Gaussian smoothing scales of $4$--$12~h^{-1}{\rm Mpc}$. Overall, the modern galaxy models reproduce the observed genus statistics substantially better than the HGC, HOD, and SAM models examined by Choi et al. (2010). Among the models considered here, the HR4 Most-Bound-Halo Particle galaxy-assignment model shows somewhat better overall agreement with the SDSS topology than IllustrisTNG300 and FLAMINGO. The observed scale dependences of the genus shift parameter $Δν_f$ and void abundance parameter $A_V$ are somewhat steeper than those predicted by the simulations, but their joint discrepancy from HR4 is not statistically significant. The smaller-volume TNG100 and EAGLE realizations show significant excesses in $A_C$, at $4.5σ$ and $7.0σ$, respectively, relative to the observational uncertainties. When the expected cosmic variance of the simulations is taken into account, they do not necessarily indicate failures of the galaxy-formation prescriptions, but they caution that individual small-volume realizations may not be representative of the observed galaxy topology. Overall, modern galaxy formation models reproduce the observed topology substantially better than earlier models, while residual differences remain that can be tested more precisely with denser future surveys.

</details>


### [133] [Minimal vacuum histories beyond $Λ$CDM: model comparison within VCDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16407)
*Antonio De Felice,Eleonora Di Valentino,Luis Escamilla Torres,Jack Zidich*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种演化暗能量模型（F42、Ag、Qui）与标准ΛCDM模型，利用CMB和星系巡天数据发现三者均能更好拟合当前观测，但信息准则和贝叶斯证据未能明确支持某一模型，表明需结合宇宙膨胀与结构增长测量进一步区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期宇宙学观测暗示ΛCDM模型可能不足，需考虑具有演化行为的暗能量模型；为检验不同物理机制的暗能量历史是否可区分，本文选取三种具代表性的单参数扩展模型进行系统比较。

Method: 在VCDM框架下实现F42（w(a)=w₀/√a）、Ag和Qui三种平滑变号有效暗能量模型，联合SPT、Planck、ACT的CMB数据与DESI DR2距离测量，并分别加入/不加入DES Y5 Dovekie超新星样本，通过似然拟合、AIC和贝叶斯证据评估模型表现。

Result: 所有三模型均优于ΛCDM的最优似然；含超新星时AIC支持F42，而Ag与Qui与ΛCDM几乎不可区分；贝叶斯证据未对三者给出稳健偏好；结果表明不同物理起源的暗能量历史可产生相似拟合优度。

Conclusion: 当前观测数据虽支持演化暗能量，但尚不足以唯一确定其具体形式；未来需融合宇宙膨胀历史与结构增长观测以有效区分候选模型。

Abstract: Recent cosmological observations motivate alternatives to $Λ$CDM with evolving dark energy. We compare three such models, each introducing one additional cosmological parameter: F42, with equation of state $w(a)=w_0/\sqrt{a}$, and two smooth sign-changing effective dark-energy profiles, Ag and Qui. All models are implemented within VCDM, which determines the background and linear perturbations without introducing an additional propagating gravitational degree of freedom. We use CMB observations from SPT, Planck, and ACT, combined with DESI DR2 distance measurements, with and without the DESY5 Dovekie supernova sample. All three alternatives improve the best-fit likelihood relative to $Λ$CDM. When supernovae are included, the Akaike information criterion favors F42, while Ag and Qui remain nearly indistinguishable from $Λ$CDM on this criterion. The Bayesian evidence, however, does not establish a robust preference among the three alternatives. Our results show that improved fits to current observations can arise from physically different dark-energy histories, motivating complementary measurements of expansion and structure growth to distinguish them.

</details>


### [134] [Euclid First Data Release 1 (DR1) - Data release overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16510)
*Euclid Collaboration,Herve Aussel*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了欧几里得任务第一数据发布（DR1）的内容，包括数据处理和产品文档的相关论文。DR1分为两部分：2026年11月发布的DR1-Foundation（含图像、光谱和星表），以及2027年6月补充的剪切测量和用于宇宙学分析的Level 3产品，总计涵盖约十亿个天体目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持宇宙学研究，需系统性地发布高质量、大规模的观测数据，并配套详尽的数据处理说明与产品文档。

Method: 组织并描述欧几里得任务第一数据发布（DR1）的结构、时间安排、数据内容（图像、光谱、星表、剪切测量、Level 3产品）及覆盖天区（宽场与深场）。

Result: 明确了DR1分阶段发布计划：2026年11月发布基础数据（DR1-Foundation），2027年6月完成全部DR1；数据涵盖约10^9个天体，覆盖总面积约2052 deg²。

Conclusion: DR1是欧几里得任务关键里程碑，为后续精确宇宙学分析（如暗能量、引力透镜等研究）奠定了坚实的数据基础。

Abstract: This paper describes the contents of the Euclid First Data Release (DR1), together with the set of papers that document the data-processing and the data products. DR1 consists of two parts: first `DR1-Foundation', releasing images, spectra, and catalogues in November 2026. Second, the shear measurements and the `Level~3' products required for cosmological analyses will be added in June 2027 to complete the full DR1. This data set contains around a billion catalogued objects, mainly from the Euclid Wide Survey (1975\,deg$^2$) and the Euclid Deep Fields (77\,deg$^2$)

</details>

Vinciguerra,M. von Wietersheim-Kramsta,N. A. Walton,L. Wang,A. H. Wright,L. Zalesky,B. P. Crill,D. Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了欧几里得任务数据发布1（DR1）中地面光学成像数据的处理与质量评估，重点在于通过OU-EXT单元实现多源地面巡天数据的统一校准与拼接，以满足弱引力透镜和测光红移所需的高精度光度稳定性（~10 mmag）要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持欧几里得任务中精确的测光红移估计和弱引力透镜PSF标定，需引入高精度、空间均匀的地面光学图像；而多源异构地面巡天数据需统一校准以抑制SED重建与红移估计中的系统误差。

Method: OU-EXT单元集成异构地面巡天数据，构建标准化处理流程，生成统一校准的拼接图像及对应PSF模型，并建立验证框架量化深度、零点稳定性与空间色差均匀性。

Result: DR1覆盖约2500 deg²，EWS区域griz波段10σ极限星等达24.8/24.3/24.0/23.0 AB；多数图块满足10 mmag光度稳定性要求，仅少量（北/南半球1.1%/1.5%）偏差超50 mmag；空间色差在南天通常<5–10 mmag，北天部分区域残差达10–25 mmag。

Conclusion: OU-EXT处理链基本实现了地面数据的高一致性整合，但部分校准与拼接缺陷仍需在DR2中改进，以满足最严格的宇宙学科学目标。

Abstract: Accurate photometric-redshift estimation and calibration of the weak-lensing point-spread function (PSF) for Euclid require complementary ground-based optical imaging with high photometric precision and spatial homogeneity. The Organisation Unit for External Data (OU-EXT) integrates heterogeneous ground-based surveys into the Euclid Science Ground Segment (SGS), delivering uniformly calibrated, co-added images, and associated PSF models. To minimise systematics in spectral energy distribution (SED) reconstruction and photometric-redshift estimation, these data must achieve relative photometric stability at the $\sim$10 mmag level on Euclid-tile scales. We present the ground-based imaging component of Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1), comprising $\sim2500$ deg$^2$ across the wide, deep, and auxiliary fields. We summarise the OU-EXT production workflow and validation framework, and quantify the achieved data quality of the co-added products in terms of depth, photometric zero-point stability, and spatial colour homogeneity. In the Euclid Wide Survey (EWS), the point-source $10σ$ depths in the $griz$ bands reach 24.8/24.3/24.0/23.0 AB mag, with the deep fields extending 1--2 magnitudes deeper. Most EWS DR1 tiles meet the 10 mmag relative photometric requirement, with 1.1% and 1.5% showing offsets exceeding 50 mmag in the northern and southern hemispheres, respectively. Spatial colour variations remain typically below 5--10 mmag across the southern EWS footprint, while larger residual structures of 10--25 mmag appear in some northern regions. These results demonstrate that the EXT production chain provides a largely homogeneous framework for integrating ground-based datasets into Euclid, while identifying calibration and co-addition limitations that have to be addressed in DR2 to meet the mission's most stringent cosmological requirements. (Abridged)

</details>

TL;DR: 本文介绍了欧几里得DR1光度掩模，用于表征巡天特性、仪器性能及外部数据的空间变化，并定义了有效覆盖区域，对科学分析（尤其是宇宙学）至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为准确界定欧几里得DR1巡天的有效观测区域并校正各类系统性影响（如仪器性能、天空发射污染、外部数据质量等），需构建高分辨率光度掩模以支持后续科学分析。

Method: 基于每个观测天区（tile）构建全天空HEALPix格式（Nside=16384，角分辨率12.88角秒）的光度掩模，涵盖宽场与深场巡天；统计分析覆盖面积、深度、PSF、系统误差等参数。

Result: DR1宽场（深场）总观测面积为2008.8（74.7）平方度，其中82%（81%）被有效覆盖掩模保留；各波段（IE/YE/JE/HE）5σ深度中位值分别为25.69、24.14、24.3、24.29 AB星等，标准差<0.2 AB星等。

Conclusion: 所构建的光度掩模准确刻画了欧几里得DR1的数据质量空间变化，验证了仪器性能符合预期，是未来宇宙学等科学分析的关键基础工具。

Abstract: This paper describes the Euclid DR1 photometric masks, which trace spatial variations in the survey properties (e.g. satellite position angles, integration time, sky tiling, and survey footprint); in the instrumental performance of Euclid and the external data (EXT) it uses (e.g. survey coverage, depth, PSF width, quality flags, systematic effects and astrometric uncertainties); and in possible contamination from sky emission (e.g. galactic extinction and zodiacal light emission). Among this, the effective coverage mask is particularly important as it defines the effective survey area and can be used to exclude spurious objects when selecting Euclid photometric samples for scientific analyses. The Euclid photometric masks are built for each observed sky region (tile) and are provided as full-sky HEALPix maps (nested ordering and explicit indexing) at Nside = 16 384, equating to a resolution of 12"88, for both the Euclid Wide Survey (EWS) and Euclid Deep Survey (EDS). We estimate the total area of the DR1 EWS (EDS) to be 2008.8 (74.7) deg$^2$ and find that 82 (81)\% of this area is preserved by the effective coverage mask. We observe that Euclid's instrumental properties are consistent with expectations, showing only minor relative variations across the sky. For DR1 EWS the standard deviation of the depth (computed for a 2" aperture and at 5 $σ$) is below 0.2 AB mag, with median values of 25.69, 24.14, 24.3, and 24.29 for the Euclid IE, YE, JE, and HE bands. Finally, we expect these photometric masks to be key to many science applications and in particular for cosmological analyses.

</details>


### [137] [Kinessence, phantom crossing and sign-switching dark energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16629)
*Philippe Brax,Tom Canaguier,Andrei Lazanu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出Kinessence模型，结合K-mouflage与quintessence机制，实现暗能量状态方程穿越幻影边界（w = -1）及暗能量密度变号；通过引入Yukawa耦合与K-essence动能项实现第五力屏蔽，满足太阳系检验，同时在宇宙学尺度产生可观测偏离ΛCDM的效应（如H₀张力缓解、S₈降低、负暗能量分数等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为缓解当前宇宙学中H₀、S₈及中微子质量和等观测张力，需构建能动态改变暗能量性质（如穿越w=-1、出现负密度）且满足局部引力检验的新模型。

Method: 构建Jordan框架下含K-essence动能项、quintessence势能及物质Yukawa耦合的标量场模型（Kinessence），系统分析其背景演化（有效状态方程、物质占比、哈勃率）与线性扰动（CMB谱、P(k)、fσ₈、CMB透镜谱）。利用K-mouflage机制屏蔽第五力以满足太阳系约束。

Result: 模型可在z∼0.5实现w<-1的幻影穿越；暗能量密度在z>2时为负；H₀在低红移偏高，S₈增长受抑；物质占比偏离ΛCDM约几个百分点；CMB谱偏差小但CMB透镜谱具可探测偏离；允许较大Yukawa耦合β∼0.1。

Conclusion: Kinessence模型在满足局部引力检验前提下，通过动力学暗能量行为自然缓解多个宇宙学张力，CMB透镜谱是关键检验窗口。

Abstract: We introduce Kinessence models where combining K-mouflage and quintessence leads to phantom-crossing of the dark energy equation of state and sign-switching of the dark energy density. This is realised by joining a dark scalar sector comprising K-essence kinetic terms and a quintessence potential together with a Yukawa coupling to matter. We investigate the dynamics of these models in the Jordan frame at the background and perturbative levels. We concentrate on the effective equation of state and the matter fraction for the background. The CMB spectra, the linear power spectrum and the growth of structure via $fσ_8$ are also analysed at the perturbative level. The quintessence potential drives the acceleration of the Universe at low redshift with an equation of state greater than -1. The Yukawa coupling to matter leads to a phantom crossing behaviour depending on its strength. The potential fifth force mediated by the scalar field is screened by the K-mouflage mechanism triggered by the K-essence kinetic terms and passes Solar System tests. This allows for relatively large Yukawa couplings to matter $β\sim 0.1$, large enough to lead to the crossing of the phantom divide at $z\sim 0.5$. Despite a significant scalar interaction on large scales, we find that the growth of structure is reduced compared to LCDM. The matter fraction deviates from the standard model by a few percent and the Hubble rate is larger at low redshift than at recombination whilst the CMB spectra show small deviations from LCDM. The dark energy fraction becomes negative at redshifts greater than 2. All these features should alleviate the various tensions such as the larger value of $H_0$ at small redshift, a possible reduction of growth as measured by $S_8$ and the bound on the sum of the neutrino masses. Deviations from LCDM of the CMB lensing spectrum may provide a detectable window for these models.

</details>


### [138] [Efficient Standard Siren Cosmological Inference with Posterior Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16697)
*Alexander Papadopoulos,Michael J. Williams,Christian E. A. Chapman-Bird,Rachel Gray,Christopher Messenger*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用ASPIRE采样器加速暗警报（dark sirens）宇宙学与种群参数联合估计，避免重复计算，显著提升分析效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准暗警报宇宙学依赖多个建模假设，每次更改假设需完整重分析，随着引力波星表增长，大量计算资源被浪费在高度重叠的数据集上。

Method: 应用ASPIRE（基于归一化流与序贯蒙特卡洛的后验重用方法），将已有后验样本直接更新为新假设下的后验分布，无需从先验重启采样。

Result: 在GWTC-5.0数据上验证，ASPIRE所得后验与嵌套采样一致，计算速度提升6–28倍。

Conclusion: ASPIRE可高效探索不同建模选择，并支持星表增量更新，大幅节省计算成本。

Abstract: Standard siren cosmology depends on several modelling assumptions, including the underlying source population, galaxy catalogue, and detection selection function. Quantifying the effect of each choice typically requires a full, independent analysis. As gravitational-wave catalogs grow incrementally, much computational effort is spent re-analysing largely overlapping datasets. We apply the recently developed ASPIRE (Accelerated Sequential Posterior Inference via Reuse) sampler to the joint estimation of cosmological and population parameters with dark sirens. ASPIRE updates existing posterior samples into the posterior under a new hypothesis without restarting from the prior. It combines normalising flows with a sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) scheme to produce accurate posteriors and unbiased evidence estimates. Applying ASPIRE to a number of dark siren analysis cases with GWTC-5.0, we recover posteriors consistent with nested sampling, while recording a performance improvement of between 6-28 times over the set of analyses when using an existing posterior. This enables efficient exploration of modelling choices and rapid updates as catalogs grow, without duplicating existing computational effort.

</details>
