Wright,D. Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过将Euclid DR1数据与COSMOS2025多波段测光数据对比，评估OU-MER处理流程中三种测光方法（孔径测光、模板拟合、模型拟合）的精度，发现模板拟合在NISP波段和较暗星等下表现最优，但系统偏差评估受限于参考数据固有不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估Euclid DR1中OU-MER流水线所采用的不同测光方法的准确性，特别是针对宽场与深场星系样本，并识别潜在系统偏差。

Method: 利用COSMOS2025的多波段测光与红移（光谱或测光）数据，通过SED拟合预测Euclid各波段流量；将预测值与OU-MER输出的三种测光结果（孔径、模板拟合、模型拟合）进行比对；同时用HSC共用波段和Gaia恒星作交叉验证。

Result: 三种方法整体一致性良好，尤其对亮而孤立源；中位偏移多在10%内，NIR波段深场模型拟合偏移最大；模板拟合在NISP波段及暗星等下偏差最小；Gaia恒星验证显示相反趋势，揭示参考数据不确定性限制了系统误差评估。

Conclusion: OU-MER测光结果总体可靠，模板拟合为NISP波段优选方法，但需谨慎对待基于外部参考星表的验证结论，未来应发展更鲁棒的内部校准策略。

Abstract: The Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1) contains catalogues of sources covering 1900 deg2, including deep coverage of legacy fields that enable an extensive comparison between Euclid imaging and multiwavelength observations from other instruments. The Cosmic Evolution Survey (COSMOS) field, observed by Euclid and included in DR1, has been observed with the James Webb Space Telescope, resulting in the COSMOS2025 catalogue. We compare the Euclid DR1 photometry with the multiwavelength COSMOS2025 data to assess the accuracy of the photometric measurements produced by the different methods implemented in the OU-MER pipeline. We use both the Euclid Wide and Deep Surveys, focussing exclusively on extragalactic sources. We use the fluxes of COSMOS2025 to predict the expected fluxes in the Euclid bands via spectral energy distribution fitting, while the redshifts are either spectroscopic, for a sample of brighter sources, or photometric, to include fainter sources. Both the fluxes in the common Hyper Suprime-Cam bands and the SEDs from COSMOS2025 are used to validate the analysis and to characterise any biases or limitations of the method. The predicted fluxes and the OU-MER measurements are in good agreement, especially for bright and isolated sources. Median offsets depend on band, photometric method, survey depth, and redshift sample, most well within 10%, the largest offset seen in the NIR bands for the deep photo-z model-fitting measurements. However, complementary measurements on Gaia stars show an opposite trend, highlighting that photometric validation approaches may suffer from intrinsic uncertainties that prevent a thorough assessment of systematic effects. Among the three methods, template-fitting photometry generally shows the smallest offset from the SED-based predictions in the NISP bands and toward fainter magnitudes, while aperture photometry and model-fitting are comparable in IE.

</details>


### [140] [Localization of gravitational-wave source behind galaxy clusters by gravitational-wave lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16916)
*Brian H. Y. Cheng,Laura Uronen,Souvik Jana,Samuel Lange,Thomas E. Collett,Otto A. Hannuksela*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用强引力波透镜效应（特别是星系团透镜）提高引力波源定位精度的可行性，通过模拟哈勃前沿场已知星系团透镜对引力波的透镜效应，验证了在理想条件下可实现亚角秒级定位和高置信度透镜识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波源的精确定位对寻找电磁对应体、识别宿主星系及宇宙学参数测量至关重要；星系团作为强透镜尚未被系统研究，因其质量结构复杂，但潜力巨大。

Method: 基于哈勃前沿场已知星系团透镜列表，利用其质量模型重建结果，模拟地面引力波探测器观测到的引力波透镜信号；采用匹配滤波与贝叶斯定位框架，评估透镜识别率与源位置/红移重建精度，并测试不同透镜模型假设下的鲁棒性。

Result: 当模拟与定位使用相同透镜模型时，约90%事件能正确识别透镜；源位置可达亚角秒精度，红移可估计；若真实模型包含在候选模型集中，定位仍有效；但若排除真实模型，则多数定位在初始阶段即失败。

Conclusion: 该工作在理想化条件下证实了星系团强引力波透镜用于高精度定位的可行性，但需进一步研究星系团建模偏差与重建系统误差，以支撑实际应用。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave observations have opened new opportunities to study massive object events like merging binary black holes. When combined with electromagnetic counterparts, accurate localization of gravitational-wave sources could allow us to identify their host galaxies and carry out follow-up studies, such as study the environment of merging black holes and determine the Hubble constant. One of the possible methods for precise localization is strong gravitational-wave lensing. Previous studies have considered strong gravitational-wave lensing with galaxy lenses. However, galaxy clusters have yet to be investigated due to the complexity of their mass structures. Here we take the list of known cluster lenses from the Hubble Frontier Fields, simulate lensing properties of gravitational waves from their reconstructions as they would be observed by the ground-based gravitational-wave detectors. When same model family is used in simulation and localization, we find that the correct lens is identified for around 90\% of the simulated events. We also show that the source positions within the cluster can be pinpointed at sub-arcsecond precision with an estimate on source redshift. When we marginalize over all possible lens models for the clusters, the lens identification and localizations remain valid as long as the correct lens model is contained in the available cluster lens models. However, most localization attempts fail at initialization if the correct cluster model is excluded. These tests establish a proof of concept of localization under idealized conditions, while more work is needed to understand the cluster lens modeling discrepancies and reconstruction bias to validate the approach further.

</details>


### [141] [Modelling redshift-space distortions with tracer-centred split densities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17009)
*Aritra K. Gon,Yan-Chuan Cai,Mark Neyrinck,Andrew Repp,Ravi K. Sheth,István Szapudi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的密度分割（DS）建模框架，通过基于示踪体中心密度概率分布函数（PDF）而非传统体积加权PDF进行分割，解决了红移空间中密度分割建模的难题，并表明DS方法能比标准两点相关函数提供更紧致的宇宙学参数约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度分割技术在提取宇宙学信息方面优于标准两点聚类，但其在红移空间观测下的建模面临挑战，尤其是密度分割中心在实空间与红移空间之间不守恒。

Method: 提出基于示踪体中心密度PDF的DS建模框架；推导红移空间中阈值密度对应的线性偏置；构建包含线性、非线性与随机项的经验模型（类晕模型），以描述各DS分箱中示踪体的各向异性红移空间聚类。

Result: 发现阈值密度场呈现‘岛屿’状环形结构；所提模型能准确复现红移空间中各DS分箱的聚类信号；DS聚类相比标准两点统计可提供更严格的宇宙学参数约束；信息增益主要源于密度PDF中包含的小尺度扰动模式。

Conclusion: 基于示踪体中心密度PDF的DS建模框架有效克服了红移空间建模难点，证实DS方法在宇宙学参数限制上具有显著优势，且其优势根源在于密度PDF对小尺度信息的编码能力。

Abstract: Analysing galaxy clustering in split environmental densities has been demonstrated to offer an advantage over standard two-point clustering for extracting cosmological information. A major challenge to realising this potential with the density-split (DS) technique is that the observations are in redshift space. This complicates its modelling, primarily because the density-split centres may not be conserved between real and redshift space. We develop a modelling framework that splits on the tracer-centred density PDF, rather than the volume-weighted density PDF commonly used in previous studies. We derive the linear bias associated with the threshold density in redshift space, enabling us to connect large-scale DS clustering to the density PDF. We find that the threshold density field consists of doughnut-like structures, which we term `Island(s)'. We propose an empirical model, analogous to the halo model framework, that comprises linear, non-linear, and stochastic terms and accurately reproduces the anisotropic redshift-space clustering of tracers in each DS bin. Using this model, we confirm that DS clustering can provide tighter constraints on cosmological parameters than the standard two-point statistic. Furthermore, we show that the gain in cosmological information from DS analyses relative to standard two-point statistics arises primarily from knowledge of the density PDF, which incorporates small-scale perturbation modes below the smoothing scale.

</details>


### [142] [Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1): The Euclid surveys at DR1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17040)
*Euclid Collaboration,I. Tereno,J. Dinis,X. Dupac,R. Scaramella,B. Altieri,H. Aussel,I. Baldry,P. Battaglia,G. Buenadicha,C. Burigana,B. Carry,P. Casenove,J. -C. Cuillandre,C. Dominguez-Tagle,A. Franco,B. R. Granett,M. Granvik,A. Gregorio,B. Y. Irureta-Goyena,R. Kohley,K. Kuijken,P. Liebing,E. L. Martin,J. Martin-Fleitas,J. Mendes,L. Miller,A. A. Nucita,R. Nakajima,K. Paterson,V. Pettorino,M. Pontinen,G. D. Racca,E. Romelli,M. Schirmer,M. Schmidt,T. Schrabback,D. Scott,C. Sirignano,R. Tata,T. Trombetti,R. Vavrek,A. Zacchei,M. Zerjal*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了欧几里得（Euclid）任务第一数据发布（DR1）的状态，涵盖宽视场巡天及其他多个巡天组件（如深场、辅助场、校准场等）的数据情况与实施流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为全面呈现欧几里得任务在不同阶段获取的多类巡天数据及其处理状态，支持科学界及时使用首批高质量数据。

Method: 梳理各巡天组件（宽场、深场、辅助场、中深度、黄道、独立科学家场等）在性能验证、相位多样性校准及科学运行等阶段的观测实施流程，描述从需求约束到实际操作产品的全过程。

Result: DR1中欧几里得宽视场巡天覆盖2100平方度（分两个区域），其中1900平方度已完成高级别数据处理验证；其余巡天组件也提供了相应数据状态说明。

Conclusion: DR1标志着欧几里得任务多巡天组件协同运行与数据交付能力的初步实现，为后续宇宙学研究奠定了坚实的数据基础。

Abstract: Euclid is a surveying mission that is progressively imaging and spectroscopically mapping a wide area of extragalactic sky in visible and near infrared bands. The Euclid mission is, however, not restricted to the Euclid Wide Survey, but it includes data from various other survey components. This paper presents the Data Release 1 (DR1) status of the multiple Euclid surveys and fields, including the Euclid Deep Survey, Euclid Auxiliary Fields, calibration fields, the Euclid Medium-Deep Survey, the Euclid Ecliptic Survey, and the Independent Legacy Scientists' field, in addition to the Euclid Wide Survey. Some of these fields were observed during the initial Performance-Verification phase, others during the Phase-Diversity calibration campaign, and the majority during the subsequent science operations phase. This paper describes the different procedures of survey implementation applied in the various phases of the mission, from the set of requirements, constraints, and limitations, to the final operational products used to operate the spacecraft and execute the science surveys. The resulting main component of the Euclid surveys, the Euclid Wide Survey, covers at DR1 an area of 2100$\,{\rm deg}^2$, in two separate regions, of which 1900$\,{\rm deg}^2$ corresponds to data validated in higher-level processing.

</details>


### [143] [On-the-Fly Gaussian Process Reconstruction of the Galaxy Redshift Distribution for 3$\times$2pt Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17085)
*Jip de Buck,Guadalupe Cañas-Herrera,Henk Hoekstra,Angus H. Wright*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高斯过程（GP）的非参数化方法，用于在3×2pt宇宙学分析中实时重建光度红移分布n(z)，以更准确地传播n(z)不确定性至宇宙学参数后验分布，尤其在w0waCDM模型下显著改善对暗能量状态方程的无偏约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参数化n(z)误差模型难以充分刻画光度红移分布的全部不确定性，可能造成宇宙学推断偏差，尤其在扩展暗能量模型中问题更突出。

Method: 采用高斯过程（GP）对每个星系样本的n(z)进行非参数化建模，在3×2pt联合分析中与宇宙学参数及其它噪声参数共同采样GP超参数，实现n(z)不确定性的端到端传播。

Result: 在模拟Euclid DR1级3×2pt数据上验证表明：相比传统shift-and-width参数化，GP方法能更稳健地抑制参数偏差；且w0waCDM模型中n(z)建模选择对结果影响远大于ΛCDM，原有ΛCDM下有效的n(z)模型在w0waCDM中未必适用。

Conclusion: Stage-IV巡天应在ΛCDM以外的宇宙学模型（如w0waCDM）中独立设定n(z)不确定性边缘化要求，以保障暗能量约束的无偏性。

Abstract: The joint analysis of weak gravitational lensing, photometric galaxy clustering, and their cross-correlation, the so-called 3$\times$2pt analysis, is one of the primary probes Stage-IV surveys will use to constrain the dark-energy equation of state. Its accuracy depends critically on the calibration of the underlying photometric redshift distributions, $n(z)$, whose residual uncertainties can bias cosmological inference. However, conventional parametric nuisance models may not adequately capture the full range of plausible $n(z)$ uncertainties. We therefore present a cosmological inference framework that instead reconstructs $n(z)$ on the fly using Gaussian Processes (GPs), replacing per-bin shift-and-width parameterisations by a non-parametric, data-driven model. We validate the method on synthetic Euclid-like 3$\times$2pt data vectors representative of Euclid Data Release 1, sampling GP hyperparameters jointly with cosmological and nuisance parameters to reliably propagate $n(z)$ uncertainties into the posterior distributions. We find that the dependence of the inferred parameters on the adopted $n(z)$ model is markedly stronger in $w_0w_a$CDM than in $Λ$CDM. Opening the dark-energy sector both amplifies the biases induced by a given $n(z)$ treatment and changes which $n(z)$ reconstruction the data prefer, so that a model validated in $Λ$CDM does not automatically remain adequate once $w_0$ and $w_a$ are free. Our results thus indicate that Stage-IV surveys should define $n(z)$ uncertainty marginalisation requirements separately in cosmologies beyond $Λ$CDM to ensure unbiased dark-energy constraints.

</details>


### [144] [The 21 cm-Lyman-$α$ forest cross-power spectrum with a minimal effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17177)
*G. Autieri,V. Iršič,M. Viel*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文测试了最小有效场论（mEFT）模型是否能同时描述21 cm自功率谱、Lyα自功率谱及二者交叉功率谱，并利用Sherwood数值模拟验证其在z=2.4–4.2范围内的适用性，结果表明该模型可高精度约束中性氢与Lyα密度偏置，但需交叉谱与先验信息联合才能准确恢复21 cm亮度温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 21 cm强度映射与Lyα森林在z≈2–4重叠探测同一宇宙体积，其交叉功率谱可抑制非相关前景污染，并结合Lyα自谱分离中性氢偏置与平均亮度温度；然而现有单探针一环EFT模型参数过多，亟需更简洁鲁棒的联合建模方法。

Method: 采用树级Kaiser模型加领头阶反项与随机项构成的最小有效场论（mEFT），在Sherwood 80 h⁻¹Mpc流体动力学模拟（HI晕涂覆）上，结合Zel'dovich控制变量法减小样本方差，对21 cm自谱、Lyα自谱及交叉谱分别及联合拟合，并依HI速度弥散与Lyα压强平滑尺度设定不同k_max截断。

Result: mEFT在五个红移处均成功拟合三类功率谱；联合拟合下中性氢偏置精度达2.0–2.5%，Lyα密度偏置达2.1–2.3%；仅用交叉谱时亮度温度估计偏高20–30%（1–2σ），而加入HI偏置先验后可恢复真实值。

Conclusion: mEFT是描述21 cm与Lyα联合统计的有效简约模型，能高精度提取关键宇宙学与天体物理参数，但亮度温度的准确标定依赖交叉谱与HI偏置先验的协同约束。

Abstract: Intensity maps of the 21 cm line and the Lyman-$α$ (Ly$α$) forest trace the same cosmic volume at $2\lesssim z\lesssim 4$. Their cross-power spectrum is insensitive to foregrounds that do not correlate between the two probes and, together with the Ly$α$ auto-spectrum, separates the mean 21 cm brightness temperature from the neutral hydrogen (HI) bias. Effective field theory models of each tracer exist, but at one loop they carry tens of nuisance parameters. We test whether a minimal effective field theory (mEFT), consisting of the tree-level Kaiser model with leading-order counterterms and stochastic terms, previously validated for the Ly$α$ auto-spectrum, describes the 21 cm auto-, Ly$α$ auto- and cross-power spectra jointly. We use the Sherwood 80 $h^{-1}$Mpc hydrodynamical simulation with HI painted onto halos at $z=2.4$-$4.2$, and reduce sample variance with Zel'dovich control variates. Each spectrum is validated separately and then in a joint fit with shared parameters. With scale cuts $k_{\rm max}=0.5$, $1.2$ and $2.0\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$ for the 21 cm auto-, cross- and Ly$α$ auto-spectra, the mEFT fits all three spectra at all five redshifts. In the joint fit the mEFT determines the HI bias to 2.0-2.5% and the Ly$α$ density bias to 2.1-2.3%. The scale cuts follow the HI velocity dispersion and the Ly$α$ pressure-smoothing scale. When the brightness temperature is left free, the fits that include the cross-spectrum prefer values 20-30% above the true one, at 1-2$σ$, while the two auto-spectra together do not constrain it. A prior on the HI bias recovers the true value of the brightness temperature.

</details>


### [145] [Parallel alignment of radio AGN jets and large cosmic web structures in the direction of the Shapley supercluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17202)
*I. Baronchelli,T. Venturi,V. Casasola,P. Merluzzi,C. Burigana,G. Busarello,M. Bonato,O. Smirnov,T. Ronconi,M. Massardi,E. Liuzzo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了Shapley超星系团（SSC）附近射电活动星系核（AGN）喷流的大尺度取向一致性及其与宇宙网结构的相关性，发现喷流显著沿X射线纤维结构（PA≈115°±10°）对齐，统计显著性达4.5σ，暗示超大质量黑洞自旋轴可能受大尺度结构或物质坍缩方向影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究射电AGN喷流在Shapley超星系团区域是否存在大尺度取向对齐，并检验其是否与已知宇宙网结构（如X射线纤维）相关，以理解黑洞自旋轴与大尺度结构之间的物理联系。

Method: 分析28个射电AGN（南北两组，覆盖相同天区），测量其喷流位置角（PA），结合光谱红移评估视线方向上的对齐持续性；重点比对其与连接SSC核心星系团的弥散X射线纤维结构的方位角关系。

Result: 检测到喷流显著沿X射线纤维（PA≈115°±10°）大尺度对齐：北组PA≈117°，南组PA≈129°，总体统计显著性为4.5σ（上下界3.8–6.1σ）；尽管光谱红移覆盖稀疏（仅7个源，z≈0.048–0.55），但对齐可能延伸至远超SSC的更大宇宙尺度。

Conclusion: 该对齐无法仅由局域动力学过程解释，可能反映宇宙大尺度结构（如暗物质分布）或物质坍缩优先方向对超大质量黑洞自旋轴的长期影响；此类系统性效应需在Euclid等精密宇宙学巡天中予以校正。

Abstract: Aims. We investigated the potential large-scale alignment of radio active galactic nuclei (AGN) jets in the proximity of the Shapley Supercluster (SSC) area and its correlation with the identified cosmic web structures. Methods. We analyzed 28 radio AGNs divided into two populations with comparable sky coverage north and south of a diffuse X-ray filamentary structure connecting clusters in the SSC core. We measured the average jet position angles (PAs) and combined them with spectroscopic redshifts to assess the persistence of the alignment along the line of sight. Results. We detected a significant large-scale alignment of the radio AGN jets with the foreground X-ray filament (oriented at PA $\sim$ 115$^\circ$ $\pm$ 10$^\circ$). Both the northern (PA $\sim$ 117$^\circ$) and southern (PA $\sim$ 129$^\circ$) groups align with this axis, yielding an overall statistical significance of 4.5 $σ$ (upper and lower bounds: 6.1$σ$, 3.8 $σ$). Although the projected alignment is highly significant, sparse spectroscopic coverage (seven sources spanning 0.048 $\lesssim$ z $\lesssim$ 0.55) suggests that this spatial coherence may extend beyond the SSC (z $\sim$ 0.048), hinting at a line-of-sight correlation across significantly larger cosmic scales. Conclusions. The observed alignment cannot be explained by localized dynamical processes alone. These findings may indicate a persistent influence of the large-scale structure of the cosmic web, or preferred directions of matter collapse, on supermassive black hole spin axes, highlighting a connection between AGN jet morphology and the underlying dark matter distribution. Such alignments represent an important systematic that must be accounted for in precision cosmology with surveys such as Euclid.

</details>


### [146] [Shedding light on modified recombination scenarios with Rayleigh scattering of the CMB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17209)
*Debabrata Adak,Elena Vanetti,R. T. Génova-Santos,Jens Chluba,Carlos Hernández-Monteagudo,J. A. Rubiño-Martín*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了宇宙微波背景（CMB）光子在再复合时期及之后由中性氢和氦引起的瑞利散射（RS）效应，该效应具有ν⁴频率依赖性，在200–800 GHz频段显著，可作为探测再复合历史及检验如电子质量、精细结构常数变化等缓解哈勃张力的新物理模型的补充探针；通过Fisher分析预测，PICO实验中纳入RS可使部分参数约束提升约31%，而LiteBIRD+Planck提升不足2%；忽略RS将导致PICO中参数偏移达~1σ，故未来CMB数据分析中必须建模RS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瑞利散射（RS）是CMB光子与中性氢/氦相互作用产生的频率依赖信号，其强度与再复合历史敏感相关，因而可作为检验修改再复合模型（如可变电子质量me或精细结构常数α）的新探针，以帮助缓解哈勃常数张力。

Method: 采用多频段Fisher矩阵分析方法，结合真实仪器噪声与前景污染模型，评估PICO和LiteBIRD+Planck对未来宇宙学参数（包括ΛCDM参数及me、α等扩展参数）的约束能力，并量化忽略RS所导致的系统偏差。

Result: 对于PICO，引入RS使部分扩展参数（H₀、me、α、Ω_b h²、Ω_c h²）约束改善约31%，ΛCDM整体改善<7%；LiteBIRD+Planck改善均<2%；忽略RS在PICO中造成最高~1σ的参数偏差，在LiteBIRD+Planck中偏差小于1σ。

Conclusion: 瑞利散射是未来高精度CMB实验中不可忽略的系统效应，必须在数据建模中予以包含，尤其对探测再复合物理及解决哈勃张力具有独特价值。

Abstract: Rayleigh scattering (RS) of cosmic microwave background (CMB) photons by neutral hydrogen and helium, during and shortly after the last scattering surface, adds a frequency-dependent contribution to the CMB anisotropies that scales approximately as $ν^4$ at leading order. The RS signal is most relevant at $200\,{\rm GHz}\lesssimν\lesssim800\,{\rm GHz}$ and could be detected using future CMB experiments. Because RS acts in a frequency-dependent manner which is sensitive to the ionisation history of the cosmological recombination process, it provides an additional probe of the recombination history. This makes it a natural test of modified recombination scenarios, such as variable electron mass $m_{\rm e}$ or fine-structure constant $α$, which have been proposed to ease the Hubble tension. The correlation between the RS and primary CMB signal, both in temperature and polarisation, further enhances its usefulness for constraining cosmological parameters. Using a multi-frequency Fisher analysis with realistic noise and foregrounds, we forecast parameter constraints and biases for PICO and LiteBIRD + Planck. For PICO, adding RS improves the $Λ$CDM constraints on cosmological parameters by $\lesssim7\%$, but improves the constraints on some parameters ($H_0$, $m_{\rm e}$, $α$, $Ω_{\rm b}h^2$, $Ω_{\rm c}h^2$) by $\simeq31\%$ for some modified scenarios in the recombination history. However, the improvement for LiteBIRD + Planck is limited to less than $2\%$ for most of the parameters. Neglecting RS biases the cosmological parameters by up to $\simeq1σ$ for PICO and below $1σ$ for LiteBIRD + Planck. Therefore RS must be modelled in the analyses of future CMB data.

</details>


### [147] [Characterising a population of fast-declining SNe Ia in galaxy cluster cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17242)
*A. Eranhalodi,J. Nordin,M. Kowalski,U. Burgaz,F. Fischer,M. Ginolin,Y. -L. Kim,T. E. Müller-Bravo,J. Sollerman,A. Townsend,A. Drake,M. Graham,D. Hale,J. C. Jaimes,R. R. Laher,J. Purdum*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用ZTF巡天数据，分析星系团核心区域Ia型超新星（SNe Ia）的光变曲线伸展度（stretch）分布，发现核心区域（r ≤ 0.5R₅₀₀）以快衰减（低stretch）SNe Ia为主，显著区别于外围及场星系中的样本，支持“前身星年龄主导stretch多样性”的两组分模型，并暗示其可能起源于亚钱德拉塞卡质量的双简并系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ia型超新星的光变多样性（如stretch参数）起源尚不明确，可能源于不同爆炸机制或不同初始条件（尤其与前身星年龄相关）；而星系团核心具有年老恒星、低恒星形成率等极端环境，是检验环境依赖性的理想场所。

Method: 基于ZTF历史数据和AMPEL平台，筛选出89,025个与星系团位置重合的暂现源，识别出4,969个光度学Ia型超新星候选体；按距星系团中心距离分为三组：核心（r ≤ 0.5R₅₀₀）、外核（0.5R₅₀₀ < r ≤ 2R₅₀₀）和非星系团对照组；统计分析stretch与colour分布，并用AIC比较单峰/双峰模型优劣。

Result: 获得迄今最大光度学星系团SNe Ia样本，其中242个位于最内核；核心SNe Ia的stretch显著偏低（~4σ差异），且stretch分布更倾向双峰模型（ΔAIC=1.53–2.3）；colour分布则无显著环境差异；剔除快爆（prompt）成分后，核心区域主要反映延迟爆发通道。

Conclusion: 观测支持Ia型超新星存在年龄驱动的两组分模型：星系团核心的低stretch SNe Ia对应年老前身星，很可能来自亚钱德拉塞卡质量双简并系统；stretch多样性主要由前身星年龄决定，而非爆炸机制本身。

Abstract: Galaxy cluster cores, characterised by old stellar population and suppressed star-formation are ideal for studying the environmental dependence of Type Ia supernovae (SNe Ia). It is unclear whether the diversity of SNe Ia, parametrised by light curve stretch, stems from distinct explosion mechanisms or varying initial conditions, both closely tied to progenitor age. We analyse the light curve stretch and colour distribution of photometrically identified SNe Ia within the galaxy clusters cores, utilising the Zwicky Transient Facility (ZTF) archival data. Using the AMPEL platform, we filter 89,025 ZTF transients spatially coincident with our galaxy cluster catalogues to identify 4,969 likely SN Ia candidates. We split this sample into three populations: core cluster SNe Ia $r \le 0.5R_{500}$, outer-core cluster SNe Ia $0.5R_{500}< r\le 2R_{500}$, and a reference population of non-cluster SN Ia. We present the largest sample of photometrically identified cluster SNe Ia to date, notably isolating a population of 242 SNe Ia within innermost cluster cores $r \le 0.5R_{500}$. This fast-declining SN Ia population is statistically different from the outer-core cluster and field SN Ia population at $\sim4σ$ level. A marginal statistical preference for a bimodal distribution over a unimodal one $ΔAIC$ of 1.53 and 2.3 for each catalogue, indicate cluster cores almost entirely host fast-declining SNe Ia. Conversely, the colour distribution show no significant environmental variation. By filtering out the prompt SN Ia population, cluster cores provide an environment to directly probe the delayed SN Ia progenitor channel. Our observations support a two-population model where progenitor age drives the light curve stretch diversity. The low-stretch values observed in core cluster SNe Ia suggest sub-Chandrasekhar mass double degenerate systems as their likely progenitors.

</details>


### [148] [Simulation-Based Priors and BAO Shifts from the Lyman-$α$ Forest at the Field Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17299)
*Divij Sharma,Roger de Belsunce,Mikhail M. Ivanov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于三阶大尺度结构微扰理论（有效场论）的莱曼-α森林通量场解析正向模型，并在CAMELS和Sherwood流体动力学模拟上验证了其精度；该模型将通量三维功率谱拟合精度提升至约5%（k~1–1.5 h/Mpc），较以往二阶模型提高达40%；进一步利用该模型实现了对Ly-α偏置参数的高精度测量、BAO尺度参数非线性偏移的亚百分比预测，并构建了可用于DESI巡天宇宙学分析的模拟先验，同时首次在解析模型中准确刻画了森林四点关联函数（trispectrum），为DESI及DESI-II协方差估计提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升Lyman-alpha森林宇宙学分析的精度，克服以往低阶微扰模型在中小尺度上的系统偏差，支持DESI等巡天的高精度BAO和RSD测量。

Method: 基于三阶有效场论（EFT）构建Lyman-alpha森林通量场的解析正向模型；在CAMELS（小体积、多参数）与Sherwood（大体积、高分辨率）两套流体模拟上进行功率谱与场级验证；结合场级拟合与cosmic-variance cancellation技术提取偏置参数；解析推导非线性效应对BAO尺度参数的偏移；并计算四点关联函数（trispectrum）。

Result: 三阶模型在k≈1–1.5 h/Mpc范围内将三维通量功率谱拟合误差降至~5%，较二阶模型提升最多40%；获得亚百分比精度的BAO径向与横向尺度偏移预测，且具明显红移与宇宙学参数依赖性；从CAMELS得到可用于DESI分析的偏置参数先验；首次在解析模型中准确复现森林trispectrum。

Conclusion: 三阶EFT正向模型显著提升了Lyman-alpha森林非线性建模能力，为当前DESI巡天提供高精度理论工具与先验约束，并拓展至更高阶统计量（如trispectrum）应用，推动未来高精度宇宙学分析。

Abstract: We present an analytic forward model for the Lyman-alpha (Ly-alpha) forest flux field based on cubic-order large-scale structure perturbation theory (effective field theory) calculations. We validate our cubic model on two sets of hydrodynamic simulations spanning $2 \leq z \leq 4$: the small-volume CAMELS suite, which spans a wide range of cosmological and astrophysical parameters, and the large-volume, high-resolution Sherwood simulations. We find qualitatively similar results for both at the power-spectrum level. The perturbative model reproduces the three-dimensional flux power spectrum of the simulations to $\sim 5\%$ down to $k \simeq 1$-$1.5\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$, depending on the redshift. This improves compared to previous quadratic perturbative forward models by up to 40%. Exploiting cosmic-variance cancellation in the field-level approach, we carry out precision measurements of the Lyman-alpha forest bias parameters from the CAMELS simulation suite. We use these measurements to analytically predict the shifts in the Baryon Acoustic Oscillations (BAO) scaling parameters induced by nonlinearities in the three-dimensional Lyman-alpha forest clustering as a function of cosmological parameters. We find sub-percent shifts in both the radial and transverse directions with a noticeable dependence on cosmology and redshift. Finally, from the field-level fits we construct simulation-based priors that can be used for cosmological analyses of the Lyman-alpha forest with the currently observing Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI). In addition, our cubic model captures the connected four-point function (trispectrum) of the forest, which opens up novel opportunities for accurate covariance matrix estimation for DESI and its successor, DESI-II.

</details>

### [149] [FRIDA: Finding Rotation In ultra-Diffuse gAlaxies. I. Programme overview, resolved gas density profiles, and HI mass-size relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14635)
*Pavel E. Mancera Piña,Tom Oosterloo,Filippo Fraternali,Elizabeth A. K. Adams,Andrea Afruni,Cecilia Bacchini,Enrico M. Di Teodoro,Lexi Gault,Anastasia A. Ponomareva,Justin I. Read,Barbara Šiljeg*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究通过FRIDA项目，利用MeerKAT和VLA对12个富气超弥散星系（UDGs）进行高分辨率中性氢（HI）观测，揭示其HI分布广泛、结构规则，中心表面密度较高且向外呈指数衰减；发现其HI尺度略大于同质量普通星系，并在HI质量-尺寸关系中处于上侧；HI径向剖面特征介于晚型盘星系与早型星系之间，与Sa型星系高度重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气体丰富的超弥散星系（UDGs）是检验低面密度下星系形成模型的重要样本，但其中性氢（HI）结构尚不清楚，限制了对该类星系的理解。

Method: 开展FRIDA观测项目，结合MeerKAT与VLA望远镜获取12个富气UDGs的高分辨率HI数据；进行数据处理，提取HI流量、质量（M_HI）及分辨的表面密度轮廓Σ_HI(R)；建模分析HI形态以估计倾角；将UDGs置于M_HI–R_HI和M_HI–R_50,HI质量-尺寸关系中进行比较；分析归一化Σ_HI(R/R_HI)和累积质量分数M_HI(<R)/M_HI的统计趋势。

Result: 发现UDGs具有延展（半径约7–30 kpc）、规则的HI辐射；中心Σ_HI达≈4 M⊙ pc⁻²，外区呈指数下降；中位倾角为43°，符合理论预期；在质量-尺寸关系中系统性偏向上方（更延展、平均Σ_HI低25–35%）；Σ_HI(R/R_HI)与M_HI(<R)/M_HI的分布介于晚型盘与早型星系之间，与Sa星系高度重合；并首次在一个UDG中发现卫星星系。

Conclusion: 富气UDGs并非异常系统，而是具有典型盘状结构、但尺度更大、气体更弥散的星系；其HI结构支持它们起源于旋转主导的盘星系，在特定选择效应下被识别为UDGs；该结果对星系形成与演化模型，尤其是低密度环境下的气体动力学和恒星形成调控，具有重要启示。

Abstract: Gas-rich ultra-diffuse galaxies (UDGs) provide powerful tests of galaxy formation models at low surface densities. However, the structure of their neutral atomic hydrogen (HI) remains poorly constrained, hampering our understanding of this population.
  We present FRIDA (Finding Rotation In ultra-Diffuse gAlaxies), a MeerKAT and VLA programme providing high-resolution HI data of 12 gas-rich UDGs. In this paper, we introduce our sample and delve into the associated observations and data reduction. We derive HI fluxes, masses ($M_{\rm HI}$), and resolved surface density profiles ($Σ_{\rm HI}(R)$). We find extended ($\sim 7-30\ \rm{kpc}$ in radius) and regular HI emission in our UDGs, and we report the discovery of a satellite around one of them. Most UDGs in FRIDA have $Σ_{\rm HI}(R)$ profiles with flat inner parts reaching $Σ_{\rm HI}(R)\approx 4\ M_\odot\ \rm{pc^{-2}}$ near their centres, which then decline exponentially in their outer parts. By modelling the HI morphology of the UDGs, we find a median inclination of $43^\circ$, in agreement with theoretical expectations for disc galaxies under UDG selection effects. Moreover, we place our UDGs in the $M_{\rm HI}-R_{\rm HI}$ and $M_{\rm HI}-R_{\rm 50,HI}$ mass-size relations, where $R_{\rm HI}$ is radius at which $Σ_{\rm HI}(R)=1\ M_\odot \rm{pc^{-2}}$, and $R_{\rm 50,HI}$ is the radius enclosing $50\%$ of $M_{\rm HI}$. While our UDGs lie within the scatter of the relations, they systematically fall on their upper side, being slightly (by $\sim 15-25\%$) more extended and of lower mean $Σ_{\rm HI}$ (by $\sim 25-35\%$) than average galaxies at similar $M_{\rm HI}$. Moreover, we find that $Σ_{\rm HI}(R/R_{\rm HI})$ and $M_{\rm HI}(<R)/M_{\rm HI}$ for UDGs follow an intermediate trend between late-type discs and early-type galaxies, with a large overlap with Sa galaxies.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于DESI光谱和XGBoost机器学习的新型恒星消光测量方法，直接通过比较红化与未红化恒星的内在颜色来估计消光，并据此推断总选择性消光比R(V)；结果发现高银纬尘埃区R(V)存在显著变化（σ=0.23）且呈双峰分布（2.9和3.4），但与常见ISM示踪体无强相关，而SFD消光误差与R(V)强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确测定消光曲线参数R(V)对校正天文测光颜色及理解星际介质至关重要；传统方法（如SFD）依赖热辐射，存在系统偏差，亟需更直接、精确的观测方法。

Method: 利用DESI光谱数据，采用XGBoost模型根据恒星大气参数（T_eff, log g, [Fe/H]）预测其本征g,r,z,W1,W2波段颜色，再与观测颜色相减得到红化量；进而构建前向模型，将g−r与g−W1红化映射为R(V)；对比分析R(V)与多种ISM示踪体的关系。

Result: 在DESI观测的高银纬尘埃区（E(g−r)>0.1），R(V)标准差达0.23，呈双峰分布（峰值2.9和3.4）；R(V)与热辐射、尘埃温度、发射谱指数β及Hα等ISM示踪体无显著关联；但SFD消光误差与R(V)高度相关。

Conclusion: R(V)在银河系高纬度区域存在真实且显著的空间变化，不能简单视为常数；本工作提出的光谱-机器学习方法可更直接、稳健地测量R(V)，并揭示SFD方法的系统误差本质上反映了R(V)的变化，为改进消光校正提供了新路径。

Abstract: The dust extinction curve, characterized by the total-to-selective extinction ratio, $R(V)$, varies with grain size and composition. Accurate $R(V)$ values are crucial for dereddening astronomical colors and understanding the interstellar medium. We measure extinction toward stars with Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) spectroscopy using a machine-learning-based relative of the "pair method," matching reddened and unreddened stars. We use XGBoost to predict stars' intrinsic colors from their spectroscopic parameters ($T_\mathrm{eff}$, $\log g$, $[\mathrm{Fe/H}]$) and subtract them from observed colors to measure reddening in combinations of the $g$, $r$, $z$, $W1$, and $W2$ bands. This approach directly measures reddening, unlike the common thermal-emission-based method of Schlegel et al. (1998; SFD). The reddening is accurate to roughly 0.02 mag in $g-r$ and $r-z$. We develop a forward model to map $g-r$ and $g-W1$ reddenings to $R(V)$ and find significant variation in $R(V)$, with $σ_\mathrm{NMAD}(R(V)) = 0.23$, in dusty regions ($E(g-r) > 0.1$) at high Galactic latitudes observed by DESI. In this footprint, $R(V)$ is bimodal, with modes at 2.9 and 3.4, likely due to large-spatial-scale correlations in the shape of the extinction curve combined with the relatively small number of distinct sightlines with substantial reddening. We find no strong relationship between $R(V)$ and other tracers of the interstellar medium, such as thermal emission, dust temperature, spectral emissivity index $β$, and $Hα$. However, the error in the SFD-predicted extinction correlates well with $R(V)$, implying that a single optical reddening combined with the far-infrared dust brightness can predict variations in the extinction curve.

</details>


### [151] [Tracing Galactic Dust Reddening with Polycyclic Aromatic Hydrocarbon Emission: SPHEREx Observations of the North Ecliptic Pole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14640)
*Dennis Lee,Brandon Hensley,Tzu-Ching Chang,Olivier Doré,Matt Ashby,Shuang-Shuang Chen,Yun-Ting Cheng,Yi-Kuan Chiang,Brendan Crill,Ari Cukierman,Andreas Faisst,Joe Hora,Zhaoyu,Huai,Howard Hui,Woong-Seob Jeong,Miju Kang,Carey Lisse,Dan Masters,Gary Melnick,Chi Nguyen,Roberta Paladini,Ji Yeon Seok,Volker Tolls,Michael Werner,Mike Zemcov*

TL;DR: 本文研究了SPHEREx望远镜观测3.3 μm多环芳烃（PAH）发射特征追踪银河系尘埃消光（E(B-V)）的能力，提出一种基于光谱模板的方法，生成高角分辨率（~6″）的消光图，并验证其精度与现有红外、恒星和HI方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有消光图角分辨率有限，而SPHEREx具备高空间分辨率观测PAH发射的能力，有望提供更高分辨率且可靠的消光图。

Method: 采用光谱模板法处理SPHEREx数据，测量每个6''.2像素中3.3 μm PAH特征强度；结合辐射场校正和线性标度因子，将PAH强度转换为E(B-V)；通过与多波段消光图比对及星系计数检验评估精度。

Result: 所得E(B-V)图在角分尺度上与远红外、恒星和HI消光图一致，典型偏差≤7 mmag；星系计数检验显示所有消光图均有≈11 mmag弥散；在高PAH丰度区存在轻微高估（≤10 mmag），但小于辐射场校正不确定性。

Conclusion: 对于像北黄极这样的高银纬弥散区域，SPHEREx基于PAH发射的消光图在精度上媲美现有方法，同时可达到~6″的更高角分辨率。

Abstract: We investigate the ability of SPHEREx observations of the $3.3$ $μ$m polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon (PAH) emission feature to trace Galactic dust reddening. Applying a spectral template-based method to SPHEREx data, we measure the strength of the $3.3$ $μ$m PAH feature in each $6''.2$ pixel of a $22^{\circ}$ by $22^{\circ}$ region centered on the north ecliptic pole (NEP). We convert the PAH feature strength to reddening by applying a radiation field correction and a linear scale factor. The resulting $E(B-V)$ map is consistent with far-infrared, stellar, and HI-based reddening maps at arcminute scales, exhibiting a typical $E(B-V)$ difference of $\lesssim 7$ mmag. A galaxy count test finds that all reddening maps have a dispersion of $\approx11$ mmag in $E(B-V)$ relative to what would be needed to produce spatially uniform galaxy counts. While there is some evidence that reddening is overestimated by our map at high PAH abundances, this effect is smaller than the uncertainties on the applied radiation field corrections and limited to $\sim$10 mmag in the most extreme regions of the NEP. For a diffuse, high Galactic latitude region like the NEP and at the sensitivities of our validation metrics, the SPHEREx PAH-emission based reddening map matches the accuracy of existing reddening maps while being capable of accessing a higher angular resolution of $\sim6''$.

</details>


### [152] [DIISC-VIII: Chemical Signatures of Gas Accretion in Star-Forming Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14701)
*Alejandro J. Olvera,Sanchayeeta Borthakur,Mansi Padave,Hansung B. Gim,Emmanuel Momjian,Timothy M. Heckman*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用DIISC巡天数据，识别出11个星系中的75个异常低金属丰度（ALM）恒星形成区，发现其可能由外来低金属气体吸积导致，并为星系内气体循环提供了空间分辨的间接证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究星系中异常低金属丰度（ALM）恒星形成区的起源及其与气体吸积、星系化学演化的关系，验证局部低金属气体吸积是否能维持恒星形成并反映气体循环过程。

Method: 结合MMT/Binospec光学光谱（542个Hα亮区）、VATT成像和VLA HI 21 cm成像；采用径向金属丰度梯度偏离法和解析质量-金属丰度关系（rMZR）两种方法识别ALM区域，并估算吸积质量和氢吸积率。

Result: 共发现75个ALM区，其中29个被两种方法共同确认，是金属稀释最强候选体；估算吸积质量log(M_acc/M⊙)在5.6–7.5之间，平均氢吸积率达1.02 M⊙/yr，与星系平均恒星形成率相当；ALM区多位于R25内或附近，但rMZR独有样本主导外盘；Lyα吸收不相关于盘内化学结构。

Conclusion: 局部低金属气体吸积可支撑星系恒星形成，ALM区域为空间分辨研究星系气体循环提供了有效探针。

Abstract: We investigate anomalously low-metallicity (ALM) star-forming regions in 11 galaxies from the Deciphering the Interplay between the Interstellar Medium, Stars, and the Circumgalactic Medium (DIISC) survey. We combine optical spectroscopy of 542 H$α$-bright regions from MMT/Binospec with imaging from the Vatican Advanced Technology Telescope and H I 21 cm imaging from the Very Large Array. We find 75 ALM regions: 19 identified using deviations from each galaxy's radial metallicity gradient, 27 using the resolved mass--metallicity relation (rMZR), and 29 via both methods. Regions identified by both methods are metal-poor relative to their galactocentric positions and local stellar mass surface densities by $>$ 0.1 dex, making them the strongest candidates for localized metallicity dilution. We estimate accreted masses of $5.6<\log(M_{\rm acc}/{\rm M_\odot})<7.5$ for the 48 radial-gradient-selected regions, comparable to observed and simulated high-velocity cloud masses. We calculate a mean hydrogen accretion rate of $1.02\ {\rm M_\odot yr^{-1}}$ per galaxy, which is comparable to the mean star formation rate of the sample. The strongest ALM candidates lie within or near $R_{25}$, while rMZR-only regions dominate the outer population. We find no evidence that circumgalactic Ly$α$ absorption along a single sightline traces disk chemical structure, as quantified by either the radial metallicity gradient or the fraction of radial-gradient-selected ALM regions. These results suggest that localized accretion of low-metallicity gas can sustain star formation in galaxies and demonstrate that ALM regions provide a spatially resolved indirect view of ongoing gas cycling.

</details>


### [153] [La Silla-QUEST RR Lyrae Survey III: The Shape of the Outer Virgo Overdensity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14734)
*R. Zinn,P. S. Coppi,C. Baltay,D. Rabinowitz,E. P. Bellinger,K. Bettwy*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用La Silla-QUEST和Gaia巡天数据识别出256颗RR Lyrae变星（RRLS），揭示外室女座过密区（OVOD）具有比以往认知更庞大且复杂的三维结构，其形态不似典型恒星流，可能源自一个大质量矮星系，并与Gaia-Sausage-Enceladus并合事件相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对OVOD的结构认识有限，本文旨在通过高精度RRLS示踪，厘清其真实空间分布、物理性质及起源。

Method: 结合La Silla-QUEST和Gaia巡天数据，识别并分析256颗RR Lyrae变星的位置、距离、周期、金属丰度等参数，推断OVOD的几何结构、动力学特征及前身天体性质。

Result: OVOD平均距离约85 kpc，长轴约57°（~85 kpc），宽度变化显著（10°–35°），整体弯曲约35°，不符恒星流特征；RRab平均周期0.59天，仅19±3%属Oosterhoff II型；平均金属丰度[Fe/H] = −1.60，弥散0.32 dex，暗示其起源于大质量矮星系；其南向延伸与中心红巨星轨道夹角约70°，支持其为Gaia-Sausage-Enceladus并合产生的壳层结构。

Conclusion: OVOD是一个复杂非流状结构，很可能是一个由大质量矮星系演化而来、并与Gaia-Sausage-Enceladus并合事件相关的壳层或复合投影结构。

Abstract: Using the La Silla-QUEST and Gaia surveys, we identify 256 RR Lyrae stars (RRLS) in the Outer Virgo Overdensity (OVOD). These show that the OVOD has a signifcantly larger and more complex shape than previously recognized. The OVOD lies at an average distance of $\sim 85$ kpc, has a long dimension of $\sim 57^\circ$ ($\sim 85$ kpc), and a width that varies from $\sim 10^\circ $ ($\sim 15$ kpc) to $\sim 35^\circ $ ($\sim 50$ kpc) in a prominent bulge. Because it bends by $\sim 35^\circ$ from a great circle, it does not appear to be a stellar stream. The type ab RRLS (RRab) span a large range in period, with a mean of $0.59$ days, and only $19\pm 3 \%$ lie near the locus of Oosterhoff II variables in the Bailey diagram. The overall large number of RRLS along with a mean RRab metallicity ([Fe/H]) of -1.60 and $1 σ$ dispersion of 0.32, as inferred from the RRab periods, suggest that the OVOD progenitor was a large dwarf galaxy. The hypothesis that the OVOD may be a shell produced by the Gaia-Sausage-Enceladus merger \citep{chandra2023} is supported by the $\sim 70^\circ$ angle that the southern extension of the OVOD makes with the orbits of red giants in its central part. The complex shape of the overall RRLS distribution, including a small north-south distance gradient, may also indicate, however, that we are seeing a blended, composite structure projected on the sky.

</details>


### [154] [How Intensity Threshold Shapes the Velocity Dispersion-Size Relation in Molecular Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14852)
*Yaru Qi,Xin Liu,Fujun Du*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过分析MWISP巡天中4916个13CO分子云，系统研究了速度弥散-尺度关系（σ_v ∝ R^γ）中幂律指数γ和归一化系数C随强度阈值T_th的变化规律，发现γ随阈值单调上升并趋于饱和（~0.56–0.70），而C则单调下降；历史文献中报道的不同γ值（如0.38、0.5、0.7）主要源于所用阈值及样本构成差异，而非物理机制本质不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同研究报道的分子云速度弥散-尺度关系幂律指数γ存在显著离散（0.38–0.5+），其成因尚未被定量归因于观测强度阈值的影响，亟需厘清该系统性偏差的来源。

Method: 基于GDCluster算法识别的MWISP巡天中4916个13CO分子云，计算不同强度阈值T_th下的γ和C；进一步按旋臂分组分析，并用DBSCAN算法对13CO和12CO样本作独立验证。

Result: γ随T_th单调递增至饱和值（全样本γ_sat≈0.56，旋臂子样本达0.62/0.70）；C(T_th)单调递减至饱和值C_sat≈0.35 km/s（对应面密度~46 M⊙/pc²）；Larson (1981) 和 Solomon et al. (1987) 的γ≈0.38与0.5分别对应低阈值（1.2 K）与近饱和阈值（2.6 K），与二者所用13CO等效边界温度一致；DBSCAN样本亦显示相同单调趋势。

Conclusion: 文献中γ值的广泛离散主要反映强度阈值选择和样本构成差异，而非湍流或引力主导机制的根本分歧；在应用Larson型关系解释物理过程时，必须明确考虑阈值效应。

Abstract: The velocity dispersion-size relation of molecular clouds, $σ_v = C\,(R/1\,{\rm pc})^γ$, has been reported with a power-law index ranging from $γ\approx$ 0.38 to 0.5 or higher, and a correspondingly varying normalization coefficient $C$, but the role of the intensity threshold in producing this scatter has not been quantitatively characterized. Using 4916 $^{13}$CO clouds identified with GDCluster (Liu & Du 2025) in the Milky Way Imaging Scroll Painting (MWISP) survey, we derive $γ$ and $C$ as functions of the intensity threshold $T_{\rm th}$. The index $γ$ increases monotonically with threshold, reaching a saturation value $γ_{\rm sat} \approx$ 0.56 for the full sample; splitting the sample by spiral arm gives higher values, $γ_{\rm sat} \approx$ 0.62 and 0.70. The normalization $C(T_{\rm th})$ declines monotonically, saturating at $C_{\rm sat} \approx$ 0.35 km/s and implying a surface density of ~46 Msun/pc^2 under a virial approximation. On the $γ(T_{\rm th})$ curve, $γ\approx$ 0.38 (Larson 1981) and $γ\approx$ 0.5 (Solomon et al. 1987) correspond to a low threshold ($T_{\rm th} \approx$ 1.2 K) and a near-saturation one (2.6 K), respectively, roughly consistent with the $^{13}$CO-equivalent boundary temperatures of these two studies. These two historically debated values, together with the broader $γ\approx$ 0.38-0.7 range reported in the literature, may largely reflect different positions along this single threshold-dependent curve and differences in sample composition. This threshold effect should therefore be considered when using Larson-type relations to assess the roles of turbulence and gravity. As an independent check, DBSCAN-identified $^{13}$CO and $^{12}$CO samples show the same monotonic trend in $γ$, though the curve shape differs quantitatively.

</details>


### [155] [Moving Beyond the DRW Model: Learning Quasar Variability with Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15040)
*Nadav Bojan Sellam,Kevin Park,Alex Bronstein,Zoltán Haiman*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Nullasar的生成模型，用于更准确地建模类星体光学连续谱变异性，以替代传统但不完善的阻尼随机游走（DRW）零假设模型，从而提升周期信号检测等任务的统计显著性估计准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DRW模型作为零假设存在不完备性，可能导致周期信号、潮汐撕裂事件或变脸类星体等现象的显著性评估出现偏差，亟需更真实反映类星体光变统计特性的零假设模型。

Method: 提出基于流匹配（flow matching）训练的生成模型Nullasar，直接从时序数据中学习类星体变异性，无需预设噪声模型，并能学习噪声参数的相关分布及生成偏离高斯DRW的光变曲线。

Result: Nullasar在合成光变曲线中检测周期信号时，性能媲美正确拟合DRW模型，且在AR(2)等更灵活模型下优于参数化拟合；同时通过主成分投影可缓解训练集受非零信号污染带来的敏感性问题。

Conclusion: Nullasar提供了一种数据驱动、更具鲁棒性和适应性的零假设建模方法，有望改进类星体时域天文学中多种关键分析任务的统计推断可靠性。

Abstract: The optical continuum variability of quasars is often described by a damped random walk (DRW) model, with characteristic damping timescales $τ_{\rm DRW}$ of hundreds of days and amplitudes $σ_{\rm DRW}$ of $\mathcal{O}(0.1)$ magnitudes. This model has been widely used as a null hypothesis to assess the significance of periodic signals in quasar light curves in the search for supermassive black hole binaries, the significance of extreme quasar variability like tidal disruption events (TDEs) and changing-look (CL) quasars, or to model time lags in reverberation mapping. However, the DRW model is known to be incomplete, and an incorrect null hypothesis can lead to biased significance estimates. We investigate the impact of such null-hypothesis mis-specification on the detection of periodic signals in synthetic quasar light curves and introduce the generative model Nullasar, capable of learning quasar variability directly from time-series data. We demonstrate that Nullasar, trained with flow matching, can learn delicate statistical properties of stochastic light-curve variability without explicitly assuming any noise model. We further demonstrate that Nullasar can learn correlated distributions of noise parameters and generate new light-curve realizations from a range of deviations from the Gaussian DRW noise model, approximating parametric descriptions of stochastic processes. When used as a null hypothesis for detecting periodic signals in synthetic light curves, Nullasar performs comparably to fitting the correct DRW model to each light curve, and outperforms parametric fitting when the variability follows a more flexible AR(2) model. Finally, we identify practical limitations, including sensitivity to contamination of the training set by non-null signals, and show that projecting the conditioning vector of the model onto its leading principal components largely mitigates it.

</details>


### [156] [Prospects for probing supermassive binary black holes in strongly lensed active galactic nuclei via microlensing effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15056)
*Zekun Li,Changshuo Yan,Youjun Lu,Zhiwei Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用半解析模型评估了在强引力透镜类星体中通过微引力透镜探测亚秒差距超大质量双黑洞（SMBBHs）的可能性，预测未来巡天项目（如Rubin、Euclid、CSST、Roman）可分辨约87个合适系统，并给出不同诊断方法（光变周期振荡与尺寸-波长关系）的预期探测数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引力透镜类星体中的微引力透镜是研究吸积盘结构和识别亚秒差距超大质量双黑洞（SMBBHs）的独特手段；即将到来的宽视场巡天将发现大量强透镜类星体，其中部分可能含有可通过微透镜识别的SMBBHs。

Method: 采用半解析模型，结合星系尺度强透镜统计及星系团透镜贡献（作为乐观扩展），估算未来巡天（Rubin、Euclid、CSST、Roman）中空间分辨的合适强透镜AGN数量，并分别基于微透镜光变曲线的周期性振荡和重建的尺寸-波长关系，评估SMBBH探测概率。

Result: 预计各巡天可空间分辨约87个适合微透镜分析的强透镜AGN；Rubin WFD巡天有望通过周期振荡识别0.12–0.68个短周期（<5年）双黑洞，通过尺寸-波长关系识别0.4–5.3个；CSST、Euclid、Roman相应识别数分别为10.6/2.3、8.9/1.9、1.2/0.3个（后三者需后续时域观测验证）。

Conclusion: 强透镜类星体为借助微引力透镜搜寻SMBBH候选体提供了有前景但观测要求高的途径。

Abstract: Gravitational microlensing in strongly lensed active galactic nuclei (AGNs) provides a powerful probe of accretion-disk structure and may offer a unique opportunity to identify sub-parsec supermassive binary black holes (SMBBHs) through distinctive signatures. Upcoming wide-field surveys are expected to discover a large sample of strongly lensed AGNs, a fraction of which may host SMBBHs detectable via microlensing. In this paper, we employ semi-analytic models to assess the detection prospects of sub-parsec SMBBHs in lensed AGNs, focusing on systems with resolvable multiple images suitable for microlensing analysis. Accounting for galaxy-scale strong-lensing statistics, and including the cluster-lensing contribution as an optimistic statistical extension, we estimate that up to approximately 87 such systems could be spatially resolved by individual upcoming surveys, including the Vera C. Rubin Observatory (Rubin), Euclid, China's Space Survey Telescope (CSST), and the Roman. For the Rubin WFD survey, we estimate that periodic oscillations in microlensing light curves could identify $\sim0.12-0.68$ short-period ($<5$\,yr) binaries, whereas diagnostics based on the reconstructed size-wavelength relation apply to a broader population, potentially identifying $\sim0.4-5.3$ systems, depending on the model assumptions. CSST, Euclid, and Roman may also reveal up to $10.6$, $8.9$, and $1.2$ systems exhibiting size-wavelength features, and $2.3$, $1.9$, $0.3$ systems showing periodic oscillations, which should be regarded as imaging-selected candidates whose microlensing-based identification requires adequate time-domain follow-up, including cadence, monitoring baseline, photometric precision, and reliable time-delay measurements. Our results show that strongly lensed AGNs provide a promising, though observationally demanding, pathway for finding candidate SMBBHs via microlensing.

</details>


### [157] [The SDSS-V Black Hole Mapper Reverberation Mapping Project: Short Lags in the COSMOS Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15166)
*Zachary Stone,Yue Shen,Scott F. Anderson,Roberto Assef,Franz E. Bauer,W. N. Brandt,Joel R. Brownstein,Megan C. Davis,Jason Dexter,Tom Dwelly,Xiaohui Fan,Catherine J. Grier,Norman A. Grogin,P. B. Hall,Lorena Hernández-García,Keith Horne,Yasaman Homayouni,Anton M. Koekemoer,Dhanesh Krishnarao,Jennifer Li,Diego Mardones-Soto,Mary Loli Martínez-Aldama,Knox Long,Sean Morrison,Leonardo Olivares Latorre,Theodore Pena,B. M. Peterson,Donald P. Schneider,Benny Trakhtenbrot,Matthew J. Temple,Jonathan R. Trump,Qian Yang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文报告了COSMOS场中首次基于SDSS-V黑洞映射器的混响映射（RM）测量，利用约6个月的光谱和测光数据，对250多个AGN进行分析，成功获得22个AGN的可靠发射线时延，并验证了半径-光度关系，为未来大样本AGN研究奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开展COSMOS场中大规模AGN的混响映射测量，以拓展高红移AGN黑洞质量测量样本，并与多设备协同开展时域天文学研究。

Method: 结合SDSS-V黑孔映射器的板式光谱观测（2020年末至2021年中）与ZTF+DECam g/r/i波段测光光变曲线，对250多个z~3内的AGN进行时变分析；采用多种时延探测方法与显著性检验提取Hα、Hβ、MgII、CIV发射线的可靠时延。

Result: 成功获得22个z≲2.5 AGN在Hα、Hβ、MgII或CIV线的<200天可靠时延；时延结果与近期大样本RM项目的半径-光度关系一致，推得黑洞质量为10^{7–9} M⊙。

Conclusion: 这是COSMOS场首个系统性RM成果，验证了SDSS-V在高红移AGN黑洞质量测量中的能力，为联合Rubin天文台与Roman空间望远镜开展大样本AGN时域研究打下基础。

Abstract: We present the first reverberation mapping (RM) measurements in the COSMOS field included in the SDSS-V Black Hole Mapper Reverberation Mapping Project. Using $\sim$6 months of plate-based spectroscopy (from late 2020 to mid-2021) and accompanying ZTF+DECam \emph{gri}-band photometric light curves, we analyze the variability of over 250 active galactic nuclei (AGNs) out to $z \sim 3$. We successfully recover reliable $\lesssim$ 200d time lags for 22 AGNs ($z \lesssim 2.5$) in H$α$, H$β$, MgII, or CIV, using a combination of multiple lag detection methods and significance tests. Our measurements are consistent with the radius-luminosity relations from recent large-scale RM programs, providing measured masses of $10^{7-9} M_{\odot}$ for these AGNs. These results and upcoming long-baseline SDSS-V spectroscopy lay the groundwork for large-sample AGN studies in conjunction with other facilities covering the COSMOS field, including the Vera C. Rubin Observatory and Roman Space Telescope.

</details>


### [158] [Diffuse interstellar bands in nearby galaxies with MaNGA: environmental trends with metallicity, extinction, ionization, and star-forming activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15272)
*Konstantinos Kouroumpatzakis,Santiago Jiménez,Boris Deshev,Richard Wünsch,Pavel Jáchym,Jan Cami,Jiří Kubát,Anežka Srbljanović*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用MaNGA光谱数据，对837个近邻星系中的DIB λ5780吸收线进行空间分辨研究，发现其强度与消光、金属丰度和特定恒星形成率密切相关，主要在尘埃丰富、化学演化充分且中等电离的环境中被探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DIB与尘埃消光的相关性已被证实，但其在不同星际介质环境中的强度变化规律仍不清楚。

Method: 利用MaNGA巡天的空间分辨光谱，对837个近邻星系进行分析；通过光谱拟合获取恒星与电离气体参数，并从高信噪比空间加权残差谱中测量DIB λ5780吸收。

Result: 在159个星系中探测到DIB λ5780，最远距离达232 Mpc；其等效宽度正相关于恒星与气体消光；偏好出现在高消光、近/超太阳金属丰度（12+log(O/H)≳8.5）及中等sSFR（−10.5≲log(sSFR/yr⁻¹)≲−9）区域；在低金属丰度或强电离环境中未被探测到。

Conclusion: DIB λ5780的探测强烈依赖于尘埃含量、化学演化程度和电离状态，在MaNGA数据灵敏度范围内，它主要存在于尘埃丰富、化学演化充分且中等电离的ISM环境中。

Abstract: Diffuse interstellar bands (DIBs) are absorption features of uncertain origin observed in the optical and near-infrared spectra of stars and galaxies. Although their correlation with dust extinction is well established, the physical conditions governing their strength across different interstellar medium (ISM) environments remain poorly understood. We investigate the environmental dependence of the DIB $\lambda5780$ using spatially resolved MaNGA spectroscopy for 837 nearby galaxies spanning a broad range of stellar masses, star-formation rates, and ISM properties. Stellar and nebular parameters are derived via spectral fitting, while DIB absorption is measured from high-signal-to-noise spatially binned residual spectra. We detect the DIB $\lambda5780$ in 159 galaxies and report the most distant spatially resolved DIB detection to date, at 232 Mpc. The DIB $\lambda5780$ equivalent width correlates positively with both stellar and gas-phase extinction. DIB $\lambda5780$ is preferentially detected in regions of high extinction, near- or super-solar metallicity ($12+\log({\rm O/H})\gtrsim8.5$), and moderate specific star-formation rates ($-10.5\lesssim\log({\rm sSFR}/{\rm yr}^{-1})\lesssim-9$). By contrast, it falls below our detection threshold in metal-poor or strongly ionized environments. It is rarely detected in regions with little or no recent star formation, with the few detections in such environments occurring primarily in regions driven by composite ionization. Our results indicate that DIB $\lambda5780$ is preferentially detected, within the sensitivity limits of the MaNGA data, in dust-rich, chemically evolved, and moderately ionized environments.

</details>


### [159] [The Distant Milky Way Halo from the Southern Hemisphere: Spatially broad and closer stellar debris associated to the Magellanic Stream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15301)
*Manuel Cavieres,Jesús Torres,Camila Navarrete,Himansh Rathore,Julio Chanamé,Gurtina Besla,Nicolás Garavito-Camargo,A. Katherina Vivas*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过构建K巨星候选体星表并分析光谱数据，发现了5颗新的麦哲伦流（MS）恒星候选体，扩展了已知样本，并指出当前模型可能低估了恒星碎片的分布范围和距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 麦哲伦流（MS）已有气体观测，但其恒星对应体极少被确认，亟需扩大样本以检验其形成机制和动力学历史。

Method: 利用DELVE DR2、VHS DR5测光与Gaia DR3自行数据构建87,763颗远距K巨星候选体星表；对其中51颗沿MS尾迹方向的目标获取VLT和Magellan光谱，结合视向速度、分光光度距离和自行进行运动学与化学丰度分析，并在时变MW-LMC-SMC引力势中开展轨道积分。

Result: 确认5颗新MS恒星候选体（距离58–95 kpc），金属丰度与SMC外围一致，轨道积分支持其麦哲伦起源；联合已有候选体发现恒星分布纬度范围远超现有模型预测，且普遍比模拟碎片更近。

Conclusion: 麦哲伦恒星碎片可能构成一个更延展而非狭窄的结构；当前模型的距离偏差或可由SMC更近、偏心率更低的轨道解释，但需新数值模拟与更大光谱样本进一步验证。

Abstract: The Magellanic Stream (MS) is a gaseous structure extending over $150^\circ$ across the sky, likely formed through interactions between the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds (LMC and SMC) as they fell into the Milky Way (MW). Its predicted stellar counterpart would provide a collisionless record of these interactions, but few candidate members have been identified. To expand this sample, we constructed a catalog of 87,763 distant K-giant candidates using DELVE DR2 and VHS DR5 photometry and Gaia DR3 astrometry, and analyzed VLT and Magellan spectra of 51 targets along the trailing MS. Combining radial velocities, spectrophotometric distances, and proper motions, we identify five new MS candidates at 58--95 kpc. Their metallicities are consistent with the outskirts of the SMC, while orbit integrations in a time-dependent MW--LMC--SMC potential support a possible Magellanic origin. Combined with previously identified candidates, these stars span a substantially broader range of Magellanic latitude than predicted by existing models, suggesting an extended debris field rather than a narrow stream. The candidates also lie predominantly closer than the simulated debris. More recent, less eccentric SMC orbits may help explain this distance discrepancy, although new simulations are required to test their implications for the stellar debris, along with larger spectroscopic samples.

</details>


### [160] [VISTA Magellanic Clouds deep observations of Classical Cepheids: Precision distances and a collision-driven SMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15303)
*V. Ripepi,R. Molinaro,M. Marconi,M. -R. L. Cioni,J. Th. van Loon,R. de Grijs,A. Omkumar,J. Storm,T. Sicignano,J. Oliveira,V. Ivanov,M. A. T. Groenewegen,G. De Somma,F. Cusano*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用VISTA深场观测数据，首次在大、小麦哲伦云中心致密区域建立了迄今最精确的造父变星近红外周光关系（PL/PW），揭示了SMC年轻造父变星沿视线方向存在约4 kpc的空间二分结构，为LMC与SMC近期直接碰撞提供了独立恒星证据，并给出金属丰度对周光律的修正系数γ ≈ -0.2 mag/dex。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大、小麦哲伦云中心区域对理解星系相互作用和校准宇宙距离尺度至关重要，但因极端恒星密集而观测困难。

Method: 利用VMCDeep巡天的J和Ks波段时序数据（每滤光片约40次观测），覆盖LMC和SMC中心各1.77 deg²区域，采用点扩散函数（PSF）测光方法构建高质量光变曲线；结合傅里叶参数分析、脉动模型拟合及年龄推断，并统一基模与一阶泛音造父变星以测定两云相对距离。

Result: 获得迄今麦哲伦云中最低光度弥散的PL/PW关系；发现SMC中心年轻造父变星（t < 116 Myr）存在沿视线方向~4 kpc的双结构空间分离；确认SMC中心双峰恒星形成历史；导出两云相对距离并支持周光律存在γ ≈ -0.2 mag/dex的金属丰度修正。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅显著提升了周光关系的精度，还为LMC-SMC近期碰撞提供了关键恒星动力学证据，并对经典周光律的金属丰度依赖性给出了更可靠的约束。

Abstract: The central regions of the Magellanic Clouds are crucial for understanding galaxy interactions and calibrating the cosmic distance scale, yet they remain observationally challenging due to extreme stellar crowding. We utilised deep VISTA observations (VMCDeep) to determine the highest-precision near-infrared Period-Luminosity (PL) and Period-Wesenheit (PW) relations to date for Classical Cepheids (delta Cep class, DCEPs) in the dense central regions of the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds (LMC and SMC). We analysed J and Ks time-series data covering the central 1.77 deg^2 of each galaxy. Utilising approximately 40 epochs per filter and PSF-based photometry, we derived high-quality light curves for 573 and 2090 DCEPs in the LMC and SMC, respectively. We derived exceptionally precise PL and PW relations, exhibiting the lowest photometric dispersion published so far for the Magellanic Clouds. Fourier parameters calculated from the light curves indicate that pulsational models incorporating a mildly non-canonical mass-luminosity relation and a mixing-length parameter of alpha = 1.7 best reproduce the observations. Age derivations confirm a bimodal star formation history in the SMC centre. Crucially, we discovered a striking line-of-sight spatial dichotomy within the young SMC DCEP population (t < 116 Myr), which is bifurcated into two distinct structures separated by ~4 kpc. This provides independent stellar evidence tracing a recent direct collision with the LMC. Finally, by unifying fundamental and first-overtone pulsators, we derived a highly constrained relative distance between the Clouds. Comparing this to detached eclipsing binary distances, our data suggest a moderate metallicity correction for the Leavitt Law with gamma ~ -0.2 mag/dex.

</details>


### [161] [Discrete Supernovae and the Limits of Continuous Models in Low-Mass Dark Matter Halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15335)
*C. Power,S. Balu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了在低质量暗物质晕中，超新星反馈的离散性与连续性建模对恒星形成的影响，发现离散模型因首颗超新星延迟爆发导致显著恒星质量过剩，且该效应随晕质量减小而增强，根源在于气体移除的非线性响应而非IMF采样随机性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多星系形成模型将超新星反馈简化为基于初始质量函数（IMF）平均的连续能量注入，但在低质量暗物质晕中，单次离散超新星可能吹走全部气体，使该近似失效。

Method: 采用一维区域模型，在z≥5红移下对比连续反馈（IMF平均、能量延时注入）与离散反馈（单个超新星瞬时注入、相同单位恒星质量预期能量）两种机制。

Result: 离散反馈导致1.5–2.7倍于当前气体质量的气体被一次吹散；首颗超新星在~10 Myr后爆发，早于连续反馈起效时间（~4 Myr），造成恒星质量过剩；该过剩强烈依赖晕质量（10⁷ M⊙时达135%，10⁸ M⊙时为15%，10⁹ M⊙时可忽略），且不源于IMF随机采样，而取决于能量分配方式（更多更小事件可消除过剩）。

Conclusion: 仅匹配单位恒星质量释放的总能量不足以保证反馈等效；在气体保留至首颗超新星爆发的晕中，反馈的离散性与事件规模对恒星形成历史有根本性影响，需在星系模拟中显式考虑。

Abstract: Many galaxy formation models represent supernova feedback by a continuous energy injection rate averaged over the stellar initial mass function (IMF). This approximation can fail in low-mass dark matter halos, where single, discrete supernovae can unbind more gas than the reservoir holds. We investigate this using a one-zone model that characterises continuous and discrete supernova feedback at redshifts $z\geq5$. Both feedback modes inject the same expected energy per unit stellar mass formed. In the discrete model, individual supernovae are resolved and energy is injected instantaneously; in the continuous model, supernovae are IMF-averaged and energy injection is spread over a finite time interval. Once stars form, a supernova can unbind 1.5-2.7 times the gas present. Continuous feedback begins removing gas after $\sim 4$ Myr, whereas the first discrete supernova occurs after $\sim 10$ Myr, which produces a stellar mass excess before feedback takes effect. The excess persists because the reservoir responds nonlinearly: an event removes at most the gas present, and star formation halts once it is empty. It depends strongly on halo mass: from 135 per cent at $10^7\, \rm{M}_\odot$, to 15 per cent at $10^8\, \rm{M}_\odot$, to negligible at $10^9\, \rm{M}_\odot$ by $z=5$. Fixing the number and timing of supernovae does not remove the stellar excess, which indicates that it is not caused by stochastic IMF sampling. Instead, it vanishes when the same energy is divided among more, smaller events. Matching the energy released per unit stellar mass therefore does not guarantee equivalent feedback in halos that retain gas until the first supernova.

</details>


### [162] [3D dust attenuation effects in galaxies: structural biases and induced negative color gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15355)
*João Duarte,Santiago González-Gaitán,Polychronis Papaderos,Ana M. Mourão,Rita P. Santos*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维尘埃消光对星系表面亮度分布（SBP）和颜色梯度观测的影响，发现尘埃显著扭曲SBP形状、改变拟合的Sérsic指数（偏差达25%），并产生虚假负颜色梯度，可能误导对恒星分布、形成历史和内部熄火机制的理解；强调必须采用全三维辐射转移建模以避免系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面亮度分布和颜色梯度常被用于研究星系结构与演化，但其受三维尘埃消光影响尚未被充分理解或校正，可能导致对恒星本征性质的误判。

Method: 利用SKIRT辐射转移代码，构建具有球对称恒星密度、恒定恒星-尘埃比和空间均匀SED的理想化Sérsic星系模型，系统模拟尘埃消光对SBP和颜色梯度的影响。

Result: 尘埃导致SBP在中心区域显著畸变，蓝波段和高核心尘埃密度下Sérsic指数偏差达~25%，可伪造中心质量缺失；高中心尘埃时核心-Sérsic模型拟合更优；仅~2×10⁶ M⊙尘埃即可产生与观测相当的负颜色梯度，且单层尘埃屏模型无法再现该效应。

Conclusion: 三维尘埃消光会严重偏倚星系结构与恒星种群参数的推断，忽略其影响将导致对星系形成历史、内部熄火机制等关键问题得出错误结论；必须采用全三维辐射转移方法进行建模校正。

Abstract: Radial surface brightness profiles (SBPs) and color gradients are useful tools for studying galaxy structure, formation, and evolution. While it is usually assumed that they are primarily driven by stellar properties, such as mass, age or metallicity, their dependence on 3D dust attenuation is not fully understood or accounted for. We analyze how 3D dust attenuation affects galaxy observations by hindering the estimation of intrinsic properties. We examine dust-induced shifts in surface brightness and color. Using SKIRT radiative transfer models, we simulated simple Sérsic galaxies, with spherically-symmetric stellar densities, constant stellar-to-dust ratios, and a spatially constant spectral energy distribution (SED). We find that attenuated SBPs differ significantly from dust-free profiles, particularly in the central galaxy regions. These differences become more pronounced at bluer wavelengths and for higher core dust densities, leading to variations in the fitted Sérsic index of up to ~25%. This can lead to incorrect conclusions about the intrinsic stellar distribution and mimic a central mass deficit. For galaxies with very centrally-dense dust distributions, a core-Sérsic model results in a better fit of the attenuated SBP. For dust masses as low as ~2*10^6 $M_{\odot}$, dust-induced negative color gradients reach the same order of magnitude as those commonly observed in galaxies, which a dust-screen fails to account for. This occurs even for a spatially invariant SED and can lead to wrong conclusions about intrinsic stellar populations and related astrophysical questions, such as their formation histories or the mechanisms of inside-out star formation quenching. A full 3D approach is necessary to properly model attenuation effects, which, if not accounted for, can bias inferences made about stellar populations, their evolutionary histories, and spatial distributions.

</details>


### [163] [A giant dark matter core extending far beyond the stars of a dwarf galaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15528)
*Qinwei Yuan,Yong Shi,Xin Li,Zhiyu Zhang,Zhiyuan Zheng,Qiusheng Gu,Xiaoling Yu,Fuyan Bian*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文发现矮星系UGCA 320中存在一个异常巨大的暗物质核（半径约7.6 kpc），远超典型矮星系尺度及恒星分布范围，对标准冷暗物质（CDM）模型构成挑战，更支持自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准宇宙学模型中重子反馈能否解释暗物质密度分布，尤其是矮星系中是否存在超出预期的大尺度暗物质核。

Method: 结合MUSE Hα动力学数据与MHONGOOSE巡天的多分辨率MeerKAT HI观测，构建延展旋转曲线；扣除中心HI非圆运动成分后，用NFW、ISO、Burkert及广义NFW模型拟合暗物质分布。

Result: 旋转曲线显著偏离尖锐的NFW轮廓，偏好ISO和Burkert核心模型；广义NFW拟合得内区密度斜率γ=0.36±0.12；ISO最佳拟合给出暗物质核半径7.62±0.80 kpc，约为恒星半光半径的4倍。

Conclusion: UGCA 320中异常延展的暗物质核难以由标准CDM或恒星反馈解释，支持SIDM模型，但与温暗物质、模糊暗物质及反馈致核机制不兼容。

Abstract: In the standard cosmological model, the distribution of cold dark matter (CDM) can be reshaped by baryonic processes through gravity, as gas outflows alter the central potential and transform steep cusps into nearly constant-density cores. However, such feedback-generated cores are generally expected to have sizes comparable to the visible stellar body of a galaxy. Here we report the discovery of an exceptionally extended dark matter core in a nearby gas-rich dwarf galaxy UGCA 320. We combine high-resolution MUSE H$α$ kinematics in the inner region with high-, medium-, and low-resolution MeerKAT HI observations from the MHONGOOSE survey tracing the galaxy out to large radii. After accounting for an additional non-circular kinematic component identified in the central HI spectra, we construct an extended rotation curve to constrain the dark matter distribution. The resulting rotation curve is poorly reproduced by a cuspy Navarro-Frenk-White (NFW) halo but well described by cored pseudo-isothermal (ISO) and Burkert halo models. Consistently, a fit with a generalized NFW model yields an inner density slope of $γ=0.36\pm0.12$, quantitatively supporting a shallow central density profile. The best-fit ISO model gives a dark matter core radius of $7.62\pm0.80$ kpc, approximately four times the stellar half-light radius and substantially larger than typically found in dwarf galaxies. We investigate the main systematic uncertainties and find that the resulting core radii remain large, all lying within 3$σ$ of the fiducial fit. The exceptionally large core in UGCA 320 may pose a challenge to the standard CDM paradigm. While its properties are broadly consistent with self-interacting dark matter (SIDM), they may be difficult to reconcile with warm and fuzzy dark matter models, as well as the stellar feedback-driven core formation scenario.

</details>


### [164] [Model-dependent metallicities of young stellar populations in TIMER galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15556)
*Geraldo Gonçalves,Paula R. T. Coelho,Luiz A. Silva-Lima,Lucimara Martins,Marie Martig,Dimitri A. Gadotti,Camila de Sá-Freitas,Jairo Méndez-Abreu,Patricia Sánchez-Blázquez,Jesús Falcón-Barroso,Glenn van de Ven,Adriana de Lorenzo-Cáceres,Isabel Pérez*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了邻近棒旋星系中心年轻恒星种群的性质，发现不同恒星种群模型（经验型与理论型）对金属丰度推断结果差异显著；经验模型显示金属丰度随年龄单调下降，与观测到的气体金属丰度不符；而理论模型给出的结果更符合单次稀释事件的物理图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒星种群在盘星系中心区域的性质可为星系形成与长期演化提供关键约束，尤其需厘清中心年轻恒星形成与气体流入、化学增丰过程的关系。

Method: 基于TIMER巡天数据，采用四种恒星种群模型（MILES、X-Shooter两种经验库；Starburst99、HR-pyPopStar两种理论库），结合发射线诊断得到的独立气体相金属丰度，分析三个邻近棒旋星系中心区的恒星金属丰度与年龄-金属丰度关系。

Result: 经验库推得的金属丰度离散度（1.11和0.98 dex）远高于理论库（0.40和0.49 dex）及对应气体丰度（0.49 dex）；气体金属丰度在中心星形成区有约0.3 dex压低，但无法由典型盘梯度下的棒驱动流入解释；年龄-金属丰度关系均呈现老（自增丰）与新（流入气体形成）两成分，但经验库要求持续输入贫金属气体，与气体观测矛盾，理论库则支持单次稀释事件。

Conclusion: 恒星种群建模方式对金属丰度推断影响极大；经验库所得年龄-金属丰度关系在物理上难以自洽，理论库结果更符合气体观测与当前星系演化图景；未来研究需谨慎选择并校准模型。

Abstract: Stellar populations in the central regions of disc galaxies provide key constraints on their formation and secular evolution. We investigate the properties of young stellar populations in the centres of nearby barred galaxies from the TIMER survey, using four stellar population models, two based on empirical (MILES, X-Shooter) and two on theoretical (Starburst99, HR-pyPopStar) stellar libraries, complemented by independent gas-phase metallicities from emission-line diagnostics. The inferred stellar metallicities depend strongly on the adopted model. Measured as the 5th-to-95th percentile spread across the three galaxies analysed in detail, the empirical libraries recover variations of 1.11 and 0.98 dex, whereas the theoretical ones return 0.40 and 0.49 dex; the gas in the same regions spans 0.49 dex. Gas metallicities are indeed lower in the central star-forming regions, but the amplitude of that depression (about 0.3 dex in NGC 1097) exceeds what bar-driven inflow of disc gas can account for, given typical abundance gradients in literature. The age-metallicity relations consistently reveal two distinct components: an older population consistent with a self-enrichment scenario, and a younger population formed from gas inflows with a different chemical enrichment history. The empirical libraries produce a monotonic decline of metallicities from old to young populations, which would require a sustained inflow of metal-poor gas outpacing its own enrichment. The theoretical libraries instead recover a young component whose metallicity departs from the older populations only at the most recent ages and by a few tenths of a dex, compatible with a single dilution episode. Our results demonstrate that model choice critically affects inferred metallicities, with empirical stellar libraries recovering age-metallicity relations that demand physical conditions the gas measurements cannot sustain.

</details>


### [165] [Measuring Milky Way Mass with RR Lyraes and Blue Horizontal Branch Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15560)
*Xiangyu Li,Yang Huang,Jifeng Liu,Xinyi Li,Qikang Feng,Haozhu Fu,Shenglan Sun,Jiahui Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用RR Lyrae星和蓝水平分支星（BHBs）两种晕族示踪体，结合多种巡天数据，通过球对称Jeans方程和示踪质量估计器（TME）方法，测定了银河系在80 kpc以内的质量分布，并外推得到修正系统误差后的银河系晕总质量约为9.2–9.8×10¹¹ M⊙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定银河系质量对理解星系形成演化、刻画银河系引力势与并合历史、以及探索暗物质本质至关重要。

Method: 使用LAMOST、SDSS和DESI数据构建7550颗RR Lyrae星和8230颗BHB星样本（距离银心10–100 kpc），采用球对称Jeans方程和示踪质量估计器（TME）两种独立方法估计质量；并用Auriga模拟的mock样本校准系统偏差。

Result: RRL和BHB分别给出80 kpc内质量约为4.76±0.23和4.66±0.35×10¹¹ M⊙；NFW外推得未修正的M₂₀₀为~8.12–8.13×10¹¹ M⊙；经系统误差修正后，M₂₀₀为9.79⁺⁰.⁹³₋₀.₈₄和9.24⁺⁰.⁹⁵₋₀.₈₄×10¹¹ M⊙。

Conclusion: 两种示踪体结果一致，系统误差校正后银河系晕质量约为(9.5±0.5)×10¹¹ M⊙，为当前最精确的银河系质量测量之一。

Abstract: Measuring the mass of the Milky Way is essential for understanding galaxy formation and evolution, characterizing the Galactic potential and assembly history, and probing the nature of dark matter. We derive the enclosed mass profile of the Milky Way out to Galactocentric distances of $\sim80\,\mathrm{kpc}$ using two halo tracers with full 3D kinematics: RR Lyraes (RRLs) and blue horizontal-branch stars (BHBs). Our sample consists of 7,550 RRLs and 8,230 BHBs constructed from the Large Sky Area Multi-Object Fiber Spectroscopic Telescope (LAMOST), the Sloan Digital Sky Survey (SDSS), and the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), spanning Galactocentric distances from $10$ to $100\,\mathrm{kpc}$. We estimate the enclosed mass using two independent methods: the spherical Jeans equation and the tracer mass estimator (TME). The two tracers yield consistent results, with $M(<81.7\,\mathrm{kpc}) = (4.76 \pm 0.23)\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ from RRLs and $M(<82.8\,\mathrm{kpc}) = (4.66 \pm 0.35)\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ from BHBs. Adopting an NFW profile to extrapolate the enclosed mass beyond the outermost radius probed by our tracers, we infer virial masses of $M_{200} = 8.12^{+0.72}_{-0.65}\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ from RRLs and $M_{200} = 8.13^{+0.78}_{-0.67}\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ from BHBs. We further validate our methodology using mock RRL and BHB samples constructed from two Auriga halos, finding that the inferred masses are underestimated by $15.5\%\pm2.8\%$ for RRLs and $11.9\%\pm3.4\%$ for BHBs, respectively. After correcting for these systematic errors, the Milky Way virial masses are $M_{200} = 9.79^{+0.93}_{-0.84}\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ from RRLs and $M_{200} = 9.24^{+0.95}_{-0.84}\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ from BHBs.

</details>


### [166] [A new indirect approach for oxygen abundance determinations across a wide range of physical conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15581)
*Sandra Zamora,Asier Castrillo,A. I. Díaz,Marta Gavilán*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于硫发射线测定HII区金属丰度的新方法，并以M101星系为基准验证该方法，成功将丰度梯度延伸至星系中心，减少了传统直接测量方法的系统偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统基于禁线（如[SIII]）直接测量电子温度及金属丰度时在高金属丰度区域受限的问题，拓展丰度梯度测量范围并改进强线标定。

Method: 利用[SIII]禁线直接测量S^{2+}电子温度；在超太阳金属丰度区域，采用SO_{23}参数间接推导；结合M101中151个HII区的径向分布进行验证。

Result: 新方法成功将M101金属丰度梯度延伸至0.04 R_e（星系中心附近），缓解了基于auroral线的直接丰度测定中的系统偏差。

Conclusion: 该基于硫线的新方法可有效扩展高金属丰度区域的丰度测量能力，为改进中间至高金属丰度区的强线丰度标定提供了新途径。

Abstract: In this work we present a new approach to derive HII region metal abundances based on sulfur emission lines. We select M101 as benchmark galaxy to test our new methodology along the wide metallicity gradient of this huge face-on star-forming spiral. Based on previous studies, we compile a sample of 151 HII regions and compare their properties base on the galaxy radial profile. The new methodology is based on the derivation of the electron temperature of the double ionised sulfur, either by the direct measurement of the [SIII] auroral line or, at supersolar metalicities, from the $SO_{23}$ parameter. This new indirect method is able to extend the abundance gradient up to the very centre of the M101 galaxy (0.04 $R_e$). This new methodology mitigates systematic biases in direct abundance determinations based on auroral lines, while opening up new opportunities to improve commonly used strong line abundance calibrations in the intermediate to high metallicity regime.

</details>


### [167] [Exploring a Correlation Between AGN Black Hole Mass and X-ray Luminosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15850)
*Wordsworth K. K. Chu,A. Kathy Romer,Paul Giles,Jessica E. Pilling,Julian A. Mayers*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用X射线光度与黑洞质量之间的相关性来替代耗时昂贵的反响映射（RM）方法，以估算I型活动星系核（AGN）中心黑洞质量（M_BH）。基于eROSITA巡天数据中35个具有RM测量M_BH的AGN样本，作者确认了X射线光度与M_BH之间存在正相关，但内在弥散较大（σ_int ≈ 0.5 dex），尚不足以用于精确单个黑洞质量预测；该关系可用于改进宇宙学模拟生成的X射线天空模拟，服务于星系团宇宙学及未来如Athena等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找比反响映射（RM）更省时省力的黑洞质量估计方法。

Method: 基于eROSITA全天空巡天中35个具有RM测得黑洞质量的I型AGN样本，分析其X射线光度与黑洞质量的相关性，并与前人（Mayers et al. 2018）结果对比。

Result: 确认X射线光度与黑洞质量存在显著正相关，但内在弥散较大（σ_int = 0.50^{+0.08}_{-0.06} dex），难以用于单个AGN的M_BH精确预测。

Conclusion: X射线光度–黑洞质量关系目前不适合作为M_BH的实用估算工具，但可用于校准和改进大尺度宇宙学模拟中的X射线天空建模，支撑未来X射线天文任务。

Abstract: The traditional method to measure Type-1 Active Galactic Nuclei central black hole masses ($M_{BH}$) is the reverberation mapping (RM) technique. Motivated by the desire to find a less observationally expensive method (than RM) to estimate $M_{BH}$, we have explored a purported correlation between X-ray luminosity and $M_{BH}$. For this we have compiled a sample of 35 AGN with RM $M_{BH}$ estimates that were detected in the eROSITA All-Sky Survey public data release. Using a sample that is larger than that of J. A. Mayers et al. 2018 and has a cleaner selection function, we confirm their XMM-Newton based result of a positive correlation. However, the scatter on the scaling relation remains too high ($σ_\mathrm{int}=0.50^{+0.08}_{-0.06}$) to make this a practical method to predict $M_{BH}$ for a given X-ray detected Type-1 AGN. That said, our findings can be used to improve mock X-ray skies generated from large volume hydrodynamical cosmological simulations. Such mocks are a necessary ingredient for cluster cosmology and preparation for future missions such as NewAthena.

</details>


### [168] [Dynamical evolution of the stellar mass function in multiple-population globular clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15855)
*J. Bruce,E. Vesperini,A. Askar,M. Giersz,E. Dalessandro,M. Scalco*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用MOCCA代码的蒙特卡洛模拟，探讨了含双星族的球状星团中恒星质量函数（MF）的长期动力学演化，发现其径向分布呈现断点幂律特征，断点略位于半质量半径内侧，并受多尺度星族结构影响；同时探索了底部轻初始质量函数（IMF）及两星族IMF差异对MF演化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期观测研究启发——如大质量球状星团可能更符合底部轻IMF，以及47 Tucanae外区第二星族MF斜率更平缓——本文旨在探究多星族结构如何通过动力学过程影响MF的空间分布与演化。

Method: 采用MOCCA代码开展一系列蒙特卡洛模拟，模拟含两个恒星星族的球状星团的动力学演化，考虑二体弛豫、质量分层和潮汐剥离等过程，分析P1、P2及整体星团的MF斜率径向分布（三维及二维投影），并引入三参数底部轻IMF及P1/P2差异化IMF进行对比实验。

Result: MF斜率的径向分布呈断点幂律，断点略在半质量半径内侧；P2因初始更集中，导致外区MF斜率更陡，进而影响全局MF；在底部轻IMF及P2 IMF比P1更平坦的设定下，外区P2 MF斜率变平的特征得到再现和强化。

Conclusion: 多星族的多尺度结构显著塑造球状星团MF的动力学演化轨迹，特别是其径向梯度与断点特征；观测到的外区P2 MF变平现象可由P2固有的较平IMF结合动力学演化共同解释，支持星族间IMF存在本质差异的假说。

Abstract: The dynamical evolution of globular clusters is significantly influenced by the multiscale structure introduced by the presence of multiple stellar populations. In this study, we investigate the long-term dynamical evolution of the stellar mass function (MF) within globular clusters hosting two stellar populations using a suite of Monte Carlo simulations run with the MOCCA code. Dynamical processes including two-body relaxation, mass segregation, and tidal stripping result in distinct radial profiles describing the variation with distance from the cluster's center of the stellar MF slope within the first population (P1), the second population (P2), and the cluster as a whole. We show that these radial profiles are well described by a broken power law with a knee located slightly interior to the half-mass radius, a feature partially driven by the multiscale structure of the multiple populations. In both 3D and 2D projected coordinates, we explore several characteristic clustercentric distances within the profile, including those where the local MF slope matches the global value of the present-day MF slope and the initial mass function (IMF) slope, and we examine how these positions evolve with the cluster's dynamical age. Our results highlight how the initially more centrally concentrated P2 drives a steeper radial gradient and a steeper MF slope in the outer regions, which subsequently affects the global cluster values. Motivated by recent observational studies suggesting that a bottom-light IMF may better describe massive globular clusters, as well as measurements of a flatter P2 MF slope in the outer regions of 47 Tucanae, we additionally explore a three-parameter bottom-light IMF and a simulation in which the P1 IMF differs from the P2 IMF, with the P2 IMF flatter than that of the P1 in the lower mass range.

</details>


### [169] [oMEGACat. XI. Kinematic Characterization of Omega Centauri's Subpopulations: A Flattened Primordial Component](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15866)
*Peter J. Smith,N. Neumayer,A. C. Seth,C. Clontz,M. Häberle,S. O. Souza,Z. Wang,G. van de Ven,M. S. Nitschai,M. Libralato,A. Feldmeier-Krause,S. Kamann,F. I. Aros,R. Leaman,A. Bellini,R. Pechetti,M. Alfaro-Cuello*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过结合哈勃太空望远镜（HST）的自行数据与MUSE光谱的视向速度数据，分析了球状星团ω Centauri中不同恒星种群（基于染色体图、金属丰度和年龄划分）的动力学特性，发现原始恒星成分呈现显著扁平化的速度弥散结构（椭率ε≈0.7），而富集成分则接近各向同性；该差异支持原始恒星继承自被吸积矮星系核的扁平轨道结构，而富集恒星可能形成于致密中心星暴或内落致密团块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ω Centauri被认为是被银河系吸积的矮星系残余核，其长弛豫时间使其恒星运动学可能保留形成历史信息，因此研究其不同化学成分恒星的动力学差异有助于理解其形成与演化机制。

Method: 联合使用HST提供的高精度自行数据和MUSE巡天（oMEGACat）提供的视向速度数据，在半光半径内，按染色体图（chromosome map）、金属丰度和年龄对恒星进行分类，并分别分析各类群的速度椭率、旋转及弥散结构。

Result: 染色体图划分出的原始星族呈现高度扁平的速度弥散（ε≈0.7），在自行与视向速度中均独立确认；富集星族在视向速度中接近各向同性（ε≈0），在自行中也不比整体样本更扁平；两成分旋转差异不显著；金属丰度分组未显示类似动力学分化，因其混杂了两类成分；最富集P2星族当前中心集中度更高，暗示其初始形成位置更靠近中心。

Conclusion: 原始恒星保留了原矮星系核的扁平轨道结构，而富集恒星更可能形成于致密、中心集中的星暴或以内落致密团块方式形成，该结果支持ω Cen多阶段、非单一坍缩的形成图像。

Abstract: Omega Centauri ($ω$ Cen; NGC 5139), the likely stripped nucleus of a dwarf galaxy accreted by the Milky Way, is one of the few galactic nuclei resolvable into individual stars. Because of its long relaxation time, the kinematics of its stars may still retain memory of how this nucleus formed. We combine HST proper motions and MUSE line-of-sight velocities from the oMEGACat survey to compare populations defined by abundance variations (the chromosome map), metallicity, and age within the half-light radius. The chromosome-map division separates two dynamically distinct components. The primordial stars have a flattened dispersion field, with kinematic ellipticity $ε\approx 0.7$ ($σ_{\rm minor} / σ_{\rm major} \approx 0.8$), which we recover independently in the line-of-sight velocities and the proper motions. The enriched stars are consistent with $ε=0$ in the line-of-sight velocities and are no more flattened than the full sample in the proper motions, and the two components show no strong difference in rotation. Metallicity bins show no comparable structure, because each bin mixes stars from both components, and the age-metallicity sample covers only the cluster core, where it cannot test for the flattening. The present-day central concentration of the most enriched (P2) stars suggests that they formed more centrally concentrated than the primordial stars, independent of assembly scenario. These results favor a picture in which the primordial stars retain the flattened orbital structure of the progenitor, while the enriched stars fell inward as dense clumps or formed in compact, centrally concentrated starbursts.

</details>
