TL;DR: 本文构建了Euclid DR1强透镜样本中9538个星系尺度强透镜的光度红移与物理性质（如恒星质量、SFR、年龄、金属丰度）的均一星表，采用PyAutoLens去混淆多波段测光与Phosphoros/Prospector进行红移和SED拟合，为强透镜星系演化与质量关系研究奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准星表测光常将前景透镜星系与背景被透镜源的光混淆，导致物理参数不可靠；Euclid任务提供了大天区高分辨率成像，亟需准确刻画其海量强透镜样本的物理性质。

Method: 基于PyAutoLens对Euclid与地面图像进行透镜建模并获取去混淆多波段测光；用Phosphoros模板拟合法估计透镜星系光度红移；用Prospector进行SED拟合（非参数SFH + Salpeter IMF）推断恒星质量、SFR、年龄和金属丰度；通过光谱红移和外部星系巡天验证参数稳健性。

Result: 获得9538个透镜星系的均一星表，红移范围0.13–1.98（中位数0.68），恒星质量范围10^10–10^12.1 M⊙（中位数log10(M∗/M⊙)=11.39）；样本以大质量宁静星系为主。

Conclusion: 该星表是Euclid时代首批大规模强透镜星系物理性质刻画成果，结合去混淆测光与均一分析流程，为星系演化、透镜星系标度关系及恒星-总质量关联研究提供了关键数据基础。

Abstract: The Euclid mission is revolutionising the study of strong gravitational lenses by providing high-resolution imaging over an unprecedented survey area, increasing the number of known lenses by two orders of magnitude compared to previous surveys. Characterising this sample of lens-galaxies requires accurate measurements of their physical properties. However, standard catalogue photometry often blends the light from the foreground lens and the lensed background source, limiting the reliability of derived galaxy parameters. We present a homogeneous catalogue of photometric redshifts and physical properties for 9538 galaxy-scale strong lenses identified in Euclid's DR1 strong-lens sample. We use deblended multiwavelength photometry obtained from the lens modelling of Euclid and ground-based images with PyAutoLens. The photometric redshifts of the lens galaxies are estimated using the template-fitting code Phosphoros. The stellar masses, SFRs, stellar ages, and metallicities are inferred through SED fitting with Prospector, adopting a flexible non-parametric SFH and a Salpeter IMF. The robustness of the inferred parameters is assessed through comparisons with spectroscopic redshifts and external galaxy surveys. The sample of lenses spans a broad redshift range of $0.13\lesssim z_{\rm phot} \lesssim 1.98$, with a median $z_{\rm phot} = 0.68^{+0.33}_{-0.29}$. The sample is dominated by massive quiescent galaxies with a $M_\ast$ range $[10^{10},\,10^{12.1}]\,M_\odot$ and a median $\log_{10}(M_\ast/M_\odot) = 11.39^{+0.29}_{-0.33}$. This catalogue represents one of the first large-scale characterisations of strong-lens galaxies in the Euclid era. By combining deblended photometry with homogeneous photometric redshift and SED-fitting analyses, it provides a foundation for studies of galaxy evolution, lens-galaxy scaling relations, and the connection between stellar and total mass in strong lenses.

</details>


### [171] [Identification of extragalactic radio sources in the VISTA survey of the Magellanic Clouds using an unsupervised machine learning algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15880)
*Clara M. Pennock,Jacco Th. van Loon,Mohammed Iddrisu Nlowie,Elizabeth Joy Leese,Maja Nowaczyk,Heinz Andernach,Duncan Farrah,Valentin D. Ivanov,Michał J. Michałowski,Joana M. Oliveira,Jahang Prathap,Syed F. Rahman,Mara Salvato,Tayyaba Zafar*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用t-SNE无监督机器学习算法，结合光学到射电多波段测光数据，对大小麦哲伦云中的近8000个源进行分类，成功区分AGN、低红移星系、耀变体、尘埃恒星天体和高自行恒星五类，并通过SALT光谱观测发现了红移达3.3的新AGN及麦哲伦发射线天体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大小麦哲伦云这类恒星密集区域中，从大量混杂源中准确识别河外天体具有挑战性，亟需更有效的分类方法。

Method: 采用t-SNE无监督机器学习算法，以VMC与EMU/ASKAP巡天的光学至射电多波段测光与颜色为特征，在二维t-SNE图上聚类源；结合已有分类样本对聚类结果进行标注与验证。

Result: 成功将6652个（大麦哲伦云）和1356个（小麦哲伦云）源划分为五类；已知分类样本与t-SNE聚类高度吻合，并揭示了未被先前研究覆盖的新区域；SALT新光谱确认多个高红移AGN和麦哲伦发射线天体。

Conclusion: 光学至射电多波段测光联合t-SNE等无监督学习方法，可高效区分恒星与河外源、AGN与普通星系，为未来大规模巡天的源分类提供了可靠框架。

Abstract: The Magellanic Clouds benefit from a wealth of data across the electromagnetic spectrum, but extracting extragalactic sources from these crowded fields is a less than straightforward endeavour. We used an unsupervised machine learning algorithm (t-distributed stochastic neighbourhood embedding; t-SNE) to classify 6,652 and 1,356 sources in the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds, respectively, that were detected in the VISTA Survey of the Magellanic Clouds (VMC) and the Evolutionary Map of the Universe (EMU) survey with the Australian Square Kilometre Array Pathfinder (ASKAP). Multi-wavelength photometry and colours from optical to radio were used as features to cluster similar sources together in 2D space on a t-SNE map. We used previously classified sources to separate the clusters into five classes: AGN, low-redshift galaxies, blazars, dusty stellar objects (e.g. young stellar objects, planetary nebulæ etc) and high proper-motion stars. Previous photometric classifications from the literature match with specific clusters identified, as well as provide insight on areas of the t-SNE map for which there are no previous classifications. This work shows that the combination of unsupervised machine learning with photometry from optical to radio has proven adept at separating the stellar from the extragalactic, and the AGN from the galaxies. Our associated new spectroscopy with SALT includes new discoveries of AGN up to redshift 3.3 and Magellanic emission-line objects.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文基于欧几里得DR1数据，利用多模型机器学习与众包视觉分类相结合的方法，识别出15072个高置信度强引力透镜候选体，构建了迄今最大、最均一的星系尺度强透镜样本，并提供自动建模、红移与恒星质量等科学就绪数据产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引力透镜是研究暗物质、星系演化和宇宙学的重要工具，但传统方法发现样本稀少且不均一；亟需大规模、高置信度、高分辨率的透镜样本以支撑统计性科学研究。

Method: 结合五个机器学习模型打分与约7000名公众科学家及67位专家的视觉分类，对欧几里得DR1中6800万源进行筛选；开发专用发现流水线，并对A+B级系统提供自动化透镜建模、光谱红移及恒星种群参数估计。

Result: 发布15072个高置信强透镜候选体（6073个A级、8999个B级），含84个双源面透镜、超1000个盘状透镜、数百个被透镜化的AGN/类星体；给出透镜体恒星质量log(M*/M⊙)=11.39^{+0.29}_{-0.33}、光度红移z=0.68^{+0.33}_{-0.29}、爱因斯坦半径0.85^{+0.44}_{-0.24}角秒；预计欧几里得宽场巡天将产出超10万个高置信候选体。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现强透镜研究从‘罕见发现’到‘统计工具’的范式转变，为星系形成、暗物质分布及宇宙学参数测量提供了前所未有的高质量数据基础。

Abstract: We present 15 072 high-confidence strong lens candidates in Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1), spanning 2000 deg$^2$. These comprise 6073 grade A ('sure lens') and 8999 grade B ('probable lens') systems, which we publish alongside a further 9010 grade C ('possible lens') candidates. With more high-confidence candidates than all previous catalogues combined, this sample establishes a large, homogeneous data set of galaxy-scale strong lenses with high-resolution imaging, transforming strong lensing from a field of rare discoveries to a robust statistical tool for astrophysics and cosmology. Of the roughly 900 million sources in DR1, we searched the 68 million passing our catalogue selection using a bespoke discovery pipeline that synthesises scores from five machine-learning models with visual classifications from around 7000 citizen scientists and 67 strong lensing experts. Beyond classical Einstein rings, our catalogue includes 84 candidate double-source-plane lenses and over 1000 candidate disc lenses, two exotic classes with few previously known examples, as well as several hundred candidate lensed AGN and quasars. We deliver automated lens models for the combined grade A+B systems, and photometric redshifts and stellar population properties for the deflector galaxies, providing immediate science-ready data products. We find that the majority of the deflector galaxies are massive and quiescent, with stellar masses of $\log_{10}(M_\ast /M_\odot) = 11.39^{+0.29}_{-0.33}$ (Salpeter initial mass function; 16th-84th percentile range). The deflector galaxies span a range of photometric redshifts ($z=0.68 ^{+0.33}_{-0.29}$), extending to higher redshifts than previous ground-based lens samples, and the lenses have a typical Einstein radius of $0.85 ^{+0.44}_{-0.24}$ arcsec. Extrapolating by area, we expect over 100 000 high-confidence strong lens candidates in the Euclid Wide Survey.

</details>


### [173] [A statistical study of ionized outflows in low-mass galaxies from DESI DR1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15884)
*Michael Romano,Amélie Saintonge,Chiara Circosta,Claudia Cicone,Darko Donevski,Dirk Scholte,Bendix Hagedorn*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI DR1数据，对z<0.45的低质量星系中电离气体外流进行了统计分析，发现其质量载荷因子η远低于宇宙学模拟预测值，表明电离相仅占总外流质量的一小部分；外流速度足以富集星系周介质，但逃逸至星际介质主要发生在复合型和AGN宿主星系中；η随恒星质量减小而增大，并与比恒星形成率正相关，说明恒星形成增强和爆发性调控反馈效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解低质量星系中电离气体外流的性质及其在星系反馈和重子循环中的作用，检验宇宙学模拟对质量载荷因子等关键参数的预测。

Method: 基于DESI DR1数据，利用[OIII]和Hα发射线识别约5800个具有强电离外流特征的矮星系，统计分析其质量载荷因子、外流速度、恒星质量、星系分类（恒星形成、复合、AGN）及与主序关系等，并与模拟结果和堆叠谱对比。

Result: 识别出~5800个低质量星系电离外流；质量载荷因子η=0.001–1，中位数~0.01，比模拟低1–2 dex；>80%样本外流可富集CGM，但IGM逃逸主要见于复合/AGN星系；η随恒星质量减小而增大，且与sSFR正相关；堆叠谱给出的η系统性偏低。

Conclusion: 电离外流是低质量星系重子循环的重要组成部分，但仅代表总外流的一小部分；反馈效率受恒星形成增强与爆发性调控；需开展多相（中性、分子、电离）外流联合研究以全面理解星系演化中的反馈过程。

Abstract: We present a statistical analysis of ionized gas outflows in a large sample of low-mass ($\mathrm{log(M_{*}/M_{\odot})\lesssim10}$) galaxies at $z<0.45$ from the first data release of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI DR1). Using [OIII] and H$α$ emission lines, we identify $\sim5800$ dwarf galaxies with robust signatures of ionized outflows. Most are star-forming systems above the star-forming main sequence, with a smaller fraction of composites and AGN hosts. The derived mass-loading factors span $η=0.001-1$, with a median $η\sim0.01$, consistent with previous studies at similar stellar masses. These values are $1$--$2$ dex lower than predicted by cosmological simulations, suggesting that the ionized phase traces only a minor fraction of the total outflow mass. Outflow velocities are sufficient to enrich the circumgalactic medium in $\gtrsim80\%$ of cases, while escape into the intergalactic medium appears efficient mainly in composite and AGN hosts. We find that mass-loading factors decrease with stellar mass, broadly reproducing simulated trends despite the normalization offset. Comparing individual detections with stacked spectra of the general low-mass star-forming population, we find systematically lower mass-loading factors in the stacks, although the difference decreases toward larger offsets above the star-forming main sequence. Together with a positive correlation between mass-loading factor and specific star-formation rate, this suggests that enhanced star formation and burstiness regulate feedback efficiency and shape galactic winds. Our results highlight the role of ionized outflows in the baryon cycle of low-mass galaxies and the need for multiphase studies to fully understand feedback in galaxy evolution.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种多阶段方法，用于在欧几里得数据发布1（DR1）中识别15000个高质量星系-星系强引力透镜候选体，结合了自动神经网络分类、公民科学家参与和专家评审，并评估了该流程的完备性与主观性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于强引力透镜系统本身极为稀有，而Euclid DR1数据量庞大，亟需一种高效、可靠且可扩展的搜寻策略，在保证高完备率的同时降低人工审查负担。

Method: 采用四阶段联合搜寻法：1）星表级初筛；2）基于机器学习与集成模型的自动识别；3）两阶段公民科学项目（Zooniverse平台）；4）强透镜专家分级评审；全程依托神经网络对6800万天体自动分类、公民科学家对30万天体完成超450万次标记、专家复核超3万天体。

Result: 共识别出15000个高置信度强透镜候选体；完备率分别为0.73_{-0.15}^{+0.08}（A级，'确定'）和0.34_{-0.06}^{+0.05}（A+B级，'确定+可能'）；揭示专家判读存在主观性，通过校准与ECLIPSE-C透镜建模可提升判定可靠性。

Conclusion: 该多阶段流水线显著提升了大规模巡天中强透镜搜寻的效率与可靠性，为未来Euclid及其他巡天项目（如LSST）提供了可复用的方法论框架；同时强调人机协同与建模验证在天文发现中的关键作用。

Abstract: We present the method used to identify the $15\,000$ high-grade galaxy-galaxy strong lens candidates in Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1). Given that lenses are intrinsically rare systems and the DR1 data set is vast, this lens search was conducted in a number of stages: a catalogue-level cut; a machine-learning and ensemble search; a two-stage citizen-science search, and finally grading of lens candidates by strong lens researchers. In this paper, we describe each search stage and their respective results. This search was enabled by the automated classification of over 68 million objects by neural networks, more than 4.5 million classifications of $300\,000$ objects by citizen-scientist volunteers, and the inspection of over $30\,000$ objects by strong lens researchers. We find this multi-stage approach helps maximise the number of lenses identified, while minimising inspection requirements of both citizen scientists and strong lens researchers. We measure a completeness in our comprehensive search of $0.73_{-0.15}^{+0.08}$ to grade A ('definite') systems and $0.34_{-0.06}^{+0.05}$ to grade A+B ('definite' + 'probable') systems. Our search therefore identifies the majority of the definitive strong lens systems in the DR1 area with the remainder likely to be fainter or more ambiguous cases. We find that researcher grading remains subjective; in this work we implement calibration to account for biases of individual inspectors and highlight the benefit of lens modelling (ECLIPSE-C) in determining the nature of a lens candidate. This paper forms part of the ECLIPSE series, here focussing on the discovery pipeline used to identify lens candidates in Euclid DR1; an overview of the lenses discovered is presented in ECLIPSE-A.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了使用PyAutoLens-JAX对欧几里得DR1中15072个强透镜候选体进行自动化建模的工作，是迄今规模最大的强透镜建模样本；通过多高斯展开、像素化和Sérsic三种源光分布模型拟合，并结合等温椭球质量模型与外部剪切，实现了高质量的透镜光减除、自适应Delaunay源重建与去混淆测光；经专家人工评估，6073个A级（确信）候选体中67.5%被判定为成功建模，结果已公开发布于ECLIPSE星表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史上的星系尺度强透镜研究受限于数百个样本，且自动化建模方法难以扩展；亟需可扩展的自动化流程以支撑欧几里得等新一代巡天产生的海量强透镜候选体分析。

Method: 采用PyAutoLens-JAX框架，对15072个Euclid DR1强透镜候选体进行三类源模型（MGE、像素化、Sérsic）拟合，质量模型为含外部剪切的等温椭球；辅以透镜光减除、自适应Delaunay源重建与去混淆测光；所有模型由20余位专家目视评估并按多数（≥75%）共识分类。

Result: 在6073个A级候选体中，4098个（67.5%）被判定为成功建模，中位爱因斯坦半径0.85角秒，中位I_E波段总放大率8.1；8999个B级候选体建模成功率降至43.1%；全部成功模型、推断参数及放大源重建已通过ECLIPSE星表公开。

Conclusion: 本工作首次实现万量级强透镜的自动化建模与质量评估，验证了该流程在Euclid尺度上的可行性，为群体强透镜科学（如暗物质分布统计、宇宙学约束）奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Galaxy-scale strong lenses provide precise measurements of galaxy mass distributions, magnified views of distant galaxies, and a competitive cosmological probe. Historically, such studies have been limited to hundreds of lenses, with automated modelling methods proving difficult to scale. We present automated lens modelling of 15 072 strong-lens candidates from Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1), over an order of magnitude larger than any previously modelled sample. Using PyAutoLens-JAX, we perform three fits with multi-Gaussian expansion (MGE), pixelised, and Sérsic representations of the source, adopting an isothermal ellipsoid mass model with external shear. These fits provide foreground lens-light subtraction, adaptive Delaunay source reconstructions, and deblended photometry. More than 20 expert inspectors visually assess the resulting models, and each model is assigned the classification on which at least 75% of its inspectors agree. Of the 6073 grade A (secure) lens candidates, inspectors classify 4098 (67.5%) as successfully modelled, 580 (9.6%) as failures, 317 (5.2%) as multi-galaxy lenses, 15 (0.2%) as double-source-plane lenses, and 7 (0.1%) as unsure if lens, while 1032 (17.0%) reach no consensus and 24 (0.4%) have incomplete models. For the 8999 grade B (probable) candidates, the modelling success rate falls to 43.1%, primarily reflecting an increase in cases without consensus (37.6%), rather than a comparable increase in agreed failures (12.9%). Successfully modelled grade A and B systems have a median effective Einstein radius of 0.85 arcsec and a median total $I_E$-band source magnification of 8.1. We publicly release the successful lens models, their inferred properties, and magnified-source reconstructions through the ECLIPSE catalogue, demonstrating automated modelling at Euclid sample sizes and enabling population-level strong-lensing science.

</details>


### [176] [Where Do Population~III Stars Form? Unveiling Late PopIII-Forming Environments with CampFIRE-6k](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15915)
*Elizabeth Bryce,Alessandra Venditti,Jenna Samuel,Volker Bromm,Julian B. Muñoz*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用CampFIRE-6k宇宙学缩放模拟，研究了第三星族（PopIII）恒星在红移z∼6之前的形成历史与环境，发现其形成率近似恒定，并可在较晚时期的原子冷却晕中持续发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管PopIII恒星被认为是宇宙早期金属零丰度下的第一代恒星，但其是否及如何在再电离时期（z∼6–10）持续低效形成仍存在显著不确定性，尤其受限于宇宙金属增丰的不均匀性。

Method: 采用高分辨率宇宙学zoom-in模拟——CampFIRE-6k，追踪从宇宙黎明（z≳15）至再电离末期（z∼6）的PopIII恒星形成历史及其宿主暗晕环境（包括质量、冷却机制等）。

Result: PopIII恒星在z∼6前以近乎恒定速率（∼10⁻³·⁵ M⊙ yr⁻¹ cMpc⁻³）持续形成；部分形成于晚期原子冷却晕（M_halo ∼10⁷–10⁸ M⊙），突破传统仅限于微晕（10⁵–10⁶ M⊙）的认知。

Conclusion: PopIII恒星并非在宇宙黎明后迅速熄灭，而可能在再电离时期持续低产率形成，尤其可发生在更大质量、原子冷却主导的暗晕中，表明其形成环境比以往认为的更广泛且持久。

Abstract: Population III (PopIII) stars are the first generation of metal-free stars, thought to form in minihalos ($M_\mathrm{halo} \sim10^5-10^6\,\mathrm{M_{\odot}}$) at Cosmic Dawn ($z\gtrsim15$) before largely giving way to Population II stars formed from the newly enriched medium. Simulations show, however, that PopIII stars may continue forming at low rates through the Epoch of Reionization (EoR, $z \sim 6 - 10$), due to the inhomogeneous enrichment of the Universe. Nonetheless, significant uncertainty remains on the rates and typical environments of late PopIII formation. We use the CampFIRE-6k cosmological zoom-in simulation to examine PopIII star formation history and environments through $z\sim6$. We find that PopIII stars form at a near constant rate ($\sim 10^{-3.5} ~\mathrm{M_{\odot}\,yr^{-1}\,cMpc ^{-3}}$) through $z=6$, including within atomic-cooling halos ($M_\mathrm{halo}\sim 10^7-10^8\,\mathrm{M_\odot}$) at late times.

</details>


### [177] [Anatomy of LyC Escape in MEGATRON: Connecting Feedback Physics to Demographics, Leakage Timescales, and Explorations of ISM Density as a Tracer of Escape Fraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15931)
*Nicholas Choustikov,Harley Katz,Taysun Kimm,Matthew J. Hayes,Aaron Smith,Crystal L. Martin,Shyam H. Menon,Larrance Xing,Joki Rosdahl,John Chisholm,Aayush Saxena,Martin P. Rey,Adrianne Slyz,Julien Devriendt,Alex J. Cameron,Anatole Storck,Corentin Cadiou*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用MEGATRON高分辨率辐射-化学模拟，研究了反馈物理如何调控高红移星系的Lyman连续谱逃逸分数（f_esc）及其对宇宙再电离的影响；发现反馈通过调节恒星爆发与LyC逃逸的时间延迟，导致不同模型产生差异显著的再电离历史；UV暗弱星系的f_esc演化遵循统一的ISM结构序列；[O II]双线推断的电子密度可作为f_esc的有效诊断指标，而传统UV斜率标定在单个年轻星系上会严重高估电离光子产率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确约束高红移星系的Lyman连续谱逃逸分数（f_esc）对理解JWST观测到的星系群体如何驱动宇宙再电离至关重要。

Method: 采用包含详细辐射传输与化学网络、并尝试多种恒星反馈模型的MEGATRON zoom-in数值模拟。

Result: 反馈物理同时调控UV源数、f_esc和逃逸电离光子产率（\dot{N}_{ion,esc}），造成不同的再电离历史；f_esc演化与ISM结构存在普适序列；[O II]双线推断的电子密度是f_esc的有力诊断工具，且能反映其早期演化；UV斜率标定在单个年轻星系上会高估\dot{N}_{ion,esc}达两个数量级。

Conclusion: LyC逃逸具有延迟的占空比特性，在将UV源计数用于再电离建模时必须考虑该延迟效应；f_esc不能仅由UV光度或斜率决定，需结合ISM物理（如电子密度）进行多维约束。

Abstract: Constraining the Lyman Continuum (LyC) escape fraction ($f_{\rm esc}$) of high-redshift galaxies is essential to connect the abundant populations revealed by \textit{JWST} to cosmic reionization. We investigate this interplay using {\small MEGATRON}, a suite of zoom simulations with detailed radiation/chemistry and varied feedback prescriptions. We find that feedback physics simultaneously regulates UV source counts, LyC escape fractions, and escaping ionizing luminosities ($\dot{N}_{\rm ion,esc}$), producing different reionization histories. These differences are governed by the delay between a starburst and LyC escape: efficient early feedback and lower-density environments enable photons to escape while pre-supernovae stellar populations remain strongly ionizing. Consequently, models that produce fewer UV-bright galaxies yield earlier LyC escape and reionize sooner than models with more abundant UV sources and delayed LyC emission. Furthermore, UV-faint galaxies follow a common physical sequence in ISM structure with increasing $f_{\rm esc}$ irrespective of feedback physics: stronger leakers ($1\%\leq f_{\rm esc}\leq 50\%$) exhibit evidence of strong photoionization and SNe feedback alongside post-starburst star formation histories, while extreme leakers ($f_{\rm esc}\geq 50\%$) have also cleared their cold/dense ISM. We find that electron densities inferred from the $\rm [O\,{II}]\,λ\lambda3727$ doublet are a candidate diagnostic of $f_{\rm esc}$, with lower densities around $\rm H~{\small II}$ regions selecting stronger leakers. Moreover, this density can trace the early evolution of $f_{\rm esc}$, whereas UV slope-based calibrations over-predict $\dot{N}_{\rm ion,esc}$ for individual young galaxies by up to two orders of magnitude. Overall, the delayed duty cycle of LyC leakage must be accounted for when UV source counts are interpreted in the context of reionization.

</details>


### [178] [Mass Loss Rates of Evolved Stars in Isolated Dwarf Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15948)
*Narges Rostami,Hamidreza Mahani*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用SEDust自动拟合谱能量分布（SED）的方法，估算了孤立矮不规则星系NGC 6822中渐近巨星支（AGB）恒星的总质量损失率和尘埃注入率，并发现仅靠AGB恒星无法解释该星系当前观测到的尘埃总量，需引入星际介质中的尘埃增长等额外机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解矮星系NGC 6822中尘埃起源，检验AGB恒星是否足以贡献其观测到的尘埃质量。

Method: 使用SEDust自动化流程拟合恒星谱能量分布（SED），推导AGB恒星的质量损失率与尘埃产生率，并结合气体-尘埃比换算尘埃注入率，再外推至10 Gyr并与Herschel观测的尘埃质量比较。

Result: AGB恒星总质量损失率为(3.7±1.1)×10⁻³ M⊙/yr，对应尘埃注入率2.0×10⁻⁵ M⊙/yr；10 Gyr累积尘埃约2×10⁵ M⊙，低于Herschel观测值2.7×10⁵ M⊙。

Conclusion: NGC 6822中当前尘埃储量不能单由AGB恒星提供，需其他来源（如星际介质中尘埃增长）补充。

Abstract: We derive the mass-loss rates of asymptotic giant branch (AGB) stars in the isolated dwarf irregular galaxy NGC 6822 using SEDust, an automated pipeline that fits spectral energy distributions (SEDs) to estimate stellar mass-loss and dust-production rates. The total mass-loss rate of the AGB population is $(3.7 \pm 1.1) \times 10^{-3} M_\odot\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$. For a gas-to-dust ratio of 200, this corresponds to a dust injection rate of $2.0 \times 10^{-5} M_\odot\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$. If sustained over 10 Gyr, this rate would yield approximately $2 \times 10^{5} M_\odot$ of dust, which is lower than the dust mass of $2.7 \times 10^{5} M_\odot$ estimated from Herschel observations. AGB stars alone therefore cannot account for the present-day dust reservoir of NGC 6822, and additional sources or processes, such as dust growth in the interstellar medium, are required.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用欧几里得巡天第一期数据，通过等龄线滤波技术，在10 kpc至数Mpc距离范围内发现了15个此前未分辨出恒星的天体系统，其中包括5个新发现（3个在银河系晕内、2个在本星系群外），并测量了它们的距离模、半光半径、椭率、位置角、绝对星等和恒星质量等参数，展示了欧几里得望远镜在低表面亮度天体研究中的革命性能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索欧几里得宽视场巡天数据在分辨遥远低表面亮度恒星系统方面的潜力，填补银河系晕至本星系团外区域中矮星系和超微弱天体的观测空白。

Method: 采用等龄线滤波技术，在欧几里得第一期宽巡天约1900平方度数据中搜寻距离10 kpc至数Mpc的已分辨恒星系统。

Result: 共发现15个此前未被分辨为恒星系统的天体：5个全新发现（含3个银河系晕内、2个本星系群外）；其余10个为先前仅作为点源记录但缺乏可靠距离的矮星系；测得了全部15个系统的距离模，以及其中14个的半光半径、椭率、位置角、绝对V波段星等和恒星质量；识别出3个表面亮度μ_V ~ 28 mag/arcsec²的极暗矮星系，以及10个可能属于M81星系群的候选成员。

Conclusion: 欧几里得巡天具备在统一、均质数据下系统研究从银河系晕到本星系团外广阔环境中的低表面亮度天体的能力，极大拓展了对最暗弱恒星系统的认知边界。

Abstract: We searched within the first data release of the Euclid Wide Survey (~1900 deg^2) for resolved stellar systems between distances from 10 kpc and several Mpc, using an isochrone filtering technique. We detect 15 systems that have never been resolved into stars before. Five are new discoveries, three in the Milky Way halo and two beyond the Local Group. The remaining ten are dwarf galaxies that were previously catalogued as unresolved sources in shallower ground-based surveys and lack reliable distance measures. We measured distance moduli for all 15 systems, and half-light radii, ellipticities, position angles, absolute magnitudes, and stellar masses for 14 of them. In the Milky Way halo, Ursa Minor II, at ~20 kpc, has Mv ~ +2.0 and a stellar mass of ~40 Msun, and is one of the least luminous stellar systems known. Fornax 7 is located near the tidal limit of the Fornax dwarf spheroidal galaxy and may be physically associated with it. Eridanus V lies at ~165 kpc and has a stellar mass of ~550 Msun, which places it in the region of the size-luminosity plane where globular clusters and ultra-faint dwarf galaxies overlap. Beyond the Local Group, the 12 dwarf galaxies lie at distances of 3--5.5 Mpc. Three of them reach or exceed a mean surface brightness within the half-light radius of mu_V~28 mag/arcsec^2, a regime in which almost no galaxies at these distances were previously known. Ten of them are candidate members of the M81 group: eight have three-dimensional distances from M81 smaller than or comparable to the radius of its zero-velocity surface, and two lie close to the peripheral member NGC 4236, one of which is a candidate satellite of this galaxy. These results demonstrate Euclid's transformative potential for low-surface-brightness science, enabling the study of an unprecedented range of environments from the MW halo out to the Local Volume within a single, homogeneous survey.

</details>

Renzi,J. Rhodes,G. Riccio,E. Romelli,M. Roncarelli,B. Rusholme,R. Saglia,Z. Sakr,D. Sapone,B. Sartoris,P. Schneider,T. Schrabback,M. Scodeggio,A. Secroun,E. Sihvola,P. Simon,C. Sirignano,G. Sirri,L. Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用欧几里得数据释放1的大量星系光学图像，通过贝叶斯推断（结合哈密顿蒙特卡洛采样）首次在大样本尺度上约束了数千万个恒星形成星系的三维椭率、三轴性及尺寸等本征形状参数，并验证了低质量星系中长椭球比例随红移减小这一演化趋势的真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系内在三维形状对研究其形成与演化至关重要，但仅从二维投影图像反推三维结构极具挑战；此前受限于样本量小，统计精度和演化分析能力不足。

Method: 采用贝叶斯推断框架，结合哈密顿蒙特卡洛（HMC）采样，对欧几里得DR1中4086万多个星系的Sérsic轴比和有效半径进行建模，推断群体层面的平均三维椭率、三轴性、尺寸及其协方差。

Result: 获得了迄今最大样本（超四千万）的星系三维形状统计结果：发现所有质量-红移区间内平均椭率高达0.600–0.821；确认低质量星系（10⁹–10⁹·⁵ M⊙）中长椭球比例随红移z→0而下降的趋势，并在多套独立数据中复现，排除小样本统计假象；同时覆盖z<0.5的以往样本稀缺区域。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了星系三维形状演化特征（如低质量星系长椭球比例下降）具有真实性，非观测或统计 artefact；验证了贝叶斯-HMC方法在大规模巡天数据中的可扩展性与鲁棒性，为后续欧几里得数据释放提供了方法论基础与改进方向。

Abstract: Understanding the intrinsic 3D shapes of galaxies provides crucial insights into their formation and evolution, but determining these from projections remains challenging. We apply a Bayesian inference framework to optical images of galaxies from the Euclid Data Release 1, leveraging its large sample size to test the scalability and robustness of this methodology. Applying Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) sampling to Sérsic axis ratios and effective radii, we constrain the population-level mean 3D ellipticity, triaxiality, and size, together with their covariances for $40\,864\,304$ star-forming galaxies with stellar masses $M_\ast=10^{9}$--$10^{10.5}M_\odot$ at redshifts $0<z<1.2$. Our sample size exceeds previous shape inference studies by factors of $440$--$52\,800$ relative to early observations by JWST, and factors of $440$--$17\,600$ relative to legacy observations from HST in overlapping mass-redshift bins, enabling finer binning alongside improved statistical constraints on shape evolution. We additionally include over 9 million galaxies at $z<0.5$, a regime historically limited by sample size in previous shape inference studies. We recover key evolutionary trends identified in previous smaller-sample studies. We find high ellipticities ($μ_E \sim 0.600$--$0.821$) across all bins, indicating that the underlying 3D shape distributions are statistically dominated by flattened geometries. Most notably, we recover the decline of the prolate fraction towards $z=0$ at low masses ($M_\ast=10^{9}$--$10^{9.5}M_\odot$) reported by previous studies. This trend is seen consistently in comparable analyses of independent data sets spanning different depths and resolution regimes, showing that it is not an artefact of small-sample statistics, suggesting it may be a genuine feature of galaxy evolution. We also identify future methodological improvements for subsequent Euclid data releases.

</details>


### [181] [MeerKAT and ASKAP EMU new radio-continuum observations of Wolf-Rayet nebula PCG 11 (Cohen's Ring)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16134)
*M. D. Filipović,Z. J. Smeaton,A. C. Bradley,R. Z. E. Alsaberi,H. Sano,L. Barnes,C. Buemi,C. Bordiu,A. Brunetti,F. Bufano,F. Cavallaro,E. J. Crawford,Y. Fukui,A. J. Green,A. M. Hopkins,A. Ingallinera,R. Kothes,S. Lazarević,D. Leahy,S. Loru,K. Luken,M. Mallows,Q. A. Parker,J. L. Payne,G. Rowell,M. Stupar,N. F. H. Tothill,K. Tsuge,G. M. Umana,D. Urošević,S. F. ur Rahman,J. Th. van Loon*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用ASKAP、ATCA和MeerKAT射电观测数据，研究了Wolf-Rayet星云Cohen's Ring的射电连续谱特性，确认其热辐射起源，测定其物理尺寸、年龄及与分子气体的关联，为研究大质量恒星反馈对分子云的长期影响提供了重要样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cohen's Ring是少数与分子气体相关联的WR环状星云之一，研究其射电特性及与分子气体的关系，有助于理解大质量恒星反馈对周围分子云的长期影响。

Method: 结合ASKAP EMU、ATCA和MeerKAT的射电连续谱观测，测量其流量密度、角尺寸和光谱指数；结合NANTEN CO观测，分析分子气体分布与恒星风反馈的时间尺度关系；利用已知距离和典型膨胀速度估算星云年龄。

Result: 测得Cohen's Ring在1000 MHz流量密度为62±2 mJy，角尺寸54″×45″，对应线尺度1.03×0.86 pc；光谱指数α=0.06±0.02，表明热辐射主导；估算年龄约13500年；CO观测显示其形成于较近的质量损失事件，而分子气体反映约1.5 Myr的累积反馈效应。

Conclusion: Cohen's Ring的射电特性证实其热辐射本质，其与分子气体的关联使其成为研究大质量恒星长期反馈效应的关键天体实验室。

Abstract: We present new radio-continuum observations of the well-known Wolf-Rayet (WR) nebula PCG 11 (Cohen's Ring; named after and in honour of the co-author on the discovery paper, Martin Cohen) using observations from the Australian Square Kilometre Array Pathfinder (ASKAP) Evolutionary Map of the Universe (EMU), Australia Telescope Compact Array (ATCA) and MeerKAT surveys. We find that the elliptical shell surrounding Cohen's Ring has a flux density of 62$\pm$2\,mJy at 1000 MHz. The angular size of the elliptical emission is measured to be 54\arcsec$\times$45\arcsec\ (at P.A.=69.4\D), which agrees well with observations of the source in H$α$ and at 24\,$μ$m in the infrared. Using the measured angular size and a distance of 3.94$\pm$0.27\,kpc, we determine that the nebula has a linear size of $1.03(\pm0.07)\times0.86(\pm0.06)$\,pc. Together with our new and previously measured flux density estimates, we determine a spectral index for Cohen's Ring to be $α$=0.06$\pm$0.02, indicating that the emission from the WR nebula is of thermal origin. Based on a typical average expansion velocity of 75~km~s$^{-1}$ for a WR star with a Red Supergiant (RSG) progenitor, we estimate the age of Cohen's Ring to be $\sim$ 13500 years. Our NANTEN telescope CO observations suggest that Cohen's Ring was formed during a relatively recent mass-loss event, while the associated CO-emitting molecular gas reflects the cumulative effects of stellar-wind feedback over a much longer timescale ($\sim$1.5\,Myr). As one of the few WR ring nebulae with linked molecular gas, Cohen's Ring provides a valuable laboratory for studying the long-term impact of massive-star feedback on surrounding molecular clouds.

</details>


### [182] [Globular Clusters in the Time of JWST. II. Looks like the second, moves like the first: the syrphid-like intermediate population of NGC 2808](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16150)
*G. Cordoni,A. P. Milone,E. Dondoglio,A. F. Marino,F. D'Antona,A. L. Varri,V. Altomonte,P. Bianchini,E. Bortolan,L. Casagrande,Y. Cavecchi,G. S. Da Costa,F. Dell'Agli,H. Jerjen,E. P. Lagioia,M. V. Legnardi,C. Li,A. Mastrobuono-Battisti,F. Muratore,A. Renzini,M. Tailo,P. Ventura,T. Ziliotto*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST和HST数据，首次详细刻画了球状星团NGC 2808中四个恒星种群（1P、2Pa、2Pb、2Pc）的动力学特性，发现不同化学丰度的第二代种群具有显著不同的运动学特征，表明其形成条件或后续演化历史存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球状星团中多个恒星种群的当前运动学信息可能保留其初始形成条件的痕迹，有助于理解多星族的形成机制。

Method: 结合JWST与HST的测光与自行数据，分析NGC 2808中四个恒星种群（1P、2Pa、2Pb、2Pc）在约八个半光半径范围内的内部动力学，重点比较其径向与切向速度弥散及各向异性。

Result: 发现2Pb和2Pc比1P和2Pa具有更低的切向速度弥散且强径向各向异性，而2Pa与1P动力学相似；外区速度弥散对恒星质量依赖弱，存在倒置动能均分迹象；不同2P子组动力学不统一。

Conclusion: 第二代恒星并非动力学同质整体，其子群运动学差异为多星族形成模型提供了关键约束。

Abstract: The present-day kinematics of multiple populations in globular clusters can retain information about their initial conditions and can therefore help trace how the different populations formed. We combine JWST and HST photometry and proper motions to study the internal dynamics of four populations in NGC 2808 out to about eight half-light radii: the first population (1P) and three second-population groups with increasing light-element processing (2Pa, 2Pb, and 2Pc), providing the first detailed kinematic characterisation of the chemically intermediate 2Pa. We find significant kinematic differences between the populations. While all four have similar sky-projected radial velocity dispersions, 2Pb and 2Pc have lower tangential dispersions than 1P and are strongly radially anisotropic, whereas 2Pa shares the only mildly anisotropic kinematics of 1P. Therefore, chemically distinct 2P groups have different dynamical properties, suggesting that they formed under different conditions and/or experienced a different subsequent dynamical evolution. In the outer JWST field, the velocity dispersion depends only weakly on stellar mass, with hints of inverted equipartition. The difference between radial and tangential equipartition is similar for 1P and 2Pa and decreases towards 2Pc, a trend that remains tentative given the uncertainties. Our results show that 2P is not a dynamically homogeneous group, and that the kinematics of its individual groups provide a key constraint for multiple population formation scenarios.

</details>


### [183] [Machine-Learning Reconstruction of Intergalactic Medium Column-Density Maps from Observed Ly$α$ Emission Cubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16286)
*Xihan Deng,Christopher Martin,Aaron Smith,Sultan Hassan,Drew M. Miles,Nikolaus Z. Prusinski,Vincent Picouet,Mateusz Matuszewski,Zeren Lin,Jake S. Bennett*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于卷积神经网络（CDE）的机器学习框架，用于从低信噪比、受共振散射影响的Lyα积分视场观测数据中重建星系际介质（IGM）的气体柱密度分布图，并在Keck KCWI对z~2.3原星团的观测中验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在z~2–6红移区间，冷宇宙网丝状结构是星系生长的主要供料机制，但Lyα发射观测受限于低信噪比和共振散射复杂性，亟需新方法从噪声数据中提取IGM结构信息。

Method: 构建名为Column Density Estimator（CDE）的定制化卷积神经网络，训练数据来自结合IllustrisTNG100模拟、简化辐射转移计算及KCWI噪声建模的合成Lyα IFU数据立方体。

Result: CDE在模拟测试集上成功重建出大尺度丝状结构；应用于实际KCWI观测数据时，预测出延伸超40角秒（约1 Mpc）的柱密度丝状结构，与IGM解释一致。

Conclusion: CDE框架展示了将弥散Lyα观测与IGM数值模拟系统连接的潜力，但当前结果依赖于所采用的物理与仪器模型假设。

Abstract: Understanding the large-scale structure of the intergalactic medium (IGM) is essential for studying galaxy evolution, particularly at $z \sim 2$--$6$, when cold filaments in the cosmic web are considered a primary mechanism for fueling galaxy growth. At these redshifts, Ly$α$ emission shifts into the optical regime and serves as the primary tracer of diffuse IGM for ground-based integral field units (IFUs). However, detections remain limited by low signal-to-noise ratios (S/N) and the complexities of resonant scattering. We present a proof-of-concept machine-learning framework to reconstruct IGM gas column-density maps from noisy Ly$α$ IFU emission cubes. Using Keck Cosmic Web Imager (KCWI) observations of $z \sim 2.3$ protoclusters as a case study, we train a custom convolutional neural network (CNN), the Column Density Estimator (CDE), on a simulated dataset combining IllustrisTNG100 with simplified radiative-transfer calculations and an approximate treatment of KCWI noise. The CDE successfully reconstructs broad filamentary structures in the simulated test set under these adopted assumptions. Applied to observed KCWI data, the CDE predicts gas column-density maps containing filamentary structures extending over $\gtrsim 40''$ ($\sim 1$ comoving Mpc), consistent with an IGM interpretation. The observed reconstructions remain conditional on the adopted models. These results illustrate the potential of the CDE framework for connecting diffuse Ly$α$ observations with simulations of the IGM.

</details>


### [184] [B-fields And Dust in InterstelLar FiLAments Using Dust POLarization (BALLAD-POL): V. Magnetic Fields and Grain Alignment Mechanisms in Serpens Main Using JCMT/POL-2 Polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16385)
*Sadhana Singh,Thiem Hoang,Pham Ngoc Diep,Nguyen Bich Ngoc,Woojin Kwon*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JCMT/POL-2对蛇夫座主区的850微米尘埃偏振观测，研究磁场强度、质量通量比和阿尔文马赫数，发现磁场虽主导湍流但弱于引力；通过分析偏振度随辐射强度、气体柱密度、尘埃温度及偏振角弥散函数的变化，指出致密区中尘埃颗粒取向效率下降（而非磁场缠绕）是去偏振主因，并在辐射转矩（RAT）理论框架下验证了该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解恒星形成区中尘埃偏振的物理起源，特别是去偏振现象的主导机制（磁场缠绕 vs. 颗粒取向效率降低），并检验辐射转矩（RAT）理论在真实致密环境中的适用性。

Method: 基于JCMT/POL-2的850 μm尘埃偏振数据，推导磁场强度图，计算质量通量比与阿尔文马赫数；拟合Ricean均值模型获得偏振度P随亮度I的斜率α；分析P及P×S（取向效率示踪）与气体柱密度N(H₂)、尘埃温度Td、偏振角弥散S的关系；结合观测参数计算RAT理论中的关键量：最小对齐粒径a_align与磁松弛强度δ_mag,sp。

Result: 发现P和P×S均随N(H₂)增加而下降，且与S无相关性，排除磁场缠绕主导去偏振；P和P×S均随a_align增大而减小，支持致密区颗粒对齐减弱是主因；但P和P×S均不随δ_mag,sp变化，表明磁松弛增强不足以抵消a_align增大导致的对齐减弱。

Conclusion: 在蛇夫座主区，去偏振主要源于高密度环境下尘埃颗粒对齐效率下降，而非磁场结构复杂化；RAT理论中粒径依赖的对齐阈值起主导作用，而磁松弛效应在此区域贡献有限。

Abstract: We investigate magnetic fields ($B$-field) and grain alignment mechanisms in Serpens Main using 850 $μ$m dust polarization observations from JCMT/POL-2. We derive a map of $B$-field strength and calculate the mass-to-flux ratio and Alfvénic Mach number, suggesting the subdominance of $B$-fields over gravity but their dominance over turbulence. Fitting the Ricean mean model to the nondebiased polarization fraction ($P$) as a function of emission intensity ($I$) yields a slope $α= 0.68\pm0.01$, revealing depolarization. To understand its origins, we examine the dependence of $P$ on gas column density ($N({\rm H}_{2})$), dust temperature ($T_{\rm d}$), and polarization angle dispersion function ($S$). We find no correlation between $P$ and $S$, whereas for $P\times S$, an observational proxy for alignment efficiency decreases with increasing $N({\rm H}_{2})$. This suggests that $B$-field tangling is not the primary cause of depolarization and instead points to reduced grain alignment in denser regions. To test this interpretation within radiative torque (RAT) alignment theory, we calculate the minimum size of aligned grains ($a_{\rm align}$) and magnetic relaxation strength ($δ_{\rm mag,sp}$) using $N({\rm H}_{2})$, $T_{\rm d}$, and our estimated $B$-field map. We find that both $P$ and $P \times S$ decrease with increasing $a_{\rm align}$, supporting reduced grain alignment as the main cause of depolarization in Serpens Main. However, neither $P$ nor $P \times S$ shows a correlation with $δ_{\rm mag,sp}$, suggesting that enhanced grain alignment due to magnetic relaxation is insufficient to compensate for the reduced alignment associated with increasing $a_{\rm align}$ toward denser regions.

</details>


### [185] [Mitigating variance shrinkage in multi-task galaxy property inference from imaging data via statistical manifold alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16598)
*Zhijian Luo,Jianzhen Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出ViTMultiTask模型，利用多尺度最大均值差异（MMD）损失函数，在再生核希尔伯特空间中对齐预测与真实分布的高阶联合分布，有效缓解深度神经网络回归中的方差收缩问题，提升多波段成像下星系光度红移及恒星种群参数推断的准确性与分布保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统点式损失函数（如MSE、Huber损失）在星系参数回归中导致方差收缩，扭曲物理分布（如双峰、重尾），难以满足LSST、CSST等大视场测光巡天对高保真分布建模的需求。

Method: 提出基于视觉Transformer的ViTMultiTask框架，引入多尺度最大均值差异（MMD）损失，在RKHS中实现预测与真实参数联合分布的高阶对齐；使用Subaru HSC五波段成像与DESI DR1光谱交叉匹配数据（83,846个星系）训练，并在5,000个独立测试样本上评估。

Result: 在测试集上达到高精度点估计：z的σ_NMAD=0.020，log(M*/M⊙)为0.169 dex，D_n4000为0.073，Hδ_A为1.185 Å；消融实验证明MMD损失显著改善分布保真度（恢复双峰与重尾），同时点估计精度（RMSE变化≤3.5%）和灾难性误差率与基线相当。

Conclusion: 基于核方法的流形对齐为深度测光时代下的星系综合属性推断提供了统计严谨且实用的新范式。

Abstract: Inferring photometric redshifts ($z$) and key stellar population properties ($\log(M_*/\mathrm{M}_\odot)$, $D_n4000$, and $\mathrm{H}δ_A$) directly from multi-band imaging is increasingly critical for spectroscopically limited large surveys such as LSST and CSST. However, conventional point-wise loss functions (e.g., mean squared error or Huber loss) employed in deep neural networks often induce ``variance shrinkage''---a regression-to-the-mean effect that contracts predicted variances, distorts non-linear physical manifolds (such as bimodal distributions), and truncates heavy-tail populations. Here, we present ViTMultiTask, a vision transformer framework that incorporates a multi-scale maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) loss to enforce higher-order joint distribution alignment in reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS). Trained on 83,846 galaxies from Subaru HSC five-band ($g,r,i,z,y$) imaging cross-matched with DESI Data Release 1 Bright Galaxy Survey spectra, the model achieves high point-estimate precision on an independent test set of 5,000 galaxies: $σ_{\mathrm{NMAD}} = 0.020$ for $z$, $0.169\,\mathrm{dex}$ for $\log(M_*/\mathrm{M}_\odot)$, $0.073$ for $D_n4000$, and $1.185\,\text{Å}$ for $\mathrm{H}δ_A$. Ablation experiments demonstrate that incorporating the MMD loss effectively mitigates variance shrinkage and more accurately reproduces the observed spectroscopic parameter distributions (including bimodal structures and heavy tails), while maintaining point-estimate accuracy statistically consistent with the baseline (RMSE changes $\le 3.5\%$) and catastrophic outlier rates fully comparable to the baseline. This kernel-based manifold alignment offers a statistically grounded and practical paradigm for comprehensive galaxy property inference in the deep imaging era.

</details>

Vielzeuf,S. Vinciguerra,M. von Wietersheim-Kramsta,N. A. Walton,L. Wang,J. R. Weaver,A. H. Wright*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了欧几里得任务第一数据发布（DR1）中非宇宙学科学数据产品的处理流程，使用NNPZ邻近算法基于COSMOS参考样本推断目标星系的光度红移及物理参数，并通过多源数据验证其高精度与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持欧几里得任务中非宇宙学科学研究，需提供高精度、自洽的星系分类、光度红移、物理参数和绝对星等数据产品。

Method: 采用NNPZ最近邻算法，在颜色-星等空间中匹配目标星系与COSMOS参考样本（基于COSMOS2020多波段测光导出红移和物理参数），并引入调整项和先验以推广至全天区。

Result: 生成了高质量、内部一致的非宇宙学科学数据产品；性能经内部指标及COSMOS2020、3D-HST、DESI等外部数据集和恒星形成主序等关系验证，符合预期和预发布模拟结果。

Conclusion: DR1非宇宙学科学数据产品可靠且适用于广泛天体物理研究，标志着欧几里得任务在星系演化等领域迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: Euclid is a European Space Agency mission mapping the extragalactic sky, aiming to provide the most-accurate 3D view of the Universe at optical and near-infrared wavelengths, by measuring the photometric redshifts of billions of galaxies. Within the Euclid Consortium, this task is executed by the PHZ Organisation Unit. PHZ takes as input the multi-wavelength photometry of Euclid and ground-based data, and returns as output the source classification, photometric redshifts for the Euclid core science (i.e., cosmology), and photometric redshifts plus physical parameters and absolute magnitudes for non-cosmological science. As part of the first Euclid Data Release (DR1), we describe the details of the DR1 pipeline for non-cosmological science, along with the released data products, and their validation. The non-cosmological science data products are obtained with NNPZ, a nearest-neighbour algorithm developed within PHZ that infers the properties of each target galaxy from its closest matches, in colour-magnitude space, within a reference sample of galaxies with known redshifts and physical parameters. We built this reference sample from Euclid observations of COSMOS, one of the Euclid Auxiliary Fields, labelling each galaxy with redshifts and physical parameters derived from the COSMOS2020 multi-wavelength photometry, consistently with the Euclid core-science results. Combined with the adjustments and priors required to generalise the results across the entire extragalactic sky, this approach yields high-quality and internally consistent data products. We tested the performance using both internal metrics and comparisons to ancillary data sets (e.g., COSMOS2020, 3D-HST, and DESI) and scaling relations (e.g., the star-forming main sequence); the results are in line with expectations and pre-DR1 simulations for both the Euclid Wide Survey and Euclid Deep Fields.

</details>


### [187] [Active Learning for Molecular Discovery: A Retrospective Test toward TMC-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16741)
*R. I. El-Kholy,Z. M. Hayman*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过回顾性测试验证了在星际分子搜寻中，基于机器学习推荐列表的动态重排序方法是否优于静态排序。结果表明，随新探测数据更新的逻辑回归重排序显著提升了检测效率（AUC 0.867 vs. 0.706），尤其在整体候选集中效果稳健，但在前100名中优势不显著；主动学习中的不确定性采样表现差，说明新探测分子更靠近已知化学空间；候选生成而非排序是当前主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在星际分子搜寻中，随着新探测数据积累而动态更新机器学习推荐列表的重排序策略是否比原始静态推荐更有效，此前该问题尚未被实证检验。

Method: 采用预注册的回顾性实验设计，以TMC-1为目标，使用已发布的1510个机器学习推荐候选（精简为1434个）和截至2026年9月报告的探测结果；在2022–2025四个时间点，基于当时已知探测结果，用7个特征拟合逻辑回归模型进行重排序，并评估AUC、检测效率及不确定性采样表现。

Result: 动态重排序平均AUC达0.867，显著优于最佳单准则基线（0.706，差异0.160）； searcher中位数仅需200次观测即可覆盖一半已探测物种（基线为332，随机搜索为717.5）；但不确定性采样AUC仅0.210，表现差于随机；前100名中重排序未显著提升检出数（12 vs. 7）；验证集中37/101物种未被原始列表覆盖，且多数未被重建池捕获。

Conclusion: 动态重排序可有效提升星际分子搜寻效率，但其收益受限于候选生成质量；当前瓶颈在于候选集覆盖度不足（尤其自由基与离子），而非排序算法本身；因此应优先改进候选生成机制，并开放100个新目标供前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Machine-learning recommendation lists can help prioritize searches for new interstellar molecules, but whether reranking such a list as detections accumulate improves on it has not been tested. We present a pre-registered retrospective test toward TMC-1, using a published machine-learning list of 1,510 recommended candidates, consolidated to 1,434 candidates, and the species reported there through September 2026. At four annual origins from 2022 to 2025, a logistic-regression ranking on seven released features, refit on the detections known at each origin, separates later detections from never-detected candidates with a pooled AUC of 0.867, against 0.706 for the best single-criterion baseline (difference 0.160; 95\% interval 0.095-0.230). The advantage persists under a shifted date boundary and two alternative feature regimes, but is not established among the top 100 candidates (12 versus 7 detections). A searcher that refits on its own observations reaches half of the 63 detected species on the list in a median of 200 observations, against 332 for the best fixed ordering and 717.5 expected for random search. Uncertainty sampling, the standard active-learning rule, performs worse than random (AUC 0.210): later detections lie at low predictive uncertainty, close to known chemistry. Of 101 species in our validation set, 37 are absent from the list, and only 13 of these appear in a pool rebuilt from its source databases, including 2 of 11 radicals and neither ion. Candidate generation, not ranking, is the main constraint. We release 100 recommended targets, with code and frozen inputs, for prospective testing.

</details>


### [188] [The Black Hole Mass of the Sunflower Galaxy NGC 5055: Direct Measurement via Stellar Kinematics with Keck/OSIRIS Laser Guide Star Adaptive Optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16490)
*Tien H. T. Ho,Dieu D. Nguyen,Alister W. Graham,Mark Durré,Hai N. Ngo,Tinh Q. T. Le,Elena Gallo,Igor V. Chilingarian,Tuan N. Le,Roberto Soria,Fan Zou,Huy G. Tong,Nghi K. N. Le,Que T. Le*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文首次利用近红外积分场光谱技术，结合激光导星自适应光学系统，对邻近旋涡星系NGC 5055（M63）中心超大质量黑洞质量进行了恒星动力学测量，得到其质量为4.56×10⁶ M⊙，并指出该星系可能是银河系的本地类似体，且可能属于罕见的无经典核球旋涡星系中已实现直接动力学黑洞质量测量的少数案例之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无经典核球旋涡星系中黑洞质量的直接动力学测量，拓展黑洞-星系协同演化关系在低质量端的认知，并寻找银河系的本地类比星系。

Method: 使用Keck/OSIRIS近红外积分场光谱仪配合激光导星自适应光学系统获取高分辨率数据，通过pPXF提取CO带吸收特征的二维恒星运动学；采用四种JAM模型（涵盖柱面与球面对称速度椭球取向及两种HST图像MGE分解）进行动力学建模。

Result: 测得NGC 5055中心黑洞质量为M_BH = 4.56^{+1.73}_{-1.82} × 10⁶ M⊙；恒星轨道呈近各向同性（β≈0）；表面亮度剖面无经典核球证据。

Conclusion: NGC 5055是目前已知极少数实现直接动力学黑洞质量测量的无核球旋涡星系之一，为低质量端黑洞-星系标度关系提供了关键新锚点，并强化了其作为银河系本地类比体的地位。

Abstract: We present the first stellar-dynamical supermassive black hole mass measurement in the nearby spiral galaxy NGC 5055 (M63) using near-infrared integral-field spectroscopy with Keck/OSIRIS assisted by the laser guide star adaptive optics system. The observations deliver two-dimensional stellar kinematics extracted from the CO bandhead absorption features via pPXF at an angular resolution of $0.045''$. The stellar velocity dispersion rises steeply from $\sim90~{\rm km~s^{-1}}$ at $r\sim1''$ to a central peak of $\sim135~{\rm km~s^{-1}}$, kinematically tracing the gravitational influence of a central mass concentration. Modeling these kinematics with an ensemble of four JAM models, spanning cylindrical and spherical velocity-ellipsoid alignments and two independent MGE decompositions of archival HST imaging, yields a black hole mass of $M_{\rm BH}=4.56^{+1.73}_{-1.82}\times10^6~{\rm M_\odot}$. The stellar orbits are consistent with near-isotropy ($β\approx0$), typical of rotating late-type galaxies. NGC 5055 emerges as a remarkable local analogue to the Milky Way in terms of its black hole, nuclear star cluster, and total stellar mass. The surface brightness profile of NGC 5055 shows no evidence for a classical bulge; if confirmed bulgeless, NGC 5055 would join the rare class of spirals with a direct dynamical $M_{\rm BH}$ determination in the absence of a bulge, a regime currently populated by only a handful of galaxies including NGC 2748 and NGC 4395. This measurement provides a new anchor for black hole scaling relations in late-type galaxies at the low-mass end of the black hole mass spectrum.

</details>

M. Zerbi,I. A. Zinchenko,E. Zucca,P. Bergamini,J. García-Bellido,C. M. Gutierrez,K. Tanidis*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用欧几里得卫星NISP蓝光栅的无狭缝光谱模式，首次生成了红移0.4–0.9星系的发射线图（如Hα–[N II]），用于研究恒星形成团块、径向恒星形成剖面、内部速度梯度等，并重建了质量–金属丰度关系与Hα径向分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Euclid NISP无狭缝光谱模式生成类窄带发射线图的能力，并拓展其在星系物理统计研究中的应用。

Method: 基于2023年11月Euclid对COSMOS场的性能验证蓝光栅观测数据，构建发射线图；通过Hα–[N II]复合线分解、PSF匹配和堆叠分析等方法开展多维度星系结构与化学演化研究。

Result: 成功获得885个星系的质量–金属丰度关系（与1D光谱结果一致）及2634个星系的Hα径向轮廓；测得Hα半光半径与Y_E波段半光半径比值平均为1.15，表明Hα分布略延展。

Conclusion: NISP slitless spectroscopy可有效支持高精度发射线空间分辨研究，为大样本星系演化统计分析提供新途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate the capability of the slitless spectroscopic mode of the Euclid Near-Infrared Spectrometer and Photometer (NISP) to produce emission-line maps analogous to narrow-band images with user-defined central wavelengths. We present the first such maps of galaxies at $0.4 < z < 0.9$, constructed from blue-grism exposures obtained during the Euclid performance-verification observations of the Cosmic Evolution Survey (COSMOS) field in November 2023. We demonstrate the use of these maps for identifying star-forming clumps, measuring radial star-formation profiles, detecting projected internal velocity gradients, deblending the H$α$--[N II] complex, and assessing spectroscopic redshift measurements. As a first statistical application, we reconstruct the galaxy mass--metallicity relation for 885 galaxies observed with the blue grism at $0.4 < z < 0.9$ by deblending the H$α$--[N II] complex in the emission-line maps, and measure stacked H$α$ radial profiles for 2634 galaxies over the same redshift range. The mass--metallicity relation is consistent with that obtained from the Euclid 1D spectroscopic measurements, demonstrating overall agreement between the two deblending approaches. The recovered H$α$ radial profiles broadly follow the $Y_{\mathrm{E}}$ stellar continuum but remain modestly more extended after matching the $Y_{\mathrm{E}}$ images to the H$α$ PSF, consistent with previous results in the literature. Across the four $Y_{\mathrm{E}}$ size bins, we measure a mean $R_{50,\mathrm{H}α}/R_{50,Y_{\mathrm{E}}}=1.15$.

</details>


### [190] [Dissecting and diagnosing the LISA Galactic binary population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16611)
*Alice E. Cravioglio,Arianna I. Renzini*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了LISA望远镜对银河系双白矮星（DWD）系统的分辨能力，指出分辨出的系统受啁啾质量、空间分布及仪器灵敏度影响，并非银河系DWD总体的代表性样本；高啁啾质量、正在并合的系统更易被分辨，空间上主要来自银心核球；混淆背景具有内在双峰啁啾质量结构，且存在长期残留混淆底噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA将探测大量银河系双白矮星，但多数将淹没于未分辨的引力波混淆前景中；为正确解读其未来星表，需量化选择效应。

Method: 基于模拟的银河系DWD星表，采用迭代减除流程，在不同观测时长下分析分辨与未分辨子总体的统计特性，并引入新诊断量评估混淆功率的源数依赖性。

Result: 分辨系统主要由高啁啾质量、正在并合的DWD主导，空间上集中于银心核球；混淆背景呈啁啾质量双模结构，且不同频段由不同子总体贡献；即使积分100年，仍存在残留混淆底噪。

Conclusion: LISA对DWD的分辨存在显著偏差，必须在利用其分辨星表和混淆背景约束银河系DWD总体时予以校正。

Abstract: LISA will individually resolve thousands of Galactic double white dwarf (DWD) binaries, while the majority stays buried in an unresolved gravitational wave (GW) confusion foreground. Which systems get resolved depends on the binary's parameters, on the global population and on instrumental sensitivity, so the resolved population is not a representative sample of the underlying Galactic population. Interpreting LISA's future catalog therefore requires understanding these selection effects. Here we characterize the resolved and unresolved subpopulations of a simulated Milky Way DWD catalog, using an iterative subtraction pipeline applied over different observation times. Resolvability is driven primarily by chirp mass: higher chirp mass, inspiraling systems are strongly favored over lower mass, outspiraling or monochromatic ones, and this reflects on the rate with which different composition subtypes are resolved. Spatially, resolved sources trace the Galactic bulge, because it is intrinsically the densest region of the Galaxy. The confusion background shows an internal bimodal chirp-mass structure, and is not generated uniformly by the same subpopulation in different frequency bands. We introduce a diagnostic for the minimum number of sources needed to reproduce a given fraction of the confusion power in a frequency band, and show that a residual confusion floor persists even after 100 years of integration. These biases are significant and need to be accounted for when using LISA's resolved DWD catalog and the confusion background to constrain the Galactic DWD population.

</details>


### [191] [Satellite pairs in the Local Group: from spatial clustering to dynamical associations in observations and $Λ$CDM simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16687)
*Mariana P. Júlio,Marcel S. Pawlowski,Noam I. Libeskind*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用HESTIA宇宙学模拟和最新观测数据，研究了本星系群中卫星星系对的出现率和物理性质。结果发现银河系（MW）的卫星星系成对率（约53%）显著高于模拟预期（17%-27%），而仙女座星系（M31）则与各向同性预期一致（约28%）；但多数成对卫星并非引力束缚或运动学一致，多为瞬时排列或共同落入遗迹，表明MW拥有异常丰富的相空间相关卫星，反映其近期吸积历史，而M31更符合标准ΛCDM预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解本星系群中卫星星系成对现象的统计特性及其物理起源，检验ΛCDM模型在小尺度上的预测能力，并探究MW与M31卫星系统差异背后的星系演化历史。

Method: 结合更新的MW与M31卫星观测数据及HESTIA约束宇宙学模拟中的三个本星系群类比样本；通过构建匹配径向分布的各向同性卫星分布作为基准，识别近距离最近邻关联；利用视向速度、自行和引力束缚判据分析成对卫星的物理性质。

Result: MW卫星成对效率约53%，显著高于模拟类比（17%-27%），且在施加恒星质量截断（M⋆ > 10⁴ M⊙）后仍成立；M31成对效率约28%，与各向同性一致；但仅少数成对候选体具有协同运动学或引力束缚特征。

Conclusion: MW拥有异常丰富的相空间相关卫星，暗示其近期经历显著的集团吸积事件；而M31更符合典型ΛCDM预期，二者差异反映了不同的并合历史，对小尺度结构形成与星系考古学具有重要启示。

Abstract: We investigate the incidence and physical nature of satellite galaxy pairs in the Local Group using updated observational data for the Milky Way (MW) and Andromeda (M31), together with analogues from the HESTIA constrained cosmological simulations. Our comparison uses three distinct simulated Local Group analogues drawn from the HESTIA suite, allowing us to assess the observed pair statistics against multiple cosmological realisations of the Local Group environment. We identify close nearest-neighbour associations by comparing the observed satellite distributions with isotropic populations that reproduce the radial distributions of each host, and investigate their physical nature using line-of-sight velocities, proper motions, and gravitational binding estimates. We find that the MW exhibits a significant excess of close satellite associations relative to isotropic expectations, with a pairing efficiency of $\sim53\%$, nearly twice that found in the simulated analogues ($\sim17$-$27\%$). This excess persists after applying a stellar mass cut ($M_\star > 10^4\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$) to match the resolution limits of the simulations. In contrast, the M31 satellite system is broadly consistent with isotropy, with a pairing efficiency of $\sim28\%$. However, only a minority of pair candidates exhibit coherent kinematics or are likely to be gravitationally bound, indicating that most represent transient alignments or remnants of common group infall rather than long-lived binary satellites. These results suggest that the MW hosts an unusually rich population of phase-space-correlated satellites, likely reflecting its recent accretion history, whereas M31 is more consistent with typical $Λ$CDM expectations.

</details>


### [192] [Magellanic Outflow and Chemistry Survey (MAGOS): the Outflow-Luminosity Scaling Relations in the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16830)
*Suinan Zhang,Kei E. I. Tanaka,Yichen Zhang,Takashi Shimonishi,Yu Cheng,Kenji Furuya,Asako Sato*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文首次系统调查了大、小麦哲伦云（LMC和SMC）中低金属丰度环境下大质量原恒星外流，发现CO在LMC中仍是有效示踪体，但在SMC中因光致解离增强而示踪能力下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解低金属丰度环境下大质量恒星形成对约束宇宙演化中恒星形成的普适性至关重要。

Method: 利用ALMA在0.85 mm波段对LMC和SMC中40个大质量年轻恒星天体（MYSOs）开展CO (3–2)谱线观测，识别蓝移与红移外流，并分析外流质量、动量、能量、力与光度的标度关系。

Result: LMC中CO外流的质量-光度关系斜率与银河系相近；SMC中CO外流在高光度端明显缺失、标度关系更平缓，可能源于低金属丰度下CO光致解离增强。

Conclusion: CO在Z∼0.5 Z⊙环境（如LMC）中仍可有效示踪大质量原恒星外流，但在Z∼0.2 Z⊙环境（如SMC）中示踪能力受限，需谨慎解读或结合其他分子示踪。

Abstract: Understanding massive star formation in low-metallicity environments is crucial for constraining the universality of star formation across cosmic evolution. In this Letter, we present the first systematic survey of massive protostellar outflows in the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds (LMC and SMC, $Z\sim$0.2--0.5 $Z_\odot$), as part of the Magellanic Outflow and Chemistry Survey (MAGOS). MAGOS targets 40 massive young stellar objects (MYSOs) with luminosities of (1--23)$\times 10^4$ $L_\odot$ with the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) at 0.85 mm at a linear resolution of $\sim$0.1 pc. Using CO (3--2), we identify blue- and red-shifted outflows associated with the primary continuum sources toward 29 targets in the LMC and 10 targets in the SMC. Comparing the outflow mass, momentum, energy, and force versus luminosity scaling relations in MAGOS with those in the Galaxy, we find that the LMC CO outflows have a similar slope in the mass-luminosity relation, suggesting that CO remains an effective tracer of massive protostellar outflows at $Z\sim$0.5 $Z_\odot$. In contrast, despite the large scatter, the SMC CO outflows are deficient at high luminosity and show flatter scaling relations than the LMC and the Galaxy. This is consistent with reduced CO abundances due to enhanced photodissociation at high luminosity and low metallicity ($Z\sim$0.2 $Z_\odot$), although the possibility of intrinsically weak outflow launching cannot be ruled out with the current data.

</details>


### [193] [Bidimensional Exploration of the warm-Temperature Ionised gaS (BETIS) III. The impact of eDIG on strong-line abundance determinations in galactic halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17135)
*R. González-Díaz,E. Pérez Montero,J. M. Vílchez,F. F. Rosales-Ortega,L. Galbany*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用MUSE积分场光谱数据，首次对边缘星系外盘电离气体（eDIG）的空间分辨化学丰度进行了研究，发现冲击电离区域会导致HII-CHI-Mistry方法系统性低估O/H和N/O（尤其N/O），而氧丰度在盘与晕间变化很小，支持星系喷泉高效循环富氧气体的图像；氮氧比则呈现显著径向和垂向变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eDIG是连接星系盘与晕、反映反馈过程的关键示踪体，但其化学丰度空间分布尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 基于BETIS样本中4个边缘星系的MUSE积分场光谱，使用HII-CHI-Mistry v5.5代码构建氧丰度（12+log(O/H)）、氮氧比（log(N/O)）和电离参数（log(U)）的空间分布图，并按电离主导机制（恒星形成vs.激波）分类分析。

Result: 激波污染区O/H和N/O被系统低估约0.1–0.15 dex（尤以N/O明显），log(U)更均一且偏低（~−3.1）；恒星形成主导区log(U)范围更广（~−3.3至−2.8），氧丰度近太阳值；盘与晕间氧丰度差异小，而N/O存在显著径向与垂向梯度。

Conclusion: 结果支持星系喷泉模型——反馈驱动富氧气体在盘晕间高效循环；N/O梯度反映不同反馈强度下氮增丰历史的差异，凸显其作为反馈强度探针的价值。

Abstract: The extraplanar diffuse ionised gas (eDIG) is a key tracer of the feedback processes that connect galactic discs and halos. We present the first study of spatially resolved gas-phase chemical abundances in the eDIG of edge-on galaxies, based on integral field spectroscopy obtained with MUSE. Using a sample of four galaxies from the BETIS sample, we derived maps of the oxygen abundance, 12+log(O/H), the nitrogen-to-oxygen ratio, log(N/O), and the ionisation parameter, log(U), with the HII-CHI-Mistry v5.5 code, and we tested the reliability of this method when different ionisation sources are present. We separated the eDIG into star-formation-dominated and shock-contaminated regimes according to the relative contribution of photoionisation and shocks, and investigated their impact on the derived chemical abundances. We find that HII-CHI-Mistry returns systematically lower O/H and N/O values in shock-contaminated regions, especially for N/O, with typical offsets of ~0.1-0.15 dex relative to star-formation-dominated regions, together with a more homogeneous and lower ionisation state (log(U) ~ -3.1). In contrast, star-formation-dominated regions span a wider range of ionisation parameters (log(U) ~ -3.3 to -2.8) while maintaining approximately solar oxygen abundances. Despite these differences, the abundance offsets are comparable to the sampling step of the HII-CHI-Mistry model grid. The inferred oxygen abundance shows little variation between discs and halos. This is compatible with a scenario in which star formation feedback and galactic fountains efficiently recycle oxygen-rich gas between both components, in agreement with hydrodynamical simulations. In contrast, the N/O ratio shows significant radial and vertical variations, particularly in environments with weaker star formation feedback.

</details>


### [194] [Probing Ultralight Higher-spin Dark Matter using Stellar Kinematics of Dwarf Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17204)
*Praniti Singh,JiJi Fan,Savvas M. Koushiappas,Isabelle S. Goldstein*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用银河系经典矮星系的恒星运动学数据，探索具有非零自旋的超轻暗物质（ULDM）模型，通过引入能刻画自旋效应的广义晕轮廓参数化方法，在贝叶斯分析中发现观测数据目前不支持更高自旋ULDM导致的更弥散核区，但亦未排除该模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用矮星系恒星动力学探针暗物质分布及物理本质，特别检验非零自旋ULDM与标量ULDM在核区结构上的差异。

Method: 在非相对论极限数值模拟基础上，提出含三个新参数（核密度归一化、核-晕质量关系指数、过渡半径）的广义ULDM晕轮廓；对六个经典矮星系开展贝叶斯动力学建模分析。

Result: 得到晕质量范围为10^{8.5}–10^{9.5} M⊙；单个星系给出粒子质量下限m ≳ 3×10^{-21} eV；核-晕指数和过渡半径与标量情形一致；核密度归一化在四个星系中与标量一致，Fornax偏高，但更高自旋导致的数倍压低尚未被任何单个星系排除。

Conclusion: 当前数据不偏好更高自旋ULDM所预期的更弥散核区，但尚不足以排除该模型。

Abstract: Stellar kinematics of the classical Milky Way dwarf galaxies provide a powerful probe of the dark matter distribution and its underlying physics. In this article we use them to explore ultralight dark matter (ULDM) with a non-zero spin. Numerical simulations in the non-relativistic limit have shown that, for the same initial conditions, higher-spin ULDM forms a more diffuse soliton core at the galactic center, with a smoother transition to the NFW outer region, than scalar ULDM. We introduce a generalized parametrization of the ULDM halo profile that captures these spin-induced effects through three additional parameters: the normalization of the central core density, the exponent of the core-halo mass relation, and the radius of the transition to the NFW envelope. Applying this profile in a Bayesian analysis of six classical dwarf galaxies, we find halo masses in the range $10^{8.5}$-$10^{9.5}\,M_\odot$ and, from each galaxy individually, a particle mass $m \gtrsim 3\times10^{-21}$ eV. The core-halo exponent and the transition radius are consistent with their scalar values. The core-density normalization is also consistent with the scalar value in four galaxies and prefers a core denser than a scalar soliton in Fornax, while the factor-of-several suppression expected for higher-spin cores is not excluded by any single galaxy. The data therefore show no preference for the more diffuse cores of higher-spin ULDM, but do not yet rule them out.

</details>


### [195] [The ultraviolet continuum slopes of galaxies at $\mathbf{z \gtrsim 12}$ selected from ${\mathbf{0.6 \, deg}^2}$ of JWST imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17208)
*C. Bondestam,D. J. McLeod,F. Cullen,R. J. McLure,J. S. Dunlop,S. Antonogiannaki,H. -H. Leung,R. Begley,A. C. Carnall,C. T. Donnan,R. S. Ellis,A. Fontana,N. A. Grogin,I. J. B. Holst,G. D. Illingworth,A. M. Koekemoer,F. -Y. Liu,T. M. Stanton,S. D. Stevenson,E. Taylor*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文分析了55个红移z≳12的星系的紫外连续谱斜率β，发现其平均值⟨β⟩ = −2.57±0.04，表明这些星系普遍极蓝、几乎无尘埃消光；未发现β与UV光度M_UV的相关性；β随红移平滑变蓝（dβ/dz ≃ −0.1），支持早期星系尘埃含量极低的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高红移（z≳12）星系的尘埃含量和恒星种群性质，检验早期宇宙中星系是否已具备显著尘埃消光能力。

Method: 基于JWST/NIRCam大视场（>0.6平方度）成像数据，选取55个z≳12的星系，测量其紫外连续谱斜率β，并进行加权平均、与M_UV关系检验及红移演化分析；结合先前JWST（z≥9）和HST（z≥4）数据进行一致性比对。

Result: z≳12星系平均β = −2.57±0.04，接近无尘埃消光理论极限；无β–M_UV相关性；β随红移平滑变蓝（dβ/dz ≃ −0.1）；个体β散射暗示可能存在少量尘埃红化星系（β≳−2）。

Conclusion: z≳12星系整体几乎无尘埃消光，支持宇宙再电离时期星系尘埃丰度极低；β的平滑红移演化反映星系物理性质（如恒星年龄、金属丰度）的连续变化；小部分红化星系的存在提示早期尘埃形成已有初步迹象。

Abstract: We present an analysis of the ultraviolet (UV) continuum slopes $(β)$ of $55$ galaxies at $z\gtrsim 12$, selected from a wide-area ($>0.6$ sq. deg) search across publicly available JWST/NIRCam imaging. Our sample spans the absolute UV magnitude ($M_{\mathrm{UV}}$) range $-21.6 \leq M_{\mathrm{UV}} \leq -17.3$ and we find an inverse variance-weighted mean $\langleβ\rangle = -2.57\pm0.04$ indicating that, on average, the galaxy population is extremely blue at $z\gtrsim 12$. The average $β$ at these redshifts is consistent with the intrinsic stellar plus nebular continuum limit in the absence of UV dust-attenuation. Consistent with earlier JWST works, our analysis suggests that the moderately dust-attenuated galaxies at $z \lesssim 10$ become essentially attenuation-free by $z \simeq 11$. We find no evidence for a $β-M_{\mathrm{UV}}$ relation in our $z\gtrsim 12$ sample, indicating that the attenuation-free interpretation applies across all observed UV magnitudes. By augmenting our sample with previous JWST constraints down to $z=9$, and incorporating $M_{\mathrm{UV}}$-matched HST studies down to $z=4$, we find a smooth evolution in $β(z)$ over a wide range in redshift. The average value of $β$ becomes progressively bluer at $z>4$, with $\mathrm{d}β/\mathrm{d}z \simeq -0.1$. Finally, although we find that the typical galaxy at $z \gtrsim 12$ is consistent with the attenuation-free limit, the scatter in our individual $β$ measurements is consistent with the existence of a small population of dust-reddened galaxies with $β\gtrsim -2$.

</details>


### [196] [The origin of Potassium in Galactic chemical evolution - The effect of C-O shell mergers in massive stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17224)
*Marta Molero,Luca Boccioli,Almudena Arcones*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文重新分析了三种不同产额集合中的194个超新星前身星模型，发现氧碳壳层合并（C-O shell merger）是钾（K）元素高效核合成的关键机制；该过程仅发生在少量大质量恒星中，但可使K产量提升10–50倍；若排除该机制，所有模型均无法再现观测到的[K/Fe]丰度趋势；而包含该机制后，[K/Fe]增强仅出现在对应金属丰度窗口内；由于C-O合并产生的K本质上是原初的，其[K/H]随[Fe/H]近似线性增长的现象暗示该事件在各金属丰度下发生频率相近，因此K丰度可用作推断C-O壳层合并发生率的探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前银河系化学演化（GCE）模型普遍低估钾（K）相对于铁（Fe）丰度（即[K/Fe]）的问题，探究C-O壳层合并是否为关键的K核合成机制，并评估其在宇宙时间尺度上的发生频率。

Method: 分析来自三个独立产额集合的194个大质量恒星（涵盖不同初始质量、金属丰度、自转速度及单星/双星剥离态）的超新星前身星模型；识别其中发生C-O壳层合并的模型；构建含/不含C-O合并贡献的GCE模型并对比预测与观测的[K/Fe]演化趋势。

Result: C-O壳层合并仅在极少数前身星中发生，但使K产量提升10–50倍；剔除该过程后所有模型均无法再现观测到的[K/Fe]；加入后仅在对应金属丰度区间内出现[K/Fe]增强；单星即可产生与双星相当的K总量；C-O合并产生的K具有原初性质，其[K/H]–[Fe/H]近似线性关系要求该事件在各金属丰度下发生频率基本一致。

Conclusion: C-O壳层合并是解释银河系中钾元素丰度演化的关键物理机制；钾可作为示踪C-O壳层合并发生率的有效天文探针；该机制的发生应近似与金属丰度无关，暗示其在恒星演化晚期具有普适性。

Abstract: The Galactic chemical evolution (GCE) of potassium (K) and of most other odd-Z elements remains poorly understood: models generally underproduce the observed [K/Fe] over a wide metallicity range, depending on the adopted massive star yields. A proposed site for efficient K nucleosynthesis is the merger between the oxygen and carbon shells (C-O shell merger) that can occur in massive stars a few hours before core collapse. Some GCE models with specific yield sets have been shown to reconcile predictions with observations. Here we re-analyse these yield sets to quantify the contribution of C-O shell mergers to K production and what the Galactic evolution of K implies about the occurrence of these events. We analyse 194 pre-supernova models from three independent yield sets, covering a wide range of initial masses, metallicities and rotational velocities, and including both single and binary-stripped stars. We identify the progenitors undergoing a C-O shell merger and compute GCE models with and without their contribution. In all three sets, the enhanced K production can be traced back to a small number of C-O merger progenitors, which eject up to ~10-50 times more K than non-merger progenitors of similar mass. Without them, no model reproduces the observations and [K/Fe] is sub-solar over most of the metallicity range. With them, [K/Fe] is enhanced only within the metallicity window where mergers occur in each grid. Binarity itself plays a minor role, as single stars alone produce similar amounts of K. Since the K synthesised in a C-O merger is essentially primary, the observed nearly linear increase of [K/H] with [Fe/H] can be reproduced only if C-O shell mergers occur with a similar frequency at all metallicities. K could therefore be used to infer the occurrence rate of C-O shell mergers in massive stars as a function of time.

</details>


### [197] [Radiation, Thermochemistry, and Stellar Feedback in DISPATCH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17290)
*William Lake,Paolo Padoan,Åke Nordlund,Troels Haugbølle*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的 DISPATCH 数值方法，耦合了定向恒星辐射、红外弥散辐射传输与热化学过程，并嵌入到自引力磁流体动力学框架中，用于模拟大质量恒星在湍流分子云中的形成过程及其反馈效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量恒星的生长受其吸积流能否抵御来自新生恒星群体的辐射和外流反馈所制约；需发展能同时处理多尺度、多物理过程（辐射、热化学、MHD、喷流）的数值方法，以研究反馈如何调控吸积效率与持续时间。

Method: 在 DISPATCH 框架中扩展实现：1）多波段（含电离/非电离）方向性直接辐射传输（短特征线法）；2）红外再处理弥散辐射（M1 与稳态射线两种方案）；3）包含 H/He 分子/原子化学、金属冷却、尘埃及组分依赖状态方程的热化学模型；4）基于气体吸积（而非预设 IMF）的恒星质量增长；5）支持 AMR 和局部时间步进，实现恒星邻域与星团大尺度环境异步演化。

Result: 测试表明：多源照明保持几何阴影，弥散辐射可加热遮蔽区；喷流、电离前沿、辐射压等行为符合预期且收敛；电离前沿跨越异步网格边界时传播速度与解析解偏差仅数个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架为定量研究辐射与机械反馈如何调制湍流分子云中大质量恒星的吸积时标与效率提供了关键工具，进而连接气体供给与高质恒星初始质量函数（IMF）的形成机制。

Abstract: The growth of massive stars depends on whether their accretion flows survive radiation and outflow feedback from the forming stellar population. We present an extension of DISPATCH that couples directional stellar radiation, diffuse infrared transport, and thermochemistry to self-gravitating magnetohydrodynamics, individual stellar accretion, and protostellar jets. This AMR-based method uses DISPATCH's local timestepping, allowing stellar environments and their larger scale cluster environments to step on separate timescales, and providing the framework needed to scale to long-duration simulations of GMCs. Direct radiation is transported in multiple bands, including both ionizing and nonionizing radiation, and uses short characteristics to maintain source directions and shadowing. Diffuse radiation is implemented for IR reprocessed light, with M1 and stationary ray options for transport. The thermochemical model includes atomic and molecular hydrogen and helium chemistry, and metal cooling and dust. It also includes a composition-dependent equation of state. Stellar masses grow through accretion of gas rather than a prescribed IMF, allowing stellar masses to respond to the evolving gas supply and feedback. In tests, multiple-source illumination retains geometric shadows while diffuse reprocessing heats shielded material, and tests of jets, ionization fronts, and radiation pressure demonstrate expected behavior and convergence. Ionization fronts also cross asynchronous refinement boundaries with late-time propagation speeds within a few percent of analytic predictions. Together, these developments provide a framework for determining how radiative and mechanical feedback modify the duration and efficiency of accretion from turbulent clouds, connecting the gas supply to individual stars with the high-mass stellar initial mass function.

</details>


### [198] [The full Gaia colour-magnitude diagram with white dwarfs reveals a declining Milky Way star formation history](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17337)
*K. Sysoliatina,Akash Vani*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了CALOMERA框架，该框架将白矮星（WD）的冷却序列、金属丰度依赖的前身星寿命和合成测光与银河系势场及恒星形成历史（SFH）自洽耦合，利用Gaia DR3数据校准18个参数，并成功再现了恒星和白矮星的数量分布，同时预测了银河系WD及褐矮星（L/T/Y型）在深空观测中的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往白矮星建模通常孤立于主序星等非简并恒星种群，缺乏对整个银河系恒星形成历史和动力学结构的统一描述；本文旨在构建一个能同时自洽描述WD与非简并恒星演化及分布的半解析化学动力学种群合成框架。

Method: 开发CALOMERA半解析化学动力学种群合成框架，结合现代WD冷却模型（Montréal/BaSTI/LPCODE）、金属丰度依赖的前身星寿命、初始-终态质量关系及合成测光，并基于Gaia DR3中798万颗恒星（含11万颗WD）在1 kpc内样本校准18个参数。

Result: 校准后模型在各颜色-星等图区域星数误差≤3%，总星数误差仅0.5%（此前主序星单独校准导致WD数量高估81%）；恢复的SFH呈早期峰值后指数衰减，并含2.4 Gyr和0.5 Gyr两个近期爆发；三种WD冷却库拟合质量相当（差异限于MG≳15 mag）；预测的WD光度函数与Gaia 100 pc样本基本一致；并外推预测了不同深度巡天的WD空间分布及JWST深场中L/T/Y型褐矮星的贡献。

Conclusion: CALOMERA实现了WD与银河系整体恒星种群的首次自洽建模，显著提升了WD计数预测精度，为利用WD化石记录反演银河系演化历史提供了新工具，并可拓展至褐矮星种群预测。

Abstract: White dwarfs (WDs) provide a fossil record of the Milky Way star formation history (SFH) through the combined effects of stellar evolution and cooling. They have, however, generally been modelled in isolation from the non-degenerate population, so that no single self-consistent set of Galactic assumptions has been required to reproduce both. We present CALOMERA, a semi-analytic chemo-dynamical population synthesis framework in which the vertical density profiles of mono-age subpopulations are computed self-consistently with the Galactic potential and coupled to modern WD cooling sequences, metallicity-dependent progenitor lifetimes and synthetic photometry. We calibrate 18 parameters describing the initial mass function, SFH, initial-final mass relation and WD population against a Gaia DR3 sample of $7.98\times10^{6}$ stars within 1 kpc, including $1.1\times10^{5}$ WDs. The calibrated model reproduces the star counts in each colour-magnitude diagram region to within 3% and the total number count to within 0.5%, compared with the 81% excess in the predicted WD count obtained with the previous main-sequence-only calibration. The recovered SFH peaks early, declines exponentially thereafter, and includes two recent bursts, located at $τ\approx2.4$ and $0.5$ Gyr. Three cooling libraries (Montréal, BaSTI and LPCODE) yield comparably good fits, with differences confined to ${\rm M_G}\gtrsim15$ mag. The predicted luminosity function is in reasonable agreement with the 100 pc Gaia WD sample. We further predict the Galactic WD distribution and differential number counts for surveys of varying depth. Extending the framework to the substellar regime using ATMO models, we predict the Galactic L, T, and Y dwarf populations and their contribution to the deep JWST fields.

</details>

### [199] [SuSy and CoDy Radiation from Evaporating Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14563)
*Giacomo Cacciapaglia,Francesco Sannino,Paola Simone,Vania Vellucci*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超出标准模型的物理与修正时空几何对蒸发黑洞光子和中微子谱的影响，结合多种新物理模型与不同黑洞几何，在BlackHawk框架下分析其瞬时次级粒子谱特征，揭示二者效应可能耦合，并指出光子与中微子谱可作为探测新物理与黑洞结构的互补探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理（如超对称、复合动力学）与修正黑洞时空几何（如聚合化、Hayward、Simpson–Visser）如何共同影响黑洞霍金辐射中的光子与中微子能谱，以寻找可观测的新物理与量子引力信号。

Method: 在BlackHawk模拟平台中实现三类超对称基准模型与一类复合动力学模型；对比施瓦西黑洞与三类正则黑洞（聚合化、Hayward、Simpson–Visser）几何；分析瞬时光子与中微子能谱，区分辐射衰变产生的窄峰与多步级联衰变导致的宽带增强等特征。

Result: 新物理在M_BH∼10¹⁰ g黑洞质量对应的霍金温度下引入可观测谱特征：辐射衰变产生温度无关的窄峰，级联衰变导致随温度升高而增强的宽带结构，压缩质量谱则无局域特征；且新物理与修正几何的效应在特定能量区发生耦合，改变整体谱形。

Conclusion: 光子与中微子能谱是探测TeV尺度新粒子物理与黑洞微观几何结构的互补且灵敏探针，其预测的谱增强落入当前及未来高能观测设备（如CTA、IceCube-Gen2）可及能区。

Abstract: We investigate the interplay between physics beyond the Standard Model and modified spacetime geometries in shaping the photon and neutrino spectra from evaporating black holes. We implement three representative supersymmetric (SuSy) benchmarks and a composite dynamics (CoDy) scenario in BlackHawk. We consider both Schwarzschild black holes and regular black holes geometries, namely polymerized, Hayward, and Simpson--Visser black holes. New degrees of freedom at the TeV scale become accessible at Hawking temperatures corresponding to black hole masses of approximately $M_{\rm BH}\sim 10^{10}\,\mathrm{g}$. We show that physics beyond the Standard Model produces distinctive signatures in both the instantaneous secondary-photon and neutrino spectra. Radiative decays generate narrow spectral features with approximately temperature-independent relative amplitudes, while multistep decay cascades produce broad enhancements that become increasingly pronounced at higher temperatures. In contrast, compressed mass spectra exhibit no localized features. Crucially, we show that the effects of new particle physics and modified spacetime geometries can become intertwined in specific energy regimes, altering the resulting spectral signatures. Our results demonstrate that photon and neutrino spectra provide complementary probes of new particle physics and black hole geometry, with predicted spectral enhancements extending into the energy ranges accessible to current and future high-energy observatories.

</details>


### [200] [Strongly supercooled first-order phase transitions: monopole abundance, entropy production and homogeneity consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14593)
*Chen Zhang,Yue Niu,Xin Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在强过冷但最终成功完成的一阶相变中，单极子产生是否能被显著抑制，并提出了基于FVF涨落的均匀性一致性诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决统一规范理论中早期宇宙一阶相变产生的单极子丰度问题，特别是强过冷情形下单极子是否可被有效抑制。

Method: 采用高斯成核模型，假设假真空能量释放到表现为辐射的部门；定义并比较三种相变完成判据（CMC、PVC、ATC）；引入Hubble尺度FVF均方根涨落诊断FLRW宇宙学均匀性自洽性。

Result: 三种完成判据共享同一完成边界；靠近该边界的参数点可任意压制单极子丰度；但过近时FLRW均匀性假设失效；Hubble尺度FVF涨落表明，相对于Kibble估计的任意强单极子压制在均匀性上不可靠。

Conclusion: 强过冷一阶相变虽可压制单极子，但受宇宙学均匀性限制，其压制程度存在实际上限；该结论也影响强过冷相变中引力波信号的预测可靠性。

Abstract: We investigate whether monopole production in the early universe from a first-order phase transition in a unified gauge theory can be significantly suppressed if the phase transition is subject to strong supercooling but still completes successfully. This is facilitated by adopting a Gaussian nucleation model and assuming the false vacuum energy released is transferred to sectors which behave effectively as radiation in terms of cosmological evolution. We distinguish three criteria of phase-transition completion: the comoving measure completion (CMC) characterized by the eventual vanishing of the false-vacuum fraction (FVF), the physical volume completion (PVC) characterized by the eventual vanishing of the physical volume of false vacuum, and the asymptotic thermodynamic completion (ATC), which refers to the finiteness of total entropy production. Assuming an idealized Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) cosmology, it is shown that the three criteria share exactly the same completion boundary, away from which they are completely equivalent. It is further shown that monopole abundance can be arbitrarily suppressed for parameter points approaching the completion boundary from the successful side. Nevertheless, for parameter points too close to the completion boundary, justification of the self-consistency of a homogeneous FLRW cosmology is difficult. We propose the use of the Hubble-scale FVF root-mean-square fluctuation to diagnose the homogeneity consistency issue, and find that arbitrarily strong suppression of monopole abundance relative to a reasonable Kibble estimate is not justifiable in terms of homogeneity consistency. These findings also have implications for gravitational wave production in strongly supercooled phase transitions.

</details>


### [201] [Cosmological Magnetic Fluctuations: Positivity Bounds and QCD Contributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14595)
*Ahmadullah Zahed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了热宇宙学磁涨落，通过两种互补方法：一是利用涨落-耗散关系、谱正性及热核的凸/凹性，不依赖谱形模型地约束平衡态磁功率谱；二是计算了QCD强子贡献的宇宙磁涨落（主导项为ρ共振态），并用QCD、电子-正电子等离子体和冷碰撞等离子体实例验证了该约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙早期热环境中磁涨落的性质与幅度，为原初磁场起源提供理论约束。

Method: 1）基于涨落-耗散关系、谱正性及热核函数的凸/凹性，推导磁功率谱的通用矩约束；2）在QCD框架下，计算强子段（特别是ρ共振态）对磁涨落的电磁主导贡献。

Result: 得到了不依赖谱形的磁功率谱上下界；明确了ρ共振态是timelike强子贡献的主导部分；所提约束经QCD及多种等离子体模型检验成立。

Conclusion: 热宇宙磁涨落可由物理基本原理（如涨落-耗散）给出普适矩约束，且强子QCD效应在特定频段不可忽略，这对原初磁场建模具有重要启示。

Abstract: We study thermal cosmological magnetic fluctuations in two complementary ways. First, we bound the equilibrium magnetic power spectrum in terms of two spectral moments. The proof uses the fluctuation--dissipation relation, spectral positivity, and the convexity and concavity of the thermal kernels. The bounds do not require a model for the spectral shape. They also apply to individual current contributions, using their own moments, and a known frequency threshold strengthens the upper bound. Second, we calculate a hadronic QCD contribution to cosmological magnetic fluctuations at leading electromagnetic order. We estimate the \(ρ\)-resonance contribution, which dominates the timelike hadronic contribution in our comparison. Examples from QCD, an electron--positron plasma and a cold collisional plasma test the bounds. The spectra describe fluctuations at the chosen cosmological epoch.

</details>
