### [202] [The NANOGrav 15-year data set: A Limit on Continuous Spin Gravitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14613)
*Chinmay Ambasht,Olivia Bitcon,Tanner Trickle,the NANOGrav Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）对连续自旋引力子（CSP graviton）的自旋尺度ρ_g进行实验约束，基于NANOGrav 15年数据，在95%置信水平下得到ρ_g < 1.23×10⁻²³ eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续自旋粒子（CSPs）是一类具有维度性自旋尺度ρ的质量为零粒子；当ρ→0时退化为普通光子或引力子。若真实引力子是CSP，则其ρ值极小，需通过实验确定其上限。低频探针因效应随ρ/ω增强而尤为敏感，故适合探测。

Method: 利用脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）对纳赫兹频段引力波的超高灵敏度，结合NANOGrav 15年数据集，采用标准贝叶斯数据分析方法，建模并拟合CSP引力子引起的时空扰动信号特征。

Result: 在95%可信水平下，得到连续自旋引力子的自旋尺度上限为ρ_g < 1.23 × 10⁻²³ eV。

Conclusion: 该结果是目前对CSP引力子最严格的实验约束，凸显了PTA作为探索新引力物理平台的重要潜力，并为未来更灵敏观测（如SKA）设定了基准。

Abstract: Continuous spin particles (CSPs) are massless particles characterized by a dimensionful spin scale, $ρ$, which, when non-zero, changes their helicity under Lorentz boosts. Since CSPs reduce to ordinary particles when $ρ\rightarrow 0$, the photon or graviton could be CSPs and only appear to behave as ordinary particles due to a small value of $ρ$. Therefore, it is important to determine what value of $ρ$ is experimentally allowed. Recent work has shown that the physical effects of CSPs scale as powers of $ρ/ω$ when $ρ\ll ω$, where $ω$ is a frequency scale, making low-frequency probes particularly beneficial for highlighting these phenomena. In this paper we use pulsar timing arrays to set constraints on the spin scale of the continuous spin graviton. Pulsar timing arrays are uniquely suited for this application as they have exceptional sensitivity to low, nanohertz frequency gravitational waves, as demonstrated by their recent evidence for a stochastic gravitational wave background. Using standard Bayesian data analysis techniques, we find that the NANOGrav 15-year data set constrains the graviton spin scale to $ρ_\text{g} < 1.23 \times 10^{-23} \, \text{eV} $ at the 95% credible level.

</details>


### [203] [$N_{\mathrm{eff}}$, Radiative Dirac Neutrinos, and Flavor Physics from Leptoquarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14616)
*Patricio Escalona,Guillermo Gambini,Vítor Pereira,C. A. de S. Pires,Farinaldo S. Queiroz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含两种混合标量leptoquark和三个右手性中微子的模型，能同时解释味异常、稀有介子衰变及狄拉克型中微子质量的辐射产生，并满足Belle II对B⁺→K⁺νν̄的测量、μ→eγ实验限制及宇宙学ΔN_eff约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一解释味物理反常、稀有介子衰变及中微子质量起源，构建含标量leptoquark与右手中微子的新物理模型。

Method: 构建含两个混合标量leptoquark和三个右手中微子的理论模型，计算其对B⁺→K⁺νν̄、μ→eγ及宇宙学有效相对论自由度ΔN_eff的贡献，并与实验数据对比分析参数空间。

Result: 该模型可在满足μ→eγ和ΔN_eff约束下解释Belle II观测到的B⁺→K⁺νν̄超出；当leptoquark质量高于12–14 TeV时，新物理贡献过小，理论预言回落至低于实验值的SM预期；由N_eff导出的leptoquark质量下限可能强于直接对撞机搜寻限制。

Conclusion: 该leptoquark模型是协调解释多个前沿观测现象（味异常、中微子质量、宇宙学参数）的可行框架，且宇宙学约束可提供比对撞机更严格的质量限制。

Abstract: Leptoquarks provide a common framework for addressing flavor anomalies, rare meson decays, and neutrino-mass generation. We study a model with two mixed scalar leptoquarks and three right-handed neutrinos that generates radiative Dirac neutrino masses and contributes to $B^+\to K^+ν\barν$, charged-lepton flavor violation, and the effective number of relativistic species. We show that the model can accommodate the Belle~II measurement of $B^+\to K^+ν\barν$ while satisfying the $μ\to eγ$ bound and the cosmological constraint on $ΔN_{\mathrm{eff}}$. When both leptoquark masses exceed approximately $12$--$14\,\mathrm{TeV}$, the new contribution to the $B$-meson decay becomes too small, and the prediction approaches the SM value, which lies below the Belle~II measurement. Interestingly, the lower bounds on the leptoquark mass scale derived from $N_{\mathrm{eff}}$ can be stronger than those from direct collider searches.

</details>


### [204] [New Physics in Ultra-High-Energy Neutrino Flavor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14624)
*Samiur R. Mir,Carlos A. Argüelles,Vedran Brdar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了下一代超高能（UHE）中微子实验通过地球掠射τ中微子结合冰内无线电探测技术，在100 PeV–10 EeV能区开展中微子味测量对新物理（以洛伦兹不变性破缺LIV为范例）的探测能力，发现其灵敏度相比现有所有限制可提升达20个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子味测量可检验超出标准模型的新物理；现有IceCube实验已测到PeV能区天体中微子味组成，但UHE能区尚属空白，而该能区因LIV效应随能量增强而极具新物理探针潜力。

Method: 以洛伦兹不变性破缺（LIV）为基准模型，理论模拟并评估下一代UHE中微子实验（结合地球掠射τ中微子与冰内无线电探测）在100 PeV–10 EeV能区利用味比测量对不同维数LIV算符的探测灵敏度，并对比单通道（仅τ中微子）与三味联合观测量的差异。

Result: UHE味比测量将成为四维及以上LIV算符最灵敏的探针，灵敏度较当前各领域最好限制提高最高达20个数量级；引入三味观测量可进一步提升灵敏度达一个数量级。

Conclusion: 超高能中微子味测量是探索高能极限下基础对称性（如洛伦兹不变性）破缺的强有力新途径，凸显未来UHE中微子实验在基础物理前沿的独特价值。

Abstract: Neutrino flavor measurements test physics beyond the Standard Model that modifies neutrino production, propagation, or detection. IceCube has measured the flavor composition of the astrophysical neutrino flux up to PeV energies. Next-generation experiments will extend flavor measurements to the ultra-high-energy (UHE) regime, $100\,\mathrm{PeV}$-$10\,\mathrm{EeV}$, by combining in-ice radio detection with Earth-skimming tau neutrinos. We present the first study of the new-physics reach of these projected UHE flavor measurements. We take Lorentz invariance violation (LIV) as a benchmark, since its effects grow as a power of the neutrino energy. We find that UHE flavor ratios will be the most sensitive probe of LIV for operators of dimension four and higher, improving on current bounds from all sectors by up to twenty orders of magnitude. Three-flavor observables improve this sensitivity by up to an order of magnitude over Earth-skimming tau neutrinos alone.

</details>


### [205] [Quantum Computing Enhanced Search for Wavelike Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14629)
*Yunjia Bao,Yu Cheng,Liang Jiang,Lian-Tao Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Grover搜索算法的量子计算增强传感方法，用于探测隐藏在宽频带中的弱随机暗物质信号；通过延长积分时间稳定相位并交替QSP角度符号来抑制误差，在超导transmon平台上实现了超越现有天体物理极限的轴子暗物质探测灵敏度，尤其在10–100 μeV质量区间，并有望覆盖QCD轴子能区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测宽频带中弱且随机的波状暗物质（如轴子）信号是当前实验物理的重大挑战，传统方法受限于灵敏度与噪声。

Method: 采用Grover搜索算法增强量子传感，引入长积分时间以稳定传感相位，并通过交替量子信号处理（QSP）角度符号来抑制近似误差的相干累积；在transmon量子比特平台上进行基准模拟。

Result: 在10^6个纠缠传感量子比特下，投影灵敏度突破现有天体物理限制，覆盖10–100 μeV轴子质量区间，可达QCD axion理论带；该协议可迁移至其他实验平台。

Conclusion: 量子计算增强传感为波状暗物质探测提供了新范式，显著拓展未探索参数空间，加速潜在发现。

Abstract: Identifying a weak, stochastic signal hidden within a broad frequency band is a central challenge in the search for wavelike dark matter. We explore quantum computing enhanced sensing based on the Grover search algorithm to address this problem and find that a long integration time stabilizes the accumulated sensing phase, reducing quantum signal processing (QSP) errors induced by dark matter fluctuations. We further propose alternating the signs of the QSP angles between oracle calls to suppress the coherent accumulation of the intrinsic QSP approximation errors during the search. Combining these strategies in a transmon-based benchmark, we obtain a projected sensitivity to axion dark matter beyond existing astrophysical limits. This opens access to substantial unexplored parameter space across the $10$-$100\,μ\mathrm{eV}$ mass range and offers an accelerated discovery channel for wavelike dark matter. With $10^6$ entangled sensing qubits, the projected sensitivity reaches the QCD axion band. Although illustrated with transmons, the protocol can be adapted to other experimental platforms for wavelike dark matter detection.

</details>


### [206] [A Grover-Accelerated Search for Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14641)
*Zachary Bogorad,Roni Harnik*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Grover算法加速类波暗物质搜索，通过将暗物质作为可重复的量子相位门，在超叠加态传感器中实现相位转移与数字化，从而在N个频率区间内实现√N倍的搜索加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波状暗物质搜索受限于其有限相干时间τ，而本文旨在探索如何突破该限制，利用量子计算技术提升搜索效率。

Method: 利用Grover算法，将具有有限相干时间τ的波状暗物质建模为可重复的量子相位门；在频率叠加态传感器中实现暗物质诱导相位的转移与数字化，并对qubit寄存器进行量子处理。

Result: 证明可在N_bins个频率区间中实现√N_bins倍的搜索加速，且无根本性物理障碍阻碍该方法跨多个相干时间的应用。

Conclusion: Grover算法可用于参数化加速波状暗物质搜索，暗物质本身可作为相干量子门参与量子计算过程，为未来量子传感与暗物质探测提供新范式。

Abstract: We show that Grover's algorithm can be used to parametrically accelerate wave-like dark matter searches. Despite having a finite coherence time $τ$, wavelike dark matter can imprint a coherent $O(1)$ phase onto a sensor through a dispersive coupling over an integration time which significantly exceeds $τ$. As such, we show that dark matter can be used as a repeatable quantum phase gate in a quantum device. If the sensor is prepared in a superposition of frequencies, we show that this phase can be transferred to a qubit register and digitized. The qubits are thus available for quantum computational processing in which dark matter can take part as a coherent gate. We show that Grover's algorithm can be used to speed up a search for dark matter in $N_{\rm bins}$ frequency bins by a factor of $\sqrt{N_{\rm bins}}$. Although the technological challenges to implementing such a search remain substantial, this demonstrates that there is no fundamental obstruction to accelerating wave-like dark matter searches with quantum computational techniques over many coherence times.

</details>


### [207] [A unified mean-field description of dense hadron matter constrained from $χ$EFT and lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15388)
*Md. Emanuel Hoque,Arunava Mukherjee,Sayantan Sharma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个相对论平均场理论（RMFT）来描述QCD禁闭相在宽温度和重子密度范围内的行为，通过两阶段贝叶斯分析约束介子-重子耦合，并结合核物质与格点QCD数据，显著改进了耦合参数与非线性介子自相互作用的约束，结果与中子星观测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为描述QCD禁闭相在宽温密区域的行为，特别是连接核物理、格点QCD与中子星天体物理观测的需求。

Method: 构建包含标量/矢量介子及至3 GeV重子共振态的RMFT；采用两阶段贝叶斯分析：第一阶段用ChEFT预测的中子物质能量约束6个耦合参数（高斯过程似然建模相关性），第二阶段以此后验为先验，结合格点QCD输入进一步约束RMFT参数。

Result: 获得对介子-重子耦合及非线性介子自相互作用更精确的约束，理论预测与中子星质量、半径和潮汐形变参数等天文观测一致。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯校准的RMFT框架成功统一了微观核理论、格点QCD与宏观中子星观测，为强相互作用物质状态方程提供了可靠且可外推的模型。

Abstract: We construct a relativistic mean field theory (RMFT) of hadrons in an attempt to describe the confined phase of QCD across a wide range of temperatures and baryon densities. This includes the scalar and vector mesons interacting with baryons and resonances up to $3$ GeV, including those which are not yet discovered but predicted within the quark model and lattice QCD. The baryon--meson couplings are constrained through a two-stage Bayesian analysis. In the first stage, we constrain six different couplings in our theory using six nuclear empirical parameters inferred from the chiral effective field theory predictions of the energy per nucleon for pure neutron matter over baryon densities $n_B \in [0.08, 0.32]~\mathrm{fm}^{-3}$, employing a Gaussian-process likelihood to account for correlations in the input data. In the second stage, this posterior is set as the prior for constraining different baryon--meson couplings in our RMFT construction, using lattice QCD input. The resulting posterior provides substantially improved constraints for different baryon--meson couplings and non-linear meson self-interactions, which are consistent with the current astrophysical observations of neutron star masses, radii and tidal deformability parameters.

</details>


### [208] [Earth-sized tests of long-range neutrino interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14642)
*Dyer F. Cardenas,Mauricio Bustamante*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究中微子可能经历一种新的长程力，该力由超轻中性玻色子介导，并导致大气中微子穿过地球时发生味依赖的振荡变化；作者精确计算了地球内部不同位置的味相关势能，并利用IceCube 8年数据对多种U(1)'对称性下的耦合强度设定了90%置信度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索中微子是否受一种新的、味依赖的长程力影响，该力由超轻玻色子介导，可能揭示超出标准模型的新物理。

Method: 精确计算中微子在地球内传播路径上每一点的味依赖势能（基于IceCube地球模型和中微子产生高度分布），并结合8年IceCube DeepCore公开数据及系统误差进行统计分析。

Result: 在介子质量10^{-15}–10^{-9} eV范围内，对14种U(1)'对称性中的8种（其势能区分ν_μ与ν_τ）给出了90% CL耦合上限；在<10^{-11}–10^{-10} eV区间，部分限制强于等效原理检验。

Conclusion: 精确建模味依赖势能显著提升对新力耦合的限制能力，为未来中微子实验探测超轻新玻色子提供了更严格约束。

Abstract: Neutrinos may experience a new long-range force that distinguishes their flavors, mediated by an ultralight neutral boson of a new $U(1)'$ gauge symmetry and sourced by the electrons and nucleons of ordinary matter. Atmospheric neutrinos that cross the Earth would then feel a flavor-dependent potential that alters their oscillations. When the range of the force is about one Earth radius or less, this potential varies from point to point along each neutrino's path, since it comes mostly from the matter near the neutrino. Earlier limits approximated it, either as constant along the path or as following the local density of matter. We compute it exactly at every point of each path, for the Earth model of the IceCube analysis and over the distribution of heights at which the neutrinos are produced, and use it to analyze the 8-yr public IceCube DeepCore sample with the systematic uncertainties of that analysis. For mediator masses from $10^{-15}$ to $10^{-9}$ eV, where the Earth alone sources the potential, we set upper limits at 90% confidence level on the coupling of eight of 14 $U(1)'$ symmetries, those whose potential differs between $ν_μ$ and $ν_τ$. A potential held at its value at the detector would weaken our limits by factors of about 1-3. Below about $10^{-11}$-$10^{-10}$ eV, depending on the symmetry, our limits are stronger than those from tests of the equivalence principle.

</details>


### [209] [Lepton flavor violation at mass dimension eight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14669)
*Julian Heeck,Mikheil Sokhashvili*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了标准模型有效场论中轻子味破坏算符的紫外完备性，发现某些模型在树图水平上仅产生d=8算符，而d=6贡献被圈图压低，从而拓展了可检验的轻子味破坏算符范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: d=8算符通常被视为d=6算符的次主导修正，但其可能在特定紫外模型中成为主导贡献，值得系统研究。

Method: 重新推导并列举了通过重标量和费米子实现的、涵盖d≤8轻子味破坏算符的全部树图级紫外完备模型。

Result: 发现部分紫外模型在树图水平上只生成d=8轻子味破坏算符，d=6贡献被圈因子甚至无关耦合进一步压低。

Conclusion: 这类‘偶然保护’的d=8场景显著扩大了当前实验可探测的轻子味破坏算符集合。

Abstract: Operators that violate lepton flavor first arise at mass dimension $d=5$ in the Standard Model Effective Field Theory, while charged lepton flavor violation first appears at $d=6$, inducing the typical exotic particle decays currently probed by experiments. Operators at $d=8$ carry the same quantum numbers as those at $d=6$ and are thus seemingly doomed to be subleading corrections. After (re)deriving the complete set of tree-level UV completions for lepton-flavor-violating $d\leq 8$ operators via heavy scalars and fermions, we show that some UV completions exclusively generate $d=8$ flavor violation at tree level, with $d=6$ contributions suppressed by loop factors and, in some cases, unrelated couplings. Such accidentally protected $d=8$ scenarios enlarge the set of testable lepton-flavor-violating operators.

</details>


### [210] [Scalar and pseudoscalar glueballonia: do they bind?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15873)
*Narmin Nasibova,Francesco Giacosa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究纯杨-米尔斯理论中由两个胶球组成的束缚态（glueballonia）的存在性，重点分析标量和赝标量通道。通过非相对论框架下的Yukawa势与接触项，结合格点YM数据和标量微扰理论，发现标量-标量及标量-赝标量胶球束缚态均可形成且在YM真空中稳定；其他通道虽具吸引力但因衰变而不稳定；并验证希格斯-希格斯束缚态不形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索纯杨-米尔斯理论中是否存在由两个胶球构成的束缚态（glueballonia），以深化对强相互作用非微扰结构的理解，并检验胶球间有效相互作用的性质。

Method: 采用近阈值下的非相对论框架，构建包含Yukawa型标量交换势与弥散接触项的有效势；利用标量微扰理论确定势参数，并结合格点YM模拟结果校准参数；分析不同胶球组合（标量-标量、标量-赝标量等）通道的束缚可能性。

Result: 标量-标量和标量-赝标量胶球束缚态均能形成，且在纯YM真空下稳定；赝标量-赝标量、标量-张量、张量-张量通道虽具吸引力，但所形成的束缚态会衰变为更轻胶球而不可观；希格斯-希格斯束缚态未形成，验证方法自洽。

Conclusion: 在纯杨-米尔斯理论中，标量和赝标量胶球可形成稳定的两体束缚态（glueballonia），表明胶球间存在足够强的有效吸引力；该结果稳健，支持胶球作为YM理论中重要非微扰激发态的角色。

Abstract: We investigate the existence of molecular two-glueball bound states, so-called glueballonia, in pure Yang-Mills (YM) theory. We mainly concentrate on the scalar and pseudoscalar channels, thus studying scalar-scalar and scalar-pseudoscalar bound states, respectively. Near the onset of binding, when a bound state first appears just below threshold, a nonrelativistic framework is justified. We employ an attractive scalar-exchange Yukawa potential together with a smeared contact term contribution and determine the critical attraction required for binding. For two scalar glueballs, we use the scalar dilaton effective theory to determine the parameters of the non-relativistic potential: the Yukawa interaction is generated by t- and u-channels involving the exchange of one scalar glueball, while the repulsion emerges by the s-channel annihilation and contact interaction. Using lattice-YM input to fix the dilaton parameters, we find that the attraction is strong enough: the scalar glueballonium binds, and the result is quite robust with respect to parameter variations. In a minimal scalar-pseudoscalar extension, the Yukawa attraction is again given by the t-channel scalar glueball, while the crossed u-channel pseudoscalar exchange induces an attractive local contribution, making the pseudoscalar glueballonium also bind in pure gauge. Importantly, both the scalar and pseudoscalar glueballonia are stable in the YM vacuum. Next, we briefly comment on the pseudoscalar-pseudoscalar, scalar-tensor, and tensor-tensor channels: all these channels are attractive too and could form bound states, which, however, would be unstable in pure YM due to decay into two lighter glueballs. Finally, as a cross-check, our formalism confirms that Higgs-Higgs bound state does not form.

</details>


### [211] [Symmetry adapted tensor networks and quantum classical geometric learning for hadronic tomography: a proton construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14711)
*Dustin Keller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个校准的质子广义横向动量依赖分布（GTMD）F_{1,1}模型，采用对称性分解的树状张量网络描述光前态，融合实验、格点与唯象输入；通过软减除、NLO演化等规范一致处理，揭示了前向输入对横向响应的强约束（81%），但无法约束非前向区中动量与动量转移的联合依赖关系；该自由度主导GTMD非前向结构，并可推广至中子、氘核及高自旋核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决GTMD建模中多源输入（实验、格点、唯象）在不同算符定义与尺度下的协同校准难题，并实现对核子及更复杂核系统GTMD结构的统一、可扩展描述。

Method: 基于共享光前态构建对称性分解的树状张量网络，内建颜色单态、交换统计及J_z/I_z守恒；结合软减除定义、一环非奇异匹配、NLO共线演化与非微扰联合闭合；用ART25横投影、HERA包容数据及格点/弹性/静态信息联合校准。

Result: 前向输入将x=0.4处横向响应范围压缩约81%；但非前向GTMD值（如0.588→0.378 GeV^{-2}）存在一个未被任何现有输入约束的形变自由度；生成了密集动量空间曲面与有限动量转移相空间图；量子线路经验证为有效特征映射与态编码。

Conclusion: 张量网络组织提供了面向组合扩展的结构化框架，同一子单元、算符层级与验证协议可自然延拓至中子、自旋-1氘核及自旋-3/2原子核，是处理快速增长的对称性与交换约束的可行路径。

Abstract: We construct a calibrated model of the proton generalized transverse-momentum-dependent distribution (GTMD) $F_{1,1}$ in the real, staple-even valence-$u$ sector at zero skewness. Experimental measurements, lattice matrix elements, and phenomenological distributions enter through a single shared light-front state, organized as a symmetry-resolved tree tensor network in which color-singlet structure, exchange statistics, and the additive $J_z$ and $I_z$ charges are built in. Classical and quantum geometric nodes represent the residual transverse response. The GTMD is defined with soft subtraction, one-loop nonsinglet matching, NLO collinear evolution, and an explicit nonperturbative joint closure, so that each input is used with its own operator convention and scale. Calibrating against correlated ART25 transverse projections, HERA inclusive data, and lattice, elastic, and static information gives a simple picture. The forward inputs narrow the attainable transverse response by about $81\%$ at $x=0.4$. The simultaneous dependence on quark momentum and momentum transfer, however, is untouched by any of them: a deformation that leaves every present input invariant moves the nonforward value from $0.588$ to $0.378\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$. Dense momentum-space surfaces and finite-transfer-window phase-space maps show the structure this freedom controls. The tensor-network organization is chosen with composition in mind. The same shared subunits, operator hierarchy, and qualification protocol extend to the neutron, the spin-1 deuteron, and spin-$3/2$ nuclei, where the number of symmetry and exchange constraints grows quickly and a structural representation becomes the practical route. The quantum circuits are verified feature maps and state encodings; their capacity and cost are measured alongside the classical blocks.

</details>


### [212] [Three-gluon vertex planar degeneracy: a perturbative explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14896)
*Daniel O. R. Azevedo,Nahuel Barrios,Marcela Peláez,Nicolás Wschebor*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Curci-Ferrari模型在Landau规范下，采用红外安全重整化方案，对杨-米尔斯理论中三胶子顶点的平面简并性给出微扰解释；一环计算表明树级形式因子主导顶点结构，且一环修正在整个相空间内近乎平坦，对称构型近似平均误差仅约3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释格点与Dyson-Schwinger研究中观察到的三胶子顶点平面简并性，并验证质量延拓Faddeev-Popov拉氏量能否用微扰方法刻画QCD红外动力学。

Method: 基于Curci-Ferrari模型和红外安全重整化方案，在三维和四维中对任意运动学构型进行一环微扰计算。

Result: 发现树级形式因子主导顶点结构，一环修正极为平坦；对称构型近似的平均误差峰值约为3%，最大偏差出现在软胶子区域，源于被压制的紫外共线发散。

Conclusion: 三胶子顶点的平面简并性具有自然的微扰起源，支持质量延拓Faddeev-Popov框架在红外区的有效性，并弥合了微扰、格点与非微扰方法之间的理解鸿沟。

Abstract: We provide a perturbative explanation for the planar degeneracy of the three-gluon vertex in Yang-Mills theory, using the Curci-Ferrari (CF) model in the Landau gauge. By employing an infrared safe renormalization scheme whose coupling remains regular at all scales, we perform a one-loop calculation for arbitrary kinematics in both three and four dimensions. Our results demonstrate that the observed planar degeneracy can be understood naturally due to perturbative reasons: i) the tree-level form factor dominates the vertex structure simply because perturbative corrections are suppressed; ii) the one loop corrections to this form factor remain remarkably flat across the allowed phase space. We quantify the deviations from the symmetric configuration, finding that the mean error of the symmetric approximation, in the case of the tree-level form factor, peaks at approximately 3% both in three and four dimensions, with the most significant departures occurring near soft-gluon configurations due to tamed ultraviolet collinear divergences. This work explains previous lattice and Dyson-Schwinger studies while reinforcing the capacity of the mass-extended Faddeev-Popov Lagrangian to capture complex infrared QCD dynamics through perturbative analytical techniques.

</details>


### [213] [Suppressed Direct Detection of Dark Matter from a Non-Invertible Selection Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15030)
*Riasat Sheikh,Koji Tsumura*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非可逆选择定则的机制，以解释暗物质-核子散射截面被压制的现象，并通过数值分析表明这种压制是可预测的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接探测实验未能发现暗物质信号，暗示暗物质与核子的散射截面被强烈压制，需解释其压制起源。

Method: 引入非可逆选择定则作为新物理机制，并开展数值分析以评估其对暗物质-核子散射截面的影响。

Result: 数值分析显示，该机制能自然导致被压制但可预测的暗物质-核子散射截面。

Conclusion: 非可逆选择定则为理解暗物质直接探测中信号缺失提供了一个有前景的新理论框架。

Abstract: Although the existence of dark matter is well established, its particle nature is still unknown. Direct detection experiments have been struggling to find any viable signal, implying that the dark matter--nucleon scattering cross-section must be suppressed. Here we propose a mechanism to explain the origin of this suppression by considering a non-invertible selection rule. Our numerical analysis shows that a suppressed yet predictable dark matter--nucleon scattering cross section is possible.

</details>


### [214] [Novel method for measuring the isovector partner of X(3872)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15158)
*Qiu-Lin Tan,Mao-Jun Yan,Chun-Sheng An,Cheng-Rong Deng,Xiao-Hai Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用三角奇点（TS）机制增强对X(3872)同位旋三重态伙伴Wc1的实验探测灵敏度，通过B0→πK D̄0D*0衰变中Ds1(2536)D̄*三角散射及耦合道动力学，在K D̄0D*0不变质量谱中产生可分辨结构，尤其在带电道中显著反映Wc1极点，为Belle II和LHCb提供新探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wc1作为X(3872)的同位旋三重态伙伴，预期在D D̄*阈值附近信号微弱，难以实验识别，亟需增强其探测灵敏度的新机制。

Method: 采用三角奇点（TS）机制分析B0→πK D̄0D*0衰变；构建Ds1(2536) D̄*三角散射图景；结合由Lippmann-Schwinger方程导出的D D̄*耦合道散射振幅，研究TS与耦合道动力学的协同效应。

Result: TS与耦合道相互作用在K D̄0D*0不变质量分布中产生特征结构：中性道对应X(3872)，带电道则呈现对邻近Wc1极点高度敏感的清晰结构。

Conclusion: TS机制可显著增强对弱信号Wc1的探测能力，为Belle II和LHCb实验提供了可行且互补的新搜索策略。

Abstract: The predicted isovector partner of the $X(3872)$, denoted as $W_{c1}$, is expected to produce only a relatively weak structure near the charged $D \bar{D}^*$ threshold, making its experimental identification challenging. In this work, we explore the triangle singularity (TS) mechanism as a means of enhancing the sensitivity to the $W_{c1}$ in the decays $B^0 \to πK \bar{D}^0 D^{*0}$. We investigate the $D_{s1}(2536) \bar{D}^*$ triangle rescattering mechanism, incorporating the coupled-channel $D \bar{D}^*$ scattering amplitudes obtained from the Lippmann-Schwinger equation. The interplay between the TS and the coupled-channel dynamics produces characteristic structures in the $K \bar{D}^0 D^{*0}$ invariant mass distributions associated with the $X(3872)$ and its isovector partner $W_{c1}$. In particular, the charged-channel contribution exhibits a distinct structure sensitive to the nearby $W_{c1}$ pole. Our results demonstrate that the TS mechanism provides a promising way to probe the otherwise weak manifestation of the $W_{c1}$, offering a complementary experimental strategy for searching for this predicted state at Belle II and LHCb.

</details>


### [215] [Rapidly Spinning Primordial Black Holes from Confinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15169)
*Michael Zantedeschi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在宇宙早期通过夸克-反夸克或磁单极-反磁单极对在禁闭相中因通量管/弦牵引而高速旋转并形成原初黑洞的机制，指出其自旋分布线性趋向极端值，平均无量纲自旋为2/3，并预言由此产生的近极端黑洞可产生可被引力波观测检验的独特自旋信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释原初黑洞（尤其是高自旋黑洞）的形成机制，并探索宇宙禁闭相（如QCD或其磁对偶）中粒子对动力学如何导致可观测的引力波信号。

Method: 基于禁闭相中通量管/弦对重粒子对（夸克-反夸克或磁单极-反磁单极）的束缚与加速机制，结合宇宙潮汐与弦弯曲引起的轨道偏转分析，推导黑洞形成的概率分布与自旋分布；辅以数值弦演化模拟验证理论预测。

Result: 得出原初黑洞的本征自旋密度线性趋向极端值，平均无量纲自旋为2/3；错过直接并合的粒子对可形成束缚态、辐射后坍缩为更轻但近极端黑洞，从而产生随机引力波背景。

Conclusion: 禁闭机制为原初黑洞提供了一种具有独特、可观测自旋特征的形成通道，该特征不依赖于具体模型细节，可由未来引力波观测直接检验。

Abstract: Confinement can turn widely separated particles into rapidly spinning primordial black holes. A flux tube pulls a heavy quark--antiquark pair together, converting its stored energy into relativistic motion. The same mechanism is possible in the magnetic dual, where a Higgs phase confines magnetic flux into a string joining a monopole--antimonopole pair. As the particles approach, cosmological tides and string bends can deflect their motion and make them miss. These disturbances can originate during inflation or be generated later. Black-hole formation then requires rare close passages from a smooth, broad encounter distribution. The resulting natal spin density therefore rises linearly to extremality, with mean dimensionless spin two thirds. Numerical string evolutions recover this law. Pairs that miss can remain bound, radiate and later collapse into lighter, near-extremal holes, sourcing a stochastic gravitational-wave background. Although the abundance remains model dependent, confinement leaves a unique spin signature that gravitational-wave observations can test.

</details>


### [216] [Enhancing Graviton--Photon Conversion through Galactic Magnetic Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15218)
*Hongliang Tian,Lei Wu,Yang Zhang,Bin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了等离子体色散对天体物理距离上引力子-光子相干转换的抑制作用，并提出银河系磁湍流可通过补偿等离子体引起的相位失配实现相位匹配，从而增强转换概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子体色散会抑制引力子-光子的相干转换，限制其在天体物理观测中的探测灵敏度，因此需寻找克服该抑制的机制。

Method: 理论建模与分析，引入银河系磁湍流补偿等离子体相位失配，推导长程传播下平均转换概率与投影磁场功率谱的关系。

Result: 在相位匹配窗口（惯性范围）内，该机制给出的引力波应变限比以往银河系转换界限更强，提升达两个数量级。

Conclusion: 银河系磁湍流可有效缓解等离子体对引力子-光子转换的抑制，为利用背景光子观测约束引力波提供新途径。

Abstract: Plasma dispersion suppresses coherent graviton-photon conversion over astrophysical distances. We show that Galactic magnetic turbulence enables phase matching by compensating the plasma-induced mismatch, allowing matched magnetic modes to contribute coherently. In the long path limit, the ensemble-averaged conversion probability grows linearly with propagation distance in the phase matched regime and is governed by the projected magnetic power spectrum. Applied to photon background observations, this mechanism yields characteristic strain limits that are stronger than previous Galactic conversion bounds in the inertial range phase matching window, with improvements of up to two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [217] [Tau polarization, new physics and CP violation in $ν_τ$-nucleus hyperon production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15252)
*E. Hernández,J. Nieves,J. E. Sobczyk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了τ中微子在核靶上的带电流相互作用，特别是通过末态π介子的角分布提取τ轻子极化信息，并提出一个与核效应无关的CP-奇异性观测量——π介子方位角不对称性，可用于探测标准模型之外的CP破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: τ中微子的带电流相互作用是标准模型味结构中约束最弱的部分；τ轻子极化可敏感探针新物理（包括CP破坏）耦合；而超子和粲重子产生过程涉及u→s和d→c跃迁，但实验总在原子核上进行，需严格处理核效应。

Method: 在冲量近似框架下，研究ν_τ + ^A Z → τ^- (→ π^- ν_τ) + Λ_c^+ X 和 arν_τ + ^A Z → τ^+ (→ π^+ arν_τ) + Λ X 这两类反应；推导任意核孔谱函数下的π介子能量与角分布；分析π介子相对于中微子—重子平面的方位角不对称性这一CP-奇观测量。

Result: 发现该方位角不对称性在标准模型中严格为零，其非零值直接表明CP破坏，且与强子形状因子无关；核效应（以^{16}O为例的局域费米气体模型）虽降低反应阈值，但π分布及极化信息仍接近自由核子情形；典型新物理场景下预测不对称性在百分之一量级或更低，且在Λ道中强烈依赖于跃迁形状因子。

Conclusion: 提出的CP-奇方位角不对称性是一个鲁棒的新物理探针，对核效应不敏感，为未来高能中微子实验（如DUNE、Hyper-K）利用τ中微子束流探索CP破坏提供了理论基础和可观测信号。

Abstract: Charged-current interactions of the $τ$ neutrino are the least constrained sector of the Standard Model (SM) flavour structure, and the polarisation of the produced $τ$ lepton probes new-physics (NP) couplings, including CP-violating ones. Hyperon and charmed-baryon production gives access to the $u\to s$ and $d\to c$ transitions, but any measurement would be made on nuclei, which calls for a proper treatment of nuclear effects. We therefore study the sequential reactions $ν_τ\,{}^A Z\toτ^-(\toπ^-ν_τ)\,Λ_c^+ X$ and $\barν_τ\,{}^A Z\toτ^+ (\toπ^+\barν_τ)\,ΛX$ within the impulse approximation. The energy and angular distributions of the pion, which encode the $τ$ polarization, are derived for an arbitrary nuclear hole spectral function. As for free nucleons, an azimuthal pion asymmetry with respect to the plane spanned by the neutrino and the outgoing baryon is shown to remain. This is a CP-odd observable that vanishes in the SM, so that a nonzero value would signal CP violation irrespective of the hadronic form factors. Local Fermi gas results for ${}^{16}$O show that nuclear effects lower the reaction thresholds but leave the pion distributions, and hence the polarization information, close to their free-nucleon form. For representative NP scenarios the predicted asymmetries are at the percent level or below, with a magnitude that, in the $Λ$ channel, depends strongly on the transition form factors.

</details>


### [218] [CP Violation in Beauty Baryon Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15273)
*Jian-Peng Wang,Qin Qin,Stephane Monteil,Fu-Sheng Yu,Yan-Xi Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了2025年重子扇区CP破坏发现这一里程碑式进展，整合理论框架（如微扰QCD、再散射机制）与实验突破（速率不对称测量），并提出构建多维观测量以推动高精度物理及超越标准模型新物理的探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补自1964年介子CP破坏发现以来长达六十年的重子CP破坏实验空白，深化对味物理和CP破坏机制的理解，并为探索超出标准模型的新物理提供新途径。

Method: 综合理论发展（如微扰QCD、再散射机制等动力学计算框架）与实验技术（基于衰变率不对称性的测量），系统梳理从理论预测到实验验证的全过程。

Result: 确认2025年重子扇区CP破坏的实验发现，确立其作为味物理新纪元的标志性成果，并提出多维观测量的构建方案。

Conclusion: 重子CP破坏的发现不仅验证了标准模型中CP破坏机制在重子体系的延拓，更开启了高精度检验标准模型与探查新物理的全新窗口；未来需依托多维观测量提升灵敏度与模型区分能力。

Abstract: The 2025 discovery of CP violation in the baryon sector marks a tectonic shift in flavor physics, bridging a sixty-year gap since the initial discovery of CP asymmetry in mesons. This review provides a comprehensive synthesis of the theoretical and experimental journey to this milestone. We integrate the development of dynamical calculation frameworks, such as perturbative QCD and the re-scattering mechanism, with the experimental breakthrough achieved through rate asymmetry measurements. Furthermore, we establish a roadmap for the future of the field by detailing the construction of multi-dimensional observables, which are instrumental in advancing the next era of precision physics and refining the search for new physics beyond the standard model.

</details>


### [219] [Six-Loop Renormalization in Quantum Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15366)
*Zeyu Li,Ming-xing Luo,Tong-Zhi Yang,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文解析计算了量子电动力学中六圈阶对电荷、费米子质量和费米子场标度演化的修正，结果在MSbar方案下对任意数量等电荷费米子成立，将QED重整化推进至六圈，并为高阶微扰理论行为提供新检验，同时为QCD六圈重整化计算奠定方法基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展量子电动力学的重整化至更高圈数，检验高阶微扰理论行为，并为量子色动力学的高阶计算提供方法支持。

Method: 在MSbar方案下，对任意数量等电荷费米子进行六圈阶解析计算。

Result: 获得了电荷、费米子质量和费米子场六圈标度演化的解析结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将QED重整化推进至六圈，提供了高阶微扰理论的新检验途径，并为QCD六圈重整化计算开辟了道路。

Abstract: We analytically compute the six-loop corrections to the scale evolution of the electric charge, fermion mass, and fermion field in quantum electrodynamics. The results are obtained in the $\MSbar$ scheme for an arbitrary number of equally charged fermions. They extend renormalization in quantum electrodynamics by one loop order and allow new tests of the behavior of perturbation theory at high orders. The methods developed here also open the way to six-loop computations of renormalization in quantum chromodynamics.

</details>


### [220] [On the reduction of negative-weight events in MC@NLO with Machine Learning techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15541)
*L. Beccatini,V. Fossa,S. Frixione,A. Valassi,M. Zaro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用机器学习（决策树和标准化流）重新定义S-事件MC@NLO短距离截面的方法，显著减少了负权重事例比例，仅带来轻微计算开销增加，是基于真实模拟环境的原理验证工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少蒙特卡洛模拟中负权重事例的比例，以提升计算效率与统计精度。

Method: 采用决策树和标准化流两种机器学习方法，对S-事件MC@NLO短距离截面进行重定义，在e+e−和pp碰撞的多种复杂物理过程中进行测试。

Result: 在多数情况下成功降低了负权重事例占比；对效果不显著的情形可识别主因；计算成本仅小幅上升。

Conclusion: 该ML重定义方法是一种可行且有前景的原理验证方案，虽尚未集成至公开代码，但已在完全真实的模拟环境中验证其有效性。

Abstract: We demonstrate that Machine Learning techniques can be successfully employed to re-define ${\mathbb S}$-event MC@NLO short-distance cross sections in a manner which in most cases leads to a reduction of their fractions of negative weights, with a modest increase of computational costs. We work with both Decision Trees and Normalizing Flows, and consider several processes, in both $e^+e^-$ and $pp$ collisions, that vastly differ in their kinematical and dynamical complexity. In those situations where the ML-induced reduction of negative weights is not significant we are able to identify the primary reason(s) for this behaviour, thus paving the way for it to be addressed in future studies. This work is still to be considered as a proof of concept, given that the proposed methodology is not implemented in a public code; yet, it is based upon a fully realistic simulation environment, in which no simplifying assumptions are made.

</details>


### [221] [A Non-Relativistic Model of Heavy Quarkonium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15628)
*Christian Farina*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非相对论、自旋依赖的重夸克偶素唯象模型，结合矩阵力学、变分法和微扰理论求解能谱，结果与实验数据吻合良好，适合作为量子力学教学范例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个适合教学且能较好描述重夸克偶素能谱的非相对论自旋依赖唯象模型。

Method: 基于含超精细自旋-自旋相互作用项的自旋依赖哈密顿量，导出类氢薛定谔方程；采用矩阵力学对角化哈密顿量求解，并用变分法优化能量；一阶微扰理论引入自旋-轨道修正。

Result: 所得能谱与常规重夸克偶素的实验数据符合良好。

Conclusion: 该框架融合了矩阵力学、变分法与微扰理论，兼具物理直观性与教学适用性，适用于具备量子力学基础的学生。

Abstract: We develop a non-relativistic, spin-dependent, phenomenological model of heavy quarkonium. The model is based on a hydrogen-like Schrödinger equation derived from a spin-dependent Hamiltonian that includes a hyperfine spin--spin interaction term. The resulting system is solved using matrix mechanics by diagonalizing the Hamiltonian to obtain the energy spectrum. The energies are further optimized using variational methods. Spin--orbit corrections are included at first order in perturbation theory. The resulting spectrum shows good agreement with experimental data for conventional heavy quarkonium states. The framework combines matrix mechanics, variational methods, and perturbation theory, and is therefore suitable for students familiar with quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [222] [Deeply inelastic scattering as an inverse problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15665)
*Hsiang-nan Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于散射振幅解析性的高能含包含QCD过程新形式理论，区别于传统因子化定理；利用复重虚康普顿散射的色散关系分析质子深度非弹性散射，通过逆问题方法直接从色散关系求解质子结构函数F2和FL，并仅以质子质量为非微扰参数实现了与实验数据的良好符合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统因子化定理的局限，建立一种概念上更根本、更少依赖模型假设的QCD高能过程描述框架，尤其针对非微扰低质量区域的结构函数计算。

Method: 基于双深度虚康普顿散射在复重子质量平面上的色散关系，输入重子散射振幅的微扰结果，将结构函数求解表述为一个逆问题；引入光子虚度Q²标度律和xB→1阈值区夸克计数律等先验信息；仅以质子质量作为主导精度下的唯一非微扰参数。

Result: 成功预测了中等xB（0.18–0.65）和宽Q²范围（10²–10⁴ GeV²）内的质子结构函数F2和FL，与实验数据一致；可从中提取部分子分布函数；同样形式用π介子质量作参数也解释了π介子F2^π数据。

Conclusion: 该解析性形式为QCD强子结构提供了新理论路径，显著减少非微扰输入，验证了色散方法在连接微扰与非微扰QCD中的有效性。

Abstract: A novel formalism for high-energy inclusive QCD processes based on analyticity of a scattering amplitude is proposed, which is conceptually distinct from the conventional factorization theorem. We illustrate how to analyze proton deeply inelastic scattering by means of the dispersion relations for double deeply virtual Compton scattering defined on a complex baryon mass plane. Inputting the perturbative results for the heavy baryon scattering amplitudes, we solve for the proton structure functions $F_2$ and $F_L$ in the nonperturbative low-mass region directly from the dispersion relations, i.e., as an inverse problem. The prior information, including the scaling of the structure functions in photon virtuality $Q^2$ and the quark counting rules for the threshold region with the Bjorken variable $x_B\to 1$, is incorporated. The only nonperturbative parameter at leading-power precision in $1/Q$ is the proton mass, with which we demonstrate the agreement between our predictions and the data for $F_2$ and $F_L$ at intermediate $x_B=0.18$-0.65 in a wide range of $Q^2=10^2$-$10^4$ GeV$^2$. Parton distribution functions can be extracted from the obtained structure functions as a byproduct of the analysis by treating the associated factorization formulas as another inverse problem. The same formalism with the pion mass as the nonperturbative parameter explains the data for the pion structure function $F_2^π$.

</details>


### [223] [Accelerating exact solutions of relativistic hydrodynamics and Levy-shaped freeze-out sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15727)
*Máté Csanád,Ágoston Benyó,Márton Nagy,Bence Szentkuti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了重离子碰撞中π介子源的Lévy稳定分布与流体动力学半径标度律共存的'Levy hydro puzzle'问题，指出源尾部幂律行为并非由理想流体动力学自身产生，而是源于初始压力幂律或特定的‘dressing’方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释重离子碰撞实验中观测到的π介子源Lévy稳定性与R²∝1/m_T流体动力学标度律同时存在的矛盾现象（即'Levy hydro puzzle'）。

Method: 基于理想流体动力学框架，分析冻结超曲面上压强对粒子源的约束；推导源尾部幂律行为的两种实现路径；构造新的相对论性自相似势流（λ-PF）及Gubser流的新dressing形式，并分析其源分布性质。

Result: 证明理想流体动力学本身不生成幂律源尾：幂律必须来自幂律压强（要求1/r型加速度）或人为引入的dressing；λ-PF经Hubble-type dressing可产生Lévy稳定源；等温dressed Gubser流则通过1/r加速度路径给出在测量范围内呈Lévy-like且满足m_T标度律的源。

Conclusion: Lévy-like源分布与流体动力学m_T标度律在理想流体框架下相容，但前者并非动力学自发产生，而是依赖于初始条件或人为构造的自由度；该结论为理解高能核碰撞中的动量空间关联提供了新视角。

Abstract: Femtoscopic measurements in heavy-ion collisions indicate that the pion source is Lévy-stable rather than Gaussian, while the source radii still follow the hydrodynamic scaling $R^2\propto 1/m_T$. We call this coexistence the Lévy hydro puzzle. For a perfect fluid with $\varepsilon=κp$ and $p=nT$, flow and pressure form a closed system, and density and temperature can be ``dressed'' by an arbitrary comoving function, generalising a freedom known from Hubble-type solutions to arbitrary flows. We show that, at nonzero pair momentum, the source is bounded by the pressure on the freeze-out hypersurface, so its tail can never be heavier than the pressure tail. This yields a criterion for power-law source tails with two routes: either the outer flow is ultra-relativistic and the temperature grows at least linearly (Hubble-type solutions), or the flow is slow, the temperature uniform, and the proper acceleration falls off as $1/r$. We present a new family of relativistic, accelerating, self-similar potential flows ($λ$-PF) and a new dressing of Gubser's flow. The acceleration of $λ$-PF generates no power-law tail, but with a Hubble-type dressing its source tail is Lévy-stable in a range of parameters, generalising the Hubble-type Lévy source to accelerating cores. The isothermally dressed Gubser flow realises the $1/r$ route: its tail exponent lies outside the Lévy-stable range, but over the measured range it gives Lévy-like sources with $1/R^2$ linear in $m_T$. Thus Lévy-like sources and the hydrodynamic $m_T$ scaling are compatible in perfect-fluid hydrodynamics, but hydrodynamics does not create the power-law tail: it is either inherited from a power-law pressure, requiring a $1/r$ acceleration, or put in through the dressing. We outline extensions towards non-spherical geometries and elliptic flow.

</details>


### [224] [Radiative stability of species-separated Kaluza--Klein spectra in warped geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15753)
*M. A. Anacleto,F. A. Brito,E. Passos,Joao R. L. Santos,J. A. V. Campos,I. E. L. M. Santana*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Randall和Riojas提出的通过标量梯度算符生成的物种依赖第五方向光锥，在弯曲额外维中分离规范场与费米子KK能标的机制是否能抵御量子修正。结果表明，轻胶子情形下该分离缺乏微扰保护，而重胶子、慢夸克情形则更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验物种依赖第五方向光锥导致的规范场与费米子KK能标分离在量子修正下的稳定性。

Method: 分析夸克圈图对胶子传播速度的修正，以及胶子交换对夸克速度的反作用；结合强耦合估计与有效理论，评估KK模对夸克速度的一阶修正大小。

Result: 轻胶子基准下（胶子5.5 TeV，夸克伙伴10.74 TeV），KK修正达O(1)量级，远超质量序所能吸收，故无微扰保护；反之，慢夸克+重胶子基准的能序鲁棒性约高十倍。

Conclusion: 第五方向光锥诱导的能标分离在量子层面并非普适稳定，其鲁棒性高度依赖于具体粒子谱安排，重规范玻色子/慢费米子构型更具理论可行性。

Abstract: Species-dependent fifth-direction lightcones, generated by scalar-gradient operators, can separate gauge and fermion Kaluza--Klein (KK) scales in a warped extra dimension, as recently shown by Randall and Riojas. In this work, we study whether this separation survives quantum corrections. Quark loops can only move the gluon speed within the interval spanned by the bare speeds. Gluon exchange pulls the quark speed toward the gluon speed, with a one-loop coefficient strongly enhanced by fifth-component exchange when the gluon is the slow species. We set the size of this effect using the strong-coupling estimates for a light-gluon benchmark, where a $5.5$~TeV gluon sits below quark partners at $10.74$~TeV. Within the effective theory, the Kaluza--Klein (KK) modes then correct the quark speed at order one. That correction is several times larger than the mass ordering can absorb, so the light-gluon ordering has no perturbative protection. The reverse benchmark, with slow quarks and a heavy gluon, keeps its ordering roughly ten times more robust.

</details>


### [225] [A triple-threat challenge for $\mathit{T}$ and $\mathit{S}$ at two loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15807)
*Ulrich Haisch,Sara Martellini,Ben A. Stefanek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Z2对称实单态标量扩展标准模型中电弱精密观测量T和S参数的两圈贡献，通过全理论精确计算、对数增强项的通用论证以及有效场论方法，系统分析了新物理对希格斯耦合的影响，并给出了当前及未来电弱精密测量对轻单态的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Z2对称实单态标量扩展标准模型中，T和S参数首次在两圈阶出现贡献，其精确预测存在挑战；同时需理解新物理如何以整体对称方式修改希格斯耦合，导致T参数仅在高于一圈阶才出现破缺。

Method: 采用三种互补方法：（1）在完整理论中进行精确两圈计算；（2）基于单态重标度希格斯-规范玻色子耦合破坏Higgs与Goldstone发散抵消的普适论证，导出对数增强项；（3）在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架下，将对数项和质量相关项归因于修改希格斯动能项的维度六算符的一圈矩阵元，其余常数项来自两圈匹配。

Result: 系统给出了T和S参数的完整两圈表达式，阐明了对数增强起源及其与希格斯耦合重标度、幺正性破缺的联系；验证了SMEFT描述的有效性；指出对轻单态，高于维度六的算符效应不可忽略。

Conclusion: 本工作为一类新物理场景（即以整体对称方式修改希格斯耦合、仅在高于一圈阶引发T参数破缺）提供了通用计算范式；强调在电弱精密检验中，对轻标量新物理，必须考虑高于维度六的高阶效应。

Abstract: In the $Z_2$-symmetric real singlet-scalar extension of the Standard Model (SM), the oblique parameters $\mathit{T}$ and $\mathit{S}$ first receive contributions at two loops, making their precise prediction a challenge. We take on this challenge with a triple threat. First, we perform an exact two-loop calculation in the full theory. Second, we derive the logarithmically enhanced terms from a general argument, according to which the singlet rescales the Higgs couplings to the electroweak gauge bosons and thereby spoils the cancellation of ultraviolet divergences between Higgs and Goldstone contributions. Third, we reproduce the results in the SM effective field theory, where the logarithms and mass-dependent terms stem from one-loop matrix elements of the dimension-six operator that modifies the Higgs kinetic term, and the remaining constants from two-loop matching. Our calculations provide a blueprint for new-physics scenarios in which the leading effects modify the Higgs couplings in a custodially symmetric way, so that custodial-symmetry violation, and hence $\mathit{T}$, arises only beyond one loop. Finally, we derive constraints from current and projected electroweak precision measurements and show that effects beyond dimension six matter for light singlets.

</details>


### [226] [The three-loop QCD helicity amplitudes for $V$+jet production at hadron colliders in full color](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15836)
*Thomas Gehrmann,Petr Jakubčík,Andreas von Manteuffel,Cesare Carlo Mella,Lorenzo Tancredi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了三圈QCD修正对矢量玻色子（Z、W±、γ*）与一个喷注在夸克-反夸克湮灭过程及其运动学交叉过程中的产生，保留了颜色数的完整依赖性；发现红外有限余项可由少量有理函数和特殊函数表示，表明振幅所处的函数空间被显著限制；并利用数值传输方法实现了全运动学区域的快速可靠计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算高阶QCD修正对强子对撞机上矢量玻色子加喷注过程的理论预言至关重要，尤其为LHC物理分析提供基准精度，并检验微扰QCD在高阶下的结构特性。

Method: 采用解析与数值结合的方法：系统计算三圈费曼图，通过红外减除得到有限振幅；将结果表达为有理函数与特殊函数（如多重对数、椭圆积分等）的组合；引入数值传输技术实现全相空间稳定高效求值。

Result: 首次获得全色依赖的qq̄→V+jet三圈QCD振幅的解析结构；揭示其红外有限部分仅占据远小于完整费曼积分函数空间的受限子空间；开发出可在任意运动学点快速、稳定求值的开源数值程序。

Conclusion: 三圈QCD振幅展现出比预期更强的函数论约束，支持‘振幅几何’与‘符号字母表简化’等现代散射振幅思想；该结果为后续更高阶计算、自动化工具发展及精确电弱物理建模奠定基础。

Abstract: We compute the three-loop Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) corrections to the production of a vector boson $V=Z,W^\pm,γ^*$ and a jet in quark-antiquark annihilation and its kinematic crossings, retaining full dependence on the number of colors. By expressing the infrared-finite remainders in terms of a small set of rational and special functions, we find that the amplitudes live in a strongly restricted function space, compared to the full space of the related Feynman integrals. We provide fast and reliable evaluation in all kinematic regions using numerical transport.

</details>


### [227] [The Dark Side of the Periodic Table: Theory and Signatures of Nuclear Atomic Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15852)
*Pouya Asadi,Zackaria Chacko,David Curtin,Micah Mellors,Ethan T. Neil*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了镜像双希格斯（MTH）模型中稳定镜像中子作为主要暗物质、少量镜像原子核作为核原子暗物质（naDM）的理论与观测特征，解释了暗物质与重子密度相近的‘物质巧合问题’，并预测了独特的双阶段暗声学振荡信号及未来探测方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决暗物质与重子密度相近的‘物质巧合问题’（Ω_DM ≈ 5Ω_b），利用镜像Z₂对称性关联可见与暗物质的质量尺度和数密度。

Method: 分析镜像核子冻结过程与镜像大爆炸核合成（BBN），估算核原子暗物质丰度及可形成的镜像元素；研究镜像中子-质子散射对暗声学振荡（DAO）的影响；构建超对称镜像Froggatt-Nielsen模型，通过味子自发破缺Z₂对称性生成所需费米子谱与希格斯势。

Result: 镜像BBN可产生至镜像碳的重元素；约百分比量级的镜像核子质量分裂可支持显著但次主导的naDM成分；强镜像中子-质子散射导致几乎全部暗物质参与双阶段DAO，形成可观测宇宙学信号；Higgs门户等相互作用使该模型成为未来方向性直接探测的理想目标。

Conclusion: 该MTH框架为暗物质本质提供了自洽且可检验的解释，兼具理论动机与丰富可观测现象，涵盖宇宙学、粒子物理与天体物理多方面检验途径。

Abstract: We study the theory and signatures of a Mirror Twin Higgs (MTH) scenario in which stable twin neutrons constitute most of the dark matter (DM), while a subdominant component of twin elements survives as nuclear atomic dark matter (naDM). This framework offers a solution to the $matter~coincidence~problem$ ($Ω_{\rm DM}\simeq5Ω_b$), as the twin $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry can correlate the visible and DM mass scales and their number densities. We analyze twin nucleon freeze-out and the onset of twin Big Bang nucleosynthesis (BBN) to estimate the total naDM abundance, and determine which elements in the dark periodic table could be populated. An appreciable but subdominant naDM component generally requires a fractional nucleon mass splitting near the percent level, with twin-BBN creating heavy elements up to twin carbon. Strong twin neutron-proton scattering allows nearly all of the dark matter, not only the atomic sub-component, to participate in $dark~acoustic~oscillations$ (DAO) at early times, giving rise to a characteristic two-stage DAO signature that may be detected with future cosmological probes. Interactions between the twin and visible sectors, including the irreducible Higgs portal, make this scenario a well-motivated target for future directional direct-detection searches, and the naDM sub-component is expected to exhibit highly exotic astrophysics. We outline a supersymmetric twin Froggatt-Nielsen realization in which spontaneous $\mathbb{Z}_2$ breaking in the flavon sector can generate the required fermion spectrum and Higgs potential.

</details>


### [228] [Beyond the Standard Model EFT: Stress Testing the Electroweak Phase Transition in the Real Singlet Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15856)
*Morgan E. Cassidy,Zhongtian Dong,Peisi Huang,Ian M. Lewis*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用有效场论方法研究了无Z2对称性的实单态标量扩展标准模型中TeV尺度新物理对电弱相变的影响，发现希格斯三线性耦合对TeV新物理敏感，而引力波信号和共振双希格斯产生率则相对稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实单态标量扩展模型虽能实现强一阶电弱相变，但缺乏额外CP破坏源，无法解释宇宙重子不对称性，因此需要TeV尺度的新物理补充。

Method: 采用有效场论方法，在标量势中引入截止尺度Λ=1 TeV、最高达六维的有效算符，系统分析其对电弱相变、希格斯三线性耦合、共振双希格斯产生及引力波信号的影响及相关性。

Result: 在实现强一阶电弱相变的参数区域内，希格斯三线性耦合的变化对TeV尺度新物理敏感；而引力波信号特征与最大共振双希格斯产额（无其他产生机制时）在弱耦合TeV及以上新物理存在下仍保持稳健。

Conclusion: 实单态模型中，电弱相变强度与希格斯三线性耦合可作为TeV新物理的探针，而引力波和共振双希格斯信号则更适合作为该模型相变动力学的稳健判据。

Abstract: The real singlet scalar extension of the Standard Model is a much studied benchmark model for a strong first order electroweak phase transition (EWPT) and its role in electroweak baryogenesis. However, this model is not sufficient to generate the baryon asymmetry of the Universe, since it does not introduce any additional sources of CP violation. That is, additional new physics is still expected in the TeV range. In this paper we use effective field theory (EFT) methods to study the effects of new physics on the EWPT in the real singlet model with no $Z_2$. Effective operators up to dimension six with a cutoff scale of $Λ=1$ TeV are included in the scalar potential. We study the correlations between a strong first order EWPT, the Higgs trilinear coupling, resonant di-Higgs production, and gravitational wave signals in the EFT. We find that in the real singlet model with a strong first order EWPT the allowed region of the Higgs trilinear coupling is sensitive to TeV scale new physics, while the predictions of the gravitational wave signal and maximum resonant di-Higgs rate (with no additional production mechanisms) are robust against additional weakly coupled physics at or above the TeV scale.

</details>


### [229] [A Scattering Phase Space Primer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15861)
*Francesca Acanfora*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次全面解析处理了2→3粒子散射过程的相空间和微分截面分布，采用非恒定矩阵元并保持所有粒子质量非零，利用Gram行列式推导出精确闭合表达式，并构建完整的物理相空间边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2→3散射过程在超越标准模型的新物理搜索（如矢量玻色子融合）中至关重要，但其解析处理面临重大挑战，通常依赖数值方法；本文旨在提供准确、普适且可计算的解析方案。

Method: 以Gram行列式为洛伦兹不变运动学的核心语言，推导非零质量、非恒定矩阵元下2→3过程的微分截面精确闭式表达式，并显式构造完整物理相空间边界。

Result: 获得适用于任意质心能量、粒子质量和相互作用类型的2→3过程通用解析结果，证明m≠0且|M|≠1问题可通过恰当运动学方法实现计算可行性。

Conclusion: 本文既是2→3过程解析计算的入门教程，也是实用参考手册，为高精度理论预言与实验分析提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: The kinematic phase space is fundamental to all particle scattering processes across experiments - from lepton and hadron colliders to dark matter searches. While $2 \to 2$ scatterings are well understood, the simplest non-trivial case of three-body final states presents major analytic challenges that are typically circumvented by numerical methods. This paper provides the first comprehensive analytic treatment of $2 \to 3$ phase space and cross section distributions that uses a non-constant matrix element and keeps all particle masses non-zero. This is fundamental for an accurate description of crucial configurations for Beyond Standard Model searches, e.g. Vector Boson Fusion. Using Gram Determinants as our primary language for Lorentz-invariant kinematics, we derive exact, closed-form expressions for differential cross section distributions and explicitly construct the complete physical phase space boundaries. This paper serves as both a pedagogical introduction and a practical reference for the analytic computation of realistic $2 \to 3$ processes, showing how the $m\neq0, |{\cal M}|\neq 1$ problem is computationally tractable through proper kinematic methods. The results apply universally across all $2 \to 3$ scatterings regardless of center-of-mass energy, particle masses, or interaction type.

</details>


### [230] [Toward the minimal set of states in axion superradiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15865)
*Enrico Cannizzaro,Thomas F. M. Spieksma,Samuel J. Witte*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轴子等轻自旋-0粒子在黑洞超辐射过程中的自相互作用效应，通过追踪近300个耦合准束缚态的演化，揭示了超辐射云的复杂动力学行为及其对观测的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于轴子等轻粒子的自相互作用会快速重分布能量和角动量，导致传统方法难以在大参数空间内计算超辐射演化及相关可观测量，亟需更全面的动力学建模。

Method: 扩展自相互作用系统的研究框架，数值演化近300个耦合准束缚态；对10 M⊙黑洞演化一个Salpeter时标，对10⁴和10⁸ M⊙黑洞演化1 Gyr，系统识别各轴子质量下主导演化的最小态集。

Result: 多数参数下演化随弱束缚态增加而收敛，至少需203个准束缚态；小部分区域无收敛解，出现n≳45的高主量子数态激发；云半径可达常规估计的多个数量级；引力波跃迁可抑制超辐射增长；引力波谱及环境相互作用比以往认知更丰富。

Conclusion: 轴子超辐射云的动力学远比此前认为的复杂，忽略高阶态或自相互作用将导致对黑洞自旋衰减、引力波信号及天体物理约束的严重误判。

Abstract: Observational searches for black hole superradiance require predicting the growth and evolution of quasi-bound states on astrophysical timescales. For axions (and most light spin-0 particles), self-interactions efficiently redistribute energy and angular momentum from the fastest-growing superradiant states into higher-order, more weakly bound states, and do so on short timescales. This has made it difficult to compute even the most mundane of observables across large regions of parameter space. In this work we extend the study of self-interacting systems, following the evolution of nearly 300 coupled quasi-bound states. We evolve a $10\,M_\odot$ black hole over a Salpeter timescale, and $10^4$ and $10^8\,M_\odot$ black holes over a Gyr, identifying for each axion mass the minimal set of states required to describe the evolution of the black hole and the shape of the superradiant cloud. In most cases the evolution converges as increasingly weakly bound states are added; we identify no less than 203 distinct quasi-bound states that play a role in establishing this convergence. In small regions of parameter space, however, we find no converged solution, and show that broad resonant features and nearly degenerate scattering rates redistribute mass to bound states with principal quantum numbers $n \gtrsim 45$. We further show that the cloud can extend to radii orders of magnitude larger than usually assumed, that gravitational-wave level transitions can prevent the growth of superradiant states, and that both the gravitational-wave spectrum and the interaction with the environment are far richer than previously appreciated.

</details>


### [231] [Ensemble Learning with Unsupervised and Semi-Supervised Models for New Physics Searches at Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15867)
*Alexandre Alves,Eduardo da Silva Almeida,Douglas Roberto de Matos Pimentel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于集成学习的模型无关的无监督/半监督新物理搜寻策略，通过结合多种分类器提升对未知信号的鲁棒性与可靠性，虽略微牺牲单模型最优性能，但显著增强了跨多种信号假设的检测稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型存在关键局限，但缺乏明确实验信号指导新物理模型构建；现有无监督学习方法性能依赖特定信号假设和模型归纳偏置，泛化能力差。

Method: 采用无监督与半监督集成学习策略，将多种分类器组合成集成模型，并系统评估其整体性能与分类鲁棒性。

Result: 集成模型在多数情况下虽略逊于最优单个模型，但在多种信号假设下展现出更稳健、可靠的异常识别能力。

Conclusion: 集成方法提升了新物理搜寻的模型无关性与可信度，降低了未知信号被遗漏的风险，为LHC等实验提供更具鲁棒性的数据分析框架。

Abstract: New physics beyond the Standard Model is widely believed to exist motivated by key limitations of the current theory. However, in the absence of undisputed experimental signals, there is little guidance on what type of extended physics model should be built. To address the challenge of training machine learning classifiers to spot unknown phenomena, many algorithms have been applied to the task of either identifying anomalous events relative to background expectations or cluster collision events together in classes of similarity. However, as in the supervised case, the no free lunch theorem assures that the performance of unsupervised models depends on the specific signal hypothesis and their inductive biases, and the successful identification of a given signal by a particular algorithm does not guarantee comparable performance for other signals. In this study, we take a step toward a more model-independent unsupervised and semi-supervised search strategy by combining classifiers into ensembles. Our goal is addressing two complementary aspects of unsupervised ensemble learning - overall performance and robustness of the classification task from a thorough assessment of ensemble models. We show that, despite sacrificing some performance relative to the best individual model in the majority of cases, the ensembles provide robust and reliable classification predictions across several signal hypotheses, increasing confidence that genuinely unknown new physics will not pass unnoticed.

</details>


### [232] [Discovering new physics with semitauonic $b$-hadron decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15869)
*Markus Prim,Dean J. Robinson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了重味强子半轻衰变中观测到的轻子味普适性破缺（LFUV）反常现象，探讨其作为超出标准模型新物理信号的可能性，并分析了当前理论与实验研究的现状、挑战及未来精确检验的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重味强子半轻衰变中观测到的轻子味普适性破缺（LFUV）反常可能预示着超出标准模型的新物理，亟需系统、自洽且无偏的新物理解读框架。

Method: 采用理论综述与实验分析相结合的方法，梳理LFUV相关的核心理论概念、实验约束条件，并评估当前分析方法的局限性与未来高精度研究所需克服的关键挑战。

Result: 明确了当前LFUV反常解释面临的主要理论不确定性与实验系统误差问题，指出了提升强子矩阵元计算精度、改进实验统计量与控制背景等关键方向。

Conclusion: LFUV反常仍是新物理的重要候选信号，但需更精确的理论预测与实验测量协同验证；未来高亮度B工厂与LHCb升级实验将起决定性作用。

Abstract: Anomalous lepton flavor universality violation (LFUV) observed in semileptonic decays of beauty hadrons may be the first signal of new physics (NP) beyond the Standard Model. We review the essential theoretical concepts and experimental considerations required for self-consistent and unbiased NP interpretations of these LFUV anomalies. We examine the status of present-day analyses, and assess the core challenges to the future precision studies that will be required to conclusively establish or reject an NP signal, as well as promising strategies to resolve them.

</details>


### [233] [Dual-species atom interferometry for axion dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15874)
*Maike Reckermann,John Carlton,Michael Werner,Agnese Mariotti,Guillermo A. Perez-Lobato,Nathaniel Sherrill,Naceur Gaaloul,Dennis Schlippert,Elina Fuchs*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双原子种类原子干涉仪方案，通过利用两种原子的质量差异和差分测量，显著抑制重力梯度噪声（GGN），提升对轴子暗物质（特别是轴子-胶子耦合）的探测灵敏度。10米基线的方案已在汉诺威VLBAI实验中接近现有实验室约束，而计划中的100米基线CERN AICE实验有望在特定轴子质量区间超越当前最优实验室限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子干涉仪可探测由轴子暗物质引起的原子质量和跃迁频率振荡，但其灵敏度受限于重力梯度噪声（GGN）。

Method: 提出双原子种类（dual-species）原子干涉测量序列，利用两种原子的质量差异实现GGN抑制（最高达三个数量级）与信号增强；结合详细噪声建模与 realistic 实验参数分析。

Result: 10米基线双原子干涉仪（如汉诺威VLBAI）对轴子衰变常数的探测灵敏度已接近当前领先实验室约束；100米基线AICE实验可在轴子质量范围 $10^{-19} - 3\times10^{-15}\,\text{eV}/c^2$ 内将灵敏度提升最多两个数量级，从而超越现有实验室约束。

Conclusion: 双原子种类干涉方案是突破GGN限制、提升轴子暗物质探测能力的有效途径，为地面长基线原子干涉仪探索超轻暗物质提供了新范式。

Abstract: Atom interferometers are sensitive to oscillations of the masses and transition frequencies of atoms, which can be induced by axion dark matter. The main limitation is the sensitivity to gravity gradient noise (GGN). We propose a dual-species atom interferometry sequence that provides a suppression of GGN by up to three orders of magnitude and a sensitivity enhancement via the mass difference of the two atoms. We show that, under realistic experimental assumptions, a 10m-baseline dual-species atom interferometer is more sensitive to the axion gluon coupling than a 100m single-species gradiometer. Including a comprehensive noise analysis, we demonstrate that the Very Long Baseline Atom Interferometer (VLBAI) in Hannover with a baseline of 10m can probe the axion decay constant at levels almost competitive with leading laboratory constraints. The proposed 100m-baseline Atom Interferometer CERN Experiment (AICE) can extend this sensitivity by up to two orders of magnitude, surpassing laboratory constraints for axion masses of $10^{-19}\;\text{eV}/c^2 \lesssim m_a \lesssim 3\times10^{-15}\;\text{eV}/c^2$.

</details>


### [234] [Dark Matter Velocimetry with Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15878)
*Miguel Angel Soto Alcaraz,Doojin Kim,Kyoungchul Kong,Myeonghun Park*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用双节点量子传感器网络通过非局域交换测量来探测银河系晕中暗物质的速度分布，特别是区分标准晕模型（SHM）与受Gaia数据启发的径向各向异性模型（SHM++），并指出地球自转可打破二者在年调制下的简并，从而将暗物质探测拓展为对银河系结构的探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用量子传感器网络探测暗物质不仅关注粒子性质，更希望揭示银河系晕的运动学结构；现有模型（如SHM）与更真实的各向异性模型（如SHM++）在年调制下存在简并，需新方法加以区分。

Method: 构建最小双节点量子传感器网络，采用非局域交换测量采样速度分布的特征函数；结合日/年调制、后选择、退偏事件和仪器偏置，对SHM、Gaia-inspired SHM++及其混合模型进行 likelihood 分析。

Result: 发现仅靠固定地方恒星时的年调制无法区分中心对称SHM与SHM++（因相位波形相同且振幅未知）；但地球自转使基线扫描不同银道方向，暴露各向异性群体的方向依赖相干性，从而打破简并；likelihood分析验证了调制与信号奇偶控制可有效分离信号与平坦背景。

Conclusion: 双节点量子传感网络不仅能探测暗物质，还可通过调制特征反演本地暗物质速度分布，进而检验银河系晕是否光滑各向同性，或含有径向偏向的吸积成分，实现从粒子探测到星系结构研究的跨越。

Abstract: Quantum sensor networks can use the spatial phase coherence of wavelike dark matter to probe not only particle properties but also the motion and structure of the Galactic halo. We explore this idea for the minimal two-node network, in which a nonlocal exchange measurement samples the characteristic function of the velocity distribution. We compare the Standard Halo Model (SHM) with a Gaia-inspired radially anisotropic component and their mixture (SHM++), taking into account daily and annual modulation, postselection, depolarizing events, and a flat instrumental offset. We show that annual modulation at one fixed local sidereal time has the same phase waveform for the centered SHM and Gaia-inspired SHM++ models, leaving them degenerate when the overall signal amplitude is unknown. Earth's rotation breaks this degeneracy by scanning the baseline through Galactic directions and exposing the direction-dependent coherence of an anisotropic population. Distinguishing these velocity distributions would test whether the local halo is smooth and nearly isotropic or contains a radially biased accreted component, turning a quantum-network detection into a probe of Milky Way structure. Our likelihood analysis shows how modulation and signal-odd controls separate this information from flat backgrounds and provides a path from model discrimination toward measurement of the local velocity distribution.

</details>


### [235] [Chains of Vector-like Leptons at Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15893)
*Tao Han,Tong Arthur Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了链味模型中矢量类轻子（VLLs）的产生、衰变及在各类对撞机上的发现潜力，提出通过单/对产生过程测量其质量、新Yukawa耦合及 hopping参数，以揭示SM费米子质量幂律起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释标准模型中费米子的质量等级和混合模式，链味模型预言了具有特定质量结构的矢量类轻子（VLLs），其质量差对应决定SM费米子质量幂律分布的hopping参数。

Method: 分析VLL链在LHC、FCC-hh及正负电子/缪子对撞机上的产生机制（包括矢量玻色子-轻子融合单产生与规范相互作用对产生），结合部分宽度、Yukawa耦合与质量差关系，量化不同对撞机的能量与亮度下的探测灵敏度。

Result: HL-LHC可探测链尺度达800–900 GeV，FCC-hh可达>2.5 TeV；在e⁺e⁻/μ⁺μ⁻对撞机中，阈值以上对产生是清晰发现道，而Z-轻子融合单产生因更低质量阈值可拓展至运动学极限。

Conclusion: VLL链的实验观测不仅能证实新物理尺度，还可直接检验链味模型中费米子质量生成机制，单产生过程尤其适合精确提取hopping参数与新Yukawa耦合。

Abstract: Vector-like leptons (VLLs) are predicted in the chain flavor models, which possess characteristic features to explain the mass hierarchy and mixing of the fermions in the Standard Model (SM). We investigate the production and decay of the VLL chains and quantify the discovery potential at the LHC, FCC-hh, and lepton colliders. The resonant mass sets the new physics scale responsible for the fermion mass generation. The observation of the VLL multiplets associated with each lepton flavor yields the chain structure. The mass difference among the VLL multiplets determines the hopping parameter that gives the power-law pattern for the observed SM fermion masses. The single VLL production via vector boson-lepton fusion directly measures the partial width, equivalently the new Yukawa coupling and the VLL mass hopping parameter. At hadron colliders, the leading production mechanism is pair production via their gauge interaction. We find that HL-LHC may have the sensitivity to reach the chain scale of $800-900\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and FCC-hh reaches $>2.5\,\mathrm{TeV}$. In $e^+e^-$ and $μ^+μ^-$ colliders, VLL pair production is a clear discovery channel once the threshold is reached. Single production via $Z$-lepton fusion becomes more accessible owing to a lower mass threshold and extends signal discovery to the collider kinematic limit.

</details>


### [236] [Probing gluon saturation with forward heavy-flavor correlations in proton-nucleus collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15897)
*Farid Salazar,Björn Schenke,Wenbin Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Color Glass Condensate理论研究重味强子方位角关联，作为高能pp和p+Pb碰撞中胶子饱和的探针，并首次结合小-x演化与Sudakov重求和计算了全方位角范围的重介子对关联，成功描述LHCb实验数据，且预测p+Pb中远端产额抑制约15%及峰宽展宽10%-25%，凸显其探测胶子饱和的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重味夸克产生对靶核小-x胶子分布高度敏感，且夸克质量压制共线辐射；重介子携带母夸克大部分动量，使其运动学更贴近部分子层次，因此重味方位角关联是探测胶子饱和的理想探针。

Method: 基于Color Glass Condensate有效理论，首次结合由HERA数据约束的小-x初值演化与背靠背极限附近的Sudakov压低重求和，计算全方位角范围的重介子（D-Dbar、b-强子）对关联。

Result: 理论计算良好复现LHCb测得的D-Dbar和b-强子对方位角关联；预测在p+Pb相对于pp碰撞中，远端产额抑制约15%，且ΔΦ=π附近高斯拟合峰宽展宽10%–25%。

Conclusion: 重味方位角关联不仅呈现产额抑制，还展现出独特峰宽展宽效应，相比轻味关联更具灵敏度和区分度，是搜寻胶子饱和现象的有力新观测量。

Abstract: We investigate heavy-flavor azimuthal correlations as probes of gluon saturation in high-energy $p$+$p$ and $p$+Pb collisions within the Color Glass Condensate effective theory. Heavy flavor has several advantages: heavy-quark production is especially sensitive to the small-$x$ gluon distribution of the target, while the quark mass suppresses collinear radiation. Further, the heavy meson carries a large fraction of the momentum of its parent quark, so that the meson pair follows the partonic kinematics more closely than for light hadrons. We present the first calculation of heavy-meson pair correlations over the full azimuthal range that combines small-$x$ evolution from an initial condition constrained by HERA data with Sudakov resummation near the back-to-back limit. It provides a good description of the $D$-$\bar D$ and $b$-hadron pair correlations measured by LHCb. In contrast to the light-flavor case, we predict, in addition to a suppression of the away-side yield in $p$+Pb relative to $p$+$p$ collisions by about $15\%$, a broadening of the peak by approximately $10\%$ to $25\%$ for Gaussian fits near $ΔΦ=π$, which makes heavy-flavor correlations a promising observable in the search for gluon saturation.

</details>


### [237] [Polarized gluon TMD fragmentation into spin-1 quarkonium at NLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15918)
*Alessia Bongallino,Miguel G. Echevarria,Samuel F. Romera*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了胶子到夸克偶素的横动量依赖碎裂函数（TMDFFs）在微扰QCD下领头阶（NLO）的完整结果，涵盖所有胶子和J^{PC}=1^{--}夸克偶素的极化构型，并基于NRQCD框架纳入所有O(α_s^2)阶的颜色八重态通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究夸克偶素在喷注、电子-离子对撞机（EIC）及e+e−湮灭中的极化现象，需提供完整的胶子到自旋-1强子（特别是夸克偶素）的横动量依赖碎裂函数的微扰输入。

Method: 在NRQCD有效理论框架下，系统计算胶子到J^{PC}=1^{--}夸克偶素的全部TMDFFs至NLO精度；包含所有相关颜色八重态通道（^3S_1^[8], ^1S_0^[8], ^3P_J^[8]），并考虑胶子与末态夸克偶素的所有极化构型。

Result: 得到了全部未极化与极化TMDFFs的完整NLO结果，其中多数极化TMDFFs及其全横动量依赖性属首次给出；结果与已知共线碎裂函数及LO TMDFFs一致，并通过已有未极化NLO结果进行了交叉验证。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了适用于胶子碎裂到任意自旋-1强子的通用TMDFF框架，为未来高能实验中夸克偶素极化测量提供了关键的微扰理论基础。

Abstract: We compute the complete set of gluon transverse-momentum-dependent fragmentation functions (TMDFFs) into quarkonium through next-to-leading order in perturbative QCD. We account for all polarization configurations of both the fragmenting gluon and the produced $J^{\rm PC}=1^{--}$ quarkonium. Within NRQCD, we include all color-octet channels that contribute through $\mathcal O(α_s^2)$ at the considered order in the heavy-quark relative velocity $v$, namely ${}^3S_1^{[8]}$, ${}^1S_0^{[8]}$ and ${}^3P_J^{[8]}$. Several structures of the calculation follow only from the spin-1 nature of the final-state hadron, so our framework applies more generally to the fragmentation of a gluon into any spin-1 hadron. Our results agree with all known collinear fragmentation functions, up to differences in $δ(1-z)$ terms that we discuss, and with the TMDFFs at LO, and we cross-check the unpolarized TMDFFs at NLO against previous calculations. Most of the polarized TMDFFs, and their full transverse-momentum dependence, are presented here for the first time. They provide the perturbative input for studies of quarkonium polarization in jets, at the EIC and in $e^+e^-$ annihilation.

</details>


### [238] [The Curious Case of Anapole Dark Matter at LZ: Minimal, Elastic and Testable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15957)
*Sourav Bera,Suvam Maharana,Poulami Mondal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出马约拉纳费米子作为暗物质候选者，其主要电磁相互作用为anapole矩，该动量依赖相互作用可解释LZ实验观测到的高能核反冲事例，并自然给出正确的暗物质丰度，且与对撞机和间接探测结果一致，具有可检验性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找电磁相互作用暗物质的最小实现形式，并解释LZ实验中难以用背景解释的单个高能核反冲事件。

Method: 基于马约拉纳费米子仅具anapole矩这一特性，构建其与普通物质的弹性散射模型，分析其动量依赖的核反冲谱，并结合宇宙学丰度、对撞机限制及间接探测约束进行参数拟合。

Result: anapole暗物质在~300 GeV质量、特定耦合强度下可同时解释LZ事例、正确热退耦丰度，并满足现有实验限制；预测LZ完整运行及XLZD等下一代实验可观测低能区事例。

Conclusion: 马约拉纳型anapole暗物质是一个自洽、简洁且可检验的暗物质候选模型，为理解LZ异常事例及暗物质本质提供了新视角。

Abstract: Majorana fermions can carry neither charge nor dipole moments, leaving the anapole moment as their leading electromagnetic interaction. A stable Majorana fermion is therefore a minimal realization of electromagnetically interacting dark matter. The momentum dependence of the anapole interaction produces a harder recoil spectrum than standard momentum-independent interactions. We show that this makes elastically scattering anapole dark matter a viable interpretation of the single high-energy nuclear-recoil event recorded by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment, in a region where almost no background is expected. Curiously, the same coupling that fits the LZ data also yields the observed dark matter abundance at a mass of about 300 GeV, and is consistent with collider searches and indirect detection. The scenario is testable, predicting further events at lower energies, which the full LZ run will probe and a next-generation detector such as XLZD will decisively test.

</details>


### [239] [Matching 2HDM to HEFT in triple Higgs boson production from WBF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15979)
*L. Barreiro,D. Domenech,D. Garcia-Tejada,M. Herrero,S. Salazar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了Higgs有效场论（HEFT）与双Higgs二重态模型（2HDM）在WW→hhh散射过程中的预言，通过匹配两模型在重标量极限下的散射振幅，确定了HEFT中关键耦合系数（a, b, c, κ₃, κ₄）用2HDM参数（c_{β−α}, tanβ, m_{12}）表示的解析表达式，并指出这些系数携带重态非消失效应；数值上发现当存在对齐破缺（c_{β−α} ≠ 0）时，HEFT匹配预言与标准模型预言存在显著偏差，该偏差可作为未来高能对撞机探测不可达重态的潜在信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索重Higgs态（H, A, H±）虽不可直接产生但仍可通过非消失效应影响轻Higgs（h）多粒子末态过程的物理信号；特别是WW→hhh这一高阶过程对2HDM参数敏感，可用于间接探测重态。

Method: 在2HDM重态质量远大于电弱能标的极限下，将WW→hhh散射振幅在HEFT与2HDM中分别计算并严格匹配，从而解出HEFT中五个主导有效耦合系数（对应WWh、WWhh、WWhhh、hhh、hhhh相互作用）关于2HDM基本参数c_{β−α}、tanβ和m_{12}的解析表达式；进而分析其对截面σ(WW→hhh)的影响，并与SM及双Higgs情形对比。

Result: 得到了HEFT系数a、b、c、κ₃、κ₄由2HDM参数决定的明确解析形式；发现这些系数体现重态非消失效应，主导的σ(WW→hhh)贡献不随重质量衰减；当c_{β−α}≠0（即对齐破缺）时，HEFT匹配预言显著偏离SM，偏差可达数倍甚至更大。

Conclusion: WW→hhh过程是探测2HDM重标量非消失效应的理想渠道；通过HEFT匹配得到的系数可系统编码重态信息；该过程中观测到的与SM的显著偏差，即使重态远超对撞机能量，也可作为其存在的强有力间接证据。

Abstract: In this work we compare the Higgs Effective Field Theory (HEFT) predictions with the Two Higgs Doublet Model (2HDM) predictions for triple Higgs production via $WW \rightarrow hhh$ scattering, under the hypothesis of very heavy Higgs bosons $H$, $A$, and $H^\pm$, and assuming the lightest one $h$ has the observed mass in the experiments, $m_h \simeq 125$ GeV. By requiring the matching of the two corresponding scattering amplitudes for this $WW \rightarrow hhh$ process in the limit of very heavy mass for these bosons, we find the solutions for the leading order HEFT coefficients. Concretely, we determine the coefficients $a$, $b$, $c$, $κ_3$ and $κ_4$ defining the effective interactions $WWh$, $WWhh$, $WWhhh$, $hhh$ and $hhhh$, respectively, in terms of the 2HDM input parameters, $c_{β-α}$, $\tan β$ and $m_{12}$. We will also compare these solutions with the corresponding solutions found for the case of double Higgs production. The solutions presented here for the matched coefficients summarize the non-decoupling effects from the heavy modes, $H$, $A$ and $H^\pm$ in the scattering process $WW \rightarrow hhh$ and provide the most relevant contributions to the cross section $σ(WW \rightarrow hhh)$ that do not decrease as inverse powers of the heavy masses. In the last part, we will also discuss the numerical predictions of the cross section $σ(WW \rightarrow hhh)$ in terms of the HEFT-matched coefficients. The focus of this discussion is set on the big gap between the HEFT-matched prediction and the SM prediction due to the non-decoupling behaviour occurring whenever misalignment is assumed in the 2HDM, i.e. for $c_{β-α} \neq 0$. This big gap could serve as potential signals of the 2HDM heavy modes, $H$, $A$ and $H^\pm$, at future high energy colliders, even if they are too heavy and cannot be produced at these colliders.

</details>


### [240] [KNO functions for dipole-dipole scattering from large Pomeron loops: log-normal tails and a test in exactly solvable models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16081)
*Michael Roa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了基于BK方程和AGK切割规则的QCD大Pomeron圈求和方法所得到的多重数分布，指出其KNO函数具有对数正态而非高斯尾部，并通过Lambert函数给出闭式表达；同时在零横维可解模型中验证发现，尽管振幅可较好逼近，但多重数分布仅在低能稀疏级联下成立，高能时KNO标度失效，熵增长行为亦不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新检验基于BK方程与AGK规则导出的多重数分布及其KNO标度性质，澄清其渐近行为（如尾部形式、唯一性）及物理适用范围。

Method: 结合t-通道幺正性与BK方程在饱和区的解，应用AGK切割规则推导多重数分布；采用高斯表象分析渐近级数，引入Lambert函数获得KNO函数闭式解；并在零横维的两个严格可解模型（幺正玩具模型）中进行数值验证。

Result: KNO函数呈现对数正态尾部（lnΨ_BK ∝ −½ ln²ξ，lnΨ_dd ∝ −¼ ln²ξ），可用Lambert函数表示；KNO标度要求舍弃级数系数中的幂律因子，而替代围道表示会导致函数变号；在幺正玩具模型中，振幅近似误差为2.5–10%，但多重数分布仅在级联稀疏时准确，高能下KNO标度破缺，熵增长为½ ln⟨n⟩。

Conclusion: BK振幅与多重数分布由不同物理机制主导（前者由稀有构型决定，后者由典型构型决定），因此振幅的良好拟合不能保证多重数分布的正确性；KNO标度并非普遍成立，其有效性依赖于能量尺度与级联密度。

Abstract: The sum of large Pomeron loops in QCD, based on the $t$-channel unitarity and on the solution of the Balitsky-Kovchegov (BK) equation in the saturation region, has been combined with the AGK (Abramovsky, Gribov and Kancheli) cutting rules to obtain multiplicity distributions with KNO (Koba, Nielsen and Olesen) scaling and an entropy equal to the logarithm of the gluon structure function. We reexamine these distributions for the BK amplitude and for dipole-dipole scattering. The KNO functions that follow from the Gaussian representation of the asymptotic series have log-normal and not Gaussian tails, $\lnΨ_{\rm BK}\simeq-\frac{1}{2}\ln^2ξ$ and $\lnΨ_{\rm dd}\simeq-\frac{1}{4}\ln^2ξ$, and we give them in closed form in terms of the Lambert function. They are log-normal mixtures of the distributions of one and two BFKL cascades, and the summation is not unique. The KNO scaling requires that power-like factors in the coefficients of the series be discarded, and the alternative contour representation leads to functions that change sign. We then test the approach in two exactly solvable models in zero transverse dimensions. In the unitary toy model we confirm that the resummed series reproduces the exact $S$-matrix to within 2.5-10%, while the $S$-matrix changes by many orders of magnitude. Nevertheless, the multiplicity distribution obtained from the same series with the AGK rules agrees with the exact one only as long as both cascades are dilute. At higher energies the exact mean grows as a power of rapidity, the KNO scaling is absent, and the entropy in the rest frame of the target grows as one half of the logarithm of the mean multiplicity. The amplitude is determined by rare configurations of the cascades and the multiplicity by the typical ones, so that the agreement of the amplitudes does not validate the multiplicity distributions.

</details>


### [241] [Connecting Electroweak Fine-Tuning, Tens-of-GeV Dark Matter, and Exotic Higgs Decays in the GNMSSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16191)
*Zhaoxia Heng,Haijing Zhou,Fei Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在GNMSSM模型中统一探讨电弱精细调节、~tens GeV量级的singlino暗物质候选者及奇特希格斯衰变，识别出一个自然参数区域，并给出LHC和HL-LHC可观测的具体信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将电弱精细调节、singlino暗物质与奇特希格斯衰变三者统一于GNMSSM框架下，寻找自然且实验可检验的参数区域。

Method: 基于GNMSSM模型，结合电弱精细调节度Δ≤40约束、LZ 2024暗物质直接探测限制、LHC对h→asas→bbττ的搜寻限制，开展参数扫描与模型分析。

Result: 发现满足Δ≤40的自然区域中，Br(h→asas)为7%–25%，mas主要位于10–20 GeV；该区域分为h1或h2为SM-like两种情形；LHC已排除部分参数点，而h2情形中大量参数在HL-LHC仍可观测。

Conclusion: GNMSSM中存在一个能同时满足自然性、暗物质和可观测奇特希格斯衰变的协调区域，为未来LHC/HL-LHC及暗物质实验提供了明确探测目标。

Abstract: In the general next-to-minimal supersymmetric standard model (GNMSSM), we connect electroweak fine-tuning, a singlino dark matter candidate with mass around tens of GeV, and Exotic Higgs Decays, identifying a natural region characterized by the mass hierarchy $m_{\tilde{S}}<m_{\tilde{B}}<m_{\tilde{H}}$ and $μ_{\rm tot}\in [200, 500]$ GeV. For fine-tuning measure $Δ\leq 40$, the branching ratio $\mathrm{Br}(h\to a_sa_s)$ falls in the range 7\%$-$25\%, with the pseudoscalar mass $m_{a_s}$ lying mostly between 10 and 20 GeV. This region splits into two viable scenarios consistent with LZ 2024 direct detection: one where the lightest CP-even Higgs $h_1$ is SM-like ($h_1$ case), and another where the second-lightest $h_2$ is SM-like ($h_2$ case). LHC searches for $h\to a_sa_s \to b\bar{b}τ^+τ^-$ exclude 12\% (10\%) of samples with 12 GeV $\leq m_{a_s} \leq$ 15.5 GeV in the $h_1$ ($h_2$) case, while a substantial fraction remains accessible at the HL-LHC in the $h_2$ case. Our analysis unifies naturalness, dark matter, and observable exotic Higgs decays, yielding concrete targets for future experiments.

</details>


### [242] [Probing the Molecular Nature of $D_{sJ}(2860)$ and $D_{s3}(2860)$ through Their Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16276)
*Qing Lu,Dan Dan Feng,Cai Cheng,Yin Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过有效拉格朗日方法研究DsJ(2860)和Ds3(2860)的强衰变，探讨其分子态解释；结果支持二者均为P波D*K*分子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解DsJ(2860)和Ds3(2860)的内部结构，检验其是否为分子态，并区分S波DK1与P波D*K*等不同构型。

Method: 采用有效拉格朗日方法分析强衰变过程，结合LHCb实验测得的Bs0→D̄0K−π+角分布与衰变宽度拟合分子耦合常数和截断参数Λ，进而计算总宽及DK与D*K分支比R。

Result: 单独考虑时，DsJ(2860)的S波D1K分子解释略好于P波D*K*；但联合解释（DsJ为D1K、Ds3为D*K*）因预测的R_Ds3超出实验上限而被排除；而二者均设为P波D*K*分子时，R_Ds3与实验一致，且预测R_DsJ≈1.26–1.29。

Conclusion: DsJ(2860)和Ds3(2860)更可能均为P波D*K*分子态，该结论有待未来R_DsJ(2860)的精确测量验证。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the molecular interpretations of $D_{sJ}(2860)$ and $D_{s3}(2860)$ by studying their strong decays within the effective Lagrangian approach. We consider two possible configurations for $D_{sJ}(2860)$: an $S$-wave $D_1K$ molecule and a $P$-wave $D^*K^*$ molecule, while treating $D_{s3}(2860)$ as a $P$-wave $D^*K^*$ molecule. By fitting the LHCb-measured angular distribution and decay widths in $B_s^0\to\bar{D}^0K^-π^+$, we determine the molecular coupling constants and cutoff parameter $Λ$. Using these parameters, we calculate the total decay widths and the ratios $R$ of the partial widths into the $D^*K$ and $DK$ channels. For $D_{sJ}(2860)$, the $S$-wave $D_1K$ assignment provides a slightly better description of the measured total width than the $P$-wave $D^*K^*$ assignment. However, the combined $D_1K$ and $D^*K^*$ assignments for $D_{sJ}(2860)$ and $D_{s3}(2860)$, respectively, are disfavored by the predicted $R_{D_{s3}(2860)}$, which exceeds the experimental upper limit. In contrast, assigning both states as $P$-wave $D^*K^*$ molecules yields good agreement with the measured $R_{D_{s3}(2860)}$ and predicts $R_{D_{sJ}(2860)}\simeq 1.26$--$1.29$. These results favor the $P$-wave $D^*K^*$ molecular interpretation for both states, which can be further tested through future measurements of $R_{D_{sJ}(2860)}$.

</details>


### [243] [High Energy Asymptotics of Quark-Gluon Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16298)
*Jian Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了质子内部结构中夸克-胶子关联在高能极限下的行为，特别是与横向自旋现象相关的味非单态夸克-胶子关联；通过建立含相干胶子插入的双对数精度递归因子化，推导出Qiu-Sterman函数及其手性奇Boer-Mulders对应函数的红外演化方程；发现这些扭度-3函数具有与其对应扭度-2函数相同的小x截距，但因相干胶子插入的色结构而额外受到1/ln(1/x)压制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解质子内部结构中量子干涉效应，尤其是夸克-胶子关联在小x区域的行为，以支持电子-离子对撞机（EIC）上的实验解释与外推。

Method: 建立含相干胶子插入的双对数精度递归因子化框架，推导扭度-3夸克-胶子关联函数（Qiu-Sterman函数及Boer-Mulders对应函数）的红外演化方程。

Result: Qiu-Sterman函数及其Boer-Mulders对应函数在小x极限下具有与其扭度-2对应函数相同的领先截距，但受1/ln(1/x)因子压制。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了扭度-3自旋相关分布函数在小x区域的独特演化特性，为EIC上夸克-胶子关联的实验分析和理论外推提供了关键理论依据。

Abstract: The internal structure of the proton is encoded not only in single parton densities, but also in the quantum interference between distinct partonic configurations.
