We study the high energy behavior of this interference by focusing on flavor-nonsinglet quark-gluon correlations relevant to transverse spin phenomena. By establishing recursive factorization at double logarithmic accuracy in the presence of a coherent gluon insertion, we derive infrared evolution equations for the Qiu-Sterman function and its chiral odd Boer-Mulders counterpart. We find that these twist-3 functions share the leading small $x$ intercepts of their respective twist-2 counterparts, but acquire an additional $1/\ln(1/x)$ suppression due to the color structure of the coherent gluon insertion. These results may guide small $x$ extrapolations of quark-gluon correlations at the Electron Ion Collider.

</details>


### [244] [Poles Are Not Enough: Zeros of Dyson-Schwinger Gluon Propagators in Yang-Mills Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16392)
*Marco Frasca,Anish Ghoshal,Stefan Groote*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了规范场论中热力学性质的计算方法，特别是通过引入零点因子来改进极点传播子对热分区函数的描述，从而更准确地恢复紫外自由度并影响相变温度和潜热等物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上仅依赖极点的传播子无法完全确定无源热分区函数；需要考虑零点以正确恢复紫外自由度（如每个伴随本征值的两个极化态）。

Method: 采用正残差有理传播子，结合其极点与介于其间零点的行列式贡献，构建背景协变的椭圆Dyson-Schwinger胶子核完成方案，并应用于SU(3)规范理论的热力学分析。

Result: 六极点情况下，零点行列式使T_c/M_g提升2.31%，潜热L/T_c^4降低12.4%；固定M_g时维度潜热下降4.0%；固定全息自由能差在保留零点时渐近趋于Weiss两极化结果，而仅用极点时大(3P−1)/2倍。

Conclusion: 零点因子对热力学量具有显著定量影响，是精确刻画非微扰胶子动力学及相变行为不可或缺的部分。

Abstract: A normalized two-point function with several poles does not, on its own, fix the source-free thermal partition function. For the Gaussian measure of the original gluon field, a positive-residue rational propagator contributes determinants from its poles and from zeros interlaced between them. The zero factors distinguish this completion from a gas of independent pole fields and restore two ultraviolet polarizations per adjoint eigenvalue at every finite truncation. We apply the construction to a background-covariant completion of the elliptic Dyson-Schwinger gluon kernel. The first-order SU(3) transition is already present with a single pole, and the saturated six-pole result shifts $T_c/M_g$ by only $1.48\%$, where $T_c$ is the coexistence temperature and $M_g$ is the lowest pole mass. At six poles, the zero determinants raise $T_c/M_g$ by $2.31\%$ and lower $L/T_c^4$ by $12.4\%$, where $L$ is the latent heat and each prescription uses its own $T_c$. At fixed $M_g$ the dimensional latent heat decreases by $4.0\%$. The fixed-holonomy free-energy difference gives an ultraviolet test independent of the additive pressure normalization. At fixed pole number $P$, its asymptotic value agrees with the two-polarization Weiss result when the zeros are retained and is $(3P-1)/2$ times as large in the poles-only prescription.

</details>


### [245] [Disentangling the Effects of Phase-Specific Transport Properties on Elliptic Flow Centrality Dependence in the AMPT Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16394)
*Yao Zhang,Xipei Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用AMPT模型，通过调控夸克相和强子相的输运性质，探究了Au+Au碰撞中带电强子椭圆流v2随碰撞中心度的变化规律，发现强子再散射对v2的贡献随中心度减小而增强，在外围碰撞中起主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能重离子碰撞中各向异性流（特别是v2）的产生机制及其对不同物态（夸克胶子等离子体QGP与强子气体）输运性质的依赖关系，尤其是厘清强子相在小系统中的作用。

Method: 采用AMPT模型，分别调节剪切粘滞系数η/s、相变温度Ttr和强子散射截面，实现对部分子相和强子相输运特性的分阶段控制，并分析其对v2中心度依赖性的影响。

Result: 强子再散射对v₂的贡献随碰撞中心度降低而增大，在外围碰撞中占主导；将强子散射截面增大四倍可显著增强外围碰撞中的v₂，并改善与STAR实验数据的一致性。

Conclusion: v₂在高中心度区域的压平现象可能反映有效强子散射截面更大，表明强子相在小系统演化中起着关键且不可忽略的作用。

Abstract: The centrality dependence of charged hadron elliptic flow $v_{2}$ in Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=200~\rm{GeV}$ is studied using the AMPT model with phase-specific control of partonic and hadronic transport properties. By separately varying the shear viscosity $η/s$, phase transition temperature $T_{tr}$, and hadronic scattering cross sections, we isolate their distinct effects on anisotropic flow. Hadronic rescattering is found to contribute increasingly to $v_{2}$ with centrality, with the contribution becoming dominant in peripheral collisions. Scaling up hadronic cross sections by a factor of four substantially amplifies $v_{2}$ in peripheral collisions and improves agreement with STAR data, suggesting that the flattening of $v_{2}$ at high centrality may reflect a larger effective hadronic cross section and highlighting the significant role of the hadronic phase in small systems.

</details>


### [246] [Probing the internal structure of the $ΩΩ$ dibaryon via its weak decays and lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16508)
*Qi-Fang Lü,Deng-Wang Tang,Yu-Bing Dong,Peng-Nian Shen,Jia-Jun Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过有效拉格朗日方法系统研究了ΩΩ dibaryon的弱衰变性质与寿命，发现其衰变模式和寿命强烈依赖于束缚能，可为RHIC和LHC实验提供理论判据，并区分紧致六夸克态与松散分子态图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ΩΩ dibaryon的弱衰变性质对揭示其内部结构（如紧致六夸克态 vs. 弱束缚分子态）至关重要，并指导高能实验（如RHIC、LHC）中的搜寻工作。

Method: 采用有效拉格朗日方法，计算单/双弱顶点图贡献的三体与四体末态衰变宽度，系统分析束缚能（几MeV至120 MeV）对衰变性质的影响。

Result: 束缚能较小时（1–20 MeV），主导衰变道为ΩΛK̄、ΩΞπ、ΞΞK̄；束缚能较大时（100–120 MeV），ΩΛK̄被禁戒，主导道为ΩΞπ和ΞΞK̄，而ΛK̄ΛK̄双弱道可忽略；总寿命随束缚能增加从~10⁻¹⁰ s延长至~10⁻⁹ s。

Conclusion: ΩΩ dibaryon的衰变模式与寿命对束缚能高度敏感，可作为实验上区分其可能结构（紧凑六夸克态或弱束缚分子态）的关键理论判据。

Abstract: The weak decay properties of the $ΩΩ$ dibaryon play a crucial role in probing its internal structure and guiding experimental efforts. Using an effective Lagrangian approach, we systematically study its weak decays and lifetime by computing the widths for both three- and four-body final states, with amplitudes receiving contributions from single- and double-weak-vertex diagrams. We investigate the dependence of the decay properties on the binding energy ranging from a few MeV to over $100$ MeV. For weakly bound states ($E_b = 1$ and $20$ MeV), the three-body channels $ΩΛ\bar{K}$, $ΩΞπ$, and $ΞΞ\bar{K}$ exhaust essentially the entire width, with the largest channel shifting from $ΩΛ\bar{K}$ at $E_b=1$ MeV to $ΩΞπ$ at $E_b=20$ MeV. For deeply bound states ($E_b = 100$ and $120$ MeV), the $ΩΛ\bar{K}$ channel is forbidden, and the dominant modes are $ΩΞπ$ and $ΞΞ\bar{K}$, while the double-weak-vertex channel $Λ\bar{K} Λ\bar{K}$ is found to be negligibly small. As the binding energy increases, the total lifetime extends from the order of $10^{-10}~\mathrm{s}$ to $10^{-9}~\mathrm{s}$. These distinct differences in decay patterns and lifetimes provide valuable theoretical reference values for experimental searches at RHIC and the LHC, and offer a clear discriminator between the compact hexaquark and weakly bound molecular interpretations.

</details>


### [247] [A perturbative construction of a modular $S_3$ model for quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16545)
*Gabriele Battimelli,Davide Meloni*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Γ₂≅S₃群的模不变超对称味模型，利用模形式权重差异自然生成夸克质量与混合层次，仅依赖模参数τ（兼为CP破坏唯一来源），以10个自由参数高精度拟合8个可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统味模型中需大量调参、引入额外标量场（flavons）及CP破坏机制不简洁的问题，追求更经济、对称性更优的夸克味结构解释。

Method: 构建基于S₃模群的超对称有效理论，质量矩阵由不同权重的模形式构成，施加广义CP（gCP）对称性约束，使模参数τ成为唯一风味源和CP相位源。

Result: 仅用10个自由参数（含τ的实虚部）即在1σ范围内成功再现4个夸克质量比和4个CKM矩阵参数，是目前S₃模模型中自由参数最少者。

Conclusion: 该模型以极简结构实现夸克味物理的有效描述，凸显模对称性与gCP联合约束的强预测能力，为统一解释质量和CP破坏提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present a modular-invariant supersymmetric model of flavour for quark masses and mixing, based on the group $Γ_2\cong S_3$. Hierarchies in the quark sector follow from the symmetric structure of the model, as they are generated by modular forms of different weights in the mass matrices, and no severe tuning of free superpotential coefficients is needed. Moreover, no flavons are employed besides the modulus $τ$ which is, given the gCP symmetry imposed on the model, the only source of CP violation. 8 dimensionless observables (4 mass ratios and 4 CKM parameters) are reproduced by making use of 10 free model parameters (including real and imaginary part of the modulus) within the $1σ$ range making our model, to the best of our knowledge, the $S_3$ modular model for quarks with the fewest free parameters.

</details>


### [248] [Inelastic Scalar Dark Matter in a Gauged $U(1)_{B-L}$ Model and its LZ Phenomenology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16553)
*Changjie Dai,Linyun He,Yonghua Wang,Wei Chao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在U(1)_{B-L}规范模型中由两个复标量Φ和S构成的非弹性标量暗物质，以解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验观测到的高能反冲事例；模型包含双组分暗物质框架，分别对应放热与吸热过程，并通过热历史分析、核反冲谱计算及三区间似然分析确定符合观测丰度与LZ信号的最佳参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验报道的高能核反冲候选事例，并构建一个能自然实现非弹性暗物质散射的理论框架。

Method: 构建基于U(1)_{B-L}规范对称性的两复标量模型（Φ和S），其中S引发对称性破缺，Φ的两个实部φ₁和φ₂构成双组分标量暗物质；分析其热退耦历史、丰度演化，并计算核反冲谱，结合LZ数据进行三区间似然拟合。

Result: 发现φ₁和φ₂在冻出时丰度近似相等；确定了能同时满足观测暗物质总丰度与LZ高能反冲信号的参数区域；给出了放热与吸热两种场景下的最佳拟合点。

Conclusion: 该U(1)_{B-L}双标量模型为LZ异常信号提供了一种自洽的非弹性暗物质解释，且双组分结构自然支持长寿命激发态（放热）或主导基态（吸热）两种可行图景。

Abstract: We study inelastic scalar dark matter (DM) in a gauged $U(1)_{B-L}$ model motivated by the high-energy recoil candidate reported by LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ). The model introduces two complex scalars, $Φ$ and $S$, charged under $U(1)_{B-L}$. The scalar $S$ is responsible for spontaneous symmetry breaking, while the two real components of $Φ$, $φ_1$ and $φ_2$, serve as DM. This two-component DM framework accommodates both exothermic and endothermic scenarios. In the exothermic scenario, the lifetime of the heavier state $φ_1$ exceeds the age of the Universe, and both states contribute to the present-day DM abundance. In the endothermic scenario, the abundance comes almost entirely from the lighter state $φ_2$. We investigate the thermal history of the dark sector and find that the two scalars have approximately equal abundances at freeze-out. We identify parameter regions that reproduce the observed relic abundance. We calculate the nuclear recoil spectra, perform a three-bin likelihood analysis, and obtain the best-fit points interpreting the LZ signal in both scenarios.

</details>


### [249] [Low-lying doubly charmed strange baryons via QCD sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16576)
*Hui-Min Yang,Hua-Xing Chen,Xuan Luo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD求和规则和光锥求和规则系统研究了低激发双重重子的质量及其B*(3/2+)→B(1/2+)γ辐射跃迁，给出了Ξcc、Ξcc*、Ωcc、Ωcc*等质量预测及辐射衰变宽度，并指出磁偶极贡献占主导，重夸克效应不可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理论预测尚未观测到的自旋3/2双重重子的质量与辐射衰变特性，为LHCb、Belle II等实验提供指导。

Method: 采用混合插值流，在QCD求和规则和光锥求和规则（LCSR）框架下计算双重重子质量与电磁形状因子，进而导出辐射衰变宽度。

Result: 得到Ξcc、Ξcc*、Ωcc、Ωcc*等质量（如M_Ξcc=3.62^{+0.14}_{-0.15} GeV），预测超精细劈裂约101 MeV与99 MeV；辐射宽度如Γ(Ξcc*++→Ξcc++γ)=5.25^{+8.57}_{-3.62} keV；磁偶极主导，电四极项小，轻/重夸克光子发射项存在显著干涉。

Conclusion: 双重重子辐射跃迁中重夸克贡献不可忽略，磁偶极机制主导，所得结果为未来在LHCb、Belle II等实验中通过辐射衰变模式搜寻自旋3/2双重重子提供了重要理论依据。

Abstract: We systematically investigate the masses of the low-lying doubly heavy baryons and their $B^*(3/2^+)\to B(1/2^+)γ$ radiative transitions within QCD sum rules and light-cone sum rules (LCSR). By constructing mixed interpolating currents, we obtain $M_{Ξ_{cc}}=3.62^{+0.14}_{-0.15}$ GeV, $M_{Ξ_{cc}^*}=3.72^{+0.14}_{-0.13}$ GeV, $M_{Ω_{cc}}=3.72^{+0.13}_{-0.15}$ GeV, and $M_{Ω_{cc}^*}=3.82^{+0.13}_{-0.15}$ GeV, together with their doubly bottom partners around $10.27$--$10.34$ GeV. The extracted masses are compatible with the observed $Ξ_{cc}$ and $Ω_{cc}$ states and predict hyperfine splittings of about $101^{+27}_{-11}$ MeV and $99^{+11}_{-12}$ MeV for the $Ξ_{cc}$ and $Ω_{cc}$ systems, respectively. Using these inputs, we derive the LCSR for the electromagnetic form factors and obtain radiative widths $Γ(Ξ_{cc}^{*++}\toΞ_{cc}^{++}γ)=5.25^{+8.57}_{-3.62}$ keV, $Γ(Ξ_{cc}^{*+}\toΞ_{cc}^{+}γ)=0.31^{+0.84}_{-0.25}$ keV, and $Γ(Ω_{cc}^{*+}\toΩ_{cc}^{+}γ)=0.41^{+0.67}_{-0.30}$ keV, while the corresponding doubly bottom widths are at the $0.002$--$0.01$ keV level. We find that the magnetic-dipole contribution dominates, whereas the electric-quadrupole form factor is numerically small. The light- and heavy-quark photon-emission terms can interfere or enhance significantly, and the heavy-quark contribution cannot be neglected in radiative transitions. These results provide useful theoretical guidance for future searches for spin-$3/2$ doubly heavy baryons through radiative decay modes at LHCb, Belle II, and other experimental facilities.

</details>


### [250] [Extended $SO(10)$-inspired leptogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16604)
*Pasquale Di Bari,Xubin Hu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有分层狄拉克中微子质量的跷跷板模型，发现其必然导致高度分层的重 中微子谱；在狄拉克中微子质量与上夸克质量相近的合理假设下，中间质量重 中微子（N₂）的质量范围恰适合通过N₂-轻子生成解释宇宙正反物质不对称，且该结论不依赖于狄拉克质量矩阵的具体形式；该工作推广了传统SO(10)启发的轻子生成机制，并使中微子倒序排列成为可能，同时将最轻中微子质量下限放宽至约10⁻⁵ eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索跷跷板模型中重 中微子质量谱的自然性及其对轻子生成（特别是N₂-leptogenesis）的影响，并检验倒序中微子质量谱在该框架下的可行性。

Method: 基于当前低能中微子实验数据，结合分层狄拉克中微子质量假设，理论推导重 中微子质量谱结构；分析N₂衰变产额及CP破坏源，评估其对热轻子生成的贡献；对比正常序与倒序质量谱下的约束条件。

Result: 1）分层狄拉克质量必然导致高度分层的重 中微子谱；2）N₂质量自然落入轻子生成可行区间，主导重子不对称产生；3）倒序谱在扩展模型中可行，但强热轻子生成仍排除倒序；4）最轻中微子质量下限放宽至m₁ ≳ 10⁻⁵ eV。

Conclusion: N₂-leptogenesis在跷跷板模型中比以往认为的更普适，不依赖SO(10)等特定大统一框架或CKM型失配假设；该机制可自然容纳倒序谱，显著拓宽了轻子生成理论的参数空间。

Abstract: We consider seesaw models with hierarchical Dirac neutrino masses. We show that, barring well known fine-tuned crossing level solutions and given current low energy neutrino experimental data, these always lead to a very hierarchical heavy neutrino spectrum. If the Dirac neutrino masses are assumed not to significantly differ from the up-quark masses, then the intermediate seesaw neutrino ($N_2$) mass falls in the correct range for its decays to explain the matter-antimatter asymmetry of the universe via ($N_2$-)leptogenesis, while the lightest seesaw neutrino decays give a negligible contribution. We show that these conclusions are valid independently of any additional condition on the Dirac neutrino mass matrix. In this way we extend the usual $SO(10)$-inspired leptogenesis scenario, assuming the mismatch between neutrino and charged lepton Dirac mass matrices at the level of the CKM matrix. We find that solutions for inverted ordered neutrino masses become viable. We still find a lower bound on the lightest neutrino mass, $m_1 \gtrsim 10^{-5}\,{\rm eV}$, but this is relaxed by two orders of magnitude. We also consider the special subset of solutions realising strong thermal leptogenesis within this extended scenario, finding that inverted ordering remains incompatible. Our results show how the emergence of the $N_2$-leptogenesis scenario within seesaw models is much more general than thought so far.

</details>


### [251] [Particle diffusion in magnetized QCD matter with a realistic equation of state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16656)
*Arnab Sarkar,Manu Kurian*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在外部磁场存在下，相互作用的高温QCD介质中的粒子数扩散问题，通过准粒子描述和Chapman-Enskog展开，导出了二阶相对论磁流体力学下的粒子数扩散流演化方程，并分析了磁场与介质相互作用对扩散输运性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一致的框架以研究磁化、相互作用QCD物质中的耗散粒子输运，并为重离子碰撞的现实流体力学描述提供关键输入。

Method: 基于准粒子自由度的有效协变介质描述，采用类似Chapman-Enskog的迭代展开方法推导二阶相对论磁流体力学中粒子数扩散流的演化方程，并进一步得到Navier-Stokes极限。

Result: 发现介质相互作用会抑制磁场诱导的粒子扩散各向异性；获得了磁场影响下的扩散流及其相关输运系数的变化规律。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁化相互作用QCD介质中的粒子输运提供了自洽理论框架，对重离子碰撞的流体模拟具有重要意义。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate particle number diffusion in an interacting hot QCD medium in the presence of an external magnetic field. Starting from an effective covariant description of the medium in terms of quasiparticle degrees of freedom, we employ a Chapman Enskog-like iterative expansion of the distribution function to derive the second-order relativistic magnetohydrodynamic evolution equation for the number diffusion current. The magnetic field introduces additional tensorial structures into the diffusion dynamics, thereby modifying the transport properties of the medium. We further obtain the corresponding Navier-Stokes limit and examine the influence of medium interactions and the magnetic field on the diffusion current and its associated transport coefficients. Our results show that medium interactions suppress the magnetic field-induced anisotropy in particle diffusion. This study provides a consistent framework for investigating dissipative particle transport in magnetized, interacting QCD matter and may serve as an important ingredient in developing realistic hydrodynamic descriptions of heavy-ion collisions.

</details>


### [252] [Next-to-leading-order QCD corrections to prompt $Υ(1S)$ pair hadroproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16686)
*Zhi-Guo He,Xiao-Bo Jin,Bernd A. Kniehl*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论性QCD因子化框架下，研究了Υ(1S)对的强子产生过程（含激发态馈赠），重点分析色单态贡献，并与CMS实验数据对比，验证了单部分子散射主导机制，同时为双部分子散射参数σ_eff提取奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Υ介子对产生的机制，检验非相对论QCD因子化在重夸克偶素产生中的适用性，并为双部分子散射（DPS）信号识别和参数σ_eff提取提供理论基准。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化框架，计算Υ(1S)对产生的下领头阶（NLO）QCD修正，包含Υ(2S)、Υ(3S)到Υ(1S)的馈赠效应，聚焦主导的色单态机制，并预测多种微分截面分布。

Result: 总截面K因子约为0.9（轻微负修正）；微分截面K因子变化显著（如p_T^Υ大时达20，|Δy|大时低至0.2）；理论预测整体与CMS 8/13 TeV数据吻合良好，但在m_ΥΥ和|Δy|最外侧区间偏低，暗示DPS贡献。

Conclusion: 单部分子散射（SPS）色单态机制可解释大部分Υ对产生数据，剩余差异为DPS提供了清晰窗口，本工作为σ_eff的精确提取建立了首个可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: Working at next-to-leading order in nonrelativistic-QCD factorization, we study prompt $Υ(1S)$ pair hadroproduction, including feed-down from the excited $Υ(2S)$ and $Υ(3S)$ states and focusing on the color singlet contributions, which are known to be dominant. Besides the integrated cross section $σ^{ΥΥ}$, we predict distributions in the invariant mass $m_{ΥΥ}$, transverse momentum $p_T^{ΥΥ}$, and rapidity separation $|Δy|$ of the $Υ(1S)$ pair, as well as the one in the transverse momentum $p_T^Υ$ of either $Υ(1S)$ meson, and compare them with measurements by the CMS Collaboration at the CERN LHC with center-of-mass energies $\sqrt{s}=8$ and $13~\mathrm{TeV}$ as far as they go. The QCD corrections to $σ^{ΥΥ}$ turn out to be mildly negative, with $K$ factors of about 0.9. By contrast, the differential cross sections receive $K$ factors as small as 0.6 and 0.2 at large values of $m_{ΥΥ}\le 100$ GeV and $|Δy|\le4$, respectively, and as large as 20 for large values of $p_T^Υ\le30$ GeV. Our predictions agree well with the data, except in the outermost bins of $m_{ΥΥ}$ and $|Δy|$, where they fall short of the data, leaving room for contributions from double parton scattering as expected. Our analysis thus provides a first solid basis for the extraction of the key parameter $σ_{\mathrm{eff}}$ of double parton scattering.

</details>


### [253] [Dark matter evolution in stellar objects: A Boltzmann transport approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16689)
*Yen-Hsun Lin,Meng-Ru Wu,Seokhoon Yun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用玻尔兹曼输运方程（BTE）研究恒星（特别是太阳）中捕获的暗物质演化，发现当暗物质与原子核散射稀少时，其无法在太阳寿命内热化，导致空间分布更弥散、湮灭率大幅下降；同时将该框架应用于非弹性希格斯微子模型，指出太阳核热运动可开启静止核无法实现的捕获通道，从而显著加强质量劈裂的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暗物质捕获研究常假设被捕获暗物质快速热化并呈等温分布，但该假设在散射截面较小时可能不成立；此外，最新实验（如LUX-ZEPLIN）暗示非弹性暗物质模型，需更精确处理核热运动对捕获的影响。

Method: 建立基于玻尔兹曼输运方程（BTE）的主方程框架，对轨道运动做平均，以能量和角动量为变量，纳入捕获、束缚轨道间散射、湮灭与蒸发过程；应用于太阳环境及非弹性希格斯微子模型，并考虑太阳核的热运动效应。

Result: 1）太阳中被捕获的暗物质在弱散射下无法在太阳年龄内热化，其空间分布比经典等温分布更延展，湮灭率可被压低多个数量级；2）在非弹性希格斯微子模型中，太阳核热运动开启新的捕获通道，使质量劈裂约束增强数十keV。

Conclusion: 经典热化假设在多数参数空间下不成立，BTE方法对准确预测暗物质信号（尤其是湮灭与直接探测）至关重要；核热运动在非弹性模型中起关键作用，不可忽略。

Abstract: Dark matter (DM) captured by stars offers a way to search for physics beyond the Standard Model. We study the evolution of captured DM with the Boltzmann transport equation (BTE), which follows both the abundance and the phase-space distribution of the bound particles from capture toward a stationary state. Averaging over orbital motion, we write the BTE as a master equation in energy and angular momentum that includes capture, scattering between bound orbits, annihilation and evaporation. Applying it to the Sun, we find that captured DM does not thermalize within the solar age when its scattering on nuclei is rare. The bound DM then remains much more extended than the isothermal distribution assumed in the classical treatment, and its annihilation rate can be suppressed by many orders of magnitude. We also apply the BTE to the inelastic Higgsino interpretation of the recent LUX-ZEPLIN result, which involves two DM components coupled through inelastic scattering. The thermal motion of solar nuclei opens capture channels that are closed for nuclei at rest and strengthens the constraint on the mass splitting by tens of keV.

</details>


### [254] [Residual Entanglement vs. Residual Coherence: A Wave Packet Approach in Neutrino Oscillation with Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16709)
*Lekhashri Konwar,Abhishek Kumar Jha,Bhavna Yadav*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在恒定物质密度下，三味中微子振荡中的量子关联，超越了标准的平面波描述，采用了波包形式。通过DUNE实验设置及其扩展基线，分析了残余l1-范数相干性、Concurrence Fill和Three-π纠缠三种量子关联度量，并发现残余l1-范数相干性占主导地位，且对CP相位δ_CP依赖较弱，而其他两种纠缠度量对δ_CP更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索中微子振荡中超越平面波近似的量子关联特性，特别是波包效应与物质效应的相互作用。

Method: 采用波包形式处理三味中微子振荡，在恒定物质密度下计算残余l1-范数相干性、Concurrence Fill和Three-π纠缠，并以跃迁概率表示，结合DUNE实验参数进行数值分析。

Result: 残余l1-范数相干性在所有距离上均主导于其他两种纠缠度量；波包宽度越小，振荡阻尼越早越强；在长距离传播下，所有量子关联趋于与基线L和CP相位δ_CP无关的常数；在DUNE基线上，Concurrence Fill和Three-π纠缠对δ_CP更敏感。

Conclusion: 波包效应显著影响中微子振荡中的量子关联行为，残余l1-范数相干性是比传统纠缠度量更稳健的量子关联指标，尤其在长距离和高精度CP破坏测量中具有重要价值。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate quantum correlations in three-flavor neutrino oscillations beyond the standard plane-wave (PW) description by employing a wave-packet formalism in the presence of constant matter density. We study three quantum correlation measures, namely the residual $l_1$-norm coherence, Concurrence Fill, and Three-$π$ entanglement, and express them in terms of transition probabilities for an initial muon-flavor state. The analysis is performed for the DUNE setup and extended beyond its baseline to study the interplay between wave-packet decoherence and matter effects over long distances. For the DUNE baseline, finite wave-packet widths suppress and smooth the oscillations relative to the PW case, with smaller widths causing stronger damping, while matter effects modify the amplitudes and positions of the oscillation features. Among the considered measures, residual $l_1$-norm coherence dominates over the entanglement measures. At very long propagation distances, wave-packet decoherence progressively washes out the oscillatory behavior, driving the oscillation probabilities and quantum correlation measures toward averaged values, with smaller wave-packet widths leading to earlier damping. Further, in the $L-δ_{\rm CP}$ plane, residual $l_1$-norm coherence exhibits weak $δ_{\rm CP}$ dependence, while Concurrence Fill and Three-$π$ entanglement show greater sensitivity at the DUNE baseline. Beyond a characteristic baseline in the extended propagation regime, all three measures approach constant values, independent of $L$ and $δ_{\rm CP}$.

</details>

Vankova-Kirilova,N. Vassilopoulos,S. Vihonen,J. Wurtz,V. Zeter,O. Zormpa*

TL;DR: 本文研究ESSnuSB大型地下水切伦科夫探测器对太阳中微子两个关键信号的灵敏度：^8B电子中微子存活概率的MSW谱上翘和稀有hep中微子通量的首次直接观测。分析考虑了探测器系统误差和环境本底，并采用形状驱动分析策略，发现能力依赖于谱形畸变而非绝对事例率。结果表明，在现实条件下，需约99%的宇宙射线μ子散裂本底抑制效率才能在20年曝光中达到5σ显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升大型水切伦科夫探测器对太阳中微子关键物理信号（MSW谱上翘与hep通量）的探测能力，以检验标准太阳模型与中微子振荡理论。

Method: 采用形状驱动的统计分析方法，浮动总体归一化；建模并纳入能量分辨率、通量归一化等系统误差，以及μ子散裂和^{222}Rn污染等环境本底；基于Super-K与JUNO经验设定90%散裂本底抑制效率作为基准场景。

Result: 在90%散裂本底抑制效率下，20年曝光无法使MSW谱上翘或hep通量达到5σ显著性；实现两者5σ发现需约99%散裂抑制效率；详细量化了散裂抑制效率与反冲能量阈值对两种信号显著性的联合影响。

Conclusion: ESSnuSB探测器具备探测太阳中微子关键谱特征的潜力，但其实现高度依赖于极高的宇宙射线μ子散裂本底抑制能力（≈99%），凸显先进本底抑制技术对下一代太阳中微子实验的关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of the ESSnuSB large underground water-Cherenkov (WC) detector to two key solar neutrino signatures: the MSW spectral upturn in the $^8$B electron neutrino survival probability $P_{ee}(E_ν)$, and the first direct observation of the rare hep neutrino flux. The analysis incorporates detector systematic uncertainties (energy resolution and flux normalization) and models environmental backgrounds from cosmic-ray muon spallation and $^{222}$Rn contamination. We adopt a shape-driven analysis strategy with a floating overall normalization, so that the discovery power relies on the spectral distortion rather than on the absolute event rate. In a realistic scenario with 90% spallation rejection efficiency, informed by the Super-Kamiokande experience and recent JUNO analyses, residual spallation backgrounds prevent either signal from reaching $5σ$ significance within 20 years of exposure. For the hep flux, we present a detailed sensitivity study to both its discovery and its precision, and quantify how the required spallation veto efficiency and recoil energy threshold jointly determine the achievable significance for both signals. At the adopted thresholds, discovery-level sensitivity for both signals requires approximately 99% spallation rejection over a 20-year exposure.

</details>


### [256] [A unified reduced-mass parametrization of effective chromoelectric interactions in hadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16827)
*Wen-Nian Liu,Duo-Jie Jia,Fu-Quan Dou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个统一的有效质量关系，通过一个共同的约化质量函数描述色电对贡献的味依赖性，并联合拟合了14个自旋平均的介子和重子质量，RMS残差为4 MeV；该模型成功预测了多重粲/底重子及四夸克态（如cccc、bbbb、bcbb̄c̄）的质量，并与格点QCD结果吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一描述介子和重子质量谱，特别是处理轻味与重味区域色电相互作用的不同饱和行为，并实现对多夸克态（如四夸克、多重重重子）质量的可靠预测。

Method: 构建含显式色因子的统一有效质量关系，引入共同约化质量函数刻画色电对贡献的味依赖性；扣除自旋加权色磁贡献后，联合拟合14个自旋平均质量；通过色-自旋重耦合理论估算S波重排衰变权重。

Result: 获得RMS残差仅4 MeV的高质量拟合；预测全粲四夸克态cc̄cc̄的张量质量为6505(9)(9) MeV，标量上限为6562(9)(12) MeV，接近X(6600)；四夸克质量对qq色磁耦合变化不敏感；给出S波衰变通道权重。

Conclusion: 色电相互作用在共同饱和极限下可吸收进有效组分夸克质量，剩余对项主导味依赖；本模型以单一参数集统一描述并成功预测介子、重子及四夸克谱，验证了色相互作用在多夸克体系中的普适建模能力。

Abstract: We construct a unified effective mass relation in which a common reduced-mass function describes the flavor dependence of chromoelectric pair contributions, with explicit color factors connecting $q\bar q$ and $qq$ pairs. After subtracting fixed spin-weighted chromomagnetic contributions, we jointly fit fourteen spin-averaged meson and baryon masses using constituent quark masses and an additive constant shared by both sectors, obtaining a root-mean-square (RMS) residual of 4 MeV. The fitted saturating form connects the nearly saturated light-flavor contribution with increasing attraction in the heavy-flavor region. In the common saturation limit, the leading chromoelectric contribution acts as a common mass shift for each constituent and can be absorbed into effective constituent masses; the residual pair term accounts for the flavor dependence away from saturation. With the chromomagnetic interaction included, this single parameter set reproduces the ground-state meson and baryon spectra and yields predictions for multiply heavy baryons outside the calibration set. By diagonalizing the sum of the chromoelectric and chromomagnetic mass matrices, we further obtain the ground-state spectra of $cc\bar c\bar c$, $bb\bar b\bar b$, and $bc\bar b\bar c$. The fully charmed tensor mass is ${6505}{(9)(9)}$ MeV, close to recent lattice QCD results, while the upper scalar mass of {${6562}{(9)(12)}$} MeV lies in the mass region near $X(6600)$. The tetraquark masses remain relatively stable under the examined variations of the $qq$ chromomagnetic couplings. The relative $S$-wave rearrangement-decay weights obtained from color--spin recoupling can be used to identify favored decay channels under the stated spatial-overlap approximation.

</details>


### [257] [Dilution of cosmological relics and gravitational waves from a supercooled electroweak phase transition in classically scale-invariant models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16845)
*Arnab Chaudhuri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了经典尺度不变标准模型扩展中电弱相变的动力学，发现该相变是强过冷但快速完成的单步过程，其释放的真空能量主要转化为等离子体的整体运动并导致宇宙再加热和熵注入，从而显著影响重子不对称性、暗物质质量和早期引力波背景，并预测LISA可观测到毫赫兹频段的引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典尺度不变的标准模型扩展中，电弱能标由辐射修正产生，且由于缺乏树图级质量项，对称相在极低温度下仍保持亚稳态；本文旨在研究此类模型中电弱相变的具体动力学行为及其宇宙学后果。

Method: 采用二维隧穿路径计算临界泡（bounce）解，并追踪假真空分数经历成核、渗透与完成的全过程；结合声学近似描述引力波产生机制，分析相变动力学、等离子体响应及宇宙学效应。

Result: 电弱相变是单步、强过冷但快速完成的，发生在QCD尺度以上；泡壁迅速达到终端速度，绝大部分真空能转化为等离子体整体动能并热化再加热宇宙；熵注入稀释原有遗迹达数个数量级，提升所需初值重子不对称性，放宽热暗物质质量的幺正性限制，并压制早期引力波背景；若声学近似适用，引力波信号峰值位于毫赫兹频段，满足由真空能决定的振幅-频率标度关系，LISA可在整个可行参数空间内探测。

Conclusion: 该类尺度不变模型中的电弱相变具有独特而可观测的宇宙学印记，尤其在引力波探测和遗迹稀释方面提供关键检验窗口；结果依赖于新增标量的多重性和希格斯门户耦合，且单态能标需≥TeV。

Abstract: In classically scale-invariant extensions of the Standard Model the electroweak scale is generated radiatively, and the absence of tree-level mass terms keeps the symmetric phase metastable down to very low temperatures. We study the resulting phase transition in a model with a real singlet and several additional scalars coupled to the Higgs doublet and to the singlet, in which electroweak symmetry is broken along a Gildener-Weinberg flat direction and a light dilaton appears in the spectrum. Computing the bounce along the two-dimensional tunnelling path and following the false-vacuum fraction through percolation and completion, we find that the transition proceeds in a single, strongly supercooled step and completes above the QCD scale over a region of parameter space consistent with Higgs measurements and perturbativity. Despite the strong supercooling the transition is fast. The bubble walls reach their terminal velocity early, and almost all of the released vacuum energy goes into bulk motion of the plasma, which thermalises and reheats the universe close to the scale set by the vacuum energy. The resulting entropy injection dilutes pre-existing relics by up to several orders of magnitude. It raises the baryon asymmetry that must be present before the transition, relaxes the unitarity bound on the mass of thermal dark matter, and suppresses earlier gravitational-wave backgrounds. If the acoustic description of gravitational-wave production extends to this regime, the signal peaks in the millihertz band, follows an approximate scaling relation between peak amplitude and frequency set by the vacuum energy, and would be observed by LISA throughout the viable region. The results depend on the multiplicity of the new scalars and their Higgs portal coupling at leading order through a single combination, and require the singlet scale to lie at or above the TeV scale.

</details>


### [258] [Exploring the Pion Vector Form Factor Beyond the Physical Sheet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16974)
*Florian Herren,Fatemeh Nouri,Méril Reboud*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用解析性和幺正性，对π介子矢量形状因子在第一和第二黎曼叶上进行了模型无关的参数化，并拟合了来自ALEPH、Belle、CLEO、OPAL、NA7和JLab的实验数据，提取了ρ±介子的极点位置、耦合常数、π介子电荷半径及低能ππ散射参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在不依赖外部散射输入的前提下，精确提取ρ介子基本参数及低能强子物理信息。

Method: 基于解析性和幺正性构建模型无关的π介子矢量形状因子参数化形式，联合拟合τ衰变与类空实验数据。

Result: 得到ρ±质量M_{ρ±} = 762.07±0.63 MeV，宽度Γ_{ρ±} = 143.62^{+0.79}_{-0.80} MeV，以及π介子电荷半径和ππ散射参数，结果与文献一致。

Conclusion: 该方法具有模型无关性与高精度，参数化结果已集成至EOS软件中供公开使用。

Abstract: Using analyticity and unitarity, we parametrise the pion vector form factor on the first and second Riemann sheets, below the inelastic $πω$ threshold. We fit this model-independent parametrisation to the available $τ^-\toπ^-π^0ν_τ$ data from ALEPH, Belle, CLEO and OPAL, together with spacelike data from NA7 and JLab, and extract the pole position and couplings of the $ρ^\pm$ along with the pion charge radius and low-energy $ππ$ scattering parameters. Our results are independent of external scattering input and agree with the literature; in particular, we find $M_{ρ^\pm} = 762.07\pm 0.63$ MeV and $Γ_{ρ^\pm} = 143.62^{+0.79}_{-0.80}$ MeV. The parametrisation is publicly available in the EOS software.

</details>


### [259] [Proton Spin Fluctuation Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17032)
*Tomoya Uji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了质子自旋涨落分解的新框架，通过夸克和胶子的自旋与轨道角动量的方差与协方差来描述，并将其与单/双部分子分布联系起来；该框架揭示了传统自旋分解无法观测到的相关性与涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统质子自旋分解无法反映内部组分间的量子涨落与关联，需建立能刻画自旋不确定性的新分解框架。

Method: 基于量子力学统计诠释，构建质子总自旋的方差分解公式，推导其在单部分子分布（SPD）和双部分子分布（DPD）中的具体表达式，并分析由总自旋守恒导致的方差-协方差约束关系（即求和规则）。

Result: 得到了质子自旋涨落的系统分解形式，明确了夸克/胶子自旋与轨道角动量的方差及相互协方差之间的补偿关系，并指出这些统计量可由实验可测的双部分子分布提取。

Conclusion: 质子自旋不仅包含平均贡献，更蕴含丰富的涨落与关联结构；该分解为理解强子内部量子多体动力学提供了新视角，拓展了自旋物理的研究维度。

Abstract: We formulate a fluctuation decomposition of proton spin in terms of the variances and covariances of the spin and orbital angular momentum of quarks and gluons. We derive their representations in terms of single- and double-parton distributions. The definite proton spin imposes sum rules requiring the variances of its spin and orbital contributions to be compensated by their covariances. This framework reveals correlations and fluctuations that are invisible to the conventional proton spin decomposition.

</details>


### [260] [Nonperturbative Kinetic Description of the Vacuum QGP Production in the Abelian Projection Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17078)
*V. V. Dmitriev,S. V. Churochkina,S. A. Smolyansky,V. A. Tseryupa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在SU(N)量子色动力学的阿贝尔投影框架下，推导出描述强准经典胶子场影响下真空夸克产生（考虑电中性破缺）的非微扰自洽方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决阿贝尔投影方法中强胶子场对真空夸克产生的反作用（back-reaction）这一非微扰问题，并纳入电中性破缺效应。

Method: 基于SU(N) QCD的阿贝尔投影方法，构建并推导非微扰、自洽的方程组。

Result: 获得了一套描述强准经典胶子场下真空夸克产生且考虑电中性破缺的非微扰自洽方程组。

Conclusion: 该方程组为研究QCD真空在强场下的非平衡响应和电荷分离等现象提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Within the framework of the Abelian projection method in the $SU(N)$ variant of QCD, a system of nonperturbative selfconsistent equations for the back-reaction problem is derived. This system describes the vacuum production of quarks under the influence of strong quasiclassical gluon fields taking into account violating of the electroneutrality condition.

</details>


### [261] [Two-loop mixed QCD-EW corrections to bottom-pair production in electron-positron collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17171)
*Simone Devoto,Chinmoy Dey,Michele Dradi,Justin T. Fagnoni,Ziyang Huang,Xinchen Li,Michael J. Ramsey-Musolf,Alessandro Vicini,Jiang Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了电子-正电子对撞中重底夸克偶素产生的两圈强-电弱混合虚修正，采用半解析方法处理含多质量标量的费曼积分，并将该方法推广至高精度电弱二阶修正计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升高能物理实验（如HL-LHC和未来轻子对撞机）中底夸克对产生过程的理论预测精度，需纳入强相互作用与电弱相互作用的混合高阶修正。

Method: 采用半解析技术计算所有相关费曼积分，包括最多五条内线、三种不同质量的图；对电弱规范玻色子引入复质量方案以处理红外与紫外发散；完成紫外重整与红外减除。

Result: 获得了底夸克对产生过程的NNLO QCD-EW截面的有限两圈混合虚修正结果。

Conclusion: 所发展的计算方法具有普适性，可拓展至其他基准过程的全电弱二阶修正计算，服务于未来高精度实验检验标准模型。

Abstract: We present the two-loop mixed strong-electroweak virtual corrections to the production of a massive bottom-antibottom quark pair in electron-positron collisions. The evaluation of all the relevant Feynman integrals, including those with up to five internal massive lines with three different mass values, has been worked out relying on semi-analytical techniques, using complex-valued masses for the electroweak gauge bosons. The ultraviolet-renormalized and infrared-subtracted finite correction contributes to the NNLO QCD-EW cross section of the inclusive production of a bottom-quark pair. The methodology adopted in this calculation can be extended to the study of fully electroweak second-order corrections to benchmark precision calculations relevant to both the high-luminosity phase of the Large Hadron Collider and prospective future lepton colliders.

</details>


### [262] [Monopole catalysis with massive fermions: the fate of the twisted sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17253)
*Valentin V. Khoze*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在最小SU(5)单极子散射中，无质量费米子在最低分波下的散射行为及其在标准模型有质量情况下的衰变过程，发现其截面饱和幺正性极限，并分析了手性翻转与(B+L)破坏通道的分支比随能量变化的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在SU(5)大统一理论框架下，单极子散射产生的分数费米子数态如何在标准模型中（考虑质量与禁闭）衰变为整数费米子数的物理渐近态。

Method: 基于量子场论与散射理论，分析最低分波散射态的拓扑结构、幺正性约束、守恒律限制及能量依赖的衰变动力学；采用Dirichlet分布描述四味费米子间能量分配，并建模高低能极限下的分支比。

Result: 发现该分波散射截面饱和幺正极限σ=π/p²；确定每个入射费米子仅有两个允许衰变道（手性翻转与(B+L)破坏）；电子入射时，(B+L)破坏道对应反核子产生，高能等概率、低能被阈值压制；夸克入射于慢质子时，(B+L)破坏道表现为近乎确定性的催化质子衰变。

Conclusion: Callan关于分数态的统计诠释被深化：该态并非等权叠加态，而是以能量依赖概率衰变为不同终态的单一态，仅在高能极限下两道概率相等。

Abstract: In the massless limit a fermion scattering on the minimal $SU(5)$ monopole in the lowest partial wave produces an outgoing state with half-integer fermion number in every flavour. This state lives in a twisted sector of the Hilbert space and is now understood as an ordinary fermion attached to a topological line running back to the monopole. In the Standard Model fermion masses and confinement destroy the line at distances greater than the inverse mass scales, causing the twisted state to decay into physical asymptotic states with integer fermion numbers. We study this decay for an incoming lepton or quark of one generation. The cross-section in this partial wave saturates the unitarity bound, $σ=π/p^2$, and the energy of the outgoing state is shared among the four fermion flavours with a broad Dirichlet distribution, which sets the thresholds and the kinematics. Conservation laws allow exactly two decay channels for each incoming fermion, the helicity flip and its $(B+L)$-violating partner. For an incoming electron the $(B+L)$-violating channel is anti-nucleon synthesis, and we determine the branching ratios in two limits: the two channels are equally likely at high energy, and only the helicity flip remains at low energy, below the anti-nucleon threshold. In between we model the energy dependence. For a quark in a slow proton the $(B+L)$-violating channel is catalysed proton decay, which has no threshold and occurs with a probability close to one. Callan's statistical interpretation of the fractional state emerges in a sharper form: the state is not a superposition of two final states with equal weights, but a single state that decays into them with energy-dependent probabilities, equal only at high energy.

</details>


### [263] [A Dark-Sector Mass Flip-Flop Mechanism for the ANITA-IV Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17328)
*Dibya S. Chattopadhyay,Carlos A. Argüelles,Vedran Brdar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种暗区质量翻转机制，解释ANITA-IV观测到的4个向上空气簇射候选事件与IceCube未探测到类似事件之间的矛盾：超高温N₁在地内因物质效应质量序反转衰变为N₂，后者穿出地球后在大气中衰变产生簇射；而IceCube深度下该衰变被运动学禁止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和ANITA-IV观测到4个向上UHE ν_τ 候选事例与IceCube在相同能量区间零事例之间的显著矛盾。

Method: 构建含两个中性重粒子N₁、N₂及超轻标量中介子的暗区模型；引入物质诱导的质量翻转机制，使N₁在地球内部衰变为N₂，后者在大气中衰变触发空气簇射；分析运动学允许条件以解释IceCube无事例现象。

Result: 成功解释ANITA-IV的4个事件且自然规避IceCube的零结果；基于ANITA-IV归一化通量，预言PUEO首次飞行将观测到约3个事件。

Conclusion: 该暗区质量翻转机制为ANITA异常提供自洽物理解释，具备可检验性，并拓展了超高能中微子天文学中暗区物理的研究范式。

Abstract: ANITA-IV has observed four upward-going air-shower candidates just below the horizon, consistent with ultra-high-energy $ν_τ$, yet IceCube, despite its much larger exposure, has detected no neutrino events at such energies. We propose a dark-sector mass flip-flop mechanism that reconciles these observations. We consider an all-sky, monoenergetic flux of ultra-high-energy $N_1$, that is lighter than its partner $N_2$ and stable in vacuum. In our scenario, an ultralight scalar mediating interactions with Earth matter inverts the mass ordering of $N_1$ and $N_2$, allowing an incident $N_1$ to decay into $N_2$ deep underground. The resulting $N_2$ propagates unimpeded through matter and exits the Earth, where the decay $N_2 \to N_1 Z'$ becomes kinematically allowed in the atmosphere, initiating an extensive air shower. At IceCube depths, neither $N_1\to N_2 Z'$ nor $N_2\to N_1 Z'$ is kinematically allowed, which explains the absence of analogous events at IceCube. Normalizing the $N_1$ flux to the four ANITA-IV events, we also predict three events during PUEO's first flight.

</details>

### [264] [Monopole crystal and effective theory for a monopole-enhanced 3D U(1) gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15922)
*Alessandro Mariani*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了带单极子增强项的U(1)格点规范理论，发现强单极子增强下会形成反铁磁Néel序基态，并推导出类似正弦-戈登模型的有效理论；同时预测了随单极子化学势变化的一级相变及随规范耦合变化的Ising普适类相变，并通过数值模拟验证部分预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解带单极子增强项的U(1)格点规范理论的相结构与低能有效描述。

Method: 结合解析方法（如Polyakov论证、有效场论推导）与数值模拟方法研究相图和相变性质。

Result: 发现强单极子增强导致反铁磁Néel有序基态；推导出类正弦-戈登有效模型；预测并部分验证了一级相变和Ising类相变。

Conclusion: 单极子密度显著升高可彻底改变系统基态与低能物理，但某些普适特征（如有效模型形式、特定相变类型）仍得以保持。

Abstract: Recently, a version of U(1) lattice gauge theory on the cubic lattice with a monopole enhancement term was considered in the literature. In this work, we further our understanding of this theory with a mixture of analytical and numerical methods. We show that for sufficiently large monopole enhancement, the monopoles are no longer dilute and they arrange themselves into an antiferromagnetic, Néel-ordered ground state. Nonetheless, a variant of Polyakov's argument for the dilute excitations over the dense ground state leads to an effective sine-Gordon model which is qualitatively similar to that of the standard theory. We also predict analytically the presence of first-order transitions as the monopole chemical potential is varied at fixed gauge coupling, corresponding to a change in the dominant monopole charge. Similarly, we predict a transition in the Ising universality class as the gauge coupling is varied. We then use numerical methods to study the phase diagram of the theory and check some of our predictions.

</details>


### [265] [Critical behavior of the bosonized Schwinger model with uniform matrix product states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16092)
*Takuya Okuda*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过玻色化模型的哈密顿格点离散化，结合VUMPS算法和Symanzik有效理论，精确计算了θ=π时连续Schwinger模型的临界质量，结果为(m/g)_c = 0.333555(5)，与现有费米子方法结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定二维量子电动力学（Schwinger模型）在θ=π时的临界质量，以验证不同理论方法的一致性并提升数值精度。

Method: 采用玻色化形式的哈密顿格点离散化，用VUMPS算法求解无限链基态，局域希尔伯特空间使用自由极限下单粒子约化密度矩阵对角化的谐振子基，并利用Symanzik有效理论约束连续极限外推拟合函数。

Result: 获得临界质量比值(m/g)_c = 0.333555(5)，误差极小，与最新交错费米子方法结果在同等精度下一致。

Conclusion: 该玻色化+张量网络方法是计算强关联规范场论临界参数的一种高精度、对称性保持的有效途径。

Abstract: Quantum electrodynamics in two spacetime dimensions, also known as the Schwinger model, admits a dual formulation in terms of a non-compact boson field. Using a Hamiltonian lattice discretization of the bosonized model, we compute the critical mass of the continuum Schwinger model at $θ=π$. We numerically obtain the ground state on an infinite chain by the variational uniform matrix product state (VUMPS) algorithm, preserving charge conjugation exactly. As a truncated basis for the local Hilbert space, we employ the oscillator basis that diagonalizes the one-site reduced density matrix of the free limit. We constrain the fitting functions for the continuum extrapolation using the Symanzik effective theory for lattice corrections. Our result for the critical mass, $(m/g)_c = 0.333\,555(5)$, agrees with recent staggered-fermion determinations at comparable precision.

</details>


### [266] [Automatic O(a) Improvement for Wilson Twisted Mass Fermions in the Hamiltonian Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16658)
*Carsten Urbach*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文应用Symanzik有效哈密顿量框架分析Wilson扭曲质量费米子，证明了在最大扭结处的自动O(a)改进，并推导了用于Wilson费米子clover壳上改进所需的O(a)抵消项结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解并改进格点QCD中Wilson费米子的离散化误差，特别是O(a)误差，需系统分析其对称性与有效理论结构。

Method: 基于格点哈密顿量的对称性构建Symanzik有效哈密顿量（不依赖欧氏时空中的Symanzik有效作用量），结合规范场理论、对称性分析和正则变换方法。

Result: 证明了最大扭结下O(a)自动改进；给出了mass与Wilson平均实现O(a)改进的机制；导出了clover壳上改进所需的O(a)抵消项结构；发现各向同性欧氏时空中的clover项分裂为两个具独立系数的项。

Conclusion: Symanzik有效哈密顿量框架可有效刻画Wilson扭曲质量费米子的O(a)效应，为构造更高精度的格点费米子方案提供了理论基础与实用工具。

Abstract: We apply the recently developed Symanzik effective Hamiltonian framework to Wilson twisted mass fermions. The effective Hamiltonian is constructed solely based on the symmetries of the lattice Hamiltonian without recourse to the Symanzik effective action in Euclidean space-time. We use it to prove automatic $O(a)$ improvement at maximal twist, as well as $O(a)$ improvement via so-called mass and Wilson average. Moreover, we derive the structure of $O(a)$ counterterms pre-requisite for clover on-shell improvement of Wilson fermions. We show how to remove on-shell redundant contributions using canonical transformations, and find that the well known clover term in isotropic Euclidean space time splits into two terms with independent coefficients.

</details>


### [267] [Numerical tests of chiral fermion weights with $η$-invariant phases in the 3450 model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16876)
*Tatsuhiro Misumi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文数值验证了一种将连续手性费米子耦合到插值格点规范场的新方案，该方案采用Pauli-Villars正则化模长与高一维Atiyah-Patodi-Singer η-不变量定义的相位；在无磁通和单位磁通两种情形下，该方案显著提升行列式与关联函数计算精度，并验证了反常抵消机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服格点QCD中手性费米子的困难（如Nielsen-Ninomiya定理限制），需构造能保持手性对称性和正确反常结构的离散化方案；本文旨在数值检验一种基于APS η-不变量相位与PV正则化模长的新型耦合处方。

Method: 在二维无反常U(1)手性理论（左右手荷为3,4和5,0）上进行数值模拟：使用16×16平直规范场构型计算Polyakov圈；通过真空极化响应提取诱导质量；在单位磁通下，于圆柱几何中采用广义APS边界条件计算十二点关联函数；对比含/不含APS相位的结果，并检验不同三维延拓下相位变化的反常抵消。

Result: 在零磁通下，含APS相位时Polyakov圈误差<0.04%，而无相位时仅剩约3%精确值；诱导质量m/e=2.82089，与理论值5/√π=2.82095高度吻合；单位磁通下十二点关联函数模长误差<0.8%，相位误差由1.8–3.0 mrad降至0.2–0.5 mrad；各费米子相位随三维延拓变化达15–46 mrad，但在反常抵消组合中残余<0.2 mrad。

Conclusion: APS η-不变量相位对恢复手性反常和精确量子效应至关重要；该方案在格点上成功实现了连续手性费米子的物理正确耦合，且反常抵消在离散设置中得以保持，为推广至四维非阿贝尔理论提供了坚实数值支持。

Abstract: We test numerically a recently proposed prescription for coupling continuum chiral fermions to interpolated lattice gauge fields, in which the fermion weight has a Pauli-Villars (PV) regularized magnitude and a phase given by an Atiyah-Patodi-Singer (APS) $η$-invariant in one higher dimension. We use the anomaly-free two-dimensional $U(1)$ theory with left-handed charges $3,4$ and right-handed charges $5,0$, whose torus determinant is known exactly. At zero magnetic flux, with gauge fields interpolated from lattice links, the Polyakov loops evaluated on a $16\times16$ grid of flat connections agree with the exact values within $0.04\%$, whereas omitting the APS phase substantially reduces them to about $3\%$ of the exact result. The vacuum-polarization response yields an induced mass $m/e=2.82089$, close to $5/\sqrtπ=2.82095$. At unit flux, where zero modes make the determinant vanish, we calculate a twelve-point correlator on a cylinder with the generalized APS boundary condition. For three smooth backgrounds its magnitude agrees with the exact result within $0.8\%$. Without the $η$-invariant its phase deviates by $1.8$-$3.0$ mrad, and with it by $0.2$-$0.5$ mrad. The phase of each charged fermion changes by $15$-$46$ mrad when the gauge field is extended differently into the third dimension, but the change cancels to below $0.2$ mrad in the anomaly-free combination.

</details>


### [268] [$NΩ$ Dibaryon near Unitarity from Physical-Point Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16989)
*Liang Zhang,Yan Lyu,Sinya Aoki,Takumi Doi,Tetsuo Hatsuda,Song Zhang,Yu-Gang Ma*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用HAL QCD方法，在物理夸克质量下通过(2+1)味格点QCD计算了NΩ系统的$^{5}S_{2}$相互作用，发现其具有吸引力并支持一个接近幺正极限的浅束缚态，给出了中子-Ω和质子-Ω束缚能的首次第一性原理结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NΩ系统是潜在的双重子候选者，因其组分核子与Ω重子之间无共有价夸克，可避免价夸克Pauli排斥效应，从而可能形成束缚态。

Method: 采用HAL QCD方法，在物理π介子质量（~137 MeV）下进行(2+1)味格点QCD模拟，提取$^{5}S_{2}$ NΩ相互作用势，并通过T矩阵极点分析确定束缚能；同时考虑有限电荷半径下的库仑吸引修正。

Result: NΩ相互作用在可分辨距离内呈吸引力，支持一个近幺正的浅束缚态；直接T矩阵极点分析得中子-Ω束缚能$B_{nΩ} = 0.61\,(^{+0.41}_{-0.30})\,(^{+0.60}_{-0.18})\,\text{MeV}$，计入库仑效应后质子-Ω束缚能为$B_{pΩ} = 1.25\,(^{+0.46}_{-0.39})\,(^{+0.69}_{-0.25})\,\text{MeV}$。

Conclusion: 该工作首次从第一性原理出发给出了NΩ相互作用与束缚能的定量预测，为未来NΩ束流粒子学（femtoscopy）及双重子实验搜索提供了关键理论基准。

Abstract: The $NΩ$ system is a promising dibaryon candidate because its constituents share no valence quarks and therefore avoid valence-quark Pauli repulsion. We determine the $^{5}S_{2}$ $NΩ$ interaction in (2+1)-flavor lattice QCD at physical quark masses ($m_π\simeq137~\text{MeV}$) using the HAL QCD method. The interaction is attractive over the resolved range and supports a shallow state near unitarity. A direct $T$-matrix pole analysis gives $B_{nΩ} = 0.61\,(^{+0.41}_{-0.30})\,(^{+0.60}_{-0.18})~\text{MeV}$; including finite-charge-size Coulomb attraction yields $B_{pΩ} = 1.25\,(^{+0.46}_{-0.39})\,(^{+0.69}_{-0.25})~\text{MeV}$. These results provide a first-principles benchmark for future $NΩ$ femtoscopy and dibaryon searches.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.IM'></div>

# astro-ph.IM [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [MAHA-z: A Mahalanobis distance kNN estimator for photometric redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14582)
*Paula S. Ferreira,Ribamar R. R. Reis,Renyue Cen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MAHA-z的光度红移估计器，结合k近邻回归与基于光谱参考样本协方差学习的马氏距离度量，在多个数据集上实现了高精度、低计算成本的红移估计与后验分布建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光度红移估计方法常需人工归一化、难以处理缺失波段和特征相关性；为提升精度、鲁棒性与计算效率，尤其面向第四代巡天（如Euclid、LSST、DES），亟需一种无需插补、自适应度量且可提供校准后验分布的新方法。

Method: MAHA-z采用k近邻回归，使用从光谱参考样本协方差学习得到的马氏距离度量进行邻域搜索；在颜色-星等（含可选形态参数）特征空间中对每个目标星系检索邻居，并通过核密度估计构建红移后验分布p(z|f)；马氏度量支持子空间限制，天然处理每星系级的缺失波段。

Result: 在Euclid Flagship 2模拟数据上达到σ_NMAD=0.0148、离群率0.27%；在DES Y3 BAO样本上σ_NMAD≈0.023、离群率<1%，后验校准媲美ANNz2；处理百万星系仅需数分钟，计算成本显著低于训练型机器学习模型。

Conclusion: MAHA-z是一种高效、鲁棒、可解释的光度红移估计算法，特别适用于第四代巡天中迭代式红移生成与验证任务，兼具高精度、良好后验校准和低计算开销优势。

Abstract: We present \texttt{MAHA-z}, a photometric-redshift (photo-$z$) estimator that combines $k$-nearest neighbour ($k$NN) regression with a Mahalanobis distance metric learned from the covariance of a spectroscopic reference sample. For each target galaxy, \texttt{MAHA-z} performs a neighbour search in a colour-magnitude feature space with optional morphological parameters, and returns both a point estimate and a redshift posterior $p(z\mid\mathbf{f})$ obtained by kernel density estimation over the neighbour redshifts. The Mahalanobis metric accounts for correlated features and heterogeneous units, eliminating ad hoc normalisation, and its subspace restriction allows \texttt{MAHA-z} to handle missing bands on a per-galaxy basis without imputation. We validate the method on four datasets spanning simulations and survey photometry: the Euclid Flagship~2 mock, the Legacy-KiDZ calibration sample, VIPERS, and the DES Year~3 BAO sample. On Euclid Flagship~2 (LSST+NISP) we achieve $σ_{\rm NMAD}=0.0148$ with a $0.27\%$ outlier fraction, and on DES Y3 we obtain $σ_{\rm NMAD}\approx0.023$ with $<1\%$ outliers and posterior calibration comparable to the \texttt{ANNz2} catalogue. \texttt{MAHA-z} processes millions of galaxies in minutes and provides competitive PDFs at substantially lower computational cost than trained machine-learning models, making it a practical option for iterative photo-$z$ production and validation in Stage-IV surveys.

</details>


### [270] [galpy with differentiable, accelerated backends: galactic dynamics in jax and PyTorch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14614)
*Jo Bovy*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了galpy（银河动力学Python库）的扩展，新增了JAX和PyTorch作为计算后端，支持GPU加速、自动微分与编译，保持原有接口不变，显著提升轨道积分、分布函数采样等任务的速度，并展示了其在势能拟合与神经网络学习中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升galpy在大规模数值模拟、可微分建模与AI融合场景下的性能与灵活性，需支持GPU加速、自动微分及前端框架集成。

Method: 设计统一的后端抽象框架，将galpy各模块（引力势、轨道积分、作用角变换、分布函数等）重构为支持numpy、C、JAX和PyTorch的多后端实现；通过输入数组类型自动切换后端；验证数值一致性并确保API兼容性。

Result: GPU上批量轨道积分、分布函数采样及潮汐流建模比单核CPU快1–5个数量级；成功应用于超高速星拟合银河势能、用神经网络学习逆作用角变换；代码主要由AI编码助手在人工指导下完成并经严格验证。

Conclusion: 该扩展标志着galpy v2的启动，显著增强了其在现代科学计算与AI驱动天体物理建模中的适用性，为可微分天体力学提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We describe an extension of galpy, the Python library for galactic dynamics, that adds jax and PyTorch as computational backends alongside its existing numpy and C implementation. With it, galpy's gravitational potentials, orbit integration, action-angle transformations and their inverses, and distribution functions, including those for tidal streams, run on GPUs, can be compiled as part of a user's program, and can be differentiated with respect to their inputs and parameters, from the force at a point to the samples drawn from a distribution function. The user-facing design is kept simple: the active backend follows from the type of the arrays that are passed in, the galpy interface is unchanged, and the numpy path gives the same results as before. We describe the design of the backend framework and its implementation in each of galpy's modules, with examples of use, and we discuss its limitations. On a GPU, batches of orbits, samples from distribution functions, and stream models run one to five orders of magnitude faster than on a single CPU core. To illustrate the new capabilities, we present simple examples of fitting the Galactic potential with a hypervelocity star and of learning the inverse action-angle transformation with a neural network trained through galpy. An AI coding agent wrote essentially all of the code under the detailed direction and review of the human author, and we describe how we organized this work and how we established the correctness of the result. This work is the start of galpy's version 2, which is available at https://github.com/jobovy/galpy and documented at https://docs.galpy.org/en/v2/ .

</details>


### [271] [ViViViT: a Video-to-Video Vision Transformer for Cosmological Lightcone Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14617)
*Federico Semenzato,Alvise Raccanelli,Michele Liguori*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出ViViViT，一种用于从21cm中性氢模拟光锥重建物质场的视觉Transformer模型，通过分解横向帧和径向演化的注意力机制，在谱功率、相位对齐及三点相关性等指标上优于3D-U-Net基线模型，并支持时间感知的宇宙学示踪物重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以在重建宇宙物质场时自然地处理沿视线方向（即随红移变化）的时间演化特性；作者希望构建一种能原生尊重宇宙时间演化的场级架构。

Method: 提出ViViViT——一种新型视觉Transformer，将注意力机制在横向空间帧与径向（红移/时间）演化维度上进行解耦建模，将光锥重建任务视为视频到视频的翻译任务，并应用于21cm中性氢模拟数据（z∈[7.66,16.00]）。

Result: 在谱功率和相位对齐上误差仅数个百分点（达奈奎斯特尺度），三点相关函数在所有三角构型（最大波数k≈0.77 h/Mpc）下均优于3D-U-Net基线；成功编码了观测量自身的演化几何结构。

Conclusion: ViViViT是首个将宇宙时间演化显式嵌入架构设计的场级重建模型，为时间感知的宇宙示踪物重建及红移依赖信号的定向搜寻提供了新路径。

Abstract: Moving along the line of sight of a cosmological lightcone also moves through cosmic time, as in a video of cosmic structure traced by an evolving observable. We recast the reconstruction of the underlying matter field from observed lightcones as a video-to-video translation task by introducing ViViViT, a vision transformer that factorizes attention across transverse frames and radial evolution. On reconstructed matter fields from 21cm neutral hydrogen simulations over $z\in[7.66,16.00]$, ViViViT preserves spectral power and phase alignment within a few percent of the ground truth up to the Nyquist scale, and three-point correlations for all triangle configurations with sides up to $k\simeq0.77\,h/\mathrm{Mpc}$, outperforming a convolutional 3D-U-Net baseline across all measured statistics. By encoding the evolving geometry of the cosmological observable itself, ViViViT marks a step toward field-level architectures that natively respect cosmic time, opening the path to time-aware reconstruction of cosmic tracers and targeted searches for redshift-dependent signatures in 21cm data.

</details>


### [272] [Beyond classification: regressing physical merger properties using solid harmonic wavelet bispectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14630)
*Alex Brown,Georgios Exarchakis,Mathilda Avirett-Mackenzie,Carolin Villforth*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于固有谐波小波散射双谱的新方法，用于星系并合的识别与回归分析，在样本需求少、抗噪性强的同时，首次实现了对并合质量比和并合时间的同步回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CNN等深度学习方法虽能较好识别星系并合，但依赖大量训练样本且仅限于分类任务；而星系并合识别本身具有复杂形态特征和回归需求，亟需更高效鲁棒的方法。

Method: 采用固有谐波小波散射双谱（solid harmonic wavelet scattering bispectrum）方法，该方法显式编码数据中的不变性与对称性，无需通过训练学习，应用于模拟星系数据集。

Result: 该方法在识别性能上优于现有标准方法，所需训练样本更少、抗噪性更强，并首次实现对并合质量比（误差~0.33 dex）和并合时间（误差~0.66 Gyr）的同步回归。

Conclusion: 固有谐波小波散射双谱是一种有前景的替代当前机器学习技术的新范式，尤其适用于小样本、需回归且具物理对称性的天体图像分析任务。

Abstract: Galaxy mergers play an important role in galaxy evolution; they enhance star formation, transform morphologies, and may drive accretion onto black holes. Due to their complex morphological features, identification of mergers poses a significant challenge. Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have been successful at identifying mergers with good accuracy. However, similar to other deep learning techniques such as Vision Transformers, they require large training samples. Current methods are also limited to classification, rather than regression. Here, we apply the solid harmonic wavelet scattering bispectrum, a method that encodes invariances and symmetries in the data which need to be learned by CNNs, as an alternative to CNNs. We apply our methodology to a simulated dataset of galaxies with a wide range of merger properties. We show that this method outperforms standard methodologies, while requiring fewer training samples and being robust to noise. Most importantly, we show that for the first time this method is able to regress on both merger mass ratio and time since merger simultaneously, with uncertainties ~ 0.33 dex uncertainty in merger mass ratio and ~ 0.66 Gyr uncertainty in time since merger. We have therefore shown that solid harmonic wavelet bispectrum presents an alternative to currently used machine learning techniques.

</details>


### [273] [MAGI (Multivariate Autoencoder for particle Generative Inference): a generative Machine Learning framework for accelerated multi-stage particle simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14710)
*Francesco Monastra,Marco Pietrosanto,Giuseppe Angora,Stefano Cavuoti,Francesco Fenu,Simone Lotti,Matteo Perri*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: MAGI是一种条件变分自编码器，用于学习粒子穿过封闭表面的联合能量、位置和方向分布，以在小质量模型中重采样并重新注入，从而加速蒙特卡洛粒子模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛粒子模拟因粒子输运计算成本高而受限，尤其在大质量包围小敏感体积时更为严重。

Method: MAGI采用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）建模粒子穿越封闭表面的联合分布，并在简化几何模型中重采样与再注入；通过Geant4仿真验证其在探测器层面谱形与计数的准确性。

Result: 注入粒子在探测器端的产额与全模拟一致（约1σ内）；统计放大因子χ⁻¹达150–12000，计算加速比S达2.1–240，取决于源类型与质量模型。

Conclusion: MAGI v1.0.0已开源，初步结果验证了其在加速高保真粒子输运模拟方面的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Monte Carlo particle simulations are hindered by the computational cost of particle transport. This is especially true when large mass surrounds a small sensitive volume. MAGI is a conditional variational autoencoder that learns the joint energy, position and direction distribution of particles crossing a closed surface in a multi-stage simulation, to be resampled and re-injected in a smaller mass model. We validated it generating crossings, variables describing particle features later in the simulation, injecting them into the same Geant4 mass model and comparing the spectrum and counts at detector level with full simulation. Rates per injected crossing agree with unity at $\sim1σ$ on our benchmark models. We measure the gain of MAGI with statistical amplification $1/χ$ and computational speed-up $S$: the first goes from 150 for high-statistics cosmic rays to 12000 for rare radioactive decays, while the latter ranges from 2.1 to almost 240, depending on source type and mass model. These first results validate MAGI v1.0.0 released on GitHub.

</details>


### [274] [Characterization of the mechanical vibrations of the Schmidt Camera at the Tonantzintla Astronomical Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14732)
*Miguel Antonio Nieto-Suarez,Daniel Ferrusca R*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文开发并验证了一种基于MEMS加速度计和微控制器的低成本、便携式天文望远镜振动监测系统，并首次对墨西哥Tonantzintla天文台历史悠久的Schmidt相机进行了振动特性表征，揭示了主导频率成分及其幅值，为振动抑制、数据滤波与图像质量建模提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械振动会降低天文望远镜成像质量，而历史望远镜（如Tonantzintla的Schmidt相机）长期缺乏振动特性数据，亟需低成本、可部署的监测手段以支撑新仪器集成与遗产保护。

Method: 设计并实现基于MEMS加速度计与微控制器的便携式振动监测系统，具备传感器采集与实时数据分析能力；先在实验室进行受控测试验证，再实地部署于Tonantzintla天文台Schmidt相机开展现场测量与频谱分析。

Result: 首次获得该80年历史Schmidt相机的实测振动频谱，识别出主导频率成分及对应幅值，证实了系统可有效捕捉结构动态行为与潜在耦合效应。

Conclusion: 低功耗惯性传感技术可行且适用于遗产级望远镜的振动监测，研究成果为后续减振策略、图像质量建模及天文台仪器升级与保护提供关键数据支撑与方法基础。

Abstract: Mechanical vibrations in astronomical telescopes can degrade image quality, making their detection and characterization essential. This work presents the development and validation of a low-cost, portable vibration monitoring system based on MEMS accelerometers interfaced with a microcontroller. The system integrates sensor acquisition and data analysis capabilities and was evaluated through controlled laboratory tests.
  The system was subsequently deployed at the historic Schmidt Camera of the Tonantzintla Astronomical Observatory in Mexico. After more than 80 years of operation, this study represents the first characterization of its mechanical vibrations. Preliminary measurements reveal dominant frequency components and their corresponding amplitudes, providing insight into the telescope's dynamic behavior and potential structural couplings that may affect image quality.
  Understanding the vibrational environment of astronomical telescopes is essential for the successful integration of new instrumentation. Identifying vibration frequencies and amplitudes can support the development of mitigation strategies, data filtering techniques, and numerical simulations to predict image quality degradation. The results demonstrate the feasibility of low-cost inertial sensing for monitoring heritage-class telescopes and provide a foundation for future instrumentation and preservation efforts at the Tonantzintla Observatory.

</details>


### [275] [VIDE 2.0: Robust, Scalable Void Finding for Extremely Large Galaxy Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14742)
*Paul M. Sutter,Benjamin D. Wandelt*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VIDE 2.0，一种显著改进的宇宙空洞（void）识别算法与分析流程，具备参数无关性、边界处理鲁棒性、高计算效率及内存优化，适用于千万级星系巡天数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对当前和未来光谱与测光星系巡天产生的海量星系数据（数千万量级）所带来的计算挑战。

Method: 基于Voronoi三角剖分与Watershed算法实现参数无关的空洞识别；引入确定性边缘星系识别与空洞污染标记机制以稳健处理巡天边界与不完备性；自动设定掩模分辨率，实现全流程参数无关；并对底层VIDE引擎进行多项性能优化。

Result: 相比VIDE 1.0，速度提升超100倍、内存使用大幅下降；1200万星系DESI Y1模拟数据可在单台笔记本15分钟内完成；可识别更靠近边界的空洞，并在多个关键统计量（空洞数密度函数、空洞-星系交叉关联、空洞椭率分布）上验证了宇宙学方差协方差收敛性。

Conclusion: VIDE 2.0已具备处理大规模模拟与真实观测数据的能力，为第四阶段（Stage IV）空洞宇宙学研究做好准备。

Abstract: Current and future spectroscopic and photometric galaxy surveys will deliver unprecedented numbers of galaxies, with expected counts in the tens of millions. To answer the computational challenges posed by these surveys, in this paper we present VIDE 2.0, a significantly improved and enhanced void finding algorithm and analysis pipeline based on VIDE. Leveraging the parameter-free identification of voids via a Voronoi tessellation and watershed scheme, we implement a robust mechanism for handling survey boundaries and incompleteness without relying on vast numbers of boundary particles. This new method identifies edge galaxies and flags voids contaminated by their presence. The mechanism is fully deterministic, producing bit-for-bit reproducible catalogs from identical inputs. An automatic rule for setting the mask resolution renders the entire pipeline parameter-free. Combined with other performance enhancements to the underlying VIDE engine, we find significant ($>100\times$) speedups and a dramatic reduction in memory usage compared to previous versions: a 12-million-galaxy DESI Y1-class mock catalog now completes in roughly 15 minutes on a single laptop, where VIDE 1.0 could not run a catalog of even $10^6$ galaxies on the same hardware. We are also able to identify voids existing closer to survey boundaries via deterministic per-void contamination flags, maximizing their statistical potential. We exercise the upgraded pipeline on two large mock suites (MultiDark-Patchy DR12 and AbacusSummit DESI Y1 ELG), demonstrating converged cosmic-variance covariances for the void number function, the void-galaxy cross-correlation, and the void ellipticity distribution -- key ingredients for Stage IV void cosmology. VIDE 2.0 is now well suited to handle large numbers of galaxies in mock and real observations, and ready to tackle the challenges of future campaigns.

</details>


### [276] [Accurate calibration method of the Back-linked Fabry Perot interferometer using null channel for space-based gravitational wave antenna](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14788)
*Yusuke Okuma,Shoki Iwaguchi,Ryosuke Sugimoto,Yutaro Enomoto,Kiwamu Izumi*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于三角激光链路中三路干涉仪输出构建零通道的方法，用于校准Back-linked Fabry-Perot干涉仪（BLFPI）的腔响应，以有效抑制激光频率噪声，显著提升引力波探测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLFPI中腔响应校准不准确会直接限制激光频率噪声的抑制，而目前尚无实用的腔响应校准策略。

Method: 利用三角激光链路的三个干涉仪输出构造零通道（GW信号抵消），通过优化腔响应参数使零通道积分功率最小化，从而估计腔响应；并解析推导技术噪声引起的估计偏差，数值评估方法性能。

Result: 校准后残余激光频率噪声在整个观测频带内比BLFPI设计灵敏度低一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 所提出的零通道校准方法达到了有效扣除激光频率噪声所需的精度要求。

Abstract: Accurate calibration of the detector response is essential for precision gravitational-wave (GW) observations. In the Back-linked Fabry-Perot Interferometer (BLFPI), which employs Fabry-Perot cavities to achieve high GW sensitivity, accurate calibration of the cavity response is particularly important because residual calibration errors directly limit the suppression of laser frequency noise. However, no practical calibration strategy for the cavity response has yet been established. In this paper, we propose a method for calibrating the cavity response using a null channel constructed from the three interferometer outputs of the triangular laser-link configuration. In null channel, GW signals are canceled and residual laser frequency noise indicates errors in the cavity response estimates. Therefore we can estimate the cavity response by optimizing the response parameters to minimize the integrated power of the null channel. We derive the estimation bias introduced by technical noise analytically and evaluate the performance of the proposed method numerically. The results show that the residual laser frequency noise after calibration is more than an order of magnitude below the designed BLFPI sensitivity throughout the observation band. This demonstrates that the proposed null-channel calibration method achieves the accuracy required for effective subtraction of laser frequency noise.

</details>


### [277] [Sky Monitor AI: classifying night-sky conditions from an observatory all-sky camera with a CNN ensemble, label-free sky types and exact evidence maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14987)
*Muhammad Waqas*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的方法，用于小规模远程天文台的夜空云量自动分类，通过结合手工特征、小型CNN和预训练ResNet模型的集成，在86.7%准确率（AUC 0.924）下实现了稳定可靠的实时天空监测，并开源了Sky Monitor AI工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型和远程操控天文台需每晚判断夜空是否足够晴朗以开展观测，传统依赖人工或简单阈值方法效率低、鲁棒性差，亟需自动化、高精度且适应实际观测条件（如月光干扰、标签噪声）的云检测方案。

Method: 使用8806张来自32个夜晚的全天相机图像，设计圆形掩模去除时间戳干扰；对比手工提取的天空统计特征、从零训练的小型CNN、以及融合两个ImageNet预训练ResNet-18变体的验证集优选集成模型；引入梯度可视化、局部logit分解、预训练嵌入聚类等可解释性分析；所有评估均按整晚留出进行。

Result: 集成模型在6个测试夜达到86.7%准确率（AUC 0.924），夜际波动最小；月光导致其准确率变化仅5个百分点，远优于手工方法的20个百分点；预训练嵌入无监督聚类可达87%纯度，并能识别镜头水滴污染帧；logit分解支持梯度无关的云量估计与证据图生成；开源Sky Monitor AI应用支持单帧、整夜及实时流式处理。

Conclusion: 匹配模型容量与有限独立夜晚数至关重要（如微调深层ResNet易过拟合）；集成轻量与预训练模型是小样本天文图像分类的有效范式；可解释性分析证实网络聚焦于天空区域，支持物理可信决策；该方法已工程化落地并开源，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Small and remotely operated observatories must decide every night whether the sky is clear enough to observe. We classify frames of the auxiliary all-sky camera of the Higashi-Hiroshima Observatory as clear or cloudy, using 8806 frames from 32 nights. A circular sky mask removes a printed date stamp that would act as a shortcut, and all evaluations use whole held-out nights. On six test nights, hand-crafted sky statistics reach 77.1% accuracy, a small CNN trained from scratch 84.3%, and a validation-selected ensemble of the small CNN and two ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-18 variants 86.7% (AUC 0.924) with the smallest night-to-night scatter. Moonlight is the dominant difficulty: across Moon altitude the ensemble's accuracy varies by 5 percentage points, the hand-crafted model's by 20. Fine-tuning deeper ResNet layers overfits with about 24 training nights, underlining the need to match model capacity to the number of independent nights. The labels were assigned in blocks of 1-2 h, and many remaining errors are mislabelled or partly cloudy frames. Without labels, clustering of pretrained CNN embeddings recovers the clear/cloudy split with 87% purity and isolates frames with water droplets on the camera dome. Gradient-based maps and a rim test indicate that the networks rely on sky regions. For networks ending in global average pooling, the decision decomposes exactly into local logits, giving gradient-free evidence maps and a cloud-cover estimate. We release Sky Monitor AI, an open-source application for single frames, whole nights and live cameras.

</details>


### [278] [Low-Cost Streetlight Shields for Dark-Sky Protection at a Stargazing Camp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15163)
*Xiaoyong Liu,Ruobin Ding,Sixuan Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了在内蒙古巴彦戈尔星光营地安装定向遮光罩以减少道路照明对天文观测的干扰。通过比较安装前后SQM-L测量值，发现低仰角（0°）下光污染显著降低，且该方法成本较低、效果明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路照明在观星营地会产生眩光和杂散光，干扰天文观测，因此需要一种低成本、高效的方法来减少光污染。

Method: 在内蒙古巴彦戈尔星光营地现有太阳能路灯上加装定向遮光罩，并在营地中心（P1）和约500米外地点（P2）使用SQM-L设备测量不同仰角（0°、30°、60°、90°）下的天空亮度变化；同时拍摄相同曝光参数的照片进行视觉对比。

Result: 在0°仰角下，P1点平均SQM读数提升1.624 mag/arcsec²，P2点朝向营地方向提升2.312 mag/arcsec²，对应等效信号分别减少约77.6%和88.1%；更高仰角变化较小；照片显示眩光明显减弱。

Conclusion: 定向遮光罩能有效抑制低仰角方向的杂散光，显著改善观星环境，是一种适用于现有路灯系统的低成本光污染缓解方案。

Abstract: Road lighting at stargazing camps can cause glare and stray light that interfere with observation. We installed directional shields on existing solar-powered streetlights at Bayan Gol Starlight Camp in Inner Mongolia, China, and compared SQM-L measurements before and after installation at the camp center (P1) and a site approximately 500~m away (P2). Measurements were taken at elevations of $0^\circ$, $30^\circ$, $60^\circ$, and $90^\circ$. At $0^\circ$, the mean SQM reading across four directions at P1 increased by 1.624~\sqmUnit{}, and the reading toward the camp at P2 increased by 2.312~\sqmUnit{}. These changes correspond to reductions of approximately 77.6\% and 88.1\% in the equivalent SQM signal within the measured fields of view. The largest changes occurred at $0^\circ$; changes at $30^\circ$ and above were smaller. Photographs taken with identical exposure settings showed less visible glare around the shielded lamps. The measurements indicate that the changes were concentrated in low-elevation views. Directional shields provide a relatively low-cost way to reduce unwanted light while retaining the existing road-lighting system.

</details>


### [279] [Performance Analysis of a Dual-Polarised Bowtie Antenna in the Low RF Band: A Low-Cost Undergraduate Laboratory Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15292)
*Robin Sen,Akshay Bhatia Neriyanuri,Geetanjali Sethi*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种用于本科射电天文实验的双极化蝴蝶结偶极子天线的简易实验表征，重点分析其性能并演示了偏振旋转测量应用，服务于Sky Watch Array Network (SWAN)阵列单元响应建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为本科生射电天文实验提供一种简单、经济且能研究射频信号偏振特性的双极化天线方案。

Method: 搭建并表征双极化蝴蝶结偶极子天线，开展偏振旋转测量实验，分析其性能。

Result: 成功实现对天线两个正交线极化响应的表征，并验证其在偏振旋转测量中的可用性，支撑SWAN阵列单元响应理解。

Conclusion: 该天线是一种适用于教学与基础科研的可靠、低成本偏振敏感射电接收前端，其表征结果有助于提升SWAN网络整体极化测量精度。

Abstract: We have set up a simple experiment to characterise a dual-polarised bowtie dipole antenna at St. Stephens College as part of undergraduate radio astronomy experiments. The antenna provides two orthogonal linear polarisation responses, allowing us to investigate the polarisation properties of radio-frequency signals using a simple and affordable experimental setup. In this article, we present our analysis of the performance of the antenna and demonstrate its use through polarisation-rotation measurements. This antenna is part of the Sky Watch Array Network (SWAN) and this analysis helps us understand the response of each individual array element in the setup.

</details>


### [280] [A Multi-Cycle Treasury Survey of Andromeda: Comprehensive, All-Encompassing Spectroscopy, Imaging, and Mid-Infrared Mapping of the Nearest Giant Spiral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15901)
*David M. Nataf,David L. Nidever,Gagandeep Anand,Magda Arnaboldi,Joss Bland-Hawthorn,Scott Call,Santi Cassisi,Marcio Catelan,Julianne Dalcanton,Ivanna Escala,Mario Gennaro,Benjamin Gibson,Karoline Gilbert,Michael Hayden,Danny Horta,Caroline Huang,Rodrigo Ibata,Christian Johnson,Evan Kirby,Chiaki Kobayashi,Andrea Kunder,Geraint Lewis,Anna Liptrott,Massimo Marengo,Melissa Ness,Rachel Pauline,Henry Prager,Zdenek Prudil,Henrique Reggiani,Michael Rich,Ricardo Schiavon,Sanjib Sharma,Brian Thorsbro,Yuan-Sen Ting,Dan Weisz,Rosemary Wyse,Gail Zasowski,Catherine Zucker*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项约800小时的多周期詹姆斯·韦布太空望远镜（JWST）观测计划，旨在对仙女座星系（M31）开展类似银河系APOGEE、Gaia和WISE巡天的综合多波段普查，获取其化学动力学、恒星形成与尘埃分布的高分辨率星族级地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为弥补当前缺乏对其他L*星系（如M31）进行星族级、多维（化学、运动学、光度、尘埃）联合测绘的空白，借鉴银河系巡天范式，利用JWST独特能力实现类银河系尺度的外部星系解析研究。

Method: 设计包含12个‘砖块’（bricks）的多仪器协同观测策略：每个砖块含3个相邻视场，NIRSpec（中高分辨率近红外光谱）、NIRCam（多波段近红外成像）与MIRI（中红外成像）以主-辅模式并行观测，提升时间效率（~40%曝光时间零成本）；覆盖核球、星系盘（主/次轴）、旋臂间区域及HST-PHAT区域。

Result: 预期获得：~4500颗红巨星/超巨星的高信噪比光谱（16种元素精度<0.05 dex，RV精度数km/s）；~2000万颗恒星的双历元NIRCam测光（涵盖~500颗造父变星与~10^4颗米拉变星）；PHANGS深度的MIRI中红外图像；首次构建基于单星解析的M31全维度物理地图。

Conclusion: 该计划将首次实现对银河系外L*星系的星族级、多物理量（化学丰度、运动学、恒星形成历史、尘埃分布）联合测绘，其空间与物理分辨率远超单周期项目，为理解星系形成与演化提供不可替代的基准样本。

Abstract: We propose a Multi-Cycle Treasury program of $\sim$800 hours that would survey Andromeda the way the Milky Way has been surveyed by APOGEE, Gaia, and WISE.
  - NIRSpec: spectra of $\sim$4,500 red giants and supergiants at S/N~80, sufficient for 16 elements at $\lesssim$0.05 dex precision and radial velocities to a few km s$^{-1}$.
  - NIRCam: $\sim$1,600 arcmin$^{2}$ in up to ten filters, reaching $\sim$20 million stars, every field imaged twice a year apart: two-epoch infrared photometry of the $\sim$500 Cepheids and $\sim$10$^{4}$ Miras in the footprint.
  - MIRI: $\sim$450 arcmin$^{2}$ in five filters at the depth of PHANGS.
  The observations are predominantly organized as twelve "bricks", each of three adjacent fields including two NIRSpec/MOS pointings, in which one instrument is prime and a second observes in parallel, so that roughly 40% of the exposure time comes at no charge. The bricks sample the nucleus, bulge, major axis to 29 kpc, minor axis, and inter-arm disk. In addition, a 100-hour grid of short NIRCam pointings with MIRI in parallel covers the HST-PHAT footprint. The result would be the first chemodynamical, star-formation, and dust map of an $L^{\star}$ galaxy other than our own built from individual stars, at a scale no single-cycle program can reach.

</details>


### [281] [SimpleClock: a satellite-disciplined oscillator for astronomical instrumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16023)
*Albert Wai Kit Lau,Bruno Pollarolo,Juan Mena-Parra*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: SimpleClock是一种低成本、高稳定性的天文实验计时参考设备，结合GNSS接收器、可调10 MHz MEMS恒温晶振、外部热调控与自主控制，硬件成本约500美元，具备多种定位模式支持和优异的长期稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为天文实验（如低频VLBI）提供低成本、高稳定性、易于部署的精密计时参考源，弥补传统原子钟成本高、体积大、功耗高的不足。

Method: 设计并实现SimpleClock硬件系统，集成GNSS接收器、数字可调MEMS OCXO、主动热控与自主反馈控制；通过三单元相位比对分离噪声源，并在五种配置下进行200,000秒连续测试，计算Allan偏差与时间偏差。

Result: 在1000秒平均时间内，等效单单元差分Allan偏差为3.5×10⁻¹³–1.0×10⁻¹²，时间偏差为115–447 ps；1–50,000秒范围内最大时间偏差为143–1090 ps；支持PPP（Galileo HAS）、SBAS及独立运行。

Conclusion: SimpleClock在性能接近中端铷钟的同时显著降低成本与复杂度，验证了其在射电天文定时应用（尤其是低频VLBI）中的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: SimpleClock is a low-cost, high-stability timing reference designed for astronomical experiments that combines a global navigation satellite system (GNSS) timing receiver, a digitally tunable 10 MHz microelectromechanical systems (MEMS) oven-controlled oscillator, external thermal regulation and autonomous control. It provides 10 MHz and pulse-per-second references at an estimated prototype hardware cost of US\$500. Independently enabled frequency and phase feedback support precise point positioning (PPP) with Galileo's High Accuracy Service (HAS), satellite-based augmentation system (SBAS) solutions and standalone operation. Three-unit phase comparisons separate component noise and characterize disciplined-output stability over 200\,000 s recordings. Across five configurations, each analysed over 200\,000 s, equivalent single-unit differential Allan deviation and time deviation at 1000 s span $3.5\times10^{-13}$--$1.0\times10^{-12}$ and 115--447 ps, respectively. The maximum equivalent time deviation over averaging times from 1 to 50\,000 s spans 143--1090 ps. These results support applications in astronomical timing, including low-frequency very long baseline interferometry (VLBI).

</details>

Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了欧几里得任务近红外处理函数（NIR PF）在首次主要数据发布（DR1）中的更新与性能评估，重点改进了探测器级处理、校准、图像重建和质量控制，并验证其达到亚角毫秒级的天体测量精度和约0.9%的测光精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足欧几里得任务对大天区覆盖下高精度天体测量和测光的需求，需对初始发布的近红外处理流程（Q1版）进行系统性升级，以支撑科学目标实现。

Method: 对NIR PF进行重构，涵盖探测器级处理、校准、图像重建与质量控制模块；基于DR1实际观测数据（>1900 deg²，71659幅NIR图像），开展系统性的天体测量（RMS精度）和测光（时间稳定性、相对精度、空间均匀性）验证。

Result: 天体测量平均RMS达10 mas，99%的dither点低于20 mas；仪器透过率时变校正达2 mmag RMS；Y_E/J_E/H_E波段19等以内星源相对测光精度为0.9%，焦平面内残余测光空间变化约10 mmag。

Conclusion: DR1版NIR PF整体性能满足任务要求，尽管存在少量局限，其生成的近红外成像产品已在大面积巡天中展现出可靠的天体测量与测光一致性。

Abstract: The Euclid near-infrared processing function (NIR PF) converts the raw observations acquired by the Near-Infrared Spectrometer and Photometer (NISP) into calibrated images and source-level products. Following the first implementation delivered with the Euclid Quick Data Release 1 (Q1), the NIR PF has been revised for the first major Euclid Data Release (DR1), with improvements to detector-level processing, calibration, image reconstruction, and quality control. The DR1 processing includes observations covering more than $1900\,\rm{deg}^2$ and has produced $71\,659$ calibrated near-infrared (NIR) images. We assess the performance of the processing chain through extensive astrometric and photometric validation. The astrometric solution reaches an average root-mean-square (RMS) of 10 mas, remaining below 20 mas for 99% of the dithers in all three NISP bands. Temporal variations in the instrumental throughput are typically corrected to a level of 2 mmag RMS, while the relative photometric accuracy reaches 0.9% for sources brighter than 19 mag in $Y_\mathrm{E}$, $J_\mathrm{E}$, and $H_\mathrm{E}$, and the residual spatial variations of the photometric calibration across the focal plane are at the level of approximately 10 mmag for the entire sample. Despite some remaining limitations, the DR1 NIR imaging products are consistent with the required astrometric and photometric performance over the large area covered by this release.

</details>
