TL;DR: 本文介绍了欧几里得任务科学地面段（SGS）为数据发布1（DR1）所实现的系统架构与运行模式，涵盖组织结构、核心组件（如欧几里得档案系统EAS）、处理流程、日常运维及DR1生产执行情况，验证了其在大规模分布式计算环境下的鲁棒性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧几里得任务需通过高精度巡天探测暗能量与暗物质，这对科学地面段（SGS）的数据处理能力、校准精度和系统可靠性提出了极高要求。

Method: 采用分布式系统架构，整合科学运行中心、科学数据中心、仪器运行团队等单元；构建基于统一数据模型的欧几里得档案系统（EAS），包含分布式存储、数据处理系统与科学档案；使用基础设施抽象层（IAL）实现工作流编排与计算资源抽象；依托公共开发环境支持软件全生命周期管理；并实施标准化的日运行、定标、质量控制及DR1批量处理流程。

Result: 成功支撑欧几里得DR1发布，完成3500万次作业、覆盖9个科学数据中心、累计消耗670万核时，验证了SGS在大规模、高并发、跨地域环境下的鲁棒性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: SGS是一个高度集成、可扩展且稳健的分布式科学数据处理系统，其架构设计与运行实践为未来大型天文巡天项目提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The Euclid mission is designed to address fundamental questions on the nature of dark energy and dark matter by mapping the extragalactic sky with a wide-area survey complemented by targeted deep observations. The required scientific performance places stringent demands on the Euclid Science Ground Segment (SGS), which transforms raw telemetry into calibrated, validated, and scientifically exploitable products. The SGS is a distributed system comprising the Euclid Science Operations Centre, the Science Data Centres, Instrument Operation Teams, and supporting Organisation Units, within a common architectural and procedural framework. This paper presents the SGS as implemented for Data Release 1 (DR1), describing its organisation, architecture, processing functions, and operational model. We outline the main components of the SGS common infrastructure. A key element is the Euclid Archive System (EAS), the central metadata repository based on a common data model. It comprises a Distributed Storage System, providing scalable storage of data files across the Science Data Centres; a Data Processing System used by the pipelines to register and access files and their lineage; and a Science Archive, distributing validated data products. Processing is coordinated by a common orchestration framework based on the Infrastructure Abstraction Layer, providing workflow management and resource abstraction of distributed compute clusters. Software development, integration, and deployment are supported by the Common Development Environment. We describe daily operations, calibration, and quality control, followed by the DR1 production campaign, including processing flows, validation strategy, and computational and data volumes. This infrastructure enabled Euclid DR1, involving 35 million jobs across nine Science Data Centres and 6.7 million core-hours, demonstrating robustness and scalability of the SGS.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文介绍了欧几里得任务近红外图像在第一数据发布中的天体测量校准方法与结果，实现了97%区域30毫角秒的精度，超出发布要求，并针对剩余3%区域的余辉问题提出修正方法，同时讨论了校准稳定性及未来改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 满足欧几里得任务第一数据发布对天体测量精度的严格要求，并解决实际观测中出现的仪器畸变与余辉等系统误差问题。

Method: 构建完整的天体测量校准流程，包括建模与校正仪器畸变、利用Gaia DR3星表评估残差、开展质量控制测试及内部一致性分析，并针对余辉效应提出专门修正方法。

Result: 在校准覆盖的97%区域内达到30毫角秒的天体测量精度，优于数据发布要求；剩余3%区域因余辉校正不足导致精度下降，所提修正方法可有效改善；校准解在时间、源特性与仪器配置上表现稳定。

Conclusion: 当前校准流程已成功支撑欧几里得第一数据发布，具备高精度与良好稳定性；后续将优化余辉处理与整体效率，进一步提升未来数据发布的天体测量性能。

Abstract: This paper presents the methodology and results of the astrometric calibration applied to the Euclid Near-Infrared (NIR) images in the Euclid Data Release 1, focusing on the techniques used to model and correct instrumental distortions, as well as the assessment of calibration quality. This work details the calibration pipeline, including quality control tests, and the evaluation of astrometric residuals against the Gaia DR3 catalogue and internal consistency metrics based on data encompassing more than one year of Euclid operation: the achieved accuracy is 30 mas for 97% of the total area, thus exceeding the requirements for Data Release 1. For the remaining 3%, accuracy declines due to a persistence correction issue: a method to correct the issue is presented. Furthermore, we discuss the stability of the solution over time, its dependence on source properties and instrument configuration, and outline the improvements planned for future Euclid data releases to enhance the astrometric calibration accuracy and efficiency.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文利用Euclid卫星NISP仪器在测光模式下采集的图像，对单帧和组合帧中检测到的源进行点扩散函数（PSF）建模，基于Q1和DR1数据优化近红外处理函数（NIR PF）中的PSF模块，并评估模型质量及其随焦平面位置、波长和观测时间的变化。结果表明，相比Q1模型，更新后的PSF拟合模型在通量估计上更优，基本满足任务关键要求，但仍需进一步改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升Euclid卫星NISP仪器近红外数据处理中PSF建模的精度，以满足弱引力透镜等科学目标对星像形变测量的高精度需求。

Method: 基于Q1和DR1实测数据，对单帧与叠加帧中的选定源进行PSF建模；采用多种常规评估方法分析模型质量，并考察其随焦平面位置、波长及观测时间的变化；将PSF拟合法与孔径测光等通量估计方法对比验证性能。

Result: 更新后的PSF模型在通量估计精度上优于Q1版本；当前模型满足多项任务关键指标要求；发现模型性能存在空间、波长和时间依赖性。

Conclusion: PSF建模已取得实质性进展，但仍有优化空间，后续工作将聚焦于这些系统性变化因素的建模改进，并在后续软件版本中实现。

Abstract: The images collected in photometric mode by the Near-Infrared Spectrometer and Photometer (NISP) instrument of the Euclid satellite are used to model the point-spread function (PSF) of a selection of sources detected in single and combined frames. We used Q1 and DR1 data to improve the development of the PSF module of the near-infrared processing function (NIR PF) and to refine the models currently provided. We explored a few methods normally used to evaluate the quality of the derived models and discuss possible variations related to the position of the sources in the focal plane and to the wavelength and time of the observations. The procedure of fitting the PSF profile, compared to other methods used for flux estimation, such as aperture photometry, indicates that updated models are better than those provided by Q1 images. The current results are in agreement with several key requirements defined for the mission, but there is still room for further improvements, which we plan to investigate and implement in future releases.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文综述了欧几里得（Euclid）任务顶层定标需求如何转化为定标计划，并评估其在DR1数据发布中的实现效果与性能，同时介绍了为DR2改进的多项定标修正策略及与其他望远镜的合成测光转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Euclid的顶层定标需求转化为可执行、可维护的定标计划，并评估其在实际巡天运行与数据发布（DR1）中的表现，以支撑后续科学分析（如星系形态测量、光谱样本构建、弱引力透镜等）。

Method: 回顾定标需求转化过程；分析DR1数据中各项定标性能（如光谱测光、PSF建模、剪切校准等）；识别并表征DR1重处理中发现的新效应；开发针对DR2的多项定标修正方法；构建Euclid与Roman、JWST、CSST之间的合成测光转换关系。

Result: DR1已满足或超越多数光谱测光定标要求；深场光谱完备纯样本构建依赖持续观测；PSF建模与剪切定标仍在收敛；识别出绝对流量定标、天体测量、电荷滞留、快门效应、波长定标及水冰污染等新问题并提出DR2修正方案；完成了与三大空间望远镜的合成测光转换。

Conclusion: Euclid定标体系总体有效，DR1成果坚实，但部分高精度要求（如剪切定标）仍需后续数据与算法优化；DR2将通过多项针对性修正显著提升数据质量，并增强多信使、多平台协同分析能力。

Abstract: This paper reviews how Euclid's top-level calibration requirements were translated into a calibration plan, how this plan is implemented and maintained during the survey, and how it performs in the current DR1 release. We show that DR1 already meets or exceeds most end-of-mission spectrophotometric calibration requirements. The construction of spectroscopically complete and pure samples relies on ongoing data acquisition for the Euclid Deep Survey, while point-spread-function modelling and shear calibration are still converging to their stringent accuracy requirements. With all DR1 data reprocessed with the DR1 pipeline configuration, more subtle effects have been detected and characterised, and correction strategies have been developed for DR2. These include improved absolute flux calibration, astrometry, charge-persistence correction, shutter effects, wavelength calibration, and spectrophotometric corrections for water-ice contamination. Finally, synthetic photometric transformations between Euclid and each of Roman, the James Webb Space Telescope, and the Chinese Space-station Survey Telescope, are presented.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Euclid空间望远镜在轨三年来的关键性能表现，涵盖热光/机械稳定性、杂散光抑制、出气率、精密导星等，验证了其设计稳健性，并揭示了若干新物理现象（如黄道光调制、辐射压与热反冲效应）及微流星体撞击特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估Euclid任务在轨三年后在热光-机械稳定性、杂散光抑制、低出气率和精密导星等核心设计指标上的实际性能，验证任务可行性并支持后续数据分析与运行优化。

Method: 基于在轨遥测数据与科学观测数据，开展多维度性能分析：包括L2晕轨道与推进剂预算审查、杂散光时变建模、热响应曲线拟合、水冰污染监测与除污策略评估、黄道光背景高精度测量、导星精度统计、辐射压与热反冲效应识别，以及微流星体撞击事件识别与质量估算。

Result: 发现太阳杂散光年增14%（与微流星体穿孔一致）；热平衡需21–46小时；水冰污染持续存在；黄道光呈现8σ显著轨道调制；杂散光预估被高估4–27倍；实现高精度导星并首次探测到太阳辐射压与热反冲；识别34次微流星体撞击，最小可探测撞击质量为5×10⁻⁷ g。

Conclusion: Euclid平台整体性能优异且稳定，多数设计指标超预期；所获数据不仅支撑巡天科学目标，还催生新的空间环境与仪器物理认知，为未来空间天文任务提供重要工程与科学参考。

Abstract: The Euclid mission was designed around the need for thermo-optical and mechanical stability, stray-light suppression, low outgassing, and precise guiding. After three years in space, a wealth of data is available to assess the performance in these areas. We review the L2 halo-orbit design and consumables budget. Solar stray-light gradually increases by 14\% per year and is consistent with meteoroid puncturing, but remains low overall. Data loss from high solar activity has declined notably since the maximum of the 25th Solar cycle passed. The thermodynamical response of the optical elements to a thermal step impulse follows sigmoidal growth curves, entering the asymptotic equilibration regime after 21--46 hours. Water-ice contamination has affected data from both instruments and requires continued monitoring; we review detection and decontamination methods, and Euclid's contamination history. High-precision measurements of the zodiacal background reveal, at eight sigma significance, the subtle modulation caused by the changing perspective on the dust distribution due to the L2 halo-orbit libration. These data also show that conservative pre-launch estimates overestimated the out-of-field stray-light by factors of 4 (H band) to 27 (I band). Telemetry data demonstrate excellent guiding, and enable highly significant detections of solar radiation pressure and of thermal-emission recoil from Euclid's unilluminated dark sides. Lastly, we present a list of 34 meteoroid impacts, discuss their mechanical and optical signatures, and derive a minimum detectable impactor mass of 5e-7 g.

</details>


### [288] [Direct estimation of the lunar-solar precession with geodetic very long baseline interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16549)
*Oleg Titov,Angelina Osetrova*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文利用大地测量VLBI延迟率观测，独立于行星运动理论估计了岁差参数和章动系数，构建了从瞬时旋转极（IRP）到地心天球参考系（GCRS）的完整转换矩阵，并引入自由核章动（FCN）效应，提出了一个不依赖非旋转原点概念的简单解析模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用VLBI延迟率独立估计岁差参数，摆脱对行星运动理论的依赖，并构建更简洁、完整的ITRS到GCRS转换模型。

Method: 基于VLBI延迟率观测估计岁差参数和章动系数，结合自由核章动（FCN）效应，构建从ITRS到GCRS的解析转换模型。

Result: 获得了一个经验性的ITRS到GCRS转换模型；估算的月-日岁差参数为ψ_A = 5038.500 ± 0.019"/cy，与IAU2006值（5038.4815"/cy）高度一致；新模型直接定义IRP方向，无需非旋转原点假设。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种基于纯观测、不依赖理论先验的岁差-章动建模途径，提升了地球定向参数建模的自主性与精度。

Abstract: Geodetic very long baseline interferometry (VLBI) is capable of measuring the Earth's instantaneous rotation pole (IRP) components (hereafter, $X_{IRP}$ and $Y_{IRP}$) with sufficient accuracy using the VLBI delay rate. The least-squares fit of the $X_{IRP}$ and $Y_{IRP}$ polynomial expansion coefficients produces the precession model parameters independently of planetary motion theories. Our aim was to estimate the precession parameters using VLBI delay rate observations as well as the nutation coefficients in order to produce a full transformation matrix from the IRP to the Geocentric Celestial Reference System (GCRS). Then, the goal was to incorporate the free core nutation (FCN) effect from the VLBI group delay to form a complete and simple analytical model. We obtained an empirical model of the transformation from International Terrestrial Reference System (ITRS) to Geocentric Celestial Reference System (GCRS) based on observational results. Similar to the IAU2006 theory, the new model produces time series of $X_{IRP}$ and $Y_{IRP}$ angles, but these two angles now point out the direction of the IRP rather than the celestial intermediate pole (CIP). A simple analytical model defines the IRP location for any epoch during the interval of observations. Therefore, the non-rotation origin concept is not required for the transformation matrix construction. The estimate of the lunar-solar precessional parameter based on analysis of VLBI delay rate is $ψ_{A}= 5038.500\pm 0.019"$/cy (referred to the observational epoch 2000.0), which is close to the adopted IAU2006 precessional parameter value $ψ_{A}= 5038.4815"$/cy.

</details>


### [289] [Metadata for the Flux Density Calibration of the April 2021 and March 2022 Event Horizon Telescope Science Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16606)
*Cristina Romero-Cañizales,Jun Yi Koay,Lindy Blackburn,Lynn D. Matthews,Vincent Fish,Remo P. J. Tilanus,Geoffrey B. Crew,Keiichi Asada,Michael Bremer,Chin-Shin Chang,Per Friberg,Ciriaco Goddi,Garret Keating,Ryan P. Keenan,Junhan Kim,Thomas P. Krichbaum,Dan P. Marrone,Satoki Matsushita,Izumi Mizuno,Juan Pablo Pérez-Beaupuits,Dominic W. Pesce,Vincent Piétu,Venkatessh Ramakrishnan,Alan L. Roy,Ignacio Ruíz,Salvador Sánchez,Pablo Torne,Michael Janßen,Dhanya G. Nair,Iniyan Natarajan,Sebastiano D. von Fellenberg*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了2021年4月和2022年3月事件视界望远镜（EHT）在1.3毫米波长下的观测元数据，延续了2018年数据的处理流程，并更新了部分台站的灵敏度与增益曲线处理方法，同时描述了自2018年以来EHT阵列的变更。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持EHT后续科学分析，需系统整理并发布2021–2022年观测的元数据，确保其与早期数据处理流程一致且具备可比性。

Method: 沿用2018年EHT数据初始幅度校准所采用的元数据采集、处理与验证流程，并针对部分台站调整灵敏度建模与增益曲线处理方式；同时汇总并说明EHT阵列配置的变化。

Result: 发布了2021年4月和2022年3月EHT观测的标准化元数据集，包含更新后的台站灵敏度与增益曲线信息，并明确了阵列组成变化。

Conclusion: 该元数据发布提升了EHT多时段观测数据的一致性与可复用性，为黑洞成像及时变研究提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: The Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) has been conducting science observations annually since 2021 after the initial campaigns in 2017 and 2018. In this memo, we report on the metadata associated to the observations from 2021 April and 2022 March at 1.3mm wavelengths. We follow a similar path for the collection, processing, and validation of the metadata used for the initial amplitude calibration of the 2018 EHT data. In this release, we introduce some differences regarding the treatment of sensitivities and gain curves for some stations. In addition, we describe the changes to the EHT array since 2018.

</details>

Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了欧几里得任务NISP近红外仪器中探测器残留信号（persistence）的特性、建模与影响，发现其在焦平面上高度不均匀，幅度通常低于激励信号的1%，衰减速率随源亮度增加而加快；在轨运行中残留信号整体下降约10%，但衰减率基本稳定；过饱和情况下的残留行为偏离经验模型；科学观测中测得的残留与暗流标定结果存在系统性差异，高能粒子击中产生的残留也不同于天文源；文章还提出了Q1和DR1数据发布中用于掩蔽受残留影响像素的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测器残留是红外观测中重要的系统误差来源，严重影响Euclid NISP的科学性能，需通过地面标定与在轨数据联合表征其特性并评估对数据处理和科学分析的影响。

Method: 结合地面标定与在轨观测数据，开展残留信号的表征；建立其时间演化经验模型；分析其在焦平面的空间非均匀性、非线性响应、衰减特性及与激励通量的关系；比较不同源（天文目标、过饱和信号、高能粒子）引发的残留差异；评估其对平场校准、背景测量及科学数据处理流程的影响；制定像素级掩蔽策略。

Result: 发现残留信号在焦平面上变化达两个数量级；幅度通常<1%激励信号，衰减速率随刺激强度增大而加快；在轨期间总残留下降约10%，衰减率基本稳定；过饱和残留不满足常规经验关系；科学数据中残留显著区别于暗流标定结果；高能粒子引起的残留行为不同；已为Q1和DR1发布制定了基于持续时间和像素位置的掩蔽方案。

Conclusion: NISP探测器残留具有复杂时空依赖性和源依赖性，不能仅靠地面标定准确预测；必须结合在轨实测建模，并在数据处理中采用针对性掩蔽策略以保障科学数据质量。

Abstract: Detector persistence is a significant source of systematic error in infrared observations and affects the scientific performance of the \Euclid Near Infrared Spectrometer and Photometer (NISP). We characterise persistence using complementary ground calibration and in-flight data, develop empirical models of its temporal evolution, and assess its impact on science observations and the \Euclid data-processing pipeline. Persistence is highly non-uniform across the focal plane, with variations of up to two orders of magnitude between and within detectors. Its amplitude is typically below 1\% of the stimulus signal and depends nonlinearly on the preceding fluence. The decay rate is faster for brighter sources. During mission operations, the persistence signal has progressively decreased by roughly 10\%, while the decay rate has remained approximately stable. Persistence following over-saturating signals does not follow the empirical relations derived for under-saturating fluences and plausibly reduces persistence response over longer timescales. The impact of charge trapping and release on flat-field observations and on the background in science exposures is also discussed. Persistence measured in science observations is observed to differ systematically from that derived from dark calibration data. In addition, persistence of energetic particle hits is observed to be different from that of astronomical sources of comparable signal. Finally, we discuss the impact of persistence on \Euclid NISP observations and describe the masking strategy adopted for the Q1 abd DR1 releases, focusing on identifying which pixels are affected by persistence and for how long they should be excluded from science observations.

</details>


### [291] [A parametrized simulation code to simulate the response of ground detector arrays to Extensive Air Showers above 100 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16906)
*Andrea Chiavassa,Andrea Negro*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过参数化探测器响应来评估大型地面水切伦科夫探测器阵列性能的新方法，以替代计算资源消耗巨大的传统CORSIKA+GEANT4两步模拟流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CORSIKA+GEANT4联合模拟在高能段（>30–100 TeV）和大填充因子下计算资源需求过高，亟需更高效的方法评估阵列性能、伽马/强子分离能力及灵敏度。

Method: 提出并实现一种基于探测器响应参数化的快速模拟方法，替代完整的物理级蒙特卡洛模拟，并对其进行了验证测试。

Result: 成功开发并验证了该参数化软件工具，并将其应用于一个典型未来地面水切伦科夫探测器阵列的性能评估中。

Conclusion: 该参数化方法可在显著降低计算成本的同时，可靠地评估EAS探测器阵列的关键性能指标，适用于高能伽马射线天文模拟研究。

Abstract: Surface arrays detecting the secondary particles of Extensive Air Showers (EAS) are one of the techniques used to detect gamma-rays from high-energy sources. To evaluate the reconstruction performance, gamma/hadron separation, and sensitivity of such experiments, many simulations of gamma rays and protons are required. The usual approach is divided into two steps. First, the CORSIKA software is used to generate EAS at the observation level. Then, a GEANT4-based full simulation code is used to simulate the array response. For very large fill factors, above 30-100 TeV, the computational resources required become too demanding. In this work, the authors present an alternative way to evaluate the performance-related quantities of a simulated array configuration through the parametrization of the detector response. We will present the implementation of such software, the validation checks we performed, and the application of this tool to a representative future ground-based water-Cherenkov detector array.

</details>


### [292] [The astrometric precision of incoherent pulsar timing arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17069)
*Anna C. Tsai,Dylan L. Jow,Ue-Li Pen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）在脉冲星距离远小于引力波波长时，对单个天体引力波源的天体测量定位精度。通过匹配滤波和制图模拟，发现中位角误差随信噪比（SNR）呈特定对数衰减关系，并指出IPTA DR2的实际定位精度受脉冲星空间分布稀疏性影响，仅14%天区能达到理论最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在脉冲星距离远小于引力波波长的条件下，PTA对引力波源的天体测量定位精度受限于脉冲星几何分布而非信号强度，需定量刻画其内在极限及实际阵列性能差异。

Method: 采用简单匹配滤波与地图重建模拟，推导角误差与信噪比（SNR）的解析关系，引入卷积核奇异核心导致的对数修正项，并将理论极限与国际脉冲星计时阵列（IPTA）DR2的实际脉冲星构型进行天空平均与局部精度对比。

Result: 理论内在定位精度在SNR=6时为3.3°；IPTA DR2实际中位精度为8.9°，但14%天区因局部脉冲星密度高而优于3.3°；角误差随SNR按√(ln2/3)/[SNR√ln(SNR)]衰减。

Conclusion: PTA的天体测量精度不仅取决于信噪比，更显著受脉冲星空间采样密度影响；理论极限仅在局部高密度区可达，凸显优化脉冲星布局长期重要性。

Abstract: We address the astrometric precision with which a pulsar timing array (PTA) can localize a single astrophysical gravitational-wave source, in the regime where pulsar distances are poorly constrained compared to the gravitational wavelength. Using simple matched-filter, map-making simulations, we calculate the astrometric precision of the intrinsic PTA residual response as a function of the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), where the SNR is that of the source in the matched-filter map, and find that the median angular error decreases as $\sqrt{\ln 2/3}\,/[\mathrm{SNR}\sqrt{\ln\mathrm{SNR}}]$ over the relevant SNR range, with a pre-factor we derive from first principles. At an SNR of 6, this log term coming from the singular core of the convolution kernel produces an angular error approximately $1.4$ times smaller than for a Gaussian beam. We compare this sky-averaged maximum precision with the precision achieved across the sky by the pulsars of the International Pulsar Timing Array (IPTA) DR2. For a map with SNR of 6, the intrinsic array precision is $3.3^\circ$. For the IPTA configuration at the same SNR, the median precision over the sky is worse due to sparse sampling of the underlying pulsar residual response, approximately $8.9^\circ$; however, for sources in $14\%$ of the sky, the high local pulsar density yields a precision better than $3.3^\circ$.

</details>


### [293] [Crest factor optimization of multi-tone signals generated for low-temperature sensor readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17207)
*L. H. Arnaldi*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文综述了大规模微波动能电感探测器（MKID）阵列（约10,000像素）的读出性能要求，重点提出一种相位生成优化算法以降低载波信号的峰均比（crest factor），从而提升电子链路的动态范围利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足射电天文和宇宙微波背景（CMB）观测中对大规模MKID阵列（~10,000像素）高效、高保真读出的需求，需解决高 crest factor 导致的动态范围浪费问题。

Method: 提出一种用于优化多载波激励信号相位生成的算法，以系统性降低合成激励信号的 crest factor。

Result: 该算法可有效降低大规模MKID阵列激励信号的 crest factor，从而提升读出电子链路的动态范围使用效率。

Conclusion: crest factor 优化是实现大规模MKID阵列高性能读出的关键环节，所提相位优化算法为未来大型低温探测器项目提供了实用技术支撑。

Abstract: Microwave kinetic inductance detectors (MKID) are a growing technology of cryogenic detectors and have a great potential in radio-astronomy and cosmic microwave background (CMB) characterization. A review of the general readout performance requirements for experiments requiring the multiplexing of large arrays (~10.000 pixels) of such cryogenic detectors is presented. The focus is on the crest factor optimization process for proper excitation and readout of these large arrays of detectors. It is shown an algorithm to optimize the phase generation of the carriers to obtain a low crest factor, favoring the dynamic range usage of the electronic chain.

</details>


### [294] [Peak Performance in Matched Filtering: Fused Correlation and Statistically Controlled Screening on CPUs and GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17229)
*Alexander H. Nitz,Keisi Kacanja,Collin D. Capano*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合峰值检测与分层筛选的高效匹配滤波方法，显著提升了引力波、地震学和射电瞬变源搜索中的大规模模板匹配计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量匹配滤波和互相关在引力波天文学、地震学和射电瞬变搜索中计算开销巨大，传统流程存在内存带宽瓶颈和冗余计算问题。

Method: 将共轭乘积融合到逆FFT蝶形运算首阶段、峰值归约融合到最后阶段，并采用2D批处理以重用缓存频谱；同时提出基于频谱功率分布和噪声自协方差的分层筛选方法，按低频到全频顺序逐步筛选并设定各阶段阈值。

Result: 在AMD Ryzen AI Max+ 395上，融合峰值过滤比AOCL-FFTW快1.4倍（CPU单核），比rocFFT快6.2倍（GPU）；分层筛选在典型引力波搜索中实现最高26倍加速。

Conclusion: 所提方法将计算瓶颈从DRAM带宽转向片上缓存带宽，并通过分层筛选大幅减少无效计算，在多种CPU/GPU架构上均取得显著加速效果。

Abstract: Batched matched filtering and cross-correlation over large template banks are a primary computational cost in gravitational-wave astronomy, seismology, and radio transient searches. Standard pipelines pair a general-purpose inverse Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) with separate multiplication and peak-finding kernels, writing intermediate spectra and full correlation series to memory for every template. We fuse the conjugate product into the first inverse-transform butterflies and the peak reduction into the last so only candidate peaks leave processor registers, and tile batches in 2D so $K^2$ correlations reuse $2K$ cached spectra. This shifts filtering from a DRAM-bandwidth-bound to an on-chip cache-bandwidth-bound regime. In some situations, the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) is concentrated toward lower frequencies while higher frequencies dominate the transform length. We develop a hierarchical screening method that exploits this via a sequence of short low-frequency transforms before evaluating the full band, deriving stage thresholds from the spectral power profile and matched-filter noise autocovariance to target a specified false-dismissal probability. We demonstrate these methods in the open-source matchedfilter library across eight CPU architectures and two integrated GPUs (AMD Radeon 8060S via Vulkan and Apple M2 via Metal). On an AMD Ryzen AI Max+ 395 at $N=4096$, fused peak-only filtering is $1.4\times$ faster than a standalone AOCL-FFTW inverse transform on one CPU core and $6.2\times$ faster than rocFFT on its Radeon 8060S GPU ($3.1\times$ over MLX FFT on the M2 GPU). For a fiducial gravitational-wave search ($ρ_\mathrm{th}=5.5$, $10^{-2}$ false-dismissal target), hierarchical screening yields a $26\times$ speedup over AOCL-FFTW on CPU, $21\times$ over rocFFT on the 8060S GPU, and $19\times$ over MLX FFT on the M2 GPU.

</details>


### [295] [Estimating distances from parallaxes. VII. Computing the three-dimensional kinegeometric posterior with one-dimensional sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17231)
*Coryn A. L. Bailer-Jones*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更高效、更精确的计算kinegeometric后验分布的方法，将原本的3D（距离+速度）后验分解为1D距离后验与2D速度高斯分布之和，显著提升了大规模恒星样本的距离估计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当视差测量质量较差时，需结合自行数据和恒星物理速度约束来改进距离估计；而传统MCMC采样kinegeometric后验计算开销大，难以扩展至大量源。

Method: 通过数学推导证明kinegeometric后验等价于一个1维距离后验与多个2维高斯速度分布的组合，并采用该解析结构替代原有MCMC采样方法。

Result: 新方法在计算速度和精度上均优于原有MCMC方法，使Gaia DR3等大规模星表的kinegeometric距离推断更可行。

Conclusion: 1D+2D参数化解析表示是kinegeometric后验建模的有效替代方案，兼顾效率与准确性，适用于大样本银河系动力学分析。

Abstract: When parallaxes are of poor quality, proper motions can improve stellar distance estimates using known constraints on stars' physical velocities. Formally this may be achieved via the kinegeometric posterior, a three-dimensional (3D) posterior in distance and velocity for given measured parallax and 2D proper motion. This posterior is the product of a covariant Gaussian likelihood in the parallax and proper motion with a distance and velocity prior that can be constructed from a Galaxy model. In earlier work, I showed how to infer this posterior for stars in Gaia DR3 by explicitly sampling it with a Markov Chain Monte Carlo method. Here I show that this posterior is equivalent to a 1D posterior in distance combined with a sum of 2D Gaussian distributions in velocity. This 1D+2D representation is both faster and more accurate to sample, thereby making the computation of the kinegeometric posterior for a large set of sources much easier.

</details>


### [296] [Global Fit Inference for LISA with samsara++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17266)
*Lorenzo Valbusa Dall'Armi,Gabriele Astorino,Riccardo Buscicchio,Angelo Ricciardone,Walter Del Pozzo*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了samsara++，一个用于LISA引力波数据分析的C++/CUDA采样器，通过出生-死亡MCMC方法联合推断未知数量的引力波源、其参数及仪器噪声，实现了对大规模双白矮星种群的高效全局拟合分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA将观测到大量重叠（时频域）的引力波信号与噪声混合，需进行能同时处理变源数目、源参数和噪声的全局拟合（Global Fit）分析，而现有方法难以兼顾效率与准确性。

Method: 基于连续时间MCMC框架samsara，提出samsara++：采用后验依赖的出生-死亡过程增删源，并结合参数变异移动；引入共享内存并行、增量似然更新和索引存储以降计算与内存开销；使用并行退火提升多峰后验探索能力。

Result: 在模拟LISA一年数据（含143/866个银河系双白矮星及高斯噪声）上，从空模型出发成功重建源并生成星表；压力测试中处理16,435个双星（13.1万参数），1500万次迭代仅耗时约16小时、内存约3 GB。

Conclusion: samsara++验证了出生-死亡采样法对大规模银河系双星种群全局拟合的计算可行性，为构建完整LISA全局拟合分析框架奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe a superposition of gravitational-wave signals, requiring joint inference of an unknown number of sources, parameters, and instrumental noise. To disentangle these contributions, that overlap in both time and frequency, a Global Fit (GF) analysis is needed. We present samsara++, a C++/CUDA sampler designed to address this trans-dimensional GF problem. Building on the continuous-time MCMC framework of samsara, the algorithm combines birth-death processes for adding and removing sources, with mutation moves for parameter estimation. Birth and death are selected through posterior-dependent rates. Shared-memory parallelism, incremental likelihood updates, and indexed storage reduce the computational and memory costs, while parallel tempering improves the exploration of multimodal posteriors. We assess the performance using simulated one-year LISA datasets containing Galactic double white dwarf binaries and stationary Gaussian instrumental noise. Analyses of populations containing 143 and 866 binaries start from empty source models, without preliminary searches or proposals tailored to the injected population. These tests demonstrate source reconstruction and catalog generation, highlighting, at the same time, the importance of source-count, overlapping signals, and incomplete mixing on joint source and noise inference. Then we perform a separate stress test, initialized at the injected parameters, exploring a population of 16,435 binaries, corresponding to 131,480 source parameters. This run completes 15 million iterations in approximately 16 hours using about 3 GB of RAM. All together, these benchmarks show the computational feasibility of birth-death sampling for large Galactic-binary populations, and provide a foundation for extending samsara++ toward a comprehensive LISA global fit.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文详细描述了Euclid空间任务中用于数据发布1（DR1）的NISP近红外光谱仪SIR校准流水线，强调其全局焦平面建模对保证光谱波长和流量精度的关键作用，并验证了当前校准结果满足任务要求，同时指出了待优化方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于Euclid任务中NISP slitless光谱数据固有的复杂性，科学观测无法由科学流水线自校准，因此必须开发专用的SIR校准流水线，利用特定指向序列和观测场生成满足波长与流量精度要求的校准产品。

Method: 设计并实现面向Data Release 1（DR1）的SIR校准流水线，采用统一建模整个NISP焦平面的全局方法，以保障全视场的天体测量与光谱连续性；结合专用定标观测策略与数据处理流程生成校准产品。

Result: DR1提供了超过1.7亿条经验证的红、蓝光栅模式光谱；校准产品与科学光谱的综合分析表明，当前校准性能满足Euclid任务的严格指标要求。

Conclusion: SIR校准流水线成功支撑了Euclid DR1高质量光谱数据的交付，验证了全局焦平面建模的有效性；但仍有若干关键环节需持续优化，以提升后续数据发布的质量。

Abstract: The Euclid space mission is dedicated to probing the nature of dark energy and dark matter, through a comprehensive mapping of the Universe's large-scale structure. Central to these cosmological goals are the spectra produced by the Near-Infrared Spectrometer and Photometer (NISP) and reduced by the scientific pipelines of the SIR (Spectral near InfraRed) processing function. Due to the intrinsic complexity of slitless spectroscopic data, scientific observations cannot be self-calibrated by the science pipelines. Consequently, the development of dedicated SIR calibration pipelines, utilising specific pointing sequences and observation fields, is essential. The resulting calibration products are fundamental to ensuring that reduced spectra are characterised within requirements in both wavelength and flux. This paper provides a detailed description of the SIR calibration pipelines responsible for generating the products utilised by the science pipelines for Data Release 1 (DR1). A significant challenge in these calibration tasks arises from the requirement to model the entire focal plane as a unified entity. Such a global modelling approach is critical for maintaining astrometric and spectroscopic continuity across the entire field of view. DR1 provides more than 170 millions validated spectra in both red- and blue-grism modes. The accuracy of the reduced spectra is strictly dependent on the performance of the calibration pipelines. Comprehensive analyses of the calibration products and the resulting science spectra demonstrate that current outputs successfully meet the mission requirements. Nevertheless, specific areas for optimisation have been identified; ongoing development is focused on addressing these critical points to further enhance data quality for future data releases.

</details>
